## נ (id: BDB05814)

נ, ן, *Nûn*, fourteenth letter; used as numeral 50 in post B. Heb.



## I נָא (id: BDB05815)

\[Str4994] I. נָא **part. of entreaty** or **exhortation I** (we) pray, now (enclitic) (Syr. ܢܺܐ, ܢܺܝ; cf. Eth. ንኢ፡ *veni, age!* Di:§ 160\. 1 Lex675 Köii. 244\);—attached: **1\.** to the impv., esp. in colloquial style, when it expresses an entreaty or admonition, as [Gn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Gen12:13) אִמְרִי־נָא say, *I pray*, [13:9](https://ref.ly/Gen13:9) נָא מֵעָלַי הִפָּרֶ־ד, v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen13:14) שָא־נָא עֵינֶיְ, [15:5](https://ref.ly/Gen15:5) הַבֶּט־נָא השׁמימה, [24:2](https://ref.ly/Gen24:2) \+ oft., [Nu 20:10](https://ref.ly/Num20:10) שׁמעו־נא המוֺרים, Ju [13:4](https://ref.ly/Judg13:4), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Judg16:6), [16:10](https://ref.ly/Judg16:10), [16:28](https://ref.ly/Judg16:28), [18:5](https://ref.ly/Judg18:5), etc., [Is 1:18](https://ref.ly/Isa1:18), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Isa5:3), [Am 7:2](https://ref.ly/Amos7:2), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Amos7:5); rarely in a command, [Gn 22:2](https://ref.ly/Gen22:2), [Is 7:3](https://ref.ly/Isa7:3)). Ironically, in a challenge, [Is 47:12](https://ref.ly/Isa47:12), [Jb 40:10](https://ref.ly/Job40:10). Once anomalously attached to a subst., or (accents) prefixed to a verb, [Nu 12:13](https://ref.ly/Num12:13) אֵ֕ל נָאֶ רְפָ֥א נָא֭ (Ew: § 246 a Di אַל־נָא; v. **3 b**). **2\.** to the pf. with *waw* consec., in a precative sense (Dr§ 119 δ) [Gn 40:14](https://ref.ly/Gen40:14). **3\.** to the impf., when leave is asked, or a prayer or desire expressed; **a.** in 1 Pers., esp. with ה cohort., [Gn 19:8](https://ref.ly/Gen19:8) אוציאה־נא let me, *I pray*, bring out, v:[20](https://ref.ly/Gen19:20) אִמָּֽלְטָה־נָּא שָׁמָּה, [24:12](https://ref.ly/Gen24:12) הַקְרֵה־נָא לְפָנַי v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14) הַטִּי־נָא v:[17](https://ref.ly/Gen24:17), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen24:23), v:[43](https://ref.ly/Gen24:43), v:[45](https://ref.ly/Gen24:45), [25:30](https://ref.ly/Gen25:30), [32:12](https://ref.ly/Gen32:12), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Gen32:30) \+ oft., [Ex 3:18](https://ref.ly/Exod3:18) נלכה־נא let us go, *I pray*, [Nu 20:17](https://ref.ly/Num20:17), [Is 5:5](https://ref.ly/Isa5:5); and in self\-deliberation, [Gn 18:21](https://ref.ly/Gen18:21) ארדה־נּא I will go down, *now !* [Ex 3:3](https://ref.ly/Exod3:3) אסורה־נּא ואראה I will turn aside, *now*, and see, [2 S 14:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:15), Ct [3:2](https://ref.ly/Song3:2), [Is 5:1](https://ref.ly/Isa5:1); with אַל [Job 32:21](https://ref.ly/Job32:21). **b.** in 2 Pers., with אַל, in deprecation, [Gn 18:3](https://ref.ly/Gen18:3) אל־נא תעבר מעל עבדך, [19:7](https://ref.ly/Gen19:7), [47:29](https://ref.ly/Gen47:29), [Nu 10:31](https://ref.ly/Num10:31), etc.; hence ellipt. אל־נא do not, *I pray*, (do this,) [Gn 19:18](https://ref.ly/Gen19:18), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Gen33:10), [Nu 12:13](https://ref.ly/Num12:13) Ew Di. **c.** in 3 Pers., [Gn 18:4](https://ref.ly/Gen18:4) יֻקַּח־נָא let there be brought, *I pray*, [26:28](https://ref.ly/Gen26:28), [33:14](https://ref.ly/Gen33:14), [44:18](https://ref.ly/Gen44:18), [44:33](https://ref.ly/Gen44:33), etc., [2 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:11), [14:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:12), [14:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:17), [1 K 17:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:21), [2 K 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:9), [Psalm 7:10](https://ref.ly/Ps7:10), [118:2](https://ref.ly/Ps118:2), [118:3](https://ref.ly/Ps118:3); ironically [Is 19:12](https://ref.ly/Isa19:12), [47:13](https://ref.ly/Isa47:13), or defiantly Jer [17:15](https://ref.ly/Jer17:15) where is the word of J.? יָבוֺא נָא; with אַל, [Gn 13:8](https://ref.ly/Gen13:8) אַל־נָא תְהִי מְרִיבָה, [18:30](https://ref.ly/Gen18:30) אַל־נָא יִחַר לַאדֹנָי, [37:27](https://ref.ly/Gen37:27), [Nu 12:12](https://ref.ly/Num12:12). **4\.** joined to conjunctions and interjections: **a.** \[אָהּ־נָא, contr.] אָ֫נָּא; v. p. 58\. **b.** אַל־נָא see above **3 b, c. c.** אִם־נָא, esp. in the phrase אם נא מצאתי חן בעיניך, used by one craving a favourable hearing † [Gn 30:27](https://ref.ly/Gen30:27), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Gen33:10), Ju [6:17](https://ref.ly/Judg6:17), [1 S 27:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:5), and with נא repeated in the request itself †[Gn 18:3](https://ref.ly/Gen18:3) [47:29](https://ref.ly/Gen47:29) [50:4](https://ref.ly/Gen50:4), [Ex 33:13](https://ref.ly/Exod33:13), [34:9](https://ref.ly/Exod34:9); otherwise [Gn 24:42](https://ref.ly/Gen24:42). **d.** הִנֵּה־נָא behold, *I pray*, craving a favourable consideration of the fact pointed to by הִנֵּה, and of the request founded upon it (with which נא is often repeated), [Gn 12:11](https://ref.ly/Gen12:11), [16:2](https://ref.ly/Gen16:2), [18:27](https://ref.ly/Gen18:27), [18:31](https://ref.ly/Gen18:31), [19:2](https://ref.ly/Gen19:2), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Gen19:8), [19:19](https://ref.ly/Gen19:19), [19:20](https://ref.ly/Gen19:20), [27:2](https://ref.ly/Gen27:2), Ju [13:3](https://ref.ly/Judg13:3), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Judg19:9), [1 S 9:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:6), [16:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:15), [2 S 13:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:24), [2 K 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:16), [2:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:19), [4:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:9) \+, [Job 13:18](https://ref.ly/Job13:18), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Job33:2), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Job40:15), [40:16](https://ref.ly/Job40:16). **e.** (לנו) אוֺי־נָא לי Woe, *now*, to me (us) ! † [Je 4:31](https://ref.ly/Jer4:31), [45:7](https://ref.ly/Jer45:7), [La 5:16](https://ref.ly/Lam5:16). **f.** אַיֵּה־נָא where, *pray ?* † [Psalm 115:2](https://ref.ly/Ps115:2). **g.** נֶגְדָּה־נָּא, peculiarly, † [Psalm 116:14](https://ref.ly/Ps116:14), [116:18](https://ref.ly/Ps116:18) my vows to J. I will perform, נגדה־נא לכל־עמו *O that* (it may be) before all his people !

> \[Str4995] II. נָא **adj.** v. ניא

> \[Str4996] † נאֹ **n. pr. loc** No \= Thebes (Eg. *nt, city*, i.e. prob. *nē(t)*, v. SteindBAS i. 596 f.; As. *Ni\-’u, Ni\-i’* DlPar 318 Steindl. c. SchrCOT gloss.; Tel Am. Nî (Ni\-i) WklTel Am. 39 \* BezTel Am. in BM 153\);—הִנְנִי פוֺקֵד אֶל־אָמוֺן מִנּאֹ [Je 46:25](https://ref.ly/Jer46:25) *Amon from* (*of*, LXX ἐν \= בְּ) *No;* נאֹ אָמוֺן [Na 3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8) *No of Amon* (\= *city of* god *Ammon*, \= LXX Διόσολις Ez [30:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:14), [30:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16) Steindl. c.; v. also Jerem. and BillerbeckBAS iii.104 f.); Ez [30:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:14) (‖פַּתְרוֺס, צֹ֫עַן), וְהִכְרַתִּי אֶת־הֲמוֺן נאֹ v:[15](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:15) (‖סִין; but LXX Co Berthol נֹף q. v. for נֹא) v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16) (‖סִין, Co סון). It lay on the right bank of the Nile, c. 400 miles (by the river) S. of Memphis.

## נָאָה (id: BDB05819)

\[Str4998] † \[נָאָה] **vb.** only **Pi‘lel** be comely, befitting (Thes Ol:§ 251 a, 187 a Sta§ 416 b Ges§. 2\. R. 4 SS Bae:[Psalm 33:1](https://ref.ly/Ps33:1) \> AE Ki HartmPlurilit.\-bildungen (1875\) 13 ff. NöZMG xxx. 1876, 185 Now Hup:[Psalm 31:1](https://ref.ly/Ps31:1), [93:5](https://ref.ly/Ps93:5) Buhl **Niph.** √ *I.* אוה *desire*, i.e. *desirable, beautiful;* NH נאה Pi. Hithp.; Targum נוֺאִי *beauty*);—**Piïlel** *Pf.* נַאֲוָה [Psalm 93:5](https://ref.ly/Ps93:5) (so van d. H Ginsb; Mass Baer Dead loc. נָֽאֲוָה \= נָאוָה v. נָאוֶה **adj.,** but v. Sta§ 155\. b. n); נָאווּ [Is 52:7](https://ref.ly/Isa52:7), Ct [1:10](https://ref.ly/Song1:10) (on form cf. Gesl. c.);—**1\.** *be comely;* of feet of messenger, (fig.) [Is 52:7](https://ref.ly/Isa52:7); of cheeks Ct [1:10](https://ref.ly/Song1:10). **2\.** *be befitting:* ־קֹדֶשׁ´לְבֵיתְ נ [Psalm 93:5](https://ref.ly/Ps93:5).

> \[Str5000] † נָאוֶה **adj.** comely, seemly;—´נ m. Ct [2:14](https://ref.ly/Song2:14) \+ 3 t.; f. נָאוָה [Psalm 33:1](https://ref.ly/Ps33:1) \+ 4 t., נָוָה [Je 6:2](https://ref.ly/Jer6:2); —**1\.** *comely, beautiful*, of woman Ct [1:5](https://ref.ly/Song1:5), [6:4](https://ref.ly/Song6:4) (‖יָפָה) of face [2:14](https://ref.ly/Song2:14) (מַרְאֶה) so נָוָה [Je 6:2](https://ref.ly/Jer6:2) (fig. of Jerus.; ‖מְעֻנָּגָה); of mouth Ct [4:3](https://ref.ly/Song4:3) (‖שְפָתוֺת). **2\.** *seemly*, of praise תְּהִלָּה [Psalm 33:1](https://ref.ly/Ps33:1), [147:1](https://ref.ly/Ps147:1); subj. שְפַת־יֶתֶר [Pr 17:7](https://ref.ly/Prov17:7) (cf. Ct [4:3](https://ref.ly/Song4:3)); תַּעֲנוּג [19:10](https://ref.ly/Prov19:10); כָּבוֺד [26:1](https://ref.ly/Prov26:1). So also [Psalm 93:5](https://ref.ly/Ps93:5) Mass. (anom. fem.) v. \[נאה] supr.

## נאם (id: BDB05822)

נאם √ of foll.; cf. نَأَمَ *groan, sigh;* perhaps also NH נוּם *speak*).

> \[Str5002] נְאֻם:376 **n. m.** utterance (BaNB 82 e KöLgb ii. 501 \> older expl. as *Pt. pass.*);—´נ always thus, as cstr.:—**1\.** *utterance, declaration, revelation*, of prophet in ecstatic state בלעם ´נ [Nu 24:3](https://ref.ly/Num24:3), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Num24:15); שֹׁמֵעַ אִמְרֵי אֵל ´נ v:[4](https://ref.ly/Num24:4); v:[6](https://ref.ly/Num24:6); הַגֶּבֶר ´נ v:[3](https://ref.ly/Num24:3), v:[15](https://ref.ly/Num24:15), [2 S 23:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:1), [Pr 30:1](https://ref.ly/Prov30:1); דוד ´נ [2 S 23:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:1); פֶּשַׁע ´נ [Psalm 36:2](https://ref.ly/Ps36:2) (transgression personified, speaking with proph. voice בְּקֶרֶב לִבּוֺ); elsewh. alw. **2\.** before divine names (exc. [Je 23:31](https://ref.ly/Jer23:31) see **vb.**): יהוה ´נ *utterance, declaration* of ´י (prophet citing divine word given through him), [Gn 22:16](https://ref.ly/Gen22:16) (J), [Nu 14:28](https://ref.ly/Num14:28) (P), [2 K 9:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:26), [9:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:26), [19:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:33) \= [Is 37:34](https://ref.ly/Isa37:34), [2 K 22:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:19) \= [2 Ch 34:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:27), [Is 14:22](https://ref.ly/Isa14:22), [30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1), [31:9](https://ref.ly/Isa31:9), Ez [13:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:6), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:7), [16:58](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:58), [37:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:14), [Ho 2:15](https://ref.ly/Hos2:15), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hos2:18), [2:23](https://ref.ly/Hos2:23), [11:11](https://ref.ly/Hos11:11), [Jo 2:12](https://ref.ly/Joel2:12), Ob:[4](https://ref.ly/Obad1:4), Ob:[8](https://ref.ly/Obad1:8), [Mi 4:6](https://ref.ly/Mic4:6), [5:9](https://ref.ly/Mic5:9), [Zp 1:2](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:2), [1:3](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:3), [1:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:10), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:8), [Zc 8:17](https://ref.ly/Zech8:17), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Zech10:12), [11:6](https://ref.ly/Zech11:6), [12:1](https://ref.ly/Zech12:1), [12:4](https://ref.ly/Zech12:4), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Zech13:8), [Mal 1:2](https://ref.ly/Mal1:2) \+ Is:2 12 t., Je 162 t., Am 14 t., Hg 5 t., Zc:1 (1–8) 6 t.; ´ח ´הַמֶּלֶח י [46:18](https://ref.ly/Jer46:18), [48:15](https://ref.ly/Jer48:15), 57:57; אדני יהוה ´נ [Is 56:8](https://ref.ly/Isa56:8), [Je 2:22](https://ref.ly/Jer2:22), [Am 4:5](https://ref.ly/Amos4:5), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Amos8:3), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Amos8:9), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Amos8:11) \+ Ez 82 t. נְאֻם begins sentence only [Is 56:8](https://ref.ly/Isa56:8), [Psalm 110:1](https://ref.ly/Ps110:1); often in middle [Is 49:18](https://ref.ly/Isa49:18) [Am 3:10](https://ref.ly/Amos3:10) \+; most frequently at end [Is 54:17](https://ref.ly/Isa54:17), [Am 2:11](https://ref.ly/Amos2:11), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Amos4:3) \+; found in all proph. except Hab., Jon.; not in HD of Hex., Chr. (except ‖ Kings), Dan., Job or the five Megilloth.

\[Str5010] \[נָאַר] **vb.** only **Pi.** exact mng. uncertain; prob. (from context) abhor, spurn (De comp. Ar. نار *abhor*, but this is med. و);—**Pi.** *Pf.* subj. ´י, נִאֵר [La 2:7](https://ref.ly/Lam2:7) (obj. מִקְדָּשׁוֺ; ‖זָנַת); 2 ms. נֵאַרְתָּה [Psalm 89:40](https://ref.ly/Ps89:40) (obj. בְּרית עַבְדְּ; ‖חִלֵּל, and זָנַח, מָאַס v:[39](https://ref.ly/Ps89:39); doubts cast on text by Hup (reading נאץ), Now, Bu:[La 2:1](https://ref.ly/Lam2:1)).

> \[Str5011] † נֹב **n. pr. loc.1\.** ancient priestly city [1 S 22:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:19) cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:11) c. ה loc. נֹ֫בֶה (Ges§ 90:2 ad fin.), [21:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:2), [22:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:9); LXX Νομβα, Νομμα, Νοβα, etc.; perh. \= נְבוֺ (q. v.) [Ezr 2:29](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:29) \= [Ne 7:33](https://ref.ly/Neh7:33), [Ezr 10:43](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:43), LXX Ναβσυ, Ναβια, etc. Site dub.; on Jerome's identif. with *Nobe* near Lydda cf. BuhlGeogr. 189; a Bēt Nūbā lies c. 13 m. WNW. fr. Jerus., c. 10 SE. fr. Lydda. **2\.** [Is 10:32](https://ref.ly/Isa10:32) just N. of Jerus., station in (ideal) Assyr. march; cf. [Ne 11:32](https://ref.ly/Neh11:32) (inhab. by Benjamites); perhaps \= **1\.**—[2 S 21:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:6) read גֹּב (q. v. p.146 supr.)

## נבא (id: BDB05835)

נבא (√ of foll.; Ew Fl De KöLgb ii, 1, 133 al. camp. Ar. نَبَأٌ \[نَبَأَ *utter a low voice, or sound*, (esp. of dog); *announce;* but also *be exalted, elevated* نَبْأَةٌ *eminence*); III, IV. *acquaint, inform*, نَبَأَ *information announcement, intelligence*]; As. *nabû, call, proclaim, name*, DlHWB 441; Eth. ነበበ፡ *speak;* Sab. תנבא ? cf. Levy\-OsZMG xix. 1865, 208 CISiv. 1, No. 31; Ges Kue al. think weakened form of נבע *bubble up, pour forth* (of flow of words under excitement of inspiration); Hup Ri Sch cf. נאם; v. careful discussions by RSProph. ii, n. 18 KöOB i. 71 ff.)

## נָבַב (id: BDB05840)

\[Str5014] † \[נָבַב] **vb.** hollow out (cf. As. *imbubu, flute*, DlHWB 443, NH אַבּוּב *id.,* Aram. ܐܰܒܽܘܒܳܐ אַבּוּבָא *id.;* hence also Ar. أَنْبُوبٌ *knots* in reed, *part of* reed *between knots, reed;* vid. HoffmLCB 1882, 321 Frä23 Fl in LevyTW i. 417\);—**Qal** *Pt. pass.* נָבוּב [Jb 11:12](https://ref.ly/Job11:12), [Je 52:21](https://ref.ly/Jer52:21); נְבוּב [Ex 27:8](https://ref.ly/Exod27:8), [38:7](https://ref.ly/Exod38:7); —*hollowed, hollow:* of altar of tabern. נְבוּב לֻחוֺת [Ex 27:8](https://ref.ly/Exod27:8), [38:7](https://ref.ly/Exod38:7) (P); of pillar in temple [Je 52:21](https://ref.ly/Jer52:21); fig. אִישׁ נָבוּב [Jb 11:12](https://ref.ly/Job11:12), i.e. *empty, hollow\-minded* man.

> \[Str5011] נֹ֫בֶה v. נֹב. p. 611

> \[Str5015] † I. נְבוֺ **n. pr. loc.** (prob. connex., at least for **2 ,** with (Bab.) god *Nebo, Nabû*, cf. II. נְבוֺ and BaeRel. 15, 89, 259; yet not certain, NöZMG xiii. 1888, 470 comp. Ar. النَبَاوَةُ *the height*, etc.): LXX Ναβαυ;—**1\. a.** city in Moab [Nu 32:3](https://ref.ly/Num32:3), [32:38](https://ref.ly/Num32:38) (where assigned to Reuben; both JE \= נבה MI14) [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2), [Je 48:1](https://ref.ly/Jer48:1), [48:22](https://ref.ly/Jer48:22), [1 Ch 5:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:8); prob. on or near Mt. Nebo (v. infr.), cf. BuhlGeogr. 266 f. TristrMoab 338\. **b.** city in Judah, ´בְּנֵי נ [Ezr 2:29](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:29) \= אַחֵר ´אַנְשֵׁי נ [Ne 7:34](https://ref.ly/Neh7:34) (*the men of the other N.*, so disting.—si אחר vera l., cf. Ryle—fr. **a** ? or fr. another נֹב ?), [Ezr 10:43](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:43); —this נבו in Judah perhaps \= נֹב **1** q. v. **2\.** mt. in Moab, where Moses died [Nu 33:47](https://ref.ly/Num33:47), [Dt 32:49](https://ref.ly/Deut32:49) (הַר־נְבוֺ), [34:1](https://ref.ly/Deut34:1) (*id.;* all P), six miles W. of Heshbon acc. to Onom. (ed. Lag283). Prob. \= mod. *Nebâ* at NE. corner of Dead Sea, SurveyE. Pal. i. 198 ff. GASmGeogr. 562 ff. BuhlGeogr. 266 f. TristrMoab 318, 338 MerrillE. of Jordan, 242 ff.—Cf. פִּסְגָּה.

> \[Str5015] † II. נְבוֺ **n. pr. div.** Nebo (loan\-wd. in Heb., cf. Ph. N. pr. m. נבו; \= As. *Nabû*, SchrCOT Gloss. And [Is 46:1](https://ref.ly/Isa46:1) JastrowRel. Bab. 124 ff. JenKosmol. pass. Tie1eAss. u. Bab. Geach. 532 f. SayRel. Bab 112 ff. (cf. Palm. n. pr. נבוזבד, ברנבו VogPalm. No. 73\), and this perh. √ *nabû, call, name*, v. נבא);—Babylonian god כָּרַע בֵּל קֹרֵם נְבוֺ [Is 46:1](https://ref.ly/Isa46:1).

## נָבַח (id: BDB05848)

\[Str5024] † \[נָבַח] **vb.** bark (of dogs) (NH *id.;* Ar. نَبَحَ; Eth. ነብሐ፡; Aram. ܢܒܰܚ, נְבַח);—**Qal** *Inf. cstr.* only כֻּלָּם כְּלָבִים אִלְּמִים לאֹ יוּכְלוּ לִנְבֹּחַ [Is 56:10](https://ref.ly/Isa56:10) fig. of helpless prophets.

> \[Str5025] † נֹ֫בַח **n. pr. m. et loc. 1\. m.** a Manassite [Nu 32:42](https://ref.ly/Num32:42) (JE), LXX Ναβαυ. **2\. loc.** in Gilead Ju [8:11](https://ref.ly/Judg8:11), LXX Ναβαι, Ναβε(θ); [Nu 32:42](https://ref.ly/Num32:42) (formerly קְנָת, q. v.), LXX Ναβωθ.

> \[Str5026] † נִבְחַז **n. pr. div.** god of men of Avva, [2 K 17:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:31); name otherwise unknown, cf. SchCOT ad loc.; LXXB τὴν Ἐβλαζερ, LXXL Ἐβλαιεζερ, A Ἀβααζερ καὶ τὴν Ναιβας; Thes comp. Mand. denom. ܢܒܰܙ \= נבאז (Cod. Nasar. Norberg.) Codd. give variant נבחן cf. Baerad loc. FrensdorffMas. Magna i. 306\.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 32:42; 2 Kings 17:31; Isaiah 56:10; Judges 8:11

> \[Str4007] † \[מַבָּט] **n. m.**:[Zc 9:5](https://ref.ly/Zech9:5) expectation \= object of hope or confidence (lit. *thing looked to*);—sf. מַבָּטֵנוּ [Is 20:6](https://ref.ly/Isa20:6), מַבָּטָם v:[5](https://ref.ly/Isa20:5), מֶבָּטָהּ [Zc 9:5](https://ref.ly/Zech9:5); —of Cush as ally of Judah [Is 20:5](https://ref.ly/Isa20:5), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Isa20:6); of Tyre as ally of l’hilistia [Zc 9:5](https://ref.ly/Zech9:5).

Sab. נבט, נבטאל, v. נָבַט supr.);—only in phrase יָֽרָבְעָם בֶּן־נְבָט [1 K 11:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:26), [12:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:2), [12:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:15) [15:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:1), [16:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:3), [16:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:26), [16:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:31), [21:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:22), [22:53](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:53), [2 K 3:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:3), [9:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:9), [10:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:29), [13:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:2), [13:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:11), [14:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:24), [15:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:9), [15:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:18), [15:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:24), [15:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:28), [17:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:21), [23:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:15), [2 Ch 9:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:29), [10:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:2), [10:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:15), [13:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:6). LXX Ναβαθ, Ναβατ.

> \[Str5032] †נְבָּיוֺת **n.pr.gent.** (Nab. נבטו \= *Nabataean* and *Nabataeans*, EutNab 1, 4 and oft., cf. נבט of Jewish trad. NöZMG xxv, 1871:124, נבט Targum:[Is 60:7](https://ref.ly/Isa60:7) (on form of name, and on ט \= ת cf. LagBN 51 f.); in As. *Nabaitai*, etc., SchrCOT [Gn 25:13](https://ref.ly/Gen25:13); KGF 99 ff.. DlPar 296 f.);—as (eldest) son of Ishmael (brother of Kedar) [Gn 25:13](https://ref.ly/Gen25:13), [28:9](https://ref.ly/Gen28:9), [36:3](https://ref.ly/Gen36:3) (all P), [1 Ch 1:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:29); people (‖ Kedar) [Is 60:7](https://ref.ly/Isa60:7), cf. *Nabataei* \+ *Cedrei* PlinNH v, 11, 65; LXX Ναβαιωθ; Gk. Ναβαταῖοι.—See further NöZMG xxv, 1871:122 f. EutNab p.78 GASmGeogr. 547–629 pass.

> \[Str5033] †\[נֵ֫בֶךְ] **n.\[m.]** spring (si vera 1\.) (√ unknown);—only pl. cstr. נִבְכֵי־יָם [Jb 38:16](https://ref.ly/Job38:16) *the springs, sources of the ocean* (‖חֵ֫קֶר תְּהוֺם);—Ol Siegf נִבְלֵי; Bu suggests נבעי \= מֵבֻּעֵי as poss.; but Gr Perles rd. נִבְכֵי [Jb 28:11](https://ref.ly/Job28:11), also, for Mass. מִבְּכִי, cf. נָהָר **2\.**

## I נבל (id: BDB05856)

†I. נבל (√ of foll.; meaning dubious).

> \[Str5035] I. נֵ֫בֶל **n.m.**:[Je 13:12](https://ref.ly/Jer13:12) **1\.** skin\-bottle, skin; **2\.** jar, pitcher;—נֵ֫בֶלabs. [1 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:5) \+; cstr.v3 \+ (on נֶכֶל v.Bear1 S 1\.24\);—**1\.** *skin of wine*, נֵ֫בֶל יַיִן [1 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:24), [10:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:3), [2 S 16:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:1), cf. also [Je 13:12](https://ref.ly/Jer13:12), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Jer13:12); pl. נִבְלֵי יַיִן [1 S 25:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:18) : fig. נִבְלֵי שָׁמַיִם [Jb 38:37](https://ref.ly/Job38:37) i.e. *clouds* (‖שְׁחָקִים). **2\.** earthen *jar, pitcher:* fig. of connexions of Eliakim כְּלֵי נְבָלִים [Is 22:24](https://ref.ly/Isa22:24); נִבְלֵיהֶם יְנַפֵָּחוּ [Je 48:12](https://ref.ly/Jer48:12) (‖כֵּלִים יָרִיקוּ) in fig. of Moab (cf. [Psalm 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ps2:9)); נִבְלֵי־תֶרֶש [La 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lam4:2) sim. of men of Judah (‖מַעֲשֵה יְדֵי יוֺצֵר); sim. of destruction of Judah, שֵׁבֶר נֵ֫כֶל יוֺצְרִים [Is 30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14).

> \[Str5035] †II. נֵ֫בֶל, נֶ֫בֶל **n.m.**:[Psalm 57:9](https://ref.ly/Ps57:9) a musical instrument, either a portable harp, or a lute, guitar (with bulging resonance\-body at lower end); (perh. \= 1\. נֵבֶל, and then shape seems to favour *lute*; perh. independent word, e.g. Egyptian loan\-word, cf. *nfr, lute*, WeHpt 222\);—abs. נֵ֫בֶל [1 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:5) \+ 3 t., נֶ֫בֶל [Psalm 71:22](https://ref.ly/Ps71:22) \+ 3 t.; ָ\+נבֶל [Am 6:5](https://ref.ly/Amos6:5) \+ 2 t.; pl. נְבָלִים [2 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:5) \+ 4 t.; sf. נְבָלֶיְ [Am 5:23](https://ref.ly/Amos5:23), [Is 14:11](https://ref.ly/Isa14:11); —*harp* (or *lute*), played at feasts and religious ceremonies, probably less simple, cheap and common than כִּנּוֺר (q.v.; oft. named with ´נ):—as mark of luxury, revelry [Am 5:23](https://ref.ly/Amos5:23), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Amos6:5), Is [14:11](https://ref.ly/Isa14:11), so [5:12](https://ref.ly/Isa5:12) (תֹּף חָלִיל ´כִנּוֺר נ), also (not in bad sense) [1 K 10:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:12); played by wandering band of prophets [1 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:5) (list as [Is 5:12](https://ref.ly/Isa5:12)); in worship [2 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:5); elsewh. only PsalmPsalm Chr. always in worship: [Psalm 57:9](https://ref.ly/Ps57:9), [81:3](https://ref.ly/Ps81:3), [92:4](https://ref.ly/Ps92:4), [108:3](https://ref.ly/Ps108:3), [150:3](https://ref.ly/Ps150:3); כְּלִי נֶבֶל [71:22](https://ref.ly/Ps71:22); עָשוֺר ´נ *a harp with ten strings* [33:2](https://ref.ly/Ps33:2), [144:9](https://ref.ly/Ps144:9); [1 Ch 13:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:8), [15:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:16), [15:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:20), [15:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:28), [16:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:5) (כְּלֵי נְבָלִים), [25:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:1), [25:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:6), [2 Ch 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:12), [9:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:11), [20:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:28), [29:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:25), [Ne 12:27](https://ref.ly/Neh12:27).—Cf. further BenzArch. 273 ff. NowArch. i..273 ff. DrAmos 234 ff. WeHpt 222 ff.

## II נָבַל (id: BDB05859)

\[Str5034] †II. נָבַל **vb.** be senseless, foolish (NH נְבָלָה, נַבְלוּת of immodesty; Ar.نَبُلَ \= (per contra) *be noble, distinguished*, نَبَلَ also *be gracious* (Frey); Aram. נְבַל Pa. *reject, despise,* v. Targum [Ne 3:6](https://ref.ly/Neh3:6), [Je 14:21](https://ref.ly/Jer14:21); in deriv. as NH);—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. נָבַלְתָּ [Pr 30:32](https://ref.ly/Prov30:32) *if thou hast been foolish* in lifting up thyself (opp. זַמּוֺתָ). **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 S. sf. consec. וְנִבַּלְתִּיח [Na 3:6](https://ref.ly/Nah3:6); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיְנַבֵּל [Dt 32:15](https://ref.ly/Deut32:15); 2 ms. תְּנַבֵּל [Je 14:21](https://ref.ly/Jer14:21); *Pt.* מִנַבֵּל [Mi 7:6](https://ref.ly/Mic7:6); —*regard* or *treat as a* נָבָל (q.v.), i.e. *with contumely,* c.acc.: בֵּן מְנַבֵּל אָב [Mi 7:6](https://ref.ly/Mic7:6) *son treateth father as a fool* (with contumely); ´וַיְנ צוּר [Dt 32:15](https://ref.ly/Deut32:15) *and he treated with contumely the Rock* of his salvation (i.e. ´י; ‖נטשִׁ); ´י subj., [Na 3:6](https://ref.ly/Nah3:6) *I will treat thee* (Nineveh) *with contumely* (‖וְהִשְלַכְתִּ֫י צָלַיִךְ שִׁקֻּצִים); [Je 14:21](https://ref.ly/Jer14:21) do not *treat with contumely* the throne of thy glory (‖נאץ).

> \[Str5036] †I. נָבָל **adj.** foolish, senseless, esp. of the man who has no perception of ethical and religious claims, and with collat. idea of *ignoble, disgraceful;*—abs. ´נ [2 S 3:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:33) \+ 14 t.; mpl. נְבָלִים [13:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:13), Ez [13:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:3) (LXX Co מִלִּבָּם); fpl. נְבָלוֺת [Jb 2:10](https://ref.ly/Job2:10); —*senseless*, esp. of religious and moral insensibility: ´עַם נ [Dt 32:6](https://ref.ly/Deut32:6) (of Isr., unappreciative of J.'s benefits; opp. חָכָם), so of heathen nation [Psalm 74:18](https://ref.ly/Ps74:18) (blaspheming name of ´י), ´גּוֺי נ [Dt 32:21](https://ref.ly/Deut32:21) (‖לאֹ עָם); ´הַנְּבִאִים הַנּ Ez [13:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:3) (si vera l., v.supr.); elsewh. as **subst.** (impious and presumptuous) fool, [Is 32:5](https://ref.ly/Isa32:5) (opp. נָדִיב *noble\-minded*), characterized as at once irreligious and churlish, v:[6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6); denying God [Psalm 14:1](https://ref.ly/Ps14:1) \= [53:2](https://ref.ly/Ps53:2); insulting God [74:22](https://ref.ly/Ps74:22), and God's servant [39:9](https://ref.ly/Ps39:9); [Pr 17:7](https://ref.ly/Prov17:7) arrogant speech becometh not *the* (impious and presumptuous) *fool* (whose faults it only makes the more conspicuous), much less do lying lips him that this is noble (נדיב), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Prov17:21) (‖כְּסִיל), [30:22](https://ref.ly/Prov30:22) ונבל כי ישבע לחם (one of the things under which the earth trembles), בְּנֵי נָבָל [Jb 30:8](https://ref.ly/Job30:8) i.e. ignoble men (‖בְּנֵי בְלִי־שֵׁם); as one who might be expected to have a contumelious end, ימות אבנר ´הַכְּמוֺת נ [2 S 3:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:33) was Abner (destined) to die, as a ´נ dieth ? of the man who amasses riches unjustly ובאחריתו יהיה נבל [Je 17:11](https://ref.ly/Jer17:11) i.e. will prove himself to be a ´נ; as acting immorally (with collat. idea of disgracefully) [2 S 13:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:13) ואתה תהיה כְּאַחַד הַנְּבָלִים בישראל (cf. נְבָלָה); f. only in כְּדַבֵּר אַחַת הַנְּבָלוֺת [Jb 2:10](https://ref.ly/Job2:10) (of Job's wife). Cf. Dr:[Dt 22:21](https://ref.ly/Deut22:21), [32:6](https://ref.ly/Deut32:6), [32:15](https://ref.ly/Deut32:15). [32:21](https://ref.ly/Deut32:21), Psalt. 457\.

> \[Str5037] II. נָבָל **n.pr.m.**(on popular etymol. see [1 S 25:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:25) infr.);—a churlish man of Carmel, whose widow David married [1 S 25:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:3), [25:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:4), [25:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:5), [25:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:9), [25:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:10), [25:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:14), [25:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:19), [25:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:25), [25:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:25) (כִּשְׁמוֺ כֶּן־ה֔וּא נָבָל שְׁמוֺ וּנְּבָלָה עִמּוֺ), v:[26](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:26) \+ 10 t. 1 S \+ [2 S 2:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:2), [3:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:3). LXX Ναβαλ.

> \[Str5039] †נְבָלָה **n.f.** senselessness (as shewn in disregard of moral and religious claims), esp. of disgraceful sins; also disgrace;—alw. abs. ´נ;—**1\.** *disgraceful folly,* esp. of sins of un\-chastity Ju [19:23](https://ref.ly/Judg19:23) (*wanton deed* GFM), so דְּבַר הַזּאֹת ´הַנּ v:[24](https://ref.ly/Judg19:24), [2 S 13:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:12); oft. בְּיִשְרָאֵל ´עָשָה נ i.e. do a thing disgraceful acc. to Isr.'s standard: [Gn 34:7](https://ref.ly/Gen34:7) (J), [Dt 22:21](https://ref.ly/Deut22:21) (cf. Dr on mng. of wd.; only here of woman), Ju [20:26](https://ref.ly/Judg20:26) (\+ זִמָּה), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10), [Je 29:23](https://ref.ly/Jer29:23) (cf. לאֹ יֵעָשֶה כֵּן בְּיִשְרָאֵלּ [2 S 13:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:12)); also of inhospitable churlishness [1 S 25:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:25) (v. I, II. נָבָל, and esp. [Is 32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6)); of profane action [Jos 7:15](https://ref.ly/Josh7:15) (Achan; ´עָשָה נ בְּיִשְרָאֵל); of senseless and irreligious language [Is 9:16](https://ref.ly/Isa9:16) דֹּבֵר [32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6) (יְדַבֵּר). **2\.** *contumely, disgrace* ´עֲשוֺת עִמָּכֶם נ [Jb 42:8](https://ref.ly/Job42:8) (of ´י), i.e. *deal out to you disgrace,* expose you and punish you as נְבָלִים (cf. [2 S 3:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:33), sub נָבָל), because of your utterances about me.

> \[Str5040] †נַבְלוּת **n.f.** immodesty, shamelessness, *lewdness* of Isr. under fig. of adulteress [Ho 2:12](https://ref.ly/Hos2:12)

## נָבֵל (id: BDB05864)

\[Str5034] †נָבֵל **vb.** sink or drop down, languish, wither and fall, fade (NH *id.;* As. *nabâlu, destroy*, DlHWB 443 f. Ar. نَبَلَ v, VIII. *die;* Aram. נְבֵילָא *corpse*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´נ [Is 40:7](https://ref.ly/Isa40:7), [40:8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:8), [Je 8:13](https://ref.ly/Jer8:13); 3 fs. נָֽבְלָה [Is 24:4](https://ref.ly/Isa24:4), [24:4](https://ref.ly/Isa24:4); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִבּוֺל [Psalm 1:3](https://ref.ly/Ps1:3) \+ 4 t. (on form cf. Ges§ 43, 3, R, 1, h Ol:§ 246 e) 2 ms. תִּבֹּל [Ex 18:18](https://ref.ly/Exod18:18); 3 mpl. יִבֹּלוּ [2 S 22:46](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:46) \= [Psalm 18:46](https://ref.ly/Ps18:46), יִבּוֺלָוּן [Psalm 37:2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:2); 1 pl. וַנָּ֫בֶל [Is 64:5](https://ref.ly/Isa64:5) (on form cf. Di and Ol:§ 236 e); *Inf. abs.* נָבֹל [Ex 18:18](https://ref.ly/Exod18:18); cstr. נְבֹל [Is 34:4](https://ref.ly/Isa34:4); *Pt.* נֹבֵל [Is 28:1](https://ref.ly/Isa28:1), [28:4](https://ref.ly/Isa28:4); f. נֹבֶ֫לָת abs. [Is 34:4](https://ref.ly/Isa34:4); cstr. [Is 1:30](https://ref.ly/Isa1:30); —**1\.** *sink or drop down,* fr. exhaustion נָבֹל תִּכֹּל [Ex 18:18](https://ref.ly/Exod18:18) (Moses and Isr.); fr. discouragement [2 S 22:46](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:46) \= [Psalm 18:46](https://ref.ly/Ps18:46) (of foreigners, foes of Isr.); of mt. הַר־נֹפֵל יִבּוֺל [Jb 14:18](https://ref.ly/Job14:18) (but נָפוֺל יִפּוֺל LagProph. Chald. Siegf Baer, after LXX, so Bu \[or יִבְלֶה]; Du נָבוֺל יִכּוֺל). **2\.** usu. *fall* like a leaf, or flower *wither and fall, fade:* כְּאֵלָה נֹבֶ֫לֶת עָלֶֽהָ [Is 1:30](https://ref.ly/Isa1:30) *like an oak falling as to it leaves* (sim. of people of Jerus.); the host of heaven יִבּוֺל כִּנְבֹל עָלֶה מגֶּפֶן וּכְנֹבֶלֶת מִתְּאֵנָה [Is 34:4](https://ref.ly/Isa34:4); עָלֵהוּ ´לאֹ יִבּ Ez [47:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:12) (trees in Ezek.'s vision), so fig. of good man [Psalm 1:3](https://ref.ly/Ps1:3); of Isr. [Je 8:13](https://ref.ly/Jer8:13), וַנָּ֫בֶל כֶּעָלֶה [Is 64:5](https://ref.ly/Isa64:5); also נָבֵל צִיץ [40:7](https://ref.ly/Isa40:7), [40:8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:8) (both ‖יָבֵשׁ חָצִיר), צִיץ נֹבֵל [28:1](https://ref.ly/Isa28:1), cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Isa28:4); of wicked ´כְּיֶרֶק דֶּשֶׁא יִבּ [Psalm 37:2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:2): (‖כֶּחָצִיר יִמָּלוּ); of devastated earth אָֽבְלָה נָֽבְלָה הָאָרֶַץ אֻמְלְלָה נָֽבְלָה תֵּבֵָל [Is 24:4](https://ref.ly/Isa24:4).

## נָבַע (id: BDB05867)

\[Str5042] †\[נָבַע] **vb.** flow, spring, bubble up, poet. and late (NH *id.* (rare); As. *nabû, id.,* DlHWB 442; Ar. نَبَعَ *well* or *issue forth* (of water); Aram. ܢܒܰܥ נְבַע; Eth. ነብዐ፡ II. *weep,* አንብዕ፡ *tear(s)*);—**Qal** *Pt.* נַ֫חַל נֹבֵעַ [Pr 18:4](https://ref.ly/Prov18:4) *a flowing wady*, metaph. of מְקוֺר חָכְמָה. **Hiph.** *Impf.* יַבִּיעַ [Psalm 19:3](https://ref.ly/Ps19:3) \+ 3 t.; אַבְּ֫יעָה [Psalm 78:2](https://ref.ly/Ps78:2), [Pr 1:23](https://ref.ly/Prov1:23); יַבִּ֫יעוּ [Psalm 94:4](https://ref.ly/Ps94:4), [145:7](https://ref.ly/Ps145:7); יַבִּיעוּן [Psalm 59:8](https://ref.ly/Ps59:8); 3 fpl. תַּבַּ֫עְנָה [Psalm 119:171](https://ref.ly/Ps119:171); —*pour out, emit, cause to bubble, belch forth:* **1\.** *pour out,* fig. אַבִּיעָה לָכֶם רוּחִי [Pr 1:23](https://ref.ly/Prov1:23) (‖אוֺדִ֫יעָה דְבָרַי אֶתְכֶם). **2\.** *cause to bubble, ferment* זְבוּבֵי מַָ֫וֶת יַבְאִישׁ יַכִּיעַ שֶׁמֶן רוֺקֵָחַ [Ec 10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1). **3\.** fig., usually of speech, *pour forth, emit, belch forth,* emphat. expression: פִּי רְשָׁעִים יַבִּיעַ רָעוֺת [Pr 15:28](https://ref.ly/Prov15:28) (opp. לֵב צַדִּיק יֶהְגֶּה לַעֲנוֺת) i.e. the righteous considers before he speaks, the wicked bursts out with reckless utterance; פִּי כְסִילִים יַבִּיעַ אִוֶּלֶת [Pr 15:2](https://ref.ly/Prov15:2) (opp. לְשׁוֺן חֲכָמִים תֵּיטִיב דָּעַת); יַבִּ֫יעוּ יְדַבְּרוּ עָתָק [Psalm 94:4](https://ref.ly/Ps94:4) (‖יִתְאַמְּרוּ כָּל־פֹּעֲלֵי אָוֶן); בְּפִיהֶם ´יַבּ [59:8](https://ref.ly/Ps59:8) (‖חֲרָבוֺת בְּשִפְתוֺתֵיהֶם); in good sense, אַבִּ֫יעָה חִידוֺת [78:2](https://ref.ly/Ps78:2) (‖אֶפְתֳּחה בְּמָשָׁל פִּי); תַּבַּעְנָה שְפָתַי תְּהִלָּה [119:171](https://ref.ly/Ps119:171); זֵכֶר רַב־טוּבְ יִבִָּיעוּ [145:7](https://ref.ly/Ps145:7) (‖וְצִדְקָֽתְ יְרַנֵָּנוּ, cf. also v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ps145:6)); metaph. אֹמֶר ´יוֺם לְיוֺם יַבּ [19:3](https://ref.ly/Ps19:3), i. e. though silent, it really *pours forth* speech.

> \[Str4002] †מַבּוּעַ **n.\[m.]** spring of water (As. *namba’u* Dl: HWB 442, Ar مَنْبَعٌ, Syr. ܡܰܒܽܘܥ);—abs. ´מ [Ec 12:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:6); pl. cstr. מַבּוּעֵי מַיִם [Is 35:7](https://ref.ly/Isa35:7), [49:10](https://ref.ly/Isa49:10) (for returning exiles).

> \[Str5044] נִבְשָׁן v. בשׁן

## נָגַד (id: BDB05872)

\[Str5046] \[נָגַד]:363 **vb.** be conspicuous (NH in deriv.; Ph. n.pr.m. נגד; Ar. نَجَدَ *conquer, overcome,* also *be apparent, conspicuous;* نَجْدٌ *high land* or *country;* نَجُدَ *be courageous, vigorous, effective*, نَجِدٌ *courageous, efficient;* Aram. ܢܓܰܕ, נְגַד *drag, draw, lead;* ܢܳܓܽܘܕܳܐ נָגוֺדָא *leader;* Eth. ነገደ፡ is *travel*);—**Hiph.**:328 *Pf.* הִגִּיד [Gn 3:11](https://ref.ly/Gen3:11) \+, 3 fs. הִגִּ֫ידָה [1 S 25:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:19) \+, הִגַּדְתָּ [Gn 12:8](https://ref.ly/Gen12:8) \+, etc.; *Impf.* יַגִּיד [1 S 9:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:6) \+; וַיַּגֵּד [Gn 9:22](https://ref.ly/Gen9:22) \+; sf. יַגִּידֶ֫הָ [Is 44:7](https://ref.ly/Isa44:7); יַגִּדָהּ [Je 9:11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:11); יַגֵּֽדְ [Dt 32:7](https://ref.ly/Deut32:7) (juss.), etc.; *Imv.* הַגֵּד [2 S 18:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:21) \+; הַגִּ֫ידָה [Gn 29:15](https://ref.ly/Gen29:15) \+, etc.; *Inf. abs.* הַגֵּד Ju [14:12](https://ref.ly/Judg14:12) \+ 2 t.; הַגֵּיד [Je 36:16](https://ref.ly/Jer36:16); *cstr.* לְהַגִּיד [Gn 32:6](https://ref.ly/Gen32:6) \+; לגיד [2 K 9:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:15) Kt. > \[Str5048] נֶ֫גֶד:151 **subst.** what is conspicuous or in front, always as **adv.** or **prep.** in front of, in sight of, opposite to, ֶ\+נגֶד, cstr. נֶגֶד, sf. נֶגְדִּי [Psalm 38:11](https://ref.ly/Ps38:11) \+, נֶגְדְּ [38:10](https://ref.ly/Ps38:10) \+, etc.; with ה *loc.* נֶ֫גְדָּה † [Psalm 116:14](https://ref.ly/Ps116:14), [116:18](https://ref.ly/Ps116:18); —**1\.** as adv. accus. *in front of*, **a.** locally (rather stronger and distincter than לִפְנֵי), (*a*) [Gn 31:32](https://ref.ly/Gen31:32) נֶגֶד אַחֵינוּ הַכֶּר־נָא *in front of* our brethren own it, now, v:[37](https://ref.ly/Gen31:37), [47:15](https://ref.ly/Gen47:15) ולמה נמות נגדך why should we die *in thy sight ?* [Ex 19:2](https://ref.ly/Exod19:2) Isr. encamped נגד ההר *in front of* the mountain, [34:10](https://ref.ly/Exod34:10) נגד כל־עמך אעשה נִפְלָאֹת, *before, in sight of, all thy people* (cf. [Psalm 78:12](https://ref.ly/Ps78:12)), [Jos 3:16](https://ref.ly/Josh3:16) the people passed over נגד ירחו *in front of* J., [1 K 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:27), etc.; [Ne 3:23](https://ref.ly/Neh3:23) *in front of* their house, v:[29](https://ref.ly/Neh3:29); (כל)נגד ישראל [Dt 31:11](https://ref.ly/Deut31:11), [Jos 8:35](https://ref.ly/Josh8:35) \= [1 S 15:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:30), [2 S 12:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:12) \+; [1 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:13) set Naboth נֶגֶד הָעָם; ´נגד י [1 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [16:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:6) מְשִׁיחוֺ ´אַךְ נֶגֶד י, so נֶגְדִּי [Psalm 89:37](https://ref.ly/Ps89:37), נֶגְדּוֺ [18:13](https://ref.ly/Ps18:13); נגד השׁמשׁ [Nu 25:4](https://ref.ly/Num25:4), [2 S 12:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:12); [Psalm 22:26](https://ref.ly/Ps22:26) יראיו ´נ, [52:11](https://ref.ly/Ps52:9) (so poet. נֶגְדָּה לְ [Psalm 116:14](https://ref.ly/Ps116:14), [116:18](https://ref.ly/Ps116:18)); hostilely, [Jb 10:17](https://ref.ly/Job10:17); with collat. idea of *to the mortification of*, [Psalm 23:5](https://ref.ly/Ps23:5), [31:20](https://ref.ly/Ps31:20), cf. [Jo 1:16](https://ref.ly/Joel1:16); with collat. idea of being *open* or *known to*, [Pr 15:11](https://ref.ly/Prov15:11) ´שׁאול ואבדון נגד י, [Jb 26:6](https://ref.ly/Job26:6); [Psalm 38:10](https://ref.ly/Ps38:10) נגדך כל־תאותי (‖ממך לא נסתרה), [69:20](https://ref.ly/Ps69:20) (‖יָדַעְתָּ), [119:168](https://ref.ly/Ps119:168), [Is 59:12](https://ref.ly/Isa59:12); so נֶגֶד פָּנַי הָיוּ [Ho 7:2](https://ref.ly/Hos7:2). (*b*) with reflex. sf., and vb. of motion, to go *in front of oneself*, i.e. *straightforward*, [Jos 6:5](https://ref.ly/Josh6:5) ועלו העם אישׁ נגדו each *straightforward*, v:[20](https://ref.ly/Josh6:20), [Am 4:3](https://ref.ly/Amos4:3), [Je 31:29](https://ref.ly/Jer31:29), [Ne 12:37](https://ref.ly/Neh12:37), cf. [Pr 4:25](https://ref.ly/Prov4:25). **b.** mentally, \= vividly present to, [Psalm 38:18](https://ref.ly/Ps38:18) ומכאובי נגדי תמיד, [44:16](https://ref.ly/Ps44:16), [51:5](https://ref.ly/Ps51:5) וחטאתי נגדי תמיד, [109:15](https://ref.ly/Ps109:15) ´י ´נ (remembered by): nearly \= בְּעֵינָיו, כְּאַיִן נֶגְדּוֺ [Is 40:17](https://ref.ly/Isa40:17), [Psalm 39:6](https://ref.ly/Ps39:6); so ´וְנ פְּנֵיהֶם נְבוֺנִים [Is 5:21](https://ref.ly/Isa5:21). But [Ex 10:10](https://ref.ly/Exod10:10) כי רעה נגד פניכם *before your face* is \= in your view, or purpose (cf. לא אשׁית לְנֶגֶד עֵינַי דבר־בליעל [Psalm 101:3](https://ref.ly/Ps101:3)).
> 
> **2\.** With preps.: **a.** †כְּנֶגֶד *acc. to what is in front of* \= *corresponding to*, [Gn 2:18](https://ref.ly/Gen2:18) I will make him עֵזֶר כְּנֶגְדּוֺ a help *corresponding to* him i.e. equal and adequate to himself, v:[20](https://ref.ly/Gen2:20) among the animals there was no עֵזֶר כְּנֶגְדּוֺ. **b.** לְנֶגֶד *in front of, before*, [Gn 33:12](https://ref.ly/Gen33:12) וְאֵֽלְכָה לְנֶנְדְֶָּ, [Nu 22:32](https://ref.ly/Num22:32), [2 K 1:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:13) fell on his knees לנגד אליהו, [Is 1:7](https://ref.ly/Isa1:7) your ground לנגדכם זרים אֹכלים אותהּ; with עָמַד [Jos 5:13](https://ref.ly/Josh5:13), [Dn 8:15](https://ref.ly/Dan8:15), [10:16](https://ref.ly/Dan10:16), in a hostile sense v:[13](https://ref.ly/Dan10:13), cf. [Pr 21:30](https://ref.ly/Prov21:30); *in the sight* or *presence of,* Hb [1:3](https://ref.ly/Hab1:3) וְשֹׁד וְחָמָס לְנֶגְדִּי, [Psalm 39:2](https://ref.ly/Ps39:2) בעוד רשׁע לנגדי, [50:8](https://ref.ly/Ps50:8) ועולֽתיך לנגדי תמיד, [Ne 3:37](https://ref.ly/Neh3:37); *opposite to* (prob. of opposite choirs), [Ne 12:9](https://ref.ly/Neh12:9), [12:24](https://ref.ly/Neh12:24); \= *parallel to*, [1 Ch 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:11); \= *over, for*, a business (peculiar) [Ne 11:22](https://ref.ly/Neh11:22); fig. of what is visible morally, [Psalm 18:23](https://ref.ly/Ps18:23) כי כל משׁפטיו לנגדי (‖לאֹ אָסִיר מֶנִּי), with *to set* or *place* [Psalm 16:8](https://ref.ly/Ps16:8), [54:5](https://ref.ly/Ps54:5) לא שמו אלהים לנגדם (cf. [86:14](https://ref.ly/Ps86:14), ), [90:8](https://ref.ly/Ps90:8); oft. in the phrase …לְנֶגֶד עֵינֵי, both lit., as [Jb 4:16](https://ref.ly/Job4:16) תְּמוּנָה עֵיָ\+ני ´לנ, [Psalm 5:6](https://ref.ly/Ps5:6) עיניך ´לא יתיצבו הוללים לנ (cf. [101:7](https://ref.ly/Ps101:7)); and in a moral sense, [Psalm 18:25](https://ref.ly/Ps18:25) עיניו ´כְּבֹר ידי לנ, [26:3](https://ref.ly/Ps26:3) עיני ´כי חסדך לנ i.e. is ever present to my mind, [36:2](https://ref.ly/Ps36:2) עיניו ´אין פחד אלהים לנ i.e. he has no eye to discern God's awe\-inspiring judgments (cf. [10:5](https://ref.ly/Ps10:5)); with לא אשׁית i.e. have in view, purpose (‖שנאתי) [101:3](https://ref.ly/Ps101:3). **c.** † מִנֶּגֶד (*a*) **adv.** *off* (מִן **1 c**) *the front, in front, opposite, ex adverso*, [Gn 21:16](https://ref.ly/Gen21:16) ותשׁב להּ מנגד sat her down *opposite*, v:[16](https://ref.ly/Gen21:16), [2 K 2:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:7); [Dt 28:66](https://ref.ly/Deut28:66) והיו חייך תלואים לך מנגד shall be hung up for thee *in front* (i.e. suspended, as by a thread, in front of thee); Ju [9:17](https://ref.ly/Judg9:17) וישׁלך נפשׁו מנגד and flung away his life *in front* or *straight away* (i.e. hazarded it); \= *some way off, from* or *at a distance*, [Nu 2:2](https://ref.ly/Num2:2), [Dt 32:52](https://ref.ly/Deut32:52) כי מִנֶּגֶד תראה את הארץ, [2 K 2:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:15) ´וַיִּרְאֻהוּ מנ *at a distance*, [3:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:22), [4:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:25); *aloof*, [2 S 18:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:13) ואתה תתיצב מנגד, [Ob 1:11](https://ref.ly/Obad1:11) (*b*) as a **prep.** (*a*) *from the front of, away from*, Ct [6:5](https://ref.ly/Song6:5) הָסֵבִּי עֵינַיִךְ מִנֶּגְדִּי, [1 S 26:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:20) אל־יפֹל ´פני י ´דמי ארצה מנ; מנגד עיני *from before the eyes of*, with verbs of removing [Is 1:16](https://ref.ly/Isa1:16), cutting off [Psalm 31:23](https://ref.ly/Ps31:23), [Jon 2:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:5), hiding [Am 9:3](https://ref.ly/Amos9:3), [Je 16:17](https://ref.ly/Jer16:17). (β) *opposite to*, [Ne 3:19](https://ref.ly/Neh3:19), [3:25](https://ref.ly/Neh3:25), [3:27](https://ref.ly/Neh3:27); *at a distance from*, [Psalm 10:5](https://ref.ly/Ps10:5) מרום משׁפטיך מנגדו, [38:12](https://ref.ly/Ps38:12) מנגד נגעי יעמדו. (γ) מִנֶּגֶד לְ *from before*, [Pr 14:7](https://ref.ly/Prov14:7), *in front of* Ju [20:34](https://ref.ly/Judg20:34). **d.** † עַד נֶגֶד *as far as the front of*, [Ne 3:16](https://ref.ly/Neh3:16), [3:26](https://ref.ly/Neh3:26). 
> 
> 617:b. נֶגֶד **2 a:** note that in NH מכנגד \= *in front of,* Ber 4:5, 9:5, Ab 2:10, מכנגד פני Git 7:7 al.

## נָגַהּ (id: BDB05875)

\[Str5050] †נָגַהּ **vb.** shine (Assyrian *nagû, shine, beam, be joyful*, DlHWB 446; Eth. ነግሀ፡ Aram. ܢܓܰܗ; NH Targum נוֺגְהָא planet *Venus*)—**Qal** *Pf.* אוֺר ´ועל־דרכיך נ [Jb 22:28](https://ref.ly/Job22:28); עליהם ´אור נ [Is 9:1](https://ref.ly/Isa9:1); *Impf.* ולא־יִגַּהּ שְׁבִיב אִשּׁוֺ [Jb 18:5](https://ref.ly/Job18:5). **Hiph.** *Impf.* **1\.** *enlighten* ויהוה יַגִּיהַּ חָשְׁכִּי [2 S 22:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:29) \= יהוה אלהי יַגִּהַּ הּשׁכי [Psalm 18:29](https://ref.ly/Ps18:29). **2\.** *cause to shine* (of moon) וְיָרֵחַ ל־א :יַגִּיהַּ אוֺרוֺ [Is 13:10](https://ref.ly/Isa13:10) (‖חָשַׁך).

> \[Str5051] †I. נֹ֫גַהּ **n.f.**:Hb [3:4](https://ref.ly/Hab3:4) brightness;—´נ abs. [Am 5:10](https://ref.ly/Amos5:10) \+; cstr. [Is 4:5](https://ref.ly/Isa4:5) \+; sf. נָגְהָם [Jo 2:10](https://ref.ly/Joel2:10), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Joel3:15); —*brightness* of a (clear, sunshiny) day [Am 5:10](https://ref.ly/Amos5:10) (‖אוֺר; opp. אָפֵל, חשֶׁךְ), [2 S 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:4) (מִמָּטָר *after rain*); so fig. of Zion's dawning, זַרְחֵךְ ´נ [Is 60:3](https://ref.ly/Isa60:3) (‖אוֺר); of her righteousness כַּנֹּגַהּ [62:1](https://ref.ly/Isa62:1) (c. art. only here and Ez [1:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:28)), ‖כְּלַפִּיד יִבְעָר; of moon [Is 60:19](https://ref.ly/Isa60:19), of stars [Jo 2:10](https://ref.ly/Joel2:10), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Joel3:15); of theophany [2 S 22:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:13) \= [Psalm 18:13](https://ref.ly/Ps18:13) (in storm); cf. Ez [1:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:4), [1:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:27), [1:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:28), ´כְּבוֺד י ´נ [10:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:4); כאור ´נ Hb [3:4](https://ref.ly/Hab3:4); לָאֵשׁ ´נ Ez [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:13), אֵשׁ לֶהָבָה ´נ [Is 4:5](https://ref.ly/Isa4:5); בְּרַק ´נ חֲנִיתְֶָ Hb [3:11](https://ref.ly/Hab3:11) (of glitter of ´י's spear; ‖אוֺר חִצֶּיְ); of individ., לוֺ ´וְאֵין נ [Is 50:10](https://ref.ly/Isa50:10) (fig. of distress ‖חֲשֵׁכִים); so ´וְאֹרַח צַדִּיקִים כְּאוֺר נ [Pr 4:18](https://ref.ly/Prov4:18) *the path of righteous men is like a light of brightness.*

> \[Str5052] †II. נֹ֫גַהּ **n.pr.m.** a son of David acc. to [1 Ch 3:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:7), [14:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:6). LXX Ναγαι, Ναγεθ, etc.

> \[Str5054] †\[נְגֹהָה] **n.f.** brightness; fig. of prosperity;—; pl. לִנְנֹהוֺת [Is 59:9](https://ref.ly/Isa59:9) (opp. אֲפֵלוֺת; ‖אוֺר).

## נָגַח (id: BDB05879)

\[Str5055] †נָגַח **vb.** push, thrust, gore (NH *id.;* Ar. نَجَحَ *succeed, attain, make progress;* Targum as BH);—**Qal** *Impf.* יִגַּח [Ex 21:28](https://ref.ly/Exod21:28), [21:31](https://ref.ly/Exod21:31), [21:32](https://ref.ly/Exod21:32) (E), יִגָּח v:[31](https://ref.ly/Exod21:31); —; *gore*, subj. שׁוֺר, sq. acc. Pers. **Pi.** *Impf.* יְנַגַּח [Dt 33:17](https://ref.ly/Deut33:17); 2 ms תְּנַגַּח [1 K 22:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:11), [2 Ch 18:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:10); תְּנַגְּחוּ Ez [34:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:21); נְנַגֵּחַ [Psalm 44:6](https://ref.ly/Ps44:6); *Pt.* מְנַגֵּחַ [Dn 8:4](https://ref.ly/Dan8:4); —; *push* or *thrust at*, sq. acc.: of Joseph under fig. of wild ox [Dt 33:17](https://ref.ly/Deut33:17), cf. [1 K 22:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:11) \= [2 Ch 18:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:10), [Psalm 44:6](https://ref.ly/Ps44:6) (‖בּוּס); v. also Ez [34:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:21) (of Isr. under fig. of sheep), cf. [Dn 8:4](https://ref.ly/Dan8:4) (of ram in vision; obj. not expr.). **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְנַגַּח עִמּוֺ מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב [Dn 11:40](https://ref.ly/Dan11:40) *engage in thrusting with*, wage war with.

> \[Str5056] †נַגָּח **adj.** addicted to goring, pred. of שׁוֺר [Ex 21:29](https://ref.ly/Exod21:29), [21:36](https://ref.ly/Exod21:36) (E), cf. BaNB 49\.

## נגל (id: BDB05881)

נגל (√ of foll., meaning unknown; Ar. نَجَلَ is *strike, split, pierce*, but v. infr.).

> \[Str4038] †מַגָּל **n.\[m.]** sickle (Ar. مِنْجَلٌ(yet poss. Aram. loan\-word Frä133), Aram. ܡܰܓܰܠܬܳܐ);—c. תָּפַש *handle, wield* [Je 50:16](https://ref.ly/Jer50:16) (‖זָרַע); c. שָׁלַח [Jo 4:13](https://ref.ly/Joel4:13) fig. of judgement.

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 50:16; Joel 4:13


## נָגַּן (id: BDB05883)

\[Str5059] †\[נָגַּן] **vb.** touch (strings), play a stringed instrument (NH Pi.; Aram. נְגַן Pa.);—**Qal** *Pt.* נֹגְנִים [Psalm 68:26](https://ref.ly/Ps68:26) *players* (‖שָׁרִים, עֲלָמוֺת תּוֺפֵפוֺת). **Pi.** *Pf.* וְנִגֵּן [1 S 16:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:16) (consec.), v:[23](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:23); *Impf.* 1 pl. נְנַגֵּן [Is 38:20](https://ref.ly/Isa38:20); *Inf. cstr.* נַגֵּן [1 S 16:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:17) \+ 5 t.; *Pt.* מְנַגֵּן [1 S 16:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:16) \+ 4 t.;—*play* בַּכִנּוֺר [1 S 16:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:16); בְּיָדוֺ v:[16](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:16), v:[23](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:23) (כִּנּוֺר in context), [18:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:10), [19:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:9); c. acc. of cogn. [Is 38:20](https://ref.ly/Isa38:20); in sim. מֵטִב נַגֵּן Ez [33:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:32) (‖שִׁיר עֲגָבִים יְפֵה־קוֺל); אִישׁ ´מֵיטִיב לְנ [1 S 16:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:17), cf. [Psalm 33:3](https://ref.ly/Ps33:3), [Is 23:16](https://ref.ly/Isa23:16) (both ‖שִׁיר), ´יָדַע נ [1 S 16:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:18); also [2 K 3:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:15); Pt. \= *player, minstrel* (cf. Qal) [2 K 3:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:15), [3:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:15); cf. also אִישׁ יֹדֵעַ מְנַגֵּן בַכִנּוֺר [1 S 16:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:16) (Ges§ 120 b).

> \[Str5058] †\[נְגִינָה] **n.f.** music;—abs. נְגִינַת [Psalm 61:1](https://ref.ly/Ps61:1) (v.infr.); sf. נְגִינָתִי [Psalm 77:7](https://ref.ly/Ps77:7); נְגִינָתָם [Jb 30:9](https://ref.ly/Job30:9) \+ 2 t.; pl. נְגִינוֺת [Psalm 4:1](https://ref.ly/Ps4:1) \+ 6 t.; נְגִינָתַי [Is 38:20](https://ref.ly/Isa38:20); תָי\- Hb [3:19](https://ref.ly/Hab3:19); —**1\.** *music* (of stringed instr.) acc. cogn. [Is 38:20](https://ref.ly/Isa38:20); as occupation [La 5:14](https://ref.ly/Lam5:14); esp. בִּנְגִינוֺת in Psalm\-titles; [Psalm 4:1](https://ref.ly/Ps4:1), [6:1](https://ref.ly/Ps6:1), [54:1](https://ref.ly/Ps54:1), [55:1](https://ref.ly/Ps55:1), [67:1](https://ref.ly/Ps67:1), [76:1](https://ref.ly/Ps76:1); so Hb [3:19](https://ref.ly/Hab3:19); cf. עַל־נְגִינַת [Psalm 61:1](https://ref.ly/Ps61:1) (rd. perh. pl. Ges§ 80 f.). **2\.** *song* (with string accomp.?) [Psalm 77:7](https://ref.ly/Ps77:7); *subject of* mocking *song* [Jb 30:9](https://ref.ly/Job30:9) (‖מִלָּה), [Psalm 69:13](https://ref.ly/Ps69:13) (שׁוֺתֵי שֵׁכָר ´נ), [La 3:14](https://ref.ly/Lam3:14) (‖שְחֹק).

> \[Str4485] †\[מַנְגִּינָה] **n.f.** (mocking, derisive) song (cf. foreg.);—מַנְגִּינָתָם [La 3:63](https://ref.ly/Lam3:63), i.e. *subject of their mocking song*.

## נָגַף (id: BDB05888)

(stumble) [Pr 3:23](https://ref.ly/Prov3:23); esp. of judgment, subj. ´י, obj. individ. (fatal stroke) [1 S 25:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:38), [26:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:10), [2 S 12:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:15), [2 Ch 13:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:20), [21:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:18) (לָחֳלִי); obj. people (abs.) [Ex 12:23](https://ref.ly/Exod12:23), [12:23](https://ref.ly/Exod12:23), [12:27](https://ref.ly/Exod12:27) (JE), [32:35](https://ref.ly/Exod32:35), [Jos 24:5](https://ref.ly/Josh24:5) (E), [Is 19:22](https://ref.ly/Isa19:22), cf. v:[22](https://ref.ly/Isa19:22); v. also [Psalm 89:24](https://ref.ly/Ps89:24); cf. c. acc. of cogn. מַגֵּפָה [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12), [14:18](https://ref.ly/Zech14:18), [2 Ch 21:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:14) (sq. also בְּעַמְּ); c. בְּ instr. ## נָגַר (id: BDB05891)

\[Str5064] † \[נָגַר **vb.** pour, flow, run (Aram. ܢܓܰܪ *be long* (of time), Aph. *prolong, potract;* נְגַר *endure, flow, stream*);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נִגְּרָה [Psalm 77:3](https://ref.ly/Ps77:3), [La 3:49](https://ref.ly/Lam3:49); **Pt.** pl. נִגָּרִים [2 S 14:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:14); נִגָּרוֺת [Jb 20:28](https://ref.ly/Job20:28); —**1\.** *be poured, spilt,* [2 S 14:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:14) (כַּמַּיִם מֻגָּרִים אַרְצָה in sim. of mortality). **2\.** *pour oneself, flow, trickle*, of eye (in tears) [La 3:49](https://ref.ly/Lam3:49). **3\.** fig. *vanish*, [Jb 20:28](https://ref.ly/Job20:28) (‖גָּלָה). **4\.** *be stretched out (?)* לַיְלָה נִגְּרָה [Psalm 77:3](https://ref.ly/Ps77:3) (or rd. עֵינִי ?). **Hiph.** *Pf.* וְהִגַּרְתִּ֫י [Mi 1:6](https://ref.ly/Mic1:6); *Impf.* וַיַּגֵּר [Psalm 75:9](https://ref.ly/Ps75:9); וַתַּגֵּר Ez [35:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:5); יַגִּירֻ֫הוּ [Psalm 63:11](https://ref.ly/Ps63:11); *Imv.* sf. הַגִּרֵם [Je 18:21](https://ref.ly/Jer18:21); —*pour down, hurl down* the stones of Samaria, subj. ´י [Mi 1:6](https://ref.ly/Mic1:6); *pour out*, fig. of ´י's wine\-cup, [Psalm 75:9](https://ref.ly/Ps75:9); sq. עַל־יְדֵי חֶ֫רֶב \= *deliver over to* [Je 18:21](https://ref.ly/Jer18:21), [Psalm 63:11](https://ref.ly/Ps63:11), Ez [35:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:5). **Hoph.** *Pt.* כְּמַיִם מֻגָּרִים בְּמוֺרָד [Mi 1:4](https://ref.ly/Mic1:4) (in sim., of mountains melting at theophany.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:11; 2 Samuel 14:14; Lamentations 3:49; Jeremiah 18:21; Psalms 75:9; Micah 1:6; Ezekiel 35:5; Psalms 77:3; Job 20:28; Micah 1:4


## נָגַש (id: BDB05892)

\[Str5065] † \[נָגַש **vb.** press, drive, oppress, exact (Assyrian *nagâšu, throw down, overthrow*, DlHWB 448; Ar. نَجَشَ *rouse* and *drive* game, *drive vehemently;* Eth. ነግሠ፡ *reign, wield power*, ንጉሥ፡ *king*);—**Qal** *Pf.* ´נ [2 K 23:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:25); *Impf.* יִגּשֹׁ [Dt 15:2](https://ref.ly/Deut15:2); 2 ms. תִּגּשֹׁ [Dt 15:3](https://ref.ly/Deut15:3); 2 mpl. תִּנְ\+גּשֹׁוּ [Is 58:3](https://ref.ly/Isa58:3); —*Pt.* נֹגֵש [Is 9:3](https://ref.ly/Isa9:3) \+, etc.;—**1\.** *press, drive* to work (obj. עַצְּבֵיכֶם your workmen), [Is 58:3](https://ref.ly/Isa58:3) (GesComm., De Di Du; *exact* \[changing ´ע, q.v.] Che, etc.); v. **3\. 2\.** exact c. acc. כֶּסֶף, [2 K 23:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:35); sq. acc. of Pers. only [Dt 15:2](https://ref.ly/Deut15:2), [15:13](https://ref.ly/Deut15:13). **3\.** Pt. (\= subst.) *driver, task\-master* [Ex 3:7](https://ref.ly/Exod3:7), [5:6](https://ref.ly/Exod5:6), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Exod5:10), [5:13](https://ref.ly/Exod5:13), [5:14](https://ref.ly/Exod5:14), [Jb 3:18](https://ref.ly/Job3:18); (ass\-) driver [Jb 39:7](https://ref.ly/Job39:7); of (arbitrary) *ruler* [Is 3:12](https://ref.ly/Isa3:12), *ruler* (good sense) [Zc 10:4](https://ref.ly/Zech10:4); \= foreign *oppressor, tyrant* [Is 9:3](https://ref.ly/Isa9:3) (c. בְּ) [14:2](https://ref.ly/Isa14:2), [14:4](https://ref.ly/Isa14:4), [Zc 9:8](https://ref.ly/Zech9:8); *exactor* of tribute, [Dn 11:20](https://ref.ly/Dan11:20); pl. abstr. *lordship \= lord, ruler*, fig. of צְדָקָה (personif.) [Is 60:17](https://ref.ly/Isa60:17) (‖פְּקֻדָּתֵךְ שָׁלוֺם). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִגַּש [1 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:6) \+ 2 t. \+ [Is 3:5](https://ref.ly/Isa3:5) (וְ consec.);—*hard pressed* by enemy [1 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:6), and so [14:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:24) (but om. by emend., after LXX, Th We Dr Kit Bu HPS); *treated harshly*, of suffering servant of ´י [Is 53:7](https://ref.ly/Isa53:7); recipr. *tyrannize over* (´ב) *each other* Is 35.

## נָדָא (id: BDB05895)

\[Str5077] †\[נָדָא] **vb.** drive away, thrust aside (‖ form of נדה, q.v., or text. err.; Eth. ነድአ፡ *drive* cattle, etc.);—only **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. ´אֶת־יִשְרָאֵל מֵאַחֲרֵי י …וידא [2 K 17:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:21) Kt (Qr וַיַדַּח √ נדח **Hiph**.). p. 622

## נָדַב (id: BDB05896)

\[Str5068] †נָדַב **vb.** incite, impel (NH *id.;* Ar. نَدَبَ *call impel, incite* (نَدُبَ *be noble, willing, generous* is denom. fr. \[نَدْبٌ cf.] נָדִיב acc. to BaNB § 125 e); VIII. ندب *ready, willing;*As *nidbu, freewill\-offering* DlHWB 448; Aram. נְדַב *be willing* Pa. Ithpa.; Zinj. נדב *inciting, instigation* DHMSendsch. 60\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. נָדַב [Ex 35:29](https://ref.ly/Exod35:29); 3 fs. נָֽדְבָה v:[21](https://ref.ly/Exod35:21); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יִדְּבֶנּוּ [25:2](https://ref.ly/Exod25:2); —אֲשֶׁר נָדַב לִבּוֺ [25:2](https://ref.ly/Exod25:2) *whose heart incited* cf. [35:29](https://ref.ly/Exod35:29); subj. רוּחַ [35:21](https://ref.ly/Exod35:21) (all P). **Hithp.** *Pf.* 1 s. הִתְנַדַּבְתִּי [1 Ch 29:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:27); 3 mpl. הִתְנַדְּבוּ [Ezr 2:68](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:68), [1 Ch 29:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:9); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִתְנַדְּבוּ v:[6](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:6); *Inf.* הִתְנַדֵּב Ju [5:2](https://ref.ly/Judg5:2) \+ 2 t.; הִתְנַדֶּ־ב [1 Ch 29:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:17); sf הִתְנַדְּבָם v:[9](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:9); *Pt.* מִתְנַדֵּב [Ezr 3:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:5) \+ 2\.t.; pl. מִתְנַדְּבִים Ju [5:9](https://ref.ly/Judg5:9), [Ne 11:2](https://ref.ly/Neh11:2); —**1\.** *volunteer* for war Ju [5:2](https://ref.ly/Judg5:2), [5:9](https://ref.ly/Judg5:9); for service of other kinds [2 Ch 17:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:16), [Ne 11:2](https://ref.ly/Neh11:2). **2\.** *offer freewill\-offerings* for the first temple [1 Ch 29:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:5), [29:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:6), [29:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:9), [29:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:9), [29:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:14), [29:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:17), [29:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:17); for the second [Ezr 1:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:4), [2:68](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:68), [3:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:5) (cf. BAram.).

> \[Str5070] †נָדָב **n.pr.m.** (*generous, noble)*—**1\.** eldest son of Aaron [Ex 6:23](https://ref.ly/Exod6:23), [24:1](https://ref.ly/Exod24:1), [24:9](https://ref.ly/Exod24:9), [28:1](https://ref.ly/Exod28:1), [Lv 10:1](https://ref.ly/Lev10:1), [Nu 3:2](https://ref.ly/Num3:2), [3:4](https://ref.ly/Num3:4), [26:60](https://ref.ly/Num26:60), [26:61](https://ref.ly/Num26:61), [1 Ch 5:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:29), [24:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:1). LXX Ναβατ, Ναβαθ, LXXL Ναδαβ. **3\.** a Jerahmeelite [1 Ch 2:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:28), [2:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:30). **4\.** a Gibeonite [1 Ch 8:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:30), [9:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:36).

> \[Str5081] † נָדִיב **adj.** and **n.m.**:[Is 32:8](https://ref.ly/Isa32:8) inclined, generous, noble;—abs. ´נ [Is 32:5](https://ref.ly/Isa32:5) \+ 6 t.; cstr. נְדִיב [Ex 35:5](https://ref.ly/Exod35:5) \+ 2 t.; f. נְדִיבָה [Psalm 51:4](https://ref.ly/Ps51:4); pl. נְדִיבִים [Is 13:2](https://ref.ly/Isa13:2) \+ 9 t.; cstr. נְדִיבֵי [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18) \+ 2 t.; sf. נְדִיבֵמוֺ [Psalm 83:12](https://ref.ly/Ps83:12); —**1\.** *incited, inclined, willing*, נְדִיב לֵב [Ex 35:5](https://ref.ly/Exod35:5), [35:22](https://ref.ly/Exod35:22) (P), [1 Ch 29:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:31) (v. נָדַב **Qal**), נָדִיב בְחָכְמָה [28:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:21); רוּחַ נְדִיבָה [Psalm 51:14](https://ref.ly/Ps51:14) *a willing spirit.* **2\.** *noble, princely,* in rank [1 S 2:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:8) (poem), [Jb 12:21](https://ref.ly/Job12:21) \= [Psalm 107:40](https://ref.ly/Ps107:40), [Jb 34:18](https://ref.ly/Job34:18), [Pr 8:16](https://ref.ly/Prov8:16), [Psalm 47:10](https://ref.ly/Ps47:10), [83:12](https://ref.ly/Ps83:12), [113:8](https://ref.ly/Ps113:8), [113:8](https://ref.ly/Ps113:8), [118:9](https://ref.ly/Ps118:9), [146:3](https://ref.ly/Ps146:3); פְּנֵי נדיב [Pr 19:6](https://ref.ly/Prov19:6), [25:7](https://ref.ly/Prov25:7); ´בית נ [Jb 21:28](https://ref.ly/Job21:28); ´פתחי נ [Is 13:2](https://ref.ly/Isa13:2); ´בת נ Ct [7:2](https://ref.ly/Song7:2); נְדִיבֵי הָעָם [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18) (poem). **3\.** *noble* in mind and character ‖צדּיק [Pr 17:26](https://ref.ly/Prov17:26); opp. נָבָל [Is 32:5](https://ref.ly/Isa32:5), [32:8](https://ref.ly/Isa32:8).

> \[Str5082] †\[נְדִיבָה] **n.f.** nobility, nobleness;—sf. נְדִבָתִי [Jb 30:15](https://ref.ly/Job30:15); pl. נְדִיבוֺת [Is 32:8](https://ref.ly/Isa32:8), [32:8](https://ref.ly/Isa32:8); —**1\.** *nobility* of rank, *honour* [Jb 30:15](https://ref.ly/Job30:15). **2\.** pl. \= *noble things* as counselled by the נדיב in character [Is 32:8](https://ref.ly/Isa32:8), [32:8](https://ref.ly/Isa32:8).

> \[Str5114] †נוֺדָב **n. pr. gent.** Arab tribe acc. to [1 Ch 5:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:19) (\+ יְטוּר, נָפִישׁ q.v. [Gn 25:15](https://ref.ly/Gen25:15) [1 Ch 1:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:31)); LXX Ναδαβαιων. (Connex. with above √ quite uncertain.)

> \[Str5072] †נְדַבְיָה **n.pr.m.** (*whom Yah impels;* in As. *Na\-ad\-bi\-ia\-a\-u* JägerBAS i. 468\);—son of Jeconiah [1 Ch 3:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:18). LXX Δενεθει, LXXL Ναδαβια.

## I נָדַד (id: BDB05903)

\[Str5074] †I. \[נָדַד] **vb.** retreat, flee, depart, stray, wander, flutter (NH *id.;* Ar. نَدَّ *flee, run away;* Aram. ܢܰܕ *hate, abominate, shrink from,* Aph. *put to flight,* etc.; נְדַד *flee* );—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. נָֽדְדָה [Is 10:31](https://ref.ly/Isa10:31), [Est 6:1](https://ref.ly/Esth6:1); 3 pl. נָֽדְדוּ [Ho 7:13](https://ref.ly/Hos7:13) \+ 4 t., נָדָדוּ [Is 21:15](https://ref.ly/Isa21:15), [Je 4:25](https://ref.ly/Jer4:25); *Impf.* יִדּוֺד [Na 3:7](https://ref.ly/Nah3:7); 3 fs. וַתִּדֹּד [Gn 31:40](https://ref.ly/Gen31:40); 3 mpl. יִדֹּדוּן [Psalm 68:13](https://ref.ly/Ps68:13), [68:13](https://ref.ly/Ps68:13); *Inf. cstr.* נְדֹד [Psalm 55:8](https://ref.ly/Ps55:8); *Pt.* נֹדֵד [Je 49:5](https://ref.ly/Jer49:5) \+; f. נוֺדֶ֫דֶת [Pr 27:8](https://ref.ly/Prov27:8); pl. נֹדְדִים [Ho 9:17](https://ref.ly/Hos9:17); —**1\.** *retreat, flee* [Is 21:15](https://ref.ly/Isa21:15) *fr. before* (מִפְּנֵי) *swords;* [33:3](https://ref.ly/Isa33:3) *at the sound* (מִקּוֺל) *of tumult;* abs. [10:31](https://ref.ly/Isa10:31), [22:3](https://ref.ly/Isa22:3) [Psalm 68:13](https://ref.ly/Ps68:13), [68:13](https://ref.ly/Ps68:13), of birds and beasts [Je 4:25](https://ref.ly/Jer4:25) [9:9](https://ref.ly/Jer9:9) (\+ הָלָכוּ); pt.\= *fugitive* [Is 16:3](https://ref.ly/Isa16:3), [21:14](https://ref.ly/Isa21:14); of fleeing in horror, disgust, c. מִן Pers. [Na 3:7](https://ref.ly/Nah3:7), [Psalm 31:12](https://ref.ly/Ps31:12). **2\.** *flee, depart*, Isr. fr. (מִן) ´י [Ho 7:13](https://ref.ly/Hos7:13); of sleep (מֵעֵינַי) [Gn 31:40](https://ref.ly/Gen31:40) (E), abs. [Est 6:1](https://ref.ly/Esth6:1). **3\.** *wander, stray* (homeless, cf. נוד), עוֺף נוֺדֵד [Is 16:2](https://ref.ly/Isa16:2) (in sim.), so [Pr 27:8](https://ref.ly/Prov27:8) (מִן loc., in sim.), cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Prov27:8) (מִן loc.); of scattered Isr. [Ho 9:17](https://ref.ly/Hos9:17), [Je 49:5](https://ref.ly/Jer49:5); (searching) for food [Jb 15:23](https://ref.ly/Job15:23) (wicked man); אַרְתִיק נְדֹד [Psalm 55:8](https://ref.ly/Ps55:8) *I will wander afar.* **4\.** trans. *flutter* wings (of bird, in fig.) [Is 10:14](https://ref.ly/Isa10:14). **Po‘ēl** *Pf.* consec. שֶׁמֶשׁ זָֽרְחָה וְנֹדַ֔ד [Na 3:17](https://ref.ly/Nah3:17) the sun hath arisen and it ( the locust swarm) *fleeth away.* **Hiph.** *chase away, Impf.* 3 mpl. sf. וּמִתֵּבֵל יְנִדָֻּהוּ [Jb 18:18](https://ref.ly/Job18:18) (of wicked, ‖יֶהְדְּפֻהוּ מֵאוֺר). **Hoph.** *be chased away, Impf.* 3 ms. וְיֻדַּד כְּחֶזְיוֺן [Jb 20:8](https://ref.ly/Job20:8) (of wicked); קוֺץ מֻנַָד [2 S 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:6) *thorns thrust away* (si vera l., cf. Dr.; Klo Bu HPS מִדְבָּר ´ק). **Hithpoïl.** *Impf.* יִתְנוֺדְדוּ [Psalm 64:9](https://ref.ly/Ps64:9) *flee away* (in horror, disgust, cf. **Qal** [Na 3:7](https://ref.ly/Nah3:7), [Psalm 31:12](https://ref.ly/Ps31:12)).

> \[Str5076] †\[נְדוּד] **n.\[m.]** tossing of sleeplessness, only pl. נְדֻדִים [Jb 7:4](https://ref.ly/Job7:4) I am sated with *tossings*.

> \[Str5079] †נִדָּה **n.f.** impurity (as *abhorrent, shunned);*—abs. ´נ Ez [18:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:6) \+; cstr. נִדַּת [Lv 12:2](https://ref.ly/Lev12:2) \+; sf. נִדָּתָהּ v:[5](https://ref.ly/Lev12:5) \+;—*impurity* (esp. P and Ezek.): **1\.** of ceremonial impurity, as union with brother's wife, [Lv 20:21](https://ref.ly/Lev20:21); esp. of menstruation Ez [18:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:6) (rd. אִשָּׁה בְּנִדָּתָהּ for MT נִדָּה ´א SyrVer Co), ´טְמֵאַת הַנּ [22:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:10), cf. [36:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:17), נִדַּת דְּוֺתָהּ [Lv 12:2](https://ref.ly/Lev12:2), cf. v:[5](https://ref.ly/Lev12:5), [15:19](https://ref.ly/Lev15:19), [15:20](https://ref.ly/Lev15:20), [15:24](https://ref.ly/Lev15:24), [15:25](https://ref.ly/Lev15:25) (´עֶת־נִדּ), v:[25](https://ref.ly/Lev15:25), v:[25](https://ref.ly/Lev15:25), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Lev15:26) (´מִשְׁכַּב נִדּ), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Lev15:26) (´טֻמְאַת נִדּ), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Lev15:33), [18:19](https://ref.ly/Lev18:19); of defilement contracted by contact with a corpse, נִּדָּה(הַ)מֵי *water of impurity* (i.e. to remove it, cf. Di) [Nu 19:9](https://ref.ly/Num19:9), [19:13](https://ref.ly/Num19:13), [19:20](https://ref.ly/Num19:20), [19:21](https://ref.ly/Num19:21), [19:21](https://ref.ly/Num19:21), [31:23](https://ref.ly/Num31:23). **2\.** fig. (with allusion to cerem. usage), *impure thing,* Ez [7:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:19), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:20) (of gold), [La 1:17](https://ref.ly/Lam1:17) (of Jerus.); of idolatry, immorality, etc., [2 Ch 29:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:5), [Ezr 9:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:11) (אֶרֶץ נִדָּה), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:11), [Zc 13:1](https://ref.ly/Zech13:1) (לְחַטַּאת וּלְנִדָּה).

## II נדד (id: BDB05907)

II. נדד (√ of foll., v. Köii. 1, 42; cf. Ar. نَدٌّ *high hill, hill rising high into the sky,* Lane2778; also *earth\-heap, sand\-heap*).

> \[Str5067] †נֵד **n.m.**:[Jos 3:13](https://ref.ly/Josh3:13) heap of waters; of Red Sea [Ex 15:8](https://ref.ly/Exod15:8) (song), [Psalm 78:13](https://ref.ly/Ps78:13); of Jordan [Jos 3:13](https://ref.ly/Josh3:13), [3:16](https://ref.ly/Josh3:16), [Psalm 33:7](https://ref.ly/Ps33:7) (sim.). נֵד קָצִיר [Is 17:11](https://ref.ly/Isa17:11) *harvest\-heap* is perhaps corrupt: Ges Ew Di Du der. from נוד, reading (probably) נָד (Di) *fled is the harvest;* CheHpt proposes לָכֵן *therefore;* BuhlLex 13 עַד, after LXX Targum.

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 3:16; Joshua 3:13; Exodus 15:8; Psalms 78:13; Psalms 33:7; Isaiah 17:11


## I נָדָה (id: BDB05909)

\[Str5077] †I. \[נָדָה] **vb. Pi.** put away, exclude (NH Pi. (*excommunicate*) Hithp. Niph.; As.*naduû, throw, overthrow, destroy*, etc., DlHWB 448 f.);—**Pi.** *Pt.* הַמְַנַדִּים לְיוֺם רַע [Am 6:3](https://ref.ly/Amos6:3) *they that thrust off the evil day* (i.e. refuse to think of it); lit. מְנַדֵּיכֶם [Is 66:5](https://ref.ly/Isa66:5) *thrust away, exclude* from association in worship (on usage v. Che).

* **Associated Passages:** Amos 6:3; Isaiah 66:5


## II נדה (id: BDB05910)

II. נדה (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. نَدِىَ *be moist, moistened,* and also *betide, befal;* نَدًى *rain, dew*, and also *bounty, liberality, a gift*, Lane: 3030\).

> \[Str5078] †ֶ\+נדֶה (so Baer Ginsb; van d. H. ֵ\+נדֶה), **n.m.** gift (on format. cf. Ges§ 84a c Ol:§ 144a Köii.1, 65 BaNB § 12a);—לְכָל־זֹנוֺת יִתְּנ־וֶּ\+נדֶה Ez [16:33](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:33) (‖נְדָבִים).

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 16:33


## נָדַח (id: BDB05912)

\[Str5080] †\[נָדַח] **vb.** impel, thrust, banish (esp. Dt. Je. and later) (NH *id., beguile*, Hiph, Niph.; Aram. נְדַח (rare) \= BH; Eth. ነድሐ፡; *thrust*; Ar. نَدَخَ is *drive* a ship to *shore*);—**Qal** *Impf.* יִדַּח [2 S 14:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:14); Inf. לִנְדֹּחַ [Dt 20:19](https://ref.ly/Deut20:19); —trans. **1\.** *impel* (i.e. *wield*) an axe, sq. עַ־ל [Dt 20:19](https://ref.ly/Deut20:19); **2\.** *thrust away, banish* sq. מִ־ן [2 S 14:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:14). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נִדְּחָה [Jb 6:13](https://ref.ly/Job6:13), etc.; (*Impf.* יִדַּ֫חוּ [Je 23:12](https://ref.ly/Jer23:12) v. דחח); *Pt.* נִדָּח [2 S 14:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:14); נִדָּחָה [Mi 4:6](https://ref.ly/Mic4:6) \+ 2 t.; נִדַּ֫חַת Ez [34:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:4), [34:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:16), etc. On נִדְחֵי [Is 11:12](https://ref.ly/Isa11:12) [56:8](https://ref.ly/Isa56:8), [Psalm 147:2](https://ref.ly/Ps147:2) v. Ki Köi. 317;—**1\.** *be impelled:* וְנִדְּחָה יָרוֺ בַּגַּרְוֶן *be impelled* with the axe [Dt 19:5](https://ref.ly/Deut19:5). **2\.** *be thrust out, banished:* of dispersion in exile, sq. שָׁם [Je 40:12](https://ref.ly/Jer40:12), [43:5](https://ref.ly/Jer43:5) (‖שׁוּב); sq. לִפְנֵי [49:5](https://ref.ly/Jer49:5) (‖קִבֵּץ); cf. also fig. תֻּשִׁיָּה נִדְּחָה מִמֶּנִּי [Jb 6:13](https://ref.ly/Job6:13) *is banished from me*, i.e. is non\-existent; esp. Pt. *banished one(s), outcast(s)*, as acc. cogn. c. נָדַח[2 S 14:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:14); c. קִבֵּץ[Mi 4:6](https://ref.ly/Mic4:6), [Dt 30:4](https://ref.ly/Deut30:4), [Zp 3:19](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:19), [Ne 1:9](https://ref.ly/Neh1:9); יִשִרָאֵל ´נ, c. אָסַף [Is 11:12](https://ref.ly/Isa11:12) (‖נְפֻצוֺת יְקַבֵּץ); c. קִבֵּץ [56:8](https://ref.ly/Isa56:8); c. כִּנֵּס [Psalm 147:2](https://ref.ly/Ps147:2); c. הֵשִׁיב [2 S 14:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:13), Ez [34:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:4), [34:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:16) (both ‖אֹבֵד); c. בּוֺא [Is 27:13](https://ref.ly/Isa27:13) (‖ *id.*); v. also [Is 16:3](https://ref.ly/Isa16:3), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Isa16:4), [Je 49:36](https://ref.ly/Jer49:36); *outcast \= neglected one*, fig. of Zion [30:17](https://ref.ly/Jer30:17) (‖דֹּרֵשׁ אֵין לָהּ). **3\.** *driven away* (from herd, i.e.) *straying* [Dt 22:1](https://ref.ly/Deut22:1). **4\.** *thrust away, aside from* ´י *to idiolatry* [Dt 4:19](https://ref.ly/Deut4:19), [30:17](https://ref.ly/Deut30:17) (sq. וְהִשְׁתַּחֲוָה).

 **Pu.** *Pt.* אֲקֵלָה מְנֻדָּח [Is 8:22](https://ref.ly/Isa8:22) *thrust into darkness*. **Hiph.** *Pf.* וְהִדִּיחַ consec. [2 S 15:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:14); הִדַּ֫חְתִּי [Je 23:3](https://ref.ly/Jer23:3) [29:14](https://ref.ly/Jer29:14), etc.; *Impf.* sf. וַיַּדַּח [2 Ch 21:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:11) \+ [2 K 17:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:21) Qr; וַיַּדִּ֫יחוּ [Dt 13:14](https://ref.ly/Deut13:14), etc.; *Imv.* sf. הַדִּיחֵ֫מוֺ [Psalm 5:11](https://ref.ly/Ps5:11); *Inf.* הַדִּיחַ [62:5](https://ref.ly/Ps62:5), etc.;—**1\.** *thrust, move, impel*, fig., obj. רָעָה [2 S 15:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:14) (sq. עַ־ל. **2\.** *thrust out, banish, esp.* of exile, subj. ´י, c. acc. sq. שָׁ֫מָּה [Dt 30:1](https://ref.ly/Deut30:1), [Je 46:28](https://ref.ly/Jer46:28); [16:15](https://ref.ly/Jer16:15) (‖הֶעֱלָה); sq. שָׁם [Je 8:3](https://ref.ly/Jer8:3), [24:9](https://ref.ly/Jer24:9), [29:18](https://ref.ly/Jer29:18), [Dn 9:7](https://ref.ly/Dan9:7) \+ Ez [4:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:13) (om. LXX Co); [Je 23:3](https://ref.ly/Jer23:3) (‖קִבֵּץ), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Jer23:8) (‖הֶעֱלָה, הֵבִיא) [29:14](https://ref.ly/Jer29:14) (‖קִבֵּץ, הֵשֹׁיב), so [32:37](https://ref.ly/Jer32:37); also [27:10](https://ref.ly/Jer27:10) (‖הַרְחִיק), v:[15](https://ref.ly/Jer27:15) (‖אָבַד), cf. ## נָדַף (id: BDB05916)

\[Str5086] † \[נָדַף] **vb.** drive, drive asunder ( NH *id., spread, be diffused, disseminated* (of odour); Ar. نَدَفَ *strike, beat*, esp. bow\-string with mallet, to clean cotton; also *play* the lyre; Aram. נְדַף \= NH; Eth. ነደፈ፡ *throw, hurl*);—**Qal** *Impf.* sf. יִדְּפֶנּוּ [Jb 32:13](https://ref.ly/Job32:13) (juss.); 3 fs. sf. תִּדְּפֶנּוּ [Psalm 1:4](https://ref.ly/Ps1:4); 2 ms. תִּנְדֹּף [Psalm 68:3](https://ref.ly/Ps68:3); —*drive about, chaff*, subj. wind [Psalm 1:4](https://ref.ly/Ps1:4); wicked, subj. God [68:3](https://ref.ly/Ps68:3) (כְּהִנְדֹּף עָשָׁן, v. infr.); cf. [Jb 32:13](https://ref.ly/Job32:13) (\= defeat, anguish).

 **Niph**. *Pf.* נִדַּף [Is 19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7); *Inf.*. כְּהִנְדֹּף [Psalm 68:3](https://ref.ly/Ps68:3) (v. Köi. 306 f); *Pt.* נִדָּף [Lv 26:36](https://ref.ly/Lev26:36) \+ 3 t.;—*be driven, driven about* (esp. by wind) [Psalm 68:3](https://ref.ly/Ps68:3), v. supr. (like a driving \[Che *drift*] of smoke); of breath (הֶבֶל) fig. of deceitful gain [Pr 21:6](https://ref.ly/Prov21:6) (LXX Hi Ew רֹדֵף; v. De Now); of withered plant [Is 19:7](https://ref.ly/Isa19:7) (where subj. עָרוֺת, מִוְרָע i.e. place; whence Che trans. ´נ *vanish*; but ref. prob. to plant\-life in these places, v. Di); of leaf [Lv 26:36](https://ref.ly/Lev26:36) (as making slight sound), [Jb 13:25](https://ref.ly/Job13:25) (fig. of sufferer, ‖קַשׁ יָבֵשׁ), of chaff (קַשׁ) [Is 41:2](https://ref.ly/Isa41:2) (sim. of bow of hostile kings ‖עָפָר).* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 21:6; Isaiah 41:2; Psalms 1:4; Job 13:25; Isaiah 19:7; Psalms 68:3; Job 32:13; Leviticus 26:36

## I נָהַג (id: BDB05920)

\[Str5090] †I. נָהַג **vb.** drive, conduct (NH *conduct oneself, behave, be accustomed;* Ar. نَهَجَ go along the road, keep to the road; Aram. נְהַג lead);—**Qal** *Pf.* ´נ [La 3:2](https://ref.ly/Lam3:2); נָהֲגוּ [1 S 30:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:20); *Impf.* יִנְהַג [Is 20:4](https://ref.ly/Isa20:4), etc.; *Imv.* נְהַג [2 K 4:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:24); *Pt. act.* נֹהֵג [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6) \+ 2 t., etc.; pass. נְהוּגִים [Is 60:11](https://ref.ly/Isa60:11); —**1\.** *drive*, flocks [Ex 3:1](https://ref.ly/Exod3:1); cf. (in predict.) [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6) (sq. בְּ); fig., obj. Joseph, [Psalm 80:2](https://ref.ly/Ps80:2) (sim. of flock); hence, obj. human being, lead on, Ct [8:2](https://ref.ly/Song8:2) (‖הֵבִיא אֶ־ל)); also fig., subj. ´י, [La 3:2](https://ref.ly/Lam3:2) (‖הוֺלִיח); pass. pt. of kings *led* in procession [Is 60:11](https://ref.ly/Isa60:11) cf. Di (rather than as captives, Che, as earlier usage; ‖הֵבִיא); *drive away, off,* (obj. מִקְנֶה) [Gn 31:18](https://ref.ly/Gen31:18) ( of Jacob, going by stealth); usually as booty (obj. flocks and herds) [1 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:5), חמור יתומים ינהגו [Jb 24:3](https://ref.ly/Job24:3) (unjustly); cf. [1 S 30:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:2), [30:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:2) (rd. וַיִּנְהֲגוּ We Dr Kit Bu, cf. Th, not HPS q. v.), v:[22](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:22) (no obj. expr. but really captives ‖הָלַח), [Is 20:4](https://ref.ly/Isa20:4); drive an ass for riding [2 K 4:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:24) (‖הָלַח); a cart (עֲגָלָה) [2 S 6:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:3) \= [1 Ch 13:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:7) (where ´בָּע); abs. of driving chariot [2 K 9:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:20); lead out an army to battle (late) [1 Ch 20:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:1), [2 Ch 25:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:11). **2\.** fig. וְלִבִּי נֹהֵג בַּחָכְמָה [Ec 2:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:3) my heart *behaving itself* in wisdom (cf. NH supr.). **Pi.** *Pf.* נִהַג [Ex 10:3](https://ref.ly/Exod10:3); נִהַ֫גְתָּ [Is 63:14](https://ref.ly/Isa63:14); *Impf.* יְנַהֵג [Dt 4:27](https://ref.ly/Deut4:27), etc.;—**1\.** *drive away, lead off,* sq. acc., daughters of Laban [Gn 31:26](https://ref.ly/Gen31:26) (‖גָּנַב); of ´י, *leading off* people into exile, sq. שָׁ֫מָּה [Dt 4:27](https://ref.ly/Deut4:27) (‖הֵפִיץ), [28:37](https://ref.ly/Deut28:37) (‖הוֺלִיח). **2\.** lead on, guide, sq. acc., subj. ´י [Psalm 78:52](https://ref.ly/Ps78:52) (כַּעֵ֫דֶר ‖הִסִּיעַ כַּצּאֹן ); [Is 49:10](https://ref.ly/Isa49:10) (‖נִהֵל, cf. [Psalm 23:2](https://ref.ly/Ps23:2)); [63:14](https://ref.ly/Isa63:14); [Psalm 48:15](https://ref.ly/Ps48:15); guide on, a wind, [Ex 10:13](https://ref.ly/Exod10:13) (sq. בָּאָרֶץ), [Psalm 78:26](https://ref.ly/Ps78:26); (‖הִסִּיעַ). **3\.** cause to drive (cf. **Qal**, [2 K 9:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:20)) [Ex 14:25](https://ref.ly/Exod14:25) (JE; subj. ´י, v. [Je 9:19](https://ref.ly/Jer9:19), where taught (‖קִינָת), also הִי Ez [2:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:10) (‖אֵכֶל, מִסְפֵּד q. v. (Ol Co rd. נתי, cf. Berthol).

[Ex 15:13](https://ref.ly/Exod15:13) (song; no obj. expressed, אֶ־ל loc.; ‖נחה); human subj. [2 Ch 28:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:15) *and they conducted* all the feeble of them *by means of* (ב) *asses* (‖הֵבִיא). **3\.** *lead, guide:* fig., אֵין מְנַהֵל לָהּ [Is 51:18](https://ref.ly/Isa51:18) *there is no one to be a guide for her,* i.e. for Jerus., drunk with cup of ´י's fury (‖מַחֲזִיק יָדָהּ); ´י subj. LXX Vulgate (\= ´וַיָּנִח לָהֶם מִסּ [1 Ch 22:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:18) \+, prob. so read here, v. Be DHM Öt). **5\.** *refresh* with food, ´וַיְנ בַּלֶּחֶם [Gn 47:17](https://ref.ly/Gen47:17) (J).

 **Hithp.** *Impf.* prob. *journey by stations, stages,* only 1 S. אֶתְנַהֲלָה [Gn 33:14](https://ref.ly/Gen33:14) (J), *I will proceed, journey on, by stages* (i.e. deliberately with family and cattle).

\[Str5098] † \[נָהַם] **vb. growl, groan** (NH id.; Ar. نَهَمَ; Aram.ܢܗܰܡ, נְהַם);—**Qal** *Pf.* וְנָהַמְתָּ֫ [Pr 5:11](https://ref.ly/Prov5:11); וּנְהַמְתֶּם Ez [24:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:23); *Impf.* יִנְהֹם [Is 5:29](https://ref.ly/Isa5:29), [5:30](https://ref.ly/Isa5:30); **Pt.** נֹהֵם [Pr 28:15](https://ref.ly/Prov28:15); —**1\.** *growl,* of a lion (viz. while it is devouring its prey: opp. שׁאנ, of *roar* with which it springs upon it RSProph. 243\) [Pr 28:15](https://ref.ly/Prov28:15), of Assyr. (under fig. of lion) [Is 5:29](https://ref.ly/Isa5:29); sq. עַל (*over*), v:[30](https://ref.ly/Isa5:30). **2\.** *groan,* of sufferer [Pr 5:11](https://ref.ly/Prov5:11), Ez [24:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:23).

## נוא (id: BDB05941)

\[Str5106] † \[נוא] **vb.** hinder, restrain, frustrate (Ar. (نوأ) نَآءَ (or نَاءَ) *rise with difficulty, rise against one* (Frey), III. *contend with*, Lane2861);—**Qal** *Impf.* 2 mpl. תנואון [Nu 32:7](https://ref.ly/Num32:7) Kt, but read Hiph. (so Qr), cf. Di. **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֵנִיא [Nu 30:6](https://ref.ly/Num30:6), etc.; *Impf.* יָנִיא v:[9](https://ref.ly/Num30:9), יָנִי [Psalm 141:5](https://ref.ly/Ps141:5), תְּנִיאוּן [Nu 32:7](https://ref.ly/Num32:7) Qr;—**1\.** *restrain, forbid* (performance of vow), sq. acc. Pers. [Nu 30:6](https://ref.ly/Num30:6), [30:6](https://ref.ly/Num30:6), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Num30:9), [30:12](https://ref.ly/Num30:12); *frustrate* (device of people), subj. ´י, sq. acc. rei, [Psalm 33:10](https://ref.ly/Ps33:10) (‖הֵפִיר); *refuse* [141:5](https://ref.ly/Ps141:5) ((text dub. v. Che). **2\.** *restrain, make averse* the heart (acc.) [Nu 32:7](https://ref.ly/Num32:7) (sq. מֵעֲבֹר), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Num32:9) (sq. לְבִלְתִּי־באֹ).

> \[Str8569] † \[תְּנוּאָה] **n. f.** opposition;—sf. תְּנוּאָתִיּ [Nu 14:34](https://ref.ly/Num14:34) *my opposition*, of ´י; pl. תְּנוּאוֺת עָלַי יִמְצָא [Jb 33:10](https://ref.ly/Job33:10) \= occasions, for hostility (‖ count as enemy).

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 32:9; Numbers 30:9; Numbers 32:7; Numbers 30:6; Job 33:10; Numbers 14:34; Psalms 33:10; Numbers 30:12; Psalms 141:5


## נוּב (id: BDB05943)

\[Str5107] † \[נוּב] **vb.** bear fruit (poet.) (Aram. נוֺבָא *fruit* (rare));—**Qal** *Impf.* יָנוּב [Psalm 62:11](https://ref.ly/Ps62:11), [Pr 10:31](https://ref.ly/Prov10:31); יְנוּבוּן [Psalm 92:15](https://ref.ly/Ps92:15); —only fig., abs. of righteous under fig. of tree [Psalm. 92:15](https://ref.ly/Ps92:15); חַיִל כִּי יָנוּב [62:11](https://ref.ly/Ps62:11) if wealth *beareth fruit;* [Pr 10:31](https://ref.ly/Prov10:31) פִּי צַדִּיק יָנוּב חָכְמָה *beareth the fruit of* wisdom. **Poï1\.** *Impf.* יְנוֺבֵב [Zc 9:17](https://ref.ly/Zech9:17), דָּגָן בְּתֻלוֺת ´בַּחוּרִים וְתִירוֺשׁ יְנ fig. for *makes flourish*.

> \[Str5108] † נוֺב Kt (Qr נִיב, q. v.) **n.**\[**m.**] fruit;—cstr. [Is 57:19](https://ref.ly/Isa57:19) *fruit of lips* (fig. for thanksgiving).

> \[Str5108] † \[נִיב] **n.**\[**m.**] fruit ;—ניב cstr. [Is 57:19](https://ref.ly/Isa57:19) Qr, v. supr.; נִיבוֺ [Mal 1:12](https://ref.ly/Mal1:12) of offerings to ´י as fruit of his table (‖אכלו).

> \[Str5109] † נוֺבָי **n. pr. m.** a chief of people [Ne 10:20](https://ref.ly/Neh10:20) Qr נִיבָי (Baer; נֵיבָי van d. H., Ginsb; LXX Νωβαι (cf. Palm. נבי n. pr. f. Cook124 Lzb321).

> \[Str8570] תְּנוּבָה **n. f.** fruit, produce;—abs. [Is 27:6](https://ref.ly/Isa27:6); cstr. תְּנוּבֵת Ez [36:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:30); תְּנוּבָתִי Ju [9:11](https://ref.ly/Judg9:11); pl. תְּנוּבֹת [Dt 32:13](https://ref.ly/Deut32:13), וֺת\- [La 4:9](https://ref.ly/Lam4:9); esp. שָדַי ´ת *fruit, produce, of field* [Dt 32:13](https://ref.ly/Deut32:13), [La 4:9](https://ref.ly/Lam4:9); cf. הַשָּדֶה ´ת Ez [36:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:30) (‖פרי העץ); of fig\-tree Ju [9:11](https://ref.ly/Judg9:11); metaph. of Isr. וּמָֽלְאוּ ´פְנֵי־תֵבֵל ת [Is 27:6](https://ref.ly/Isa27:6) (‖´יַשְׁרֵשׁ יעקב יציץ ופרח יש).
