## נוּד (id: BDB05948)

\[Str5110] † נוּד **vb.** move to and fro, wander, flutter, shew grief (NH *id.* (rare); Aram. ܢܳܕ נוּד, *shake, move to and fro, be disturbed, agitated;* BAram. *flee* (v. infr.); Ar. (نود)نَادَ *waver, totter*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. נָד so rd. for MT נֵד [Is 17:11](https://ref.ly/Isa17:11) Ges Hi Ew Di Kit CheHpt RV; 3 pl. נָ֫דוּ [Je 50:3](https://ref.ly/Jer50:3); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָנוּד [1 K 14:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:15) \+ 3 t.; 2 ms. תָּנוּד [Je 4:1](https://ref.ly/Jer4:1) juss. תָּנֹד [16:5](https://ref.ly/Jer16:5); 3 mpl. וַיָּנֻ֫דוּ [Jb 42:11](https://ref.ly/Job42:11); 2 mpl. תָּנֻ֫דוּ [Je 22:10](https://ref.ly/Jer22:10); *Imv.* fs. פָנ֫וּדִי [Psalm 11:1](https://ref.ly/Ps11:1) Qr (Kt נודו); mpl. נֻ֫דוּ Je 48:l7 \+ 2 t. Je; *Inf.* לָנוּד [Jb 2:11](https://ref.ly/Job2:11) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* נָד [Gn 4:12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14); —**1\. a.** *move to and fro, wander* aimlessly, as fugitive, נָע וָנָד [Gn 4:12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14) (J), [Je 4:1](https://ref.ly/Jer4:1) (Gie, as apod., so RV; but Ew Hi Gf Che Ke, as prot., *if thou wanderest not* \[morally], and swearest, etc.,—Ew Hi reading with LXX שׁקוציך מִפִּיְ וּמִפָּנַי לא תנוד; Co del. v1; v2); *take flight* (perh. metaplast. form of נדד q. v.), נֻסוּ נֻּדוּ מְאֹד [Je 49:30](https://ref.ly/Jer49:30) (on dagh. v. Ges§ 20 g), of harvest Is 17:1l i.e. be destroyed, in fig. (v. supr.); [Je 50:3](https://ref.ly/Jer50:3) (‖הלך), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Jer50:8) (sq. מִתּוֺךְ; ‖יצא). **b.** *flutter*, of bird [Pr 26:2](https://ref.ly/Prov26:2) (‖עוּף); fig. of soul [Psalm 11:1](https://ref.ly/Ps11:1). **c.** *waver, wave, shake*, of reed [1 K 14:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:15) (sim. of smitten Isr.). **2\.** *shew grief* (i. e. by shaking or nodding head), usu. sq. ל Pers.: **a.** *lament* for the dead (Josiah) [Je 22:10](https://ref.ly/Jer22:10) (‖בכה), for Jerus. [Je 15:5](https://ref.ly/Jer15:5) (‖חמל), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Jer16:5) (‖ספד), [Is 51:19](https://ref.ly/Isa51:19); for Nineveh [Na 3:7](https://ref.ly/Nah3:7); Moab Je 48:l7\. **b.** *condole, shew sympathy* with [Jb 2:11](https://ref.ly/Job2:11), [42:11](https://ref.ly/Job42:11); abs. [Psalm 69:21](https://ref.ly/Ps69:21). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יָנִיד [Je 18:16](https://ref.ly/Jer18:16); 3 fs. sf. תְּנִדֵנִי [Psalm 36:12](https://ref.ly/Ps36:12); *Imv.* ms. sf. הֲנִידֵָמוֺ [Psalm 59:12](https://ref.ly/Ps59:12) (so rd. for MT הורידמו SyrVer LagProph. Chald, xlviii. Dr:[2 S 15:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:20) cf. Checrit. n. Hup\-Now Bae Du); *Inf.* לְהָנִיד [2 K 21:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:8); —**1\.** *cause to wander* aimlessly, as fugitives, c. acc. Pers. [Psalm 36:12](https://ref.ly/Ps36:12), [59:12](https://ref.ly/Ps59:12) (v. supr.), sq. רֶגֶל [2 K 21:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:8). **2\.** *make a wagging, wag* with the head, בְּראֹשׁ [Je 18:16](https://ref.ly/Jer18:16). **Hoph.** *Pt.* כְּקוֺץ מֻנַָד [2 S 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:6) and the worthless, *like briers made to flutter* (tossed away) are they (text dub.; Klo Bu HPS rd. מדבר). **Hithpoïlel** *Pf.* 3 fs. וְהִתְנוֺדֲדָה consec. [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20); *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּתְנוֺדָד [Je 48:27](https://ref.ly/Jer48:27); *Pt.* מִתְנוֺדֵד [Je 31:18](https://ref.ly/Jer31:18); —**1\.** *move oneself to and fro, sway, totter*, [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20) (of earth). **2\.** *shake oneself*, in excitement [Je 48:27](https://ref.ly/Jer48:27) (perh. of scorn \= *wag* the head, as Hiph. [Je 18:16](https://ref.ly/Jer18:16) cf. Gf RV). **3\.** *bemoan oneself* (cf. **Qal 2**), [Je 31:18](https://ref.ly/Jer31:18) (the lament follows in orat. recta).

> \[Str5112] † I. \[נוֺד, נֹד] **n.**\[**m.**] wandering of aimless fugitive;—only sf. נֹדִי [Psalm 56:9](https://ref.ly/Ps56:9) *my wandering* (word\-play with נאֹדְֶָ vb).

> \[Str5113] † II. נוֺד appar. **n. pr. terr.** ´ארץ־נ [Gn 4:16](https://ref.ly/Gen4:16) of region into which Cain wandered (cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12); v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14)); LXX (strangely) Ναιδ; Vulgate appellat., *profugus* in terra, cf. Symm Theod, v. NeMarg. 9\.

> \[Str5205] † נִיד **n. m.**:(v. infr.) quivering motion of lips;—only cstr. שְפָתַי ´נ [Jb 16:5](https://ref.ly/Job16:5) *the motion of my lips* (in expressing sympathy).—MT makes subj. of יחשך, but rd. prob. אחשך, v. חשך.

> \[Str5206] נִידָה v. נִדָּה sub נדד

> \[Str4493] † \[מָנוֺד] **n.**\[**m.**] a shaking, wagging;—only cstr. מְנוֺד ראֹשׁ [Psalm 44:15](https://ref.ly/Ps44:15) *object of head\-shaking*, in derision (of Isr.; ‖מָשָׁל; v:[14](https://ref.ly/Ps44:14) חֶרְפָּה, לַעַג, קֶלֶס).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 44:15; Jeremiah 15:5; Isaiah 17:11; Jeremiah 4:1; 2 Samuel 23:6; Genesis 4:16; Psalms 44:14; 2 Kings 21:8; Psalms 59:12; Job 16:5; Jeremiah 49:30; Proverbs 26:2; Psalms 69:21; Jeremiah 50:8; 1 Kings 14:15; Jeremiah 18:16; Jeremiah 48:27; Genesis 4:14; Psalms 56:9; Isaiah 24:20; Genesis 4:12; Jeremiah 16:5; Isaiah 51:19; 2 Samuel 15:20; Job 2:11; Jeremiah 22:10; Jeremiah 50:3; Psalms 36:12; Jeremiah 31:18; Psalms 11:1; Nahum 3:7; Job 42:11

## נוהּ (id: BDB05954)

נוהּ (prob. √ of foll., si vera 1\., cf. Ar. نَاهَ (نوه) *be high, eminent*).

## I נָוָה (id: BDB05956)

\[Str5115] † I. \[נָוָה] **vb.** only **Hiph.** (si vera l.) lit. beautify (then connected with נאה *be comely*, so Buhl SS; cf. NH נוה Niph. *shew oneself beautiful*, Nithp. *adorn oneself*, נָוֶה adj. *beautiful*, LevyNHWB);—*Impf.* 1 S. sf. אַנְוֵהוּ [Ex 15:2](https://ref.ly/Exod15:2) *I will beautify, adorn him* (with praises; obj. ´י; ‖רומם; LXX δυξάσω, Targum *glorificabo*).—Adj. נָוָה [Je 6:2](https://ref.ly/Jer6:2), v. נָאוֶה sub \[נָאָה].

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 6:2; Exodus 15:2


## II נוה (id: BDB05957)

II. נוה (√ of foll., mng. dub.; Ar. نَوَى is *aim at, propose to oneself as aim* (e.g. of journey), VIII. *betake oneself to a place of alighting*, or *abode;* نَوَني, نِيَّةٌ *place to which one purposes journeying* Lane3040; hence poss. I. נָוֶה as goal of shepherd; cf. Sab. נוי *meadow, pasture*, DHMSüdar. Alt. 32; see however Dr:[1 S 19:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:18) HPSib.; NH נָוָה *dwelling, habitation*).

> \[Str5116] † I. נָוֶה **n. m.**:[Is 27:10](https://ref.ly/Isa27:10) abode of shepherd, or flocks, poet. habitation;—abs. [Is 27:10](https://ref.ly/Isa27:10) \+; cstr. נְוֵה [Ex 15:13](https://ref.ly/Exod15:13) \+; נְוַת [Jb 8:6](https://ref.ly/Job8:6) (as if fr. נָוָה; or rd. נְוֵה ?); sf. נָֽוְ [Jb 5:24](https://ref.ly/Job5:24), נָוֵ֫הוּ [2 S 15:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:25) \+, נְוֵהֶם [Je 49:20](https://ref.ly/Jer49:20), Ez [34:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:14) (cf. Sta§ 351 c.), נְוֵהֶן [Je 23:3](https://ref.ly/Jer23:3) (so Baer Ginsb; נְוֵיהֶן van d. H., pl. acc. to Köii, 1, p. 77\);—**1\. a.** *adode*, of sheep [2 S 7:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:8) (‖מֵאַחַר הַצּאֹן) \= [1 Ch 17:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:1); [Is 65:10](https://ref.ly/Isa65:10) (‖רָבַץ); of people under fig. of sheep [Je 23:3](https://ref.ly/Jer23:3), [49:20](https://ref.ly/Jer49:20) \= [50:45](https://ref.ly/Jer50:45), [50:19](https://ref.ly/Jer50:19) Ez [34:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:14), [34:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:14) (‖רָבַץ, מִרְעֶה); of camels Ez [25:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:5) (‖מִרְבַּץ צאֹן). **b.** *adode of shepherds* [Je 33:12](https://ref.ly/Jer33:12) (\+ מַרְבִּיצִים צאֹן). **c.** \= *meadow*, in fig. of Ephr. planted (שָׁתוּל) as tree [Ho 9:13](https://ref.ly/Hos9:13). **2\.** *habitation*, usu. of country, or of domains in the country (chiefly poet.), [Jb 5:3](https://ref.ly/Job5:3), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Job8:6) (נְוַת צִדְקְֶָ, v. supr.), [Pr 21:20](https://ref.ly/Prov21:20); ‖אֹהֶל [Jb 5:24](https://ref.ly/Job5:24), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Job18:15); ‖בַּיִת [Pr 3:33](https://ref.ly/Prov3:33); ‖רֵבֶץ [24:15](https://ref.ly/Prov24:15); of ´י in Canaan [2 S 15:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:25); of Can. as place of ´י's sanctuary, קָדְשְֶָׁ ´נ [Ex 15:13](https://ref.ly/Exod15:13); fig. of ´י himself, צֶדֶק ´נ [Je 50:7](https://ref.ly/Jer50:7); habit. of nation [Je 10:25](https://ref.ly/Jer10:25), [25:30](https://ref.ly/Jer25:30), [Is 32:18](https://ref.ly/Isa32:18) (שָׁלוֺם ´נ), [Psalm 79:7](https://ref.ly/Ps79:7); \= city [Is 27:10](https://ref.ly/Isa27:10), esp. Jerus. [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20) (שַׁאֲנָן ´נ, ‖אֹהֶל); אֵיתָן ´נ [Je 49:19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19) (fig. of Edom) \= [50:44](https://ref.ly/Jer50:44) (of Bab.); of land of Isr., צֶדֶק ´נ [Je 31:23](https://ref.ly/Jer31:23) (‖הַר הַקֹּדֶשׁ). Twice of habitation of jackals [Is 34:13](https://ref.ly/Isa34:13), [35:7](https://ref.ly/Isa35:7) (both ‖חָצִיר).

> \[Str5115] † II. \[נָוָה] **vb. denom.** dwell, abide (si vera l.);—only **Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. לאֹ יִנְוֶה Hb [2:5](https://ref.ly/Hab2:5) *he shall not adide*, so Ke; Hi *rest* (cf. Da); We prop. יִרְוֶה *be satiated*, cf. SyrVer; Krochm יָנוּחַ.

[19:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:18)); LXX Ἀυαθ (ν dropped out after ἐν), so LXXL A Ναυιωθ.

## נוּחַ (id: BDB05963)

\[Str5117,5118,3240] † נוּחַ **vb.** rest (NH *id.;* Aram. נוּחַ, ܢܳܚ; Ph. נחת n. *rest;* poss. also vb. ינח (Iph. Pf.), cf. Levy cited CISi. 118 Lzb322; As. *nâhu, rest (inûh)*, and deriv.; Eth. ኖኅ፡ *be extended, long*, rarely *rest;* Ar. ناخ IV. is *make camel lie down* on his breast; مُنَاخٌ *resting\-place of camel*, cf. DoughtyArab. Des. i, 397, ii, 63, 486, 642\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. נָ֫חָה [Is 7:2](https://ref.ly/Isa7:2) \+ 2 t., consec. וְנָחָה [Is 11:2](https://ref.ly/Isa11:2); 1 S. ָ\+נחְתְּי [Jb 3:26](https://ref.ly/Job3:26); 3 pl. נָ֫חוּ [Est 9:22](https://ref.ly/Esth9:22), consec. וְנָחוּ [Is 7:19](https://ref.ly/Isa7:19); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָנוּחַ [Ex 23:12](https://ref.ly/Exod23:12) \+ 6 t., וַיָּ֫נַח [Ex 10:14](https://ref.ly/Exod10:14), [20:11](https://ref.ly/Exod20:11); 3 fs. תָּנוּחַ [Is 25:10](https://ref.ly/Isa25:10), [Pr 14:33](https://ref.ly/Prov14:33), וַתָּ֫נַח [Gn 8:4](https://ref.ly/Gen8:4), [Nu 11:26](https://ref.ly/Num11:26); 2 ms. תָּנוּחַ [Dn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Dan12:13) etc.; *Inf. abs.* נוֺחַ [Est 9:16](https://ref.ly/Esth9:16), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Esth9:17), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Esth9:18); *cstr.* לָנוּחַ [2 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:10); כְּנוֺחַ [Nu 11:25](https://ref.ly/Num11:25), [Jos 3:13](https://ref.ly/Josh3:13) [Ne 9:28](https://ref.ly/Neh9:28); sf. 3 ms. בְּנֻחֹה [Nu 10:36](https://ref.ly/Num10:36); —לְנוּחְֶָ [2 Ch 6:41](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:41) v. מְנוּחָה;—**1\.** *rest, settle down and remain*, sq. עַל; of birds [2 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:10); ark [Gn 8:4](https://ref.ly/Gen8:4) (P); נָ֫חָה אֲרָם עַל־אֶפְרַיִם [Is 7:2](https://ref.ly/Isa7:2) *Aram hath settled down upon Ephraim;* of spirit of ´י [Nu 11:25](https://ref.ly/Num11:25), [11:26](https://ref.ly/Num11:26) (E), [Is 11:2](https://ref.ly/Isa11:2); spirit of Elijah [2 K 2:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:15); sceptre of wicked [Psalm 125:3](https://ref.ly/Ps125:3) (in fig.); sq. בְּ loc.: of insects [Ex 10:14](https://ref.ly/Exod10:14) (J), [Is 7:19](https://ref.ly/Isa7:19) (fig. of invaders); of soles of feet *resting* in water [Jos 3:13](https://ref.ly/Josh3:13) (JE); hand of ´י [Is 25:10](https://ref.ly/Isa25:10); wisdom [Pr 14:33](https://ref.ly/Prov14:33); anger [Ec 7:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:9); בִּקְהַל רְפָאִים יָנוּחַ [Pr 21:16](https://ref.ly/Prov21:16) *in the assembly of Shades shall he settle down;* abs., \= *stop*, of ark at stages of journey [Nu 10:36](https://ref.ly/Num10:36) (JE); *cease* speaking [1 S 25:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:9). **2\.** *repose, be quiet, have rest:* after labour [Ex 20:11](https://ref.ly/Exod20:11) (E; of God), [23:12](https://ref.ly/Exod23:12) (E; of cattle), [Dt 5:14](https://ref.ly/Deut5:14) (of slave); *have rest from* (מִן) enemies [Est 9:16](https://ref.ly/Esth9:16) (inf. abs. נוֺחַ, \+ עָמוֺד, הָרוֺג), v:[22](https://ref.ly/Esth9:22), cf. (abs.) v:[17](https://ref.ly/Esth9:17), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Esth9:18) (in both \+ עָשה), [Ne 9:28](https://ref.ly/Neh9:28); *be at rest* (from trouble), abs., [Jb 3:26](https://ref.ly/Job3:26) (\+ שָׁלַוְתִּי, שָׁקַטְתִּי); of the earth [Is 14:7](https://ref.ly/Isa14:7); in couch of the grave [57:2](https://ref.ly/Isa57:2); in Shₑ’ôl [Jb 3:17](https://ref.ly/Job3:17), so prob. [Dn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Dan12:13); also לאֹ תָּנוּחַ לָח [Is 23:12](https://ref.ly/Isa23:12) \= *thou shalt not be at rest*, cf. impers. יָנוּחַ לִי [Jb 3:13](https://ref.ly/Job3:13) \= *I should be at rest* (in Shₑ’ôl).—אָנוּחַ לְיוֺם צָרָה Hb [3:16](https://ref.ly/Hab3:16) is dub.: *I wait quietly for the day of distress* De Ke Hi\-St SS, so Now, doubtfully; We conj. אֶנָּחֵם as [Is 1:24](https://ref.ly/Isa1:24) *I will appease* me, GASm leaves untransl. **Hiph. A.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הֵנִיחַ [Jos 22:4](https://ref.ly/Josh22:4) \+; 1 S. וַהֲנִיחֹ֫תִי [2 S 7:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:11) \+; הֵנִ֫יחוּ [Zc 6:8](https://ref.ly/Zech6:8); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָנִיחַ [Ex 17:11](https://ref.ly/Exod17:11) \+; וַיָּ֫נַח [Jos 21:42](https://ref.ly/Josh21:42) \+; sf. וַיְנִיחֵנִי Ez [37:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:1), [40:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:2); 3 fs. sf. תְּנִיחֶנּוּ [Is 63:14](https://ref.ly/Isa63:14) (but v. infr.), etc.; *Imv.* mpl. הָנִ֫יחוּ [Is 28:12](https://ref.ly/Isa28:12); *Inf. cstr.* הָנִיּחַ [Is 14:3](https://ref.ly/Isa14:3) \+, sf. הֲנִיחִי Ez [24:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:13); *Pt.* מֵנִיחַ [Jos 1:13](https://ref.ly/Josh1:13); —**1\.** *cause to rest \= give rest* to: **a.** sq. acc. יָדוֺ [Ex 17:11](https://ref.ly/Exod17:11) (E), רוּחִי [Zc 6:8](https://ref.ly/Zech6:8); so appar. sq. חֲמָתִי \+ ב Pers., *quiet* (i.e. sate) *my fury by* (wreak it upon) Ez [5:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:13) (del. Co), [16:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:42), [24:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:13), also [21:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:22) (Co adds ב Pers.). **b.** usu. sq. ל Pers.: (1\) *give rest to* i.e. bring to resting\-place [Ex 33:14](https://ref.ly/Exod33:14) (J), [Dt 3:20](https://ref.ly/Deut3:20), [Jos 1:13](https://ref.ly/Josh1:13), [1:15](https://ref.ly/Josh1:15), [22:4](https://ref.ly/Josh22:4) (all D), [1 Ch 23:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:25); so, c. sf., [Is 63:14](https://ref.ly/Isa63:14) (si vera l.; Vrss Lo Ew Brd CheHpt rd. תַּנְחֶנּוּ, *leadeth him*, √ נחה); (2\) of freedom from enemies, [Is 28:12](https://ref.ly/Isa28:12), [2 Ch 14:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:5); \+ מִכָּל־איבים \[מִסָּבִיב] [Dt 12:10](https://ref.ly/Deut12:10), [25:19](https://ref.ly/Deut25:19), [Jos 23:1](https://ref.ly/Josh23:1) (D), [2 S 7:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:1), [7:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:11), [1 Ch 22:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:9); so \+ מִסָּבִיב alone [1 K 5:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:18), [Jos 21:42](https://ref.ly/Josh21:42) (P), [1 Ch 22:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:18), [2 Ch 14:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:6), [15:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:15), [20:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:30), so also prob. [32:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:22) (for MT וַיְנַהֲלֵם מִסָּבִיב); (3\) of freedom suffering, \+ ´מֵעָצְבְּ וגו [Is 14:3](https://ref.ly/Isa14:3). **c.** *make quiet* in mind, *set at rest* וִינִיחְֶָ [Pr 29:17](https://ref.ly/Prov29:17) discipline thy son, *that he may give thee quiet* (of mind; ‖יִתֵּן מַעֲדַנִּים לְנַפְשְֶָׁ). **2\.** *cause to rest* (i.e. *light*) upon, c. acc. *staff* \+ עַל Pers. [Is 30:32](https://ref.ly/Isa30:32); c. acc. *blessing* \+ אֶ־ל Ez [44:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:30); *cause to alight, set down*, c. sf. Pers. \+ בְּתוֺח loc. Ez [37:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:1); \+ אֶ־ל [40:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:2); —For this mng. v. esp. **B.** infr. **B.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הִנִּיחַ Ju [3:1](https://ref.ly/Judg3:1) \+, הִנִּחַ [1 K 8:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:9), sf. וְהִנִּיחוֺ [Dt 26:4](https://ref.ly/Deut26:4), וְהִנִּיחָם [Lv 16:23](https://ref.ly/Lev16:23) both consec.; 2 ms. וְהִנַּחְתָּ֫ [Dt 14:28](https://ref.ly/Deut14:28), sf. וְהִנַּחְתּוֺ [26:10](https://ref.ly/Deut26:10) consec., etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יַנִּיחַ [Lv 7:15](https://ref.ly/Lev7:15), וַיַּנַּח Ju [2:23](https://ref.ly/Judg2:23) \+, sf. וַיַּנִּיחֵהוּ [Ex 16:34](https://ref.ly/Exod16:34); 3 fs. וַתַּנַּח [Gn 39:16](https://ref.ly/Gen39:16); 2 ms. juss. אַל־תַּנַּח [Ec 7:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:18) \+ 2 t., etc.; *Imv.* ms. הַנַּח [Ho 4:7](https://ref.ly/Hos4:7) \+ 2 t., הַנִּ֫יחָה [Ex 32:10](https://ref.ly/Exod32:10), Ju [16:26](https://ref.ly/Judg16:26), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* sf. לְהַנִּיחוֺ [Nu 32:15](https://ref.ly/Num32:15), לְהַנִּיחָם [Est 3:8](https://ref.ly/Esth3:8); *Pt.* מַנִּיחַ [Ec 5:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:11); —**1\.** *lay* or *set down, deposit, let lie*, c. acc. rei, usu. \+ word of place: stones at ford of Jordan [Jos 4:3](https://ref.ly/Josh4:3), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Josh4:8) (JE), ark [1 S 6:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:18), garments [Gn 39:16](https://ref.ly/Gen39:16) (J), [Lv 16:23](https://ref.ly/Lev16:23) (P), Ez [42:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:14), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:19); cf. [Ex 16:23](https://ref.ly/Exod16:23), [16:24](https://ref.ly/Exod16:24), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Exod16:33), [16:34](https://ref.ly/Exod16:34), [Nu 17:19](https://ref.ly/Num17:4), [17:22](https://ref.ly/Num17:7) (all P), [Dt 26:4](https://ref.ly/Deut26:4), [26:10](https://ref.ly/Deut26:10), [1 K 8:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:9), [13:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:31), Ez [40:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:42), [42:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:13); so, acc. om., Ju [6:18](https://ref.ly/Judg6:18); [6:20](https://ref.ly/Judg6:20) [Dt 14:28](https://ref.ly/Deut14:28), [Nu 19:9](https://ref.ly/Num19:9) (P), [1 S 10:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:25); *place, put*, sq. acc. Pers. \+ local modif., [Gn 2:15](https://ref.ly/Gen2:15) (J), [19:16](https://ref.ly/Gen19:16) (J), [Jos 6:23](https://ref.ly/Josh6:23) (JE), [Lv 24:12](https://ref.ly/Lev24:12), [Nu 15:34](https://ref.ly/Num15:34) (both P), [Is 14:1](https://ref.ly/Isa14:1), [46:7](https://ref.ly/Isa46:7), Ez [37:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:14); prob. also [Zc 5:11](https://ref.ly/Zech5:11) (rd. וְהִנִּיחֻהָ, v. Ges§ 72 ee); rd. וַיַּנִּיחֵם also [2 K 18:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:11) (LXX ἔθετο; for MT וַיַּנְחֵם; cf. וַיּשֶׁב אוֺתָם ‖ [2 K 17:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:6)); *place* corpse on (אֶ־ל) ass [1 K 13:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:29), in grave v:[30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:30); horsemen and chariots in cities [2 Ch 1:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:14), [9:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:25), so rd. also ‖ [1 K 10:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:26) (LXX ἔθετο; for MT וַיַּנְחֵם); tables in temple [2 Ch 4:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:8); idols in shrines [2 K 17:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:29) (acc. om.). Here belongs perh. also וְגַם מִזֶּה אַל־תַּנַּח אֶת־יָדְֶָ [Ec 7:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:18) *and also from this do not lest thy hand lie* (idle), i.e. engage in it, cf. [11:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:6); *lay down forcibly, thrust down* הִנִּיחַ לָאָרֶץ בְּיָדָ [Is 28:2](https://ref.ly/Isa28:2), cf. [Am 5:7](https://ref.ly/Amos5:7); perh. also Ez [22:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20) (abs.; but del. Co Berthol, after LXX). **2\.** *let remain, leave* (in present condition), obj. nations Ju [2:23](https://ref.ly/Judg2:23), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Judg3:1), [Je 27:11](https://ref.ly/Jer27:11), people in wilderness [Nu 32:15](https://ref.ly/Num32:15) (JE); וְהִנִּיחוּח עֵירֹם וְעֶרְיָה Ez [16:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:39); sq. acc. rei [Lv 7:15](https://ref.ly/Lev7:15), [1 K 7:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:47) (leave unweighed); *leave behind* sq. acc. Pers. [Gn 42:33](https://ref.ly/Gen42:33) (E), [2 S 16:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:21), [20:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:3), [1 K 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:3), [Je 43:6](https://ref.ly/Jer43:6); \+ אֶת־פְּנֵי [1 S 22:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:4) (rd. וַיַּנִּחֵם, for MT וַיַּנְחֵם, *and he left them with* SyrVer Targum Vulgate We Dr Klo Bu Löhr HPS; *leave* name, for a curse [Is 65:15](https://ref.ly/Isa65:15); *leave* or *bequeath* to (ל), c. acc. rei, [Psalm 17:14](https://ref.ly/Ps17:14), [Ec 2:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:18). **3\.** *leave* \= depart from, מְקוֺמְ אַל־תַּנַּח [Ec 10:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:4) *do not leave thy place*. **4\.** *abandon*, sq. acc. Pers. [Je 14:9](https://ref.ly/Jer14:9); בַּל־תַּנִּיחֵנִי לְעשְׁקַָי [Psalm 119:121](https://ref.ly/Ps119:121) *abandon me not to my oppressors*. **5\.** *let alone* (refrain from interfering with), sq. ל Pers. [Ex 32:10](https://ref.ly/Exod32:10) (JE; obj. ´י), [Ho 4:17](https://ref.ly/Hos4:17), [2 S 16:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:11), [2 K 23:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:18); sq. acc. Pers. [Est 3:6](https://ref.ly/Esth3:6); \= *avoid* יַנִּיחַ חֲטָאִים גְּדוֺלִים [Ec 10:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:4) (but rd. perh. יָנִיחַ, *causeth to rest* \= *allayeth* cf. Wild). **6\.** *permit*, c. acc. Pers. Ju [16:26](https://ref.ly/Judg16:26); \+ inf. לאֹ הִנִּיחַ אָדָם לְעָשְׁקָם [Psalm 105:14](https://ref.ly/Ps105:14) \= 1 Ch 16:2l, but here הִנִּיחַ לְאִישׁ, cf. מַנִּיחַ לוֺ לִישׁוֺן [Ec 5:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:11). **Hoph. A.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הוּנַּח־לָנוּ [La 5:5](https://ref.ly/Lam5:5) i.e. *no rest is granted to us;* for 3 fs. וְהֻנִ֫יחָה [Zc 5:11](https://ref.ly/Zech5:11) \[so Baer Ginsb; van d. H. ´והֻנּ] rd. prob. וְהִנִּיחֻהָ v. supr. **Hiph. B 1\. B.** *Pt.* מֻנָּ֔ח as subst. \= *space left, open space* Ez [41:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:9), [41:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:11), [41:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:11).

> \[Str5119] † נוֺחָה **n. pr. m.** 4th son of Benjamin acc. to [1 Ch 8:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:2). LXX Ιωα, A Νωα, LXXL Νουαα.

> \[Str5183,5183–84] † I. נַ֫חַת **n.**\[**f.**] (appar. **m.** [Jb 36:16](https://ref.ly/Job36:16), but v. infr.) quietness, rest (under this √ Thes O1:§ 164 c Sta§ 201 d Köii. 172 f.; \> sub נחת DlProl. 118 ff.);—´נ [Is 30:15](https://ref.ly/Isa30:15) \+ 2 t., ָ\+נחַת [Jb 17:16](https://ref.ly/Job17:16) \+ 2 t.; cstr. נַ֫חַת [Jb 36:16](https://ref.ly/Job36:16); —**1\.** *quietness, quiet attitude* [Is 30:15](https://ref.ly/Isa30:15) (‖הַשְׁקֵט); נִשְׁמָעִים ´דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים בְּנ [Ec 9:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:17); opp. עָמָל וּרְעוּת רוּחַ [4:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:6); opp. רָגַז, שָחַק [Pr 29:9](https://ref.ly/Prov29:9); of comfort of one's table, i.e. its satisfying plenty, שֻׁלְחָֽנְ מָלֵא דָשֶׁן ´וְנ [Jb 36:16](https://ref.ly/Job36:16) *the comfort of thy table, which* (i.e. the table) *was full of fat* (Di, cf. Bu; \> *that which is set on thy table shall be*, etc. De al., making ´נ subj. of מָלֵא, and so masc.). **2\.** *rest* of death [Jb 17:16](https://ref.ly/Job17:16); of a stillborn child [Ec 6:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:5).—II, III. נַחַת v. נחת.

## נוּעַ (id: BDB05992)

\[Str5128] † נוּעַ **vb.** quiver, wave, waver, tremble, totter (NH Pilp. נִעְנַע *shake*, Aram. נוּעַ *waver, stagger* (rare); Ar. نوع, نَاعَ *bend* (of boughs), v. commotus fuit (of *id.*), so Kam Frey; also *change*, نَوْعٌ *kind, species, variety;* Di cp. Eth. ነዐወ፡ *hunt*);—**Qal** *Pf.* נָ֫עוּ [Jb 28:4](https://ref.ly/Job28:4) \+; וְנָע֫וּ [Am 4:8](https://ref.ly/Amos4:8) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* וַיָּ֫נַע [Is 7:2](https://ref.ly/Isa7:2); 3 fs. תָּנוּעַ [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20); אֲנוּעְֲ [2 S 15:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:20) Kt (but rd Qr Hiph q. v.); יָנ֫וּעוּ [Psalm 109:10](https://ref.ly/Ps109:10); יְנוּעָוּן [Psalm 59:16](https://ref.ly/Ps59:16) Kt (so rd.; not Qr Hiph.), etc.; *Inf. abs.* [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20), [Psalm 109:10](https://ref.ly/Ps109:10);  *cstr.* נוּעַ Ju [9:9](https://ref.ly/Judg9:9) \+3 t.; נוֺעַ [Is 7:2](https://ref.ly/Isa7:2); *Pt.* נָע [Gn 4:12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14); נָעִים [Pr 22:19](https://ref.ly/Prov22:19); נָעוֺת [1 S 1:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:13):—**1\.** *wave*, of trees, sq. עַל Ju [9:9](https://ref.ly/Judg9:9), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Judg9:11), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Judg9:13) sq. מִפְּנֵי of cause [Is 7:2](https://ref.ly/Isa7:2); *quiver, vibrate*, of lips [1 S 1:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:13); *swing* (to and fro) of miners [Jb 28:4](https://ref.ly/Job28:4) (‖דלל); *stagger* like drunkard, of mariners in storm [Psalm 107:27](https://ref.ly/Ps107:27) (‖חגג), cf. [Is 29:9](https://ref.ly/Isa29:9) (‖שׁכר); hence *be unstable*, fig. of ways of harlot [Pr 5:6](https://ref.ly/Prov5:6); *tremble*, of doorposts, [Is 6:4](https://ref.ly/Isa6:4) (sq. מִן caus.), idols [19:1](https://ref.ly/Isa19:1) (sq. מִפְּנֵי), earth [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20) (‖התנודד), people [Ex 20:18](https://ref.ly/Exod20:18) (E); fig. of heart [Is 7:2](https://ref.ly/Isa7:2). **2\.** *totter, go tottering* (faint and uncertain) [La 4:14](https://ref.ly/Lam4:14) v:[15](https://ref.ly/Lam4:15) (‖נוּץ), [Am 8:12](https://ref.ly/Amos8:12) (‖שׁוטט); as beggars [Psalm 109:10](https://ref.ly/Ps109:10) (‖שִׁאֵל), cf. [59:16](https://ref.ly/Ps59:16); [Am 4:8](https://ref.ly/Amos4:8) sq. אֶ־ל; *pt. vagabond* [Gn 4:12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14) (both ‖נָד); fig. \= *err, sin*, [Je 14:10](https://ref.ly/Jer14:10). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִנּוֺעַ [Am 9:9](https://ref.ly/Amos9:9) *be tossed about* (of corn) in a sieve; יִנּוֺעוּ *id.*, of bulwarks as fig\-trees [Na 3:12](https://ref.ly/Nah3:12) (‖נפל). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. הֵנִ֫יעָה [2 K 19:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:21) \= [Is 37:22](https://ref.ly/Isa37:22); וַהֲנִֽעוֺתִ֫י [Am 9:9](https://ref.ly/Amos9:9); *Impf.* יָנִיעַ [Zp 2:15](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:15); juss. יָ֫נַע [2 K 23:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:18); אֲנִיעְֲ [2 S 15:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:20) Qr (cf. **Qal**); יְנִיעוּן [Psalm 109:25](https://ref.ly/Ps109:25) \+ [59:16](https://ref.ly/Ps59:16) Qr (rd. Kt **Qal** q. v.), etc.; *Imv.* sf. הֲנִיעֵ֫מוֺ [Psalm 59:12](https://ref.ly/Ps59:12); —**1\.** *toss about* [Am 9:9](https://ref.ly/Amos9:9) subj. ´י, obj. Isr. sq. בַּגּוֺיִם (cf. **Niph.**). **2\.** *shake, cause to totter:* \= *set me tottering* [Dn 10:10](https://ref.ly/Dan10:10) *on my knees and hands;* esp. *shake* or *wag* the head, in mockery [2 K 19:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:21) \= [Is 37:22](https://ref.ly/Isa37:22) (‖בוז, לעג) cf. [Psalm 22:8](https://ref.ly/Ps22:8), [109:25](https://ref.ly/Ps109:25); sq. עַ־ל [Jb 16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4) (´בְּמוֺ ר \[Ges§ 119 q]), also [La 2:15](https://ref.ly/Lam2:15) (‖שׁרק), [Zp 2:15](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:15) יָדוֺ (‖ *id.*), \[v. also [Ecclus 12:18](https://ref.ly/Sir12:18), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Sir13:7) (´בְּר)]. **3\.** (*shake*), *disturb*, bones of dead [2 K 23:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:18). **4\.** *cause to wander* [Nu 32:13](https://ref.ly/Num32:13) (J); [2 S 15:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:20) (sq. ללכת); [Psalm 59:12](https://ref.ly/Ps59:12) (‖הוריד, for which LagProph. Chald. xlviii. prop. הניד).

> \[Str5270] † נֹעָה **n. pr. f.** a daughter of Zelophahad (of Manasseh), [Nu 26:33](https://ref.ly/Num26:33), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Num27:1), [36:11](https://ref.ly/Num36:11), [Jos 17:3](https://ref.ly/Josh17:3), LXX Νουα.

> \[Str4517] † \[מְנַעֲנֵעַ] **n.\[m.]** appar. a kind of rattle, only וּבִמְנַֽעַנְעִים [2 S 6:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:5), in list of musical instruments; LXX κύμβαλα, but this usu. (12 t.) \= מְצִלְתַּיִם; Vulgate *sisitra*; the sistrum (Gr. σεῖστρον, fr. σείω) was much used in Egypt; it was a small metal frame with loose metal bars carrying loose rings, borne and *swung* or *shaken* in the hand, v. WilkinsonAnc. Kgyptians (1878\), i. 497 ff. NowArch. l. 273 BenzArch. 278 WePsalm Psalm Eng. Trans. p. 233\.

## I נוּף (id: BDB05997)

\[Str5130] I. \[נוּף] **vb.** move to and fro, wave, besprinkle (NH Hiph., Pilp. *wave, blow, fan*, cf. נָפָה  *sift*, נָפָה n. *sieve* (as BH); Targum Aph. as BH; Syr. ܢܳܦ *bend, wave, shake;* Di cp. Eth. quadril. ነፈየ፡ነፍነፈ፡ *distil, drop like dew;* *sift*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 S. ´נַ֫פְתִּי מִשְׁכָּבִי מֹר וגו [Pr 7:17](https://ref.ly/Prov7:17) *I have besprinkled my couch with myrrh*, etc. **Poïle1** *Impf.* 3 ms. ´יְנֹפֵף יָדוֺ הַר וגו [Is 10:32](https://ref.ly/Isa10:32) *he brandisheth his hand toward the mt.* (on acc. הַר cf. Ges :§ 118 f. DaSynt. § 69, R 2\). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הֵנִיף [Jos 8:31](https://ref.ly/Josh8:31) \+, 2 ms. הֵנַ֫פְתָּ [Ex 20:25](https://ref.ly/Exod20:25) (Ges§ 72 k), וְהֵנַפְתָּ֫ [Ex 29:24](https://ref.ly/Exod29:24) \+ 3 t.; 1 S. הֲנִיפֹ֫תִי [Jb 31:21](https://ref.ly/Job31:21); *Impf.* וַיָּ֫נֶף [Lv 8:27](https://ref.ly/Lev8:27), [Nu 8:21](https://ref.ly/Num8:21), sf. וַיְנִיפֵ֫הוּ [Lv 8:29](https://ref.ly/Lev8:29); 2 ms. תָּנִיף [Dt 23:26](https://ref.ly/Deut23:26)\+ 2 t., etc.; *Imv.* mpl. הָנִ֫יפוּ [Is 13:2](https://ref.ly/Isa13:2); *Inf. cstr.* הָנִיף [Is 10:15](https://ref.ly/Isa10:15) \+ 2 t.; לַהֲנָפָה [Is 30:28](https://ref.ly/Isa30:28) (Ges§ 72 z BaNB 90, v. also הֲנָחָה), etc.; *Pt.* מֵנִיף [Is 19:16](https://ref.ly/Isa19:16) [Zc 2:13](https://ref.ly/Zech2:13), etc.;—*swing, wield, wave:*—**1\.** *wield*, move tool to and fro in using it, c. עַ־ל of material: כִּי חֵרְבְּ הֵנַפְתָּ עָלֶיהָ [Ex 20:25](https://ref.ly/Exod20:25) (E), i.e. over stone, so [Dt 27:5](https://ref.ly/Deut27:5), [Jos 8:31](https://ref.ly/Josh8:31) (D), and [Dt 23:26](https://ref.ly/Deut23:26) a sickle *thou shalt not wield over* the standing grain of thy neighbour; abs. מְנִיפוֺ [Is 10:15](https://ref.ly/Isa10:15) against *him that wieldeth it*(i.e. a saw), and כְּהָנִיף שֵׁבֶט וְאֶת־מְרִימוֺ v:[15](https://ref.ly/Isa10:15) (rd. אֶ־ת) *like a rod's wielding him that lifteth it*.

 2\.*Shake* or *wave* the hand; **a.** *wave* hand אֶל־הַמָּקוֺם [2 K 5:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:11), in healing ceremony (i.e. prob. toward sanctuary KmpKau; \> *toward the spot* \[where leprosy appears], so most). **b.** c. עַ־ל, *shake* or *brandish against,* [Is 11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15), [19:16](https://ref.ly/Isa19:16), [Zc 2:13](https://ref.ly/Zech2:13) (all of ´י) [Jb 31:21](https://ref.ly/Job31:21) \[cf. [Ecclus 12:18](https://ref.ly/Sir12:18) (without עַל) in mockery]. **c.** *wave* hand, as a signal, [Is 13:2](https://ref.ly/Isa13:2).**3\.** *swing* to and fro בְּנָפַת,*in a sieve,* [Is 30:28](https://ref.ly/Isa30:28) (fig., of nations). **4\.** Oft. term. techn. in P (H), of rite in which orig.ly the priest lifted his share of offering and *waved* it, i.e. moved it toward altar and back, in token of its presentation to God and its return by him to priest: in H, ´וְהֵנִיף אֶת־הָעֹמֶר לִפְנֵי י [Lv 23:11a](https://ref.ly/Lev23:11) cf. v:[11b](https://ref.ly/Lev23:11); v:[12](https://ref.ly/Lev23:12), also v:[20](https://ref.ly/Lev23:20) (on text v. Di Dr\-WhHpt) ; in P, וְהֵנַפְתָּ֫ אֹתוֺ ´תְּנוּפָה לִפְנֵי י [Ex 29:26](https://ref.ly/Exod29:26), so [Lv 7:30](https://ref.ly/Lev7:30), [8:29](https://ref.ly/Lev8:29), [10:15](https://ref.ly/Lev10:15) (obj. om.) [Nu 6:20](https://ref.ly/Num6:20); thus also Levites are set apart for service of the priests [Nu 8:11](https://ref.ly/Num8:11) (Di del. v.), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Num8:13), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Num8:21), cf. vl4 (´לִפְנֵי י om.); but same phr, of entire lamb, with oil, [Lv 14:12](https://ref.ly/Lev14:12), [14:24](https://ref.ly/Lev14:24), and of offerings wh. were burnt, entirely [Ex 29:24](https://ref.ly/Exod29:24) (cf. v:[25](https://ref.ly/Exod29:25)), [Lv 8:27](https://ref.ly/Lev8:27) (cf. v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev8:28)), or in part [Nu 5:25](https://ref.ly/Num5:25) (´הֵנִיף הַמִּנְחָה לִפְנֵי י),—in these the orig. signif. seems lost; so, clearly, of contributions for tabernacle, \= *offer* ´הֵנִיף תְּנוּפַת זָהָב לְי [Ex 35:22](https://ref.ly/Exod35:22).—Cf. תְּנוּפָה infr. **5\.** *Shed abroad*(si vera l.) גֶּשֶׁם נְדָבוֺת תָּנִיף [Psalm 68:10](https://ref.ly/Ps68:10) *bounteous rain thou didst shed abroad* \[cf. [Ecclus 43:17c](https://ref.ly/Sir43:17)], but vb. not wholly suitable; Lag Gr prop. תַּטִּיף, yet this also questionable. **Hoph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הוּנַף, *be waved,* [Ex 29:27](https://ref.ly/Exod29:27) (P), pass. of **Hiph. 4\.**

## I נָזָה (id: BDB06013)

\[Str5137] †I. \[נָזָה] **vb.** spurt, spatter; **Hiph.** sprinkle (NH **Hiph.** *sprinkle;* Aram. נְדָא, אַדֵּי [Lv 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lev4:6) and oft., for הִזָּה Syr. ܢܕܳܐ is *erupit, stillavit* [Is 63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3), also*prominutt*, etc. PS2291);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִזֶּה [Lv 6:20](https://ref.ly/Lev6:20), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Lev6:20), וְיֵז [Is 63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3) (but rd. וַיֵּז Che Di Du Ges§ 53, p. N., 107 bN. al.), וַיִּז [2 K 9:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:33); —*spurt, spatter*, alw. of blood:—וַיִּז מִדָּמָהּ אֶ־ל [2 K 9:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:33) *and some of her blood spurted against* the wall; so ויז נִצְחָם [Is 63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3) (c.—עַ־ל rei, in metaph.); יִזֶּה מִדָּמָה [Lv 6:20](https://ref.ly/Lev6:20), cf. v:[20](https://ref.ly/Lev6:20) (both c. עַ־ל rei). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְהִזָּת consec. [Lv 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lev4:6) \+ 12 t.; 2 ms. וְהִזֵּיתָ֫ [Ex 29:21](https://ref.ly/Exod29:21); *Impf.* יַזֶּה [Lv 16:14](https://ref.ly/Lev16:14) ([Is 52:15](https://ref.ly/Isa52:15) v. infr.); וַיַּז [Lv 8:11](https://ref.ly/Lev8:11), [8:30](https://ref.ly/Lev8:30); *Imv.* הַזֵּה [Nu 8:7](https://ref.ly/Num8:7); *Pt.* cstr. מַזֵּה [Nu 19:21](https://ref.ly/Num19:21); —*cause to spurt, sprinkle* upon, in ceremonials of P: c. acc. \+ עַ־ל Pers. [Nu 8:7](https://ref.ly/Num8:7) (water), \+ עַ־ל rei, לִפְנֵי [Lv 16:15](https://ref.ly/Lev16:15); obj. oft. מִן partit., or implied in context; sq. עַ־ל [Lv 5:9](https://ref.ly/Lev5:9), [14:7](https://ref.ly/Lev14:7), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Lev16:14), [16:19](https://ref.ly/Lev16:19) (all of blood); [8:11](https://ref.ly/Lev8:11) (oil); [Nu 19:18](https://ref.ly/Num19:18), [19:19](https://ref.ly/Num19:19) (water), [Ex 29:21](https://ref.ly/Exod29:21), [Lv 8:30](https://ref.ly/Lev8:30) (both blood and oil); sq. אֶ־ל [Lv 14:51](https://ref.ly/Lev14:51) (blood and water), אֶל־נֹכַח פְּנֵי [Nu 19:4](https://ref.ly/Num19:4) (blood); sq. לִפְנֵי [Lv 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lev4:6), [4:17](https://ref.ly/Lev4:17), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Lev16:14) (blood), [14:16](https://ref.ly/Lev14:16), [14:27](https://ref.ly/Lev14:27) (oil); once without prep. מַזֵּה [Nu 19:21](https://ref.ly/Num19:21) *the sprinkler of* the water.—[Is 52:15](https://ref.ly/Isa52:15) v. II. נזה.

CheHpt.

## נָזַל (id: BDB06017)

\[Str5140] †\[נָזַל] **vb.** flow, trickle, drop, distil (poet.) (Targum נְזַל of *flowing* water ; Syr. ܢܙܠ *descendit, defluet* PS2331; Ar. نَزَلَ *descend* (.milk into udder, but also in gen.));—**Qal** *Pf.* נָֽזְלוּ Ju [5:5](https://ref.ly/Judg5:5); *Impf.* יִזַּל [Nu 24:7](https://ref.ly/Num24:7) etc; *Pt.* נֹזְלִים[Ex 15:8](https://ref.ly/Exod15:8) \+, etc.;—**1\.** *flow* subj. water, [Nu 24:7](https://ref.ly/Num24:7) (JE); [Psalm 147:18](https://ref.ly/Ps147:18); cf. [Je 18:14](https://ref.ly/Jer18:14); subj. clouds, [Jb 36:28](https://ref.ly/Job36:28) sq. rain as acc. mat. Ges§ 117 z (‖רעף and, v:[27](https://ref.ly/Job36:27), זקק), cf. [Je 9:17](https://ref.ly/Jer9:17) (of eyelids); so .fig. [Is 45:8](https://ref.ly/Isa45:8) sq. צֶדֶק (‖רעף); of mts. (i.e. their torrents) Ju [5:6](https://ref.ly/Judg5:6) acc. to Vulgate Ew GFM al., but v. I. זלל esp. pt. as subst., \= *streams, floods,* [Ex 15:8](https://ref.ly/Exod15:8) (‖מים, תהום of Red Sea); [Psalm 78:44](https://ref.ly/Ps78:44) (‖יְאֹר); for drinking [Psalm 78:16](https://ref.ly/Ps78:16) (‖מים כַנהרות, תהום v:[15](https://ref.ly/Ps78:15)), [Pr 5:15](https://ref.ly/Prov5:15) out of well (‖מים); for irrigation [Is 44:3](https://ref.ly/Isa44:3) (‖מים); fig. of Shulamite מִן־לְבָנוֺן ´נ Ct [4:15](https://ref.ly/Song4:15) (‖מעין גנים, באר מים) **2\.** *distil*, of spices בְּשָמִים Ct [4:16](https://ref.ly/Song4:16); fig. of words, like dew [Dt 32:2](https://ref.ly/Deut32:2) (‖ערף). **Hiph.** *Pf.* *lyZIhi* [Is 48:21](https://ref.ly/Isa48:21) *cause to flow*, water from rock (‖זוב).—Vid. also I. זלל.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 45:8; Jeremiah 9:17; Psalms 147:18; Isaiah 48:21; Job 36:28; Numbers 24:7; Exodus 15:8; Song of Songs 4:16; Job 36:27; Isaiah 44:3; Psalms 78:44; Judges 5:6; Judges 5:5; Jeremiah 18:14; Psalms 78:16; Proverbs 5:15; Deuteronomy 32:2; Song of Songs 4:15; Psalms 78:15

[Zc 7:3](https://ref.ly/Zech7:3) *consecrating my(self )*, i.e. by fasting. **Hiph.** *Pf.* מִטֻּמְאָתָם ´וְהִזֵּרְתֶּם אֶת־בְּנֵי־יִש Lv [15:31](https://ref.ly/Lev15:31) (P) *and ye shall keep the sons of Isr. sacredly separate from their uncleanness* (but rd. perh. וְהִזְהַרְתָּם *warn* away from—as Ez [3:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:18), [33:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:8), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:9), —so Sam LXX—as to sense—Di Kau).—Vid. also II. \[נָוַר].

## I נחל (id: BDB06028)

I. I. נחל (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. نَحَلَ *give for one's own, bestow,* so Sab. נחל Sab. DenkmNo. 9\. i. 10; No. 15, i. 4; cf. pp. 41, 65; hence נַחֲלָה orig. *gift,* as Sab. נחלת Levy\-OsZMG xix (1865\), 284 Sab. Denkml.c.; Min. *id.,* HomSüdar. Chrest. 128\).

## נָחַל (id: BDB06032)

> \[Str5158]† II. \[נַ֫חַל] **n.\[m.]** perh. palm\-tree (Ar. نَخْلٌ, n. unit. نَخْلَةٌ; v. PerlesJQ, July, 1899, 688\);—only pl. abs., כִּנְחָלִים נִטָּיוּ [Nu 24:6](https://ref.ly/Num24:6) like palm\-trees, which are stretched out, spread out (as to foliage). So Perlesl.c, who compares כערבי נחל [Ecclus 50:12e](https://ref.ly/Sir50:12), LXX ὡς στελέχη φοινικων.

> \[Str5160] † נַחֲלִיאֵל **n.pr.loc.** (\= valley—or plam—of Ēl);—station of Isr. E. of Dead Sea [Nu 21:19](https://ref.ly/Num21:19), [21:19](https://ref.ly/Num21:19) (JE), poss. (if \= valley) one of main tributaries of Arnon, e.g. *W. Wâle* (v. BlissPEF 1895, 204, 215\).

> \[Str5161] † נֶחֱלָמִי **adj. gent.** (noun not found);—[Je 29:24](https://ref.ly/Jer29:24); also v:[31](https://ref.ly/Jer29:31), v:[32](https://ref.ly/Jer29:32) (Where van d. H. נֶחְלָמִי).

## נחם (id: BDB06038)

\[Str5172] II. \[נָחַשׁ **vb.** only **Pi.** *practise divination, divine, observe signs* (denom. fr. נָהָשׁ acc. to NöZeitschr. f. Völkerpsychol. i (1860\), 413 BauSem. Rel. i. 287 LagBN 188 GerberVerb. Denom. 29; otherwise WeSkizzen iii. 147 MeChrest. Targ. 8\.v. Baes 48 (agst. him v. StaTh Lz April 28, 1894, 235\); chief difficulty is that Aram., which has נַחֵשׁ, has no נָחָשׁ cf. RSJPh.xiv.115; —NH Qal Pt. נוֺחֵשׁ, and Pi.; Aram. Pa. נַחֵשׁ, ܢܰܚܶܫ, all \= *divine, learn by omens;* perh. cp. also Ar. نَحِسَ *be unlucky* Lane2775 cf. Me1.c);—*Pf.* 3 ms. וְנִחֵשׁ [2 K 21:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:6), [2 Ch 33:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:6); 1s. נִחַ֫שְׁתִּי [Gn 30:27](https://ref.ly/Gen30:27); *Impf.* יְגַחֵשׁ [Gn 44:5](https://ref.ly/Gen44:5), [44:15](https://ref.ly/Gen44:15); 3 mpl. יְנַחֲשׁוּ [1 K 20:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:33), וַיְנַחָשׁוּ [2 K 17:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:17); 2 mpl. תְּנַחֲשׁוּ [Lv 19:26](https://ref.ly/Lev19:26); *Inf. abs.* נַחֵשׁ[1 K 20:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:33); *Pt.* מְנַחֵשׁ [Dt 18:10](https://ref.ly/Deut18:10) (cf. esp. RSJPh. xiv..113 ff.);—**1\.** *practise divination* [Gn 44:5](https://ref.ly/Gen44:5) (J; by means of cup, i.e. by hydromancy), with implied power to learn secret things v:[15](https://ref.ly/Gen44:15); condemned by proph. writer [2 K 21:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:6) \= [2 Ch 33:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:6) (\+ עוֺנֵן וְעָשָח אוֺב וְיִדְּעֹנִים), [2 K 17:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:17) (‖וַיִּקְסְמוּ קְסָמִים); forbidden in D and H: [Dt 18:10](https://ref.ly/Deut18:10) ( \+ קֹסֵם קְסָמִים מְעוֺנֵן וּמְכַשֵּׁף), [Lv 19:26](https://ref.ly/Lev19:26) (H; ‖ תְּעוֺנֵ\+נוּ). **2\.** *observe the signs or omens* [Gn 30:27](https://ref.ly/Gen30:27) (j); so prob. יְנַחֲשׁוּ [1 K 20:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:33) now the men *were observing the signs*.

> \[Str5173] †נַ֫חַשׁ **n. \[m.]** divination, enchantment, only abs. ´נ [Nu 23:23](https://ref.ly/Num23:23) (JE; ‖קֶסֶם), and pl. abs. נְחָשִׁים [Nu 24:1](https://ref.ly/Num24:1) (JE), both in story of Balsam.

## IV נחשׁ (id: BDB06070)

IV. נחשׁ (poss. √ of foll., si vera l.; but precise meaning uncertain; GeiUrschrift 392 cp. Ar. نَخَسَ \[\= *goad, prick;* نِخَاسٌ a certain piece of *perforated wood* (Frey)], NH נְחשֶׁת \[*bottom* of vessel, LevyNHWB iii. 374]).

> \[Str5178] † II. \[נְח֫שֶׁת] **n.\[f.]** sf. נְחֻשְׁתֵּח Ez [16:36](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:36) where context (‖עֶרְוָה) favours mng. *lust, harlotry*, or specif. sens. obscoen. (Co \[citing Ki Ra] Berthol, v. also Da); text perh. crpt. (cf. NöZMG xl (1886\). 730\); Sm prop. תַּזְנוּתַיִךְ, so appar. ToyHpt; Or בָּשְׁתֵּךְ.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 16:36


## נָחֵת (id: BDB06072)

\[Str5181] † \[נָחֵת] **vb.** (Aram. and poet.) go down, descend (Aram. נְחֵת, ܢܚܶܬ; Palm. נחת Lzb323);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יֵחַת [Je 21:13](https://ref.ly/Jer21:13); 3 fs. תֵּ֫חַת [Pr 17:10](https://ref.ly/Prov17:10), וַתִּנְחַת [Psalm 38:3](https://ref.ly/Ps38:3); 3 mpl. יֵחַָתּוּ [Jb 21:13](https://ref.ly/Job21:13) (forms יֵחַת, יֵחַתּוּ v. also חתת);—**1\.** *go down, descend*, to attack [Je 21:13](https://ref.ly/Jer21:13); into Shₑ’ôl [Jb 21:13](https://ref.ly/Job21:13). **2\.** fig. *descend* in chastisement [Psalm 38:3](https://ref.ly/Ps38:3) (hand of ´י; ‖ **Niph.** v. infr.); sq. בְּ *descend into* i.e. make impression on, [Pr 17:10](https://ref.ly/Prov17:10) (of reproof). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. נִחֲתוּ sq. בְּ \= *penetrate* [Psalm 38:3](https://ref.ly/Ps38:3), (of arrows of ´י). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. consec. וְנִחַת [2 S 22:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:35) (v. Ges§ 145\. 7\); \= 3 fs. וְנִחֲתָה [Psalm 18:35](https://ref.ly/Ps18:35); *Inf. abs.* נַחֵת [Psalm 65:11](https://ref.ly/Ps65:11); —*cause to descend* \= *press down*, acc. to most, in קֶשֶׁת נְחוּשָׁה זְדֹעֹתָי ´וְנ [2 S 22:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:35) \= [Psalm 18:35](https://ref.ly/Ps18:35) *so that my arms press down, stretch*, a bow of bronze, but dub.; Bu conj. Hiph. יַנְחִת; HPS וַיָּ֫שֶׁת *and makes my arms a bow of bronze;* of *pressing down*, smoothing out, furrows of land [Psalm 65:11](https://ref.ly/Ps65:11). **Hiph.** *Imv.* הַנְחַת [Jo 4:11](https://ref.ly/Joel4:11) thither *bring down* thy heroes, ´י! (on form cf. Ges§ 64 R. 3 Köi. 314 f.).

> \[Str5183] † II. נַ֫חַת **n.\[m.]** descent;—only cstr., of ´י's arm in judgment [Is 30:30](https://ref.ly/Isa30:30).—I. נַחַת v. sub נוח. p. 629

> \[Str5185] † \[נָחֵת] **adj.** descending;—mpl. נְחִתִּים (on form cf. Köii. 81\), [2 K 6:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:9) (to battle); but rd. prob. with Th Klo Benz after LXX נֶחְבִּים *hidden*.

> \[Str5184] † III. נַ֫חַת **n. pr. m. 1\.** son of Reuel, grandson of Esau [Gn 36:13](https://ref.ly/Gen36:13), [36:17](https://ref.ly/Gen36:17) (P; LXX Ναχομ, Ναχοθ LXXL Ναχεθ), [1 Ch 1:37](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:37) (LXX Ναχες, A LXXL Ναχεθ). **2\.** grandson of Elkanah [1 Ch 6:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:11), LXX Καιναθ, LXXL Νααθ;—prob. crpt. for תּוֺחַ v:[19](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:19), or תֹּחוּ [1 S 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:1) (v. these words and Wede gent. 37 f. \[who prop. תַּחַת for נַחַת], Dr:[1 S 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:1)). **3\.** an overseer, Hezekian's time [2 Ch 31:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:13), LXX Μαεθ, A Ναεθ, LXXL Νααθ.

Psalm Psalm Pr; fs. הַטִּי [Gn 24:14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14), [Psalm 45:11](https://ref.ly/Ps45:11); mpl. הַטּוּ [Jos 24:23](https://ref.ly/Josh24:23) \+ 3 t.; *Inf. cstr.* לְהַטּוֺת [Is 10:8](https://ref.ly/Isa10:8) \+ 4 t., לְהַטֹּת [Ex 23:2](https://ref.ly/Exod23:2); sf. לְהַטּוֺתָהּ [Nu 22:23](https://ref.ly/Num22:23); *Pt.* מַטֶּה [Dt 27:19](https://ref.ly/Deut27:19); mpl. מַטִּים [Psalm 125:5](https://ref.ly/Ps125:5), cstr. מַטֵּי [Mal 3:5](https://ref.ly/Mal3:5); —**1\.** rarely *stretch out* (as **Qal 1**), hand [Is 31:3](https://ref.ly/Isa31:3) (of ´י in hostility), c. עַל [Je 6:12](https://ref.ly/Jer6:12), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Jer15:6). **2\.** rarely *spread out* cloth on (אֶל) rock [2 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:10); as **Qal 2:** tent [16:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:22) (\+ ל Pers.), tent\-curtains (fig. of growth) [Is 54:2](https://ref.ly/Isa54:2). **3\.** usu. *turn, incline*, c. acc., in many senses: **a.** lit. *turn* ass into (acc.) road [Nu 22:23](https://ref.ly/Num22:23) (JE); *take aside* [2 S 3:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:27) (acc. Pers. \+ אֶ־ל), [6:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:10) (acc. rei \+ acc. \= *to*) \= [1 Ch 13:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:13) (אֶ־ל); *incline, turn* jar of water [Gn 24:14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14) (J; to give drink); intrans. only יַטּוּ עַ־ל [Am 2:8](https://ref.ly/Amos2:8) *on* garments taken in pledge *they recline*, and הַטּוּ מִנֵּי־אֹרַח [Is 30:11](https://ref.ly/Isa30:11) *turn aside, out of the path* (i.e. do not interfere with us; ‖סוּרוּ מִנֵּי־דֶרֶךְ). **b.** fig., הַמַּטִּים עֲקַלְקַלּוֺתָם [Psalm 125:5](https://ref.ly/Ps125:5) *those turning aside their crooked* (ways), i.e. making their ways crooked; cf. (neg.) [Jb 23:11](https://ref.ly/Job23:11). **c.** *turn*, \= influence, heart [2 S 19:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:15); *turn* (away) heart (cause to apostatize) [1 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:2), [11:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:4) (c. אַחֲרֵי), v:[3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:3) (abs.); לֵב subj., c. acc. [Pr 2:2](https://ref.ly/Prov2:2) (ל); subj. ´י, [1 K 8:58](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:58), [Psalm 119:36](https://ref.ly/Ps119:36), c. עַ־ל [Pr 21:1](https://ref.ly/Prov21:1); neg. (c. ל) [Psalm 141:4](https://ref.ly/Ps141:4). **e.** esp. *incline* the ear, of men (in obedience to God), usu. ‖שָׁמַע: abs. [Je 7:24](https://ref.ly/Jer7:24) \+ 6 t. Je (all neg.), so [25:4](https://ref.ly/Jer25:4), [44:5](https://ref.ly/Jer44:5) (\+ inf.), [Is 55:3](https://ref.ly/Isa55:3); to inspired teacher [Pr 4:20](https://ref.ly/Prov4:20) (‖הִקְשִׁיב), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Prov5:1) (‖ *id.*) v:[13](https://ref.ly/Prov5:13) (all c. ל), [22:17](https://ref.ly/Prov22:17), [Psalm 45:11](https://ref.ly/Ps45:11), [78:1](https://ref.ly/Ps78:1) (הַאֲזִינָה); to receive revelation [Psalm 49:5](https://ref.ly/Ps49:5); of God, listening to men, sts. ‖שָׁמַע: [2 K 19:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:16) \= [Is 37:17](https://ref.ly/Isa37:17), [Dn 9:18](https://ref.ly/Dan9:18), c. ל [Psalm 17:6](https://ref.ly/Ps17:6); also (c. אֶ־ל [31:3](https://ref.ly/Ps31:3), [71:2](https://ref.ly/Ps71:2), [102:3](https://ref.ly/Ps102:3), c. לְ [88:3](https://ref.ly/Ps88:3), [116:2](https://ref.ly/Ps116:2), abs. [86:1](https://ref.ly/Ps86:1); once וְאַט אֵלָיו [Ho 11:4](https://ref.ly/Hos11:4) *and I used to incline* etc. (either rare intrans., or obj. om., e.g. אָזְנִי rd. perh. וָאַט (וָאֵט ?) *and I inclined*, after וָאָהְיֶה). **f.** *bend down* heavens, of ´י (cf. **Qal 3** b), [Psalm 144:5](https://ref.ly/Ps144:5); \= *hold out, extend* unto (from above, עַל) [Ezr 7:28](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:28), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:9) (obj. חֶסֶד). **g.** *thrust aside*, esp. c. acc. מִשְׁפָּט of *perverting* or *wresting* justice: [Ex 23:6](https://ref.ly/Exod23:6) (E), [1 S 8:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:3), [Dt 16:19](https://ref.ly/Deut16:19), [24:17](https://ref.ly/Deut24:17), [27:19](https://ref.ly/Deut27:19), [La 3:35](https://ref.ly/Lam3:35), cf. Pers. לְהַטּוֺת מִדִּין דַּלִּים [Is 10:2](https://ref.ly/Isa10:2), [Am 5:12](https://ref.ly/Amos5:12), [Is 29:21](https://ref.ly/Isa29:21), [Mal 3:5](https://ref.ly/Mal3:5), יַטּוּ אֶבְיֹנִים מִדָּרֶךְ [Jb 24:4](https://ref.ly/Job24:4), לְהַטּוֺת צַדִּיק בַּמִּשְׁפָּט [Pr 18:5](https://ref.ly/Prov18:5); here belongs prob. לְהַטֹּת [Ex 23:2](https://ref.ly/Exod23:2) (E; abs.), perh. ins. מִשְׁפָּט (cf. BuZAW xi. (1891\), 113 Ryad loc.). **h.** *thrust away*, of ´י, אַל־תַּט בְּאַף עַבְדְֶָּ [Psalm 27:9](https://ref.ly/Ps27:9); cf. עֲוֺנוֺתֵיכֶם הִטּוּ־אֵלֶּה [Je 5:25](https://ref.ly/Jer5:25) *your iniquities have thrust away* (deprived you of) these (harvests).

> \[Str4295] † מַ֫טָּה, מָ֫טָּה **adv.** downwards (from \[מַט] for \[מַטֶּה] *a place of declension* (cf. נָטָה, **3 a**), with ה—ָ *loc.*, O1222 b Köii. 280: cf. the opp. מַ֫עְלָהִ, sub עָלָה):—**l.** alone, [Dt 28:43](https://ref.ly/Deut28:43) and thou shalt descend מַ֫טָּה מָ֫טָּה *downwards, downwards*, i.e. lower and lower, [Pr 15:24](https://ref.ly/Prov15:24) ´לְמַעַן סוּר מִשְּׁאוֺל מ. **2\.** לְמַ֫טָּה: **a.** *downwards* [Dt 28:13](https://ref.ly/Deut28:13) ´וְלאֹ תִהְיֶה למ, [Is 37:31](https://ref.ly/Isa37:31) \= [2 K 19:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:30) ´שֹׁרֶשׁ למ (cf. Ph CIS1. i. 2, 11 f. אל יכן לם שׁרשׁ למט ופר למעל let them not have root *downwards*, or fruit upwards), [Je 31:37](https://ref.ly/Jer31:37), [Ec 3:21](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:21), [2 Ch 32:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:30); [Ezr 9:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:13) כי אתה חָשַכְתָּ לְמַ֫טָּה מֵעֲוֺנֵנוּ either (Be) hast kept back, *downward*, part of our iniquity (prevented it from appearing, and being counted against us), or, held us (Ges), or thy anger (Ke), back, *below* (cf. לְמַ֫עְלָה מֵעַל *above*, [2 Ch 34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4)) our iniq. (punished us less than our iniq. deserved). **b.** וּלְמַ֫טָּה, (*a*) of space, *and downwards*, Ez [1:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:27) ´וּמִמַּרְאֵה מָתְנָיו ול, [8:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:2); (*b*) of age, *and under*, [1 Ch 17:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:23) לְמִבֶּן עשרים ´שׁנה וְ. **3\.** מִלְּמַ֫טָּה *beneath* (v. מִן **9 a:** opp. מִלְמַעְלָה *above*), [Ex 26:24](https://ref.ly/Exod26:24) \= [36:29](https://ref.ly/Exod36:29), [27:5](https://ref.ly/Exod27:5) \= [38:4](https://ref.ly/Exod38:4), [28:27](https://ref.ly/Exod28:27) \= [39:20](https://ref.ly/Exod39:20).

[2 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:2), [2 Ch 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:11), cstr. [Am 6:4](https://ref.ly/Amos6:4), [Est 1:6](https://ref.ly/Esth1:6); —*couch, bed*, common article of furniture [2 K 4:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:10), cf. v:[21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:21), v:[32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:32), [1 K 17:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:19), for repose, esp. at night, or in illness [Gn 47:31](https://ref.ly/Gen47:31) (J; ´ראֹשׁ הַמּ), cf. [48:2](https://ref.ly/Gen48:2) (J), [49:33](https://ref.ly/Gen49:33) (P), [Psalm 6:7](https://ref.ly/Ps6:7), [Pr 26:14](https://ref.ly/Prov26:14), [2 Ch 24:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:25) (prob.), and for sitting, or reclining by day [1 S 28:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:23), [1 K 21:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:4); ´פְּאַת מ [Am 3:12](https://ref.ly/Amos3:12); seat of King Solomon Ct [3:7](https://ref.ly/Song3:7); in bed\-chamber (חֲדַר מִשְׁכָּב) [Ex 7:28](https://ref.ly/Exod7:28) (J), [2 S 4:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:7); sts. portable [1 S 19:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:15) cf. v:[13](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:13), v:[16](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:16), hence \= *bier* [2 S 3:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:31); sts. high [2 K 1:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:4), [1:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:6), [1:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:16) (עָלָה שָׁם opp. יָרַד מִן); sts. costly, (with frames) of ivory [Am 6:4](https://ref.ly/Amos6:4); for reclining at feasts, made of gold and silver [Est 1:6](https://ref.ly/Esth1:6); cf. כְּבוּדָּה ´מ Ez [23:41](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:41) *glorious couch;* חֲדַר הַמִּטּוֺת [2 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:2) \= [2 Ch 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:11) (v. חֶדֶר).—Cf. also מִשְׁכָּב (שׁכב), עֶרֶש.

Frä65 f.);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´נ [La 3:28](https://ref.ly/Lam3:28); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִטּוֺל [Is 40:15](https://ref.ly/Isa40:15); *Pt. act.* נוֺטֵל [2 S 24:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:12); —*lift,* c. acc. [Is 40:15](https://ref.ly/Isa40:15) (fig. subj. ´י); *lift over thee* (על) i.e. offer [2 S 24:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:12), but rd. נוֺטֶה (as ‖ [1 Ch 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:10)) We Bu HPS; *lift (and lay) upon* (עַל) [La 3:28](https://ref.ly/Lam3:28). **Pi.** *Impf.* sf. וַיְנַטְּלֵם [Is 63:9](https://ref.ly/Isa63:9) *and he bare them,* fig. of ´י bearing his people (‖וַיְגַשֵּׁאֵם).

## נָטַף (id: BDB06091)

\[Str5197] † \[נָטַף] **vb.** drop, drip, fig. (esp. of prophet) discourse (NH *id.;* Targum נְטַף *drop, drip;* Syr. ܢܛܰܦ *drip,* ܛܘܽܦܬܳܐ *drop* (NöSyr. Gr. § 105\) etc.; Ar. نَطَفَ *id.;* Eth. ነጥበ፡ *id.,* ነጠፈ፡ *colare* (stillando), *expercolare*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 mpl. נָֽטְפוּ Ju [5:4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4) \+ 2 t.; נָטָּפוּ Ju [5:4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4); *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּטֹּף [Jb 29:22](https://ref.ly/Job29:22); 3 mpl. יִטּפוּ [Jo 4:18](https://ref.ly/Joel4:18); 3 fpl. תִּטֹּפְנָה [Pr 5:3](https://ref.ly/Prov5:3), Ct [4:11](https://ref.ly/Song4:11); *Pt* נֹטְפוֺת Ct [5:13](https://ref.ly/Song5:13); —*drop, drip,* usu. trans., c. acc., of clouds *dropping* water (מַיִ), in storm Ju [5:4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4), ‖ heavens v:[4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4) (obj. om.), [Psalm 68:9](https://ref.ly/Ps68:9) (*id.;* all theoph.); of hands *dripping* myrrh (מוֺר) Ct [5:5](https://ref.ly/Song5:5); in predict. יִטְּפוּ עָסִיס [Jo 4:18](https://ref.ly/Joel4:18) the mts. *shall drip must;* fig. of (seductive) speech ´נֹפֶת תִּטּ [Pr 5:3](https://ref.ly/Prov5:3) the lips of the strange woman *drop honey*, so perh. Ct [4:11](https://ref.ly/Song4:11) (Bu of kisses); מוֺר ´שִפְתוֺתָיו נ [5:13](https://ref.ly/Song5:13) *his lips drip myrrh* (perh. of sweet breath). Intrans. only עָלֵימוֺ תִּטֹּף מִלָּתִי [Jb 29:22](https://ref.ly/Job29:22)  *upon them my speech used to drop.* **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. וְהִטִּיפוּ consec. [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13); *Impf.* 2 ms. תַּטִּיף [Am 7:16](https://ref.ly/Amos7:16); 1 S. אַטִּף [Mi 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11); 3 mpl. יִטִּיפָוּן v:[6](https://ref.ly/Mic2:6); 2 mpl. תַּטִּיפוּ v:[6](https://ref.ly/Mic2:6), תַּטִּפוּ v:[6](https://ref.ly/Mic2:6); *Pt.* מַטִּיף [Mi 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11); —**1\.** *drip*, עָסִיס ´וְהַטּ [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13) and the mts. *shall drip must* (\= **Qal** [Jo 4:18](https://ref.ly/Joel4:18)). **2\.** of speech, esp. prophecy (without acc.): c. עַל *against,* [Am 7:16](https://ref.ly/Amos7:16) (‖הִנָּבֵא), c. אֶ־ל toward, [Ex 21:2](https://ref.ly/Exod21:2) (‖ *id.*), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Exod21:7) (‖ *id.*); c. ל *concerning* [Mi 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11) cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11); —אַל־תַּטִּפוּ יַטִּיכָּוּן לאֹ־יַטִּפוּ לָאֵלֶּה [Mi 2:6](https://ref.ly/Mic2:6) *‘talk not’—so they talk—‘they* (rd. *ye*?) *shall not talk of these things*’ (where ´תַּטּ, יַטִּפוּ ref. to prophets).

> \[Str5198] † I. \[נָטָף] **n.m.** drop;—נִטְפֵי־מָיִם [Jb 36:27](https://ref.ly/Job36:27) (‖מָטָר, *rain*).

> \[Str5200] † נְטֹפָתִי, נְטוֺפָתִי **adj. gent.;**—´נְטֹפ [2 S 23:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:28) \+ 5 t., ´נְטוֺפ [1 Ch 2:54](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:54) \+ 4 t. ## נָטַשׁ (id: BDB06099)

\[Str5203] † נָטַשׁ **vb.** leave, forsake, permit (Targum *forsake; draw off* skin of dead animal; Ar. نَطِسٌ \= *abstinens ab impuro* acc. to Ḳam (Frey));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´נ [1 S 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:2), sf. 1 pl. נְטָשָׁ֫נוּ Ju [6:13](https://ref.ly/Judg6:13); 2 ms. נָטַשְׁתָּ [1 S 17:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:28), נָטַשְׁתָּה [Is 2:6](https://ref.ly/Isa2:6), sf. נְטַשְׁתַּ֫נִי [Gn 31:28](https://ref.ly/Gen31:28), וּנְטַשְׁתָּהּ consec. [Ex 23:11](https://ref.ly/Exod23:11), etc.; *Impf.* יִטּשׁ [1 S 12:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:22), [Psalm 94:14](https://ref.ly/Ps94:14), יִטּוֺשׁ [Ho 12:15](https://ref.ly/Hos12:15), sf. 1 pl. יִטְּשֵׁנוּ [1 K 8:57](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:57); 1 pl. נִטּשׁ [Ne 10:32](https://ref.ly/Neh10:32), etc.; *Imv.* נְטוֺשׁ [Pr 17:14](https://ref.ly/Prov17:14); *Pt. pass.* f. נְטוּשָׁה [Is 21:15](https://ref.ly/Isa21:15); mpl. נְטֻשִׁיס [1 S 30:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:16); —**1\.** *leave, let alone* [Ex 23:11](https://ref.ly/Exod23:11) (E; \= let field lie fallow, ‖שָׁמַט), cf. וְנִטּשׁ אֶת־הַשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁכִיעִיתּ וּמַשָּׁא כָ־לָ\+יד [Ne 10:32](https://ref.ly/Neh10:32) (i.e. leave field fallow and debts unclaimed); obj. quails, *let them lie*, (עַל loc.) [Nu 11:31](https://ref.ly/Num11:31) (JE), of Amalekites נְטֻשִׁים עַל־פְּנֵי פָל־הָאָרֶץ [1 S 30:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:16) *left to themselves over all the land;* וְדָמָיו עָלָיו יִטּוֺשׁ [Ho 12:15](https://ref.ly/Hos12:15) and his mortal guilt *shall he leave upon him; leave* in charge of, *entrust* to (עַל Pers.): sheep [1 S 17:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:20), [17:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:28); baggage v:[22](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:22). **2\.** usu. *forsake, abandon*, דִּבְרֵי הָאֲחֹנוֺת ´נ [1 S 10:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:2) thy father *hath abandoned the matter of the asses* (ceased to be concerned about them); הָרִיב נְטוֺשׁ [Pr 17:14](https://ref.ly/Prov17:14) *abandon contention;* oft. subj. ´י c. acc. Pers. (under fig. of cedar) Ez [31:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:12), [31:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:12). **3\.** *permit*, only וְלאֹ נְטַשְׁתַּנִי לְנַשֵּׁק ´לְבָנַי וגו [Gn 31:28](https://ref.ly/Gen31:28) (E) *and hast not permitted me to kiss my sons*, etc.—For וַתִּטּשׁ הַמִּלְחָמָה [1 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:2) rd. prob. וַתֵּט (LXX ἔκλινεν) Dr Klo Bu, or \< וַתִּקֶשׁ (קשׁה) *grew fierce* HPS (Löhr prop. תִּנָּטֵשׁ cf. > \[Str5223] † \[נָכֵה] **adj.** smitten, stricken (cf. \[נָכָא]);—only cstr.: נְכֵה רַגְלַיִם [2 S 4:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:4), [9:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:3) *crippled of feet;* נְכֵה־רוּחַ [Is 66:2](https://ref.ly/Isa66:2) *contrite of spirit* (\+ עָנִי).

> \[Str5222] † \[נֵכֶה] **adj.** id.;—pl. נֵכִים [Psalm 35:15](https://ref.ly/Ps35:15) *smitten ones,* but rd. prob. נָכְרִים *aliens* Ol Che Bae al.

## נכח (id: BDB06129)

נכח (√ of foll.: prob. *be in front of;* LagBN 30 cp. Ar. نَكَحَ *marry* (perh. orig. in phys. sense); Syr. ܢܟܚ *is gentle*, ܢܟܝܚܘܬܐ *gentleness*).

> \[Str5226–27] † נֹ֫כַח **subst.** front, always in **prep.** or **adv.** phrases, with sf. נִכְחוֺ [Ex 14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2), Ez [46:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:9):—**1\.** as adv. acc. *in front of, opposite to*, [Ex 14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2) נִכְחוֺ תַּחֲנוּ, [26:35](https://ref.ly/Exod26:35) the candlestick נֹכַח הַשֻּׁלְחָן, [40:24](https://ref.ly/Exod40:24), [Jos 18:17](https://ref.ly/Josh18:17) (in the ‖ [15:7](https://ref.ly/Josh15:7), נֹכַח לְ: v. לְ **5 c**), [1 K 20:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:29) וַיַּחֲנוּ אֵלֶּה נֹכַח אֵלֶּה, [22:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:35) (\= [2 Ch 18:34](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:34)), [Est 5:1](https://ref.ly/Esth5:1), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Esth5:1); Ez [46:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:9) Qr כִּי נִכְחוֺ יֵצֵא shall go out *in front of himself*, i.e. straightforward; cf. נֶגְדּוֺ, נֶגֶד **1 a** (*b*). Fig. [Pr 5:21](https://ref.ly/Prov5:21) דַּרְכֵי אִישׁ ´כִּי נֹכַח עֵינֵי י *in the sight* of ´י's eyes are, etc., Ju [18:6](https://ref.ly/Judg18:6) דַּרְכְּכֶם ´נֹכַח י *before* ´י is your way, i.e. under his eye and favourable regard. In the phr. נֹכַח פְּנֵי, [Je 17:16](https://ref.ly/Jer17:16) מוֺצָא שְפָתַי נֹכַח פָּנֶיְ הָיָה was *before* thy face, [La 2:19](https://ref.ly/Lam2:19), Ez [14:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:3) מִכְשׁוֺל עֲוֺנָם נָֽתְנוּ נֹכַח פְּנֵיהֶם (i.e. they contemplate it with pleasure), v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:4); v:[7](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:7). **2\.** combined with preps.:—**a.** אֶל־נֹכַח, [Nu 19:4](https://ref.ly/Num19:4) פני אהל ´מועד וְהִזָּה אל־נ and sprinkle it *towards the front* of, etc. **b.** לְנֹכַח: (*a*) as **adv.** [Pr 4:25](https://ref.ly/Prov4:25) יביטו ´עיניך לנ let thine eyes look *to the front* or *right on* (‖ועפיך יַישִׁרוּ נֶגְדְֶָּ); (*b*) as **prep.** [Gn 30:38](https://ref.ly/Gen30:38) לְנֹכַח הַצּאֹן *in front of* the flocks [25:21](https://ref.ly/Gen25:21) לְנֹכַח אִשְׁתּוֺ …וַיֶּעְתַּר *in fornt of*, i.e. *on behalf of* (cf. *pro*), his wife; (*c*) עַד־נֹכַח, Ju [19:10](https://ref.ly/Judg19:10) יְבוּס ´וַיָּבאֹ עַד־נ *as fas as in front of* Jebus, [20:43](https://ref.ly/Judg20:43), Ez [47:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:20) (עַד־נֹכַח לְ). Syn. נֶגֶד, q.v.

> \[Str5228,5229] † \[נָכֹחַ] **adj.** and **subst.** straight, right, straightness, f. נְכֹחָה, pl. נְכֹחִים, וֺת\-:—[Is 57:2](https://ref.ly/Isa57:2) הֹלֵח נְכֹחוֺ he that walks *in* (cf. [33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15), [50:10](https://ref.ly/Isa50:10)) *his straightness* (Ges ‘der seinen *geraden* Weg geht’), fig. for straightforwardly (cf. [Pr 4:25](https://ref.ly/Prov4:25)); נְכֹחָה *what is straight in front*, fig. *straightforwardness, honesty*, [Am 3:10](https://ref.ly/Amos3:10) ´לאֹ יָֽדְעוּ עֲשוֺת נ, [Is 59:14](https://ref.ly/Isa59:14) לאֹ תוּכַל לָבוֺא ´ונ (‖אֱמֶת); [2 S 15:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:3) דבריך טובים וּנְכֹחִים thy pleadings are good and *right* (i.e. *true*), [Pr 8:9](https://ref.ly/Prov8:9) (of words of Wisdom) כֻּלָּם נְכֹחִים לַמֵּבִין i.e. they lead *straight* to the right goal (‖ישׁרים; comp. v:[8b](https://ref.ly/Prov8:8) אֵין בָּהֶם נִפָתָּל וְעִקֵּשׁ), [24:26](https://ref.ly/Prov24:26) מֵשִׁיב דְּבָרִים נְכֹחִים i.e. either *correct* (leading to the right point), or *honest* answers; [Is 30:10](https://ref.ly/Isa30:10) לאֹ־תֶחֱזוּ לָנוּ נְכֹחוֺת *true* things (opp. חֲלָקוֺת, מַהֲתַלּוֺת *illusions*), [26:10](https://ref.ly/Isa26:10) בְּאֶרֶץ נְכֹחוֺת יְעַוֵּל in a land of *rectitude* (Che) he will do wickedly.

## נָכַל (id: BDB06132)

\[Str5230] † \[נָכַל] **vb.** be crafty, deceitful, knavish (As. *nakâlu, be crafty, cunning;* Targum נְכֵל *be crafty*, Pa. *deceive;* Syr. ܢܟܠ *deceive, plot;* cf. perh. ወነጀል፡ *act faithlessly*, ወነጌል፡ *faithless*, PräAmhar. Spr. 90\);—**Qal** *Pt.* נוֺכֵל [Mal 1:14](https://ref.ly/Mal1:14) *knave*. **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. c. acc. cogn. נִכְלֵיהֶם אֲשֶׁר נִכְּלוּ לָכֶם [Nu 25:18](https://ref.ly/Num25:18) *their wiles with which they beguiled you*. **Hithp.** *Impf.* וַיִּתְנַכְּלוּ אֹתוֺ לַהֲמִיתוֺ [Gn 37:18](https://ref.ly/Gen37:18) *they knavishly planned* against him (Köiii. 9\), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* לְחִתְנַכֵּל בְ [Psalm 105:25](https://ref.ly/Ps105:25) *to deal knavishly with*.

> \[Str5231] † \[נֵ֫כֶל] **n. \[m.]** wiliness, craft, knavery;—only pl. sf. נִכְלֵיהֶם [Nu 25:18](https://ref.ly/Num25:18) *their craft, their wile* (as acc. cogn. c. נִכֵּל).

> \[Str3596] † כִּילַי, כֵּלַי **n.m.** knave (for נְכִילַי v. Köii.1, 118\);—abs. כִּילַי [Is 32:5](https://ref.ly/Isa32:5) (opp. שׁוֺעַ, ‖נָבָל); כֵּלַי v:[7](https://ref.ly/Isa32:7) (assonance with כֵּלָיו, fr. כְּלִי q.v.).

> \[Str5233] † \[נֶ֫כֶס] **n.m.**:[Jos 22:8](https://ref.ly/Josh22:8) only pl. נְכָסִים riches, treasures (prob. As. or Aram. loan\-word, cf. As. *nikâsu, property, wealth, gain*, DlHWB 463, Syr. ܢܟܣܐ *wealth;* on deriv. fr. As. *nikâsu, cut off, hew down, behead*, etc., Syr. ܢܟܣ *slay*, Targum נְכַס, NH נָכַס *id.,* through meaning *cattle for slaughter*, cf. Frä98 HptHbr.iii (Jan.1887\), 107 ff. SchwallyIdiot. 120 f. Brockad voc.);—[Jos 22:8](https://ref.ly/Josh22:8) (P), [2 Ch 1:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:11), [1:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:12), [Ec 6:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:2) (all ‖עשֶׁר, כָּבוֺד), [5:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:18) (‖עשֶׁר).

## I נָכַר (id: BDB06136)

\[Str5234] †I. \[נָכַר] **vb.** (not **Qal**) regard, recognize (NH Hiph. *know, be acquainted with;* Targum Aph. *id.;* Syr. Aph. ܐܰܟܰܪ *id.;* cf. also Ar. نَكِرَ in sense *be shrewd, cunning*;—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. לאֹ נִכְּרוּ [La 4:8](https://ref.ly/Lam4:8) *they are not recognized*. **Pi.** *Pf.* לאֹ נִכַּר שׁוֺעַ לִפְנֵי־דָל [Jb 34:19](https://ref.ly/Job34:19) *he regardeth not the opulent above the poor* (‖לאֹ־נָשָא פְּנֵי שָרִים); *Impf.* לאֹ תְנַכֵָּרוּ [Jb 21:29](https://ref.ly/Job21:29) *do ye not recognize their tokens*. **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִכִּיר [Dt 33:9](https://ref.ly/Deut33:9), [Dn 11:39](https://ref.ly/Dan11:39) Kt; sf. הִכִּירוֺ [Gn 27:23](https://ref.ly/Gen27:23); 3 pl. הִכִּירוּ Ju [18:3](https://ref.ly/Judg18:3) \+ 2 t., sf. הִכִּירֻהוּ [Jb 2:12](https://ref.ly/Job2:12), הִכִּרֻהוּ [Gn 42:8](https://ref.ly/Gen42:8); *Impf.* יַכִּיר [Dt 21:17](https://ref.ly/Deut21:17) \+ 3 t. \+ [Dn 11:39](https://ref.ly/Dan11:39) Qr; וַיַּכֵּר [Gn 38:26](https://ref.ly/Gen38:26) \+; sf. וַיַּכִּרֵ֫הוּ [1 K 18:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:7), יַכִּירֶ֫נּוּ [Jb 7:10](https://ref.ly/Job7:10), וַיַּכִּירָהּ [Gn 37:33](https://ref.ly/Gen37:33), etc.; *Imv.* הַֽכֶּ־ר [Gn 31:32](https://ref.ly/Gen31:32) \+ 2 t.; *Inf. abs.* הַכֵּֽ־ר (Ginsb van d. H. הַֽכֶּ־ר) [Pr 24:23](https://ref.ly/Prov24:23) [28:21](https://ref.ly/Prov28:21); *cstr.* sf. הַכִּירֵנִי [Ru 2:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:10); *Pt.* מַכִּיר [Psalm 142:5](https://ref.ly/Ps142:5), sf. מַכִּירֵח [Ru 2:19](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:19), etc.;—**1\.** *regard, observe*, esp. with a view to recognition, c. obj. cl., [Gn 31:32](https://ref.ly/Gen31:32), [37:32](https://ref.ly/Gen37:32), [38:25](https://ref.ly/Gen38:25); *pay attention to*, c. acc. Pers. [Ru 2:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:10), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:19); acc. rei (of God) [Jb 34:25](https://ref.ly/Job34:25); acc. rei om. [2 S 3:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:36); ´י subj. לְטוֺבָה …כֵּן אַכִּיר [Je 24:5](https://ref.ly/Jer24:5) *so will I regard* the exiles ... *for good;* Antiochus subj. [Dn 11:39](https://ref.ly/Dan11:39); הִכִּיר פָּנִים *pay regard to* (shew partiality, \= ´נָשָא פְּנֵי פ) [Dt 1:17](https://ref.ly/Deut1:17), [16:19](https://ref.ly/Deut16:19) (‖הִטָּה מִשְׁפָּט), [Pr 24:23](https://ref.ly/Prov24:23), [28:21](https://ref.ly/Prov28:21).

 **2\.** *Recognize* (as formerly known) c. acc. Pers. vel rei, [Gn 27:23](https://ref.ly/Gen27:23), [37:33](https://ref.ly/Gen37:33), [38:26](https://ref.ly/Gen38:26) (all J), [42:7](https://ref.ly/Gen42:7), [42:8](https://ref.ly/Gen42:8), [42:8](https://ref.ly/Gen42:8) (E), [1 K 18:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:7), [20:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:41), [Jb 2:12](https://ref.ly/Job2:12); proverb. בְּטֶרֶם יַכִּיר אִישׁ אֶת־רֵעֵהוּ [Ru 3:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:14) *before a man could recognize his fellow* (i.e. before dawn); *recognize* voice (קוֺל), Ju [18:3](https://ref.ly/Judg18:3), [1 S 26:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:17); \= *perceive* the true situation, וָאַכִּירָה וְהִנֵּה [Ne 6:12](https://ref.ly/Neh6:12) *and I perceived, and lo!* God had not sent him. **3\.** *Be willing to recognize, acknowledge* [Dt 21:17](https://ref.ly/Deut21:17), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Deut33:9), [Is 63:16](https://ref.ly/Isa63:16) (‖יָדַע), [Psalm 142:5](https://ref.ly/Ps142:5); *acknowledge with honour* [Is 61:9](https://ref.ly/Isa61:9). **4\.** *Be acquainted with,* לאֹ יַכִּירֶנּוּ עוֺד מְקוֺמוֺ [Jb 7:10](https://ref.ly/Job7:10) *his place shall be acquainted with him no more* (he shall be gone and forgotten), cf. [Psalm 103:16](https://ref.ly/Ps103:16); c. acc. rei [Jb 24:17](https://ref.ly/Job24:17). **5\.** *Distinguish, understand*, לְקוֺל …הִכִּיר קוֺל [Ezr 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:13) *could not distinguish*, etc.; cf. [Jb 4:16](https://ref.ly/Job4:16) *could not distinguish its appearance;* הִכִּיר לְדַבֵּר [Ne 13:24](https://ref.ly/Neh13:24) *understand* how to *speak*, etc. **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְנַכֶּ־רָ\+נעַר [Pr 20:11](https://ref.ly/Prov20:11) by his deeds *a youth maketh himself known*, whether his doing be pure, etc.

## II נכר (id: BDB06139)

II. נכר (√ of foll., poss. \= I. נכר, whence the *foreign, strange*, as that which is intently regarded, so Thes, but precarious; cf. As. *nakâru, rebel*, Pa. *change, nakiru*, and *nakaru, enemy, nukurtu, enmity*, etc.; Ar. نَكِرَ in sense *be bad, evil;* II. *change, alter* a thing; Sab. נכר II. *reject, injure*, etc., נכרם *injury* Sab Denkm76 CISiv. 81\.9; 29\.5; Min. נכר *alter* HomSüdarab. Chrest. 128; Syr. ܢܰܟܰܪ *reject*, ܢܘܽܟܪܳܝܳܐ *alienus*, etc.; Targum נוּכְרַי *strange, foreign*).

648:b. II. נכר, 11\. 5 f.: for ‘نَكِرَ in sense *be bad, evil,*’ etc., rd. نَكُرَ *be ignorant of,* Qor 11:73; II. *change, alter,* so as not to be known, *disguise* Qor 27:41; IV\=I, also *deny,* Qor 16:85 *repudiate.’*

> \[Str5235] † נֶ֫כֶר, \[נֹ֫כֶר **n.\[m.]** misfortune, calamity;—abs. נֶכֶר [Jb 31:3](https://ref.ly/Job31:3) *calamity* (‖אֵיד); sf. נָכְרוֺ Ob:[12](https://ref.ly/Obad1:12) *his calamity*.
> 
> — נֵכָר, 1\.1: after ‘foreign’ ins. ‘(prop. *unknown,* cf. Ar. √).’

> \[Str5237] † נָכְרִי **adj.** foreign, alien (NH נָכְרִי \= *Gentile*);—´נ abs. m. [Zp 1:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:8) \+; f. נָכְרִיָּה [Ex 2:22](https://ref.ly/Exod2:22) \+; mpl. נָכְרִים [Is 2:6](https://ref.ly/Isa2:6) \+ 2 t. \+ [Pr 20:16](https://ref.ly/Prov20:16) Kt (\> Qr נָכְרִיָּה), \+ [27:13](https://ref.ly/Prov27:13) (so read for MT נָכְרִיָּה); fpl. נָכְרִיּוֺת [Gn 31:15](https://ref.ly/Gen31:15) \+;—**1\. a.** *foreign:* עַם נָכְרִי [Ex 21:8](https://ref.ly/Exod21:8) (E) *a foreign* (non\-Isr.) people, ´אִישׁ נ [Dt 17:15](https://ref.ly/Deut17:15) (‖לאֹ אָתִיְ); ´מַלְבּוּשׁ נ [Zp 1:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:8); אֶרֶץ נָכְרִיָּה [Ex 2:22](https://ref.ly/Exod2:22) (J) *foreign land*, so [18:3](https://ref.ly/Exod18:3) (E); esp. נָשִׁים נָכְרִיּוֺת *foreign* (non\-Isr.) *women* [1 K 11:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:18), [Ezr 10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:2), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:10), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:11), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:14), [10:17](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:17), [10:18](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:18), [10:44](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:44), [Ne 13:26](https://ref.ly/Neh13:26), [13:27](https://ref.ly/Neh13:27). **b.** as subst. נָכְרִי *foreigner* (non\-Isr.) Ju [19:12](https://ref.ly/Judg19:12) (\+ אֲשֶׁר לאֹ מִבְּנֵי יִשְרָאֵל), [1 K 8:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:41) (\+ אֲשֶׁר לאֹ מֵעַמְּ), \= [2 Ch 6:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:32) (\+ *id.*), [1 K 8:43](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:43) \= [2 Ch 6:33](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:33), [Dt 14:21](https://ref.ly/Deut14:21) (‖גֵּר), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Deut15:3), [23:21](https://ref.ly/Deut23:21) (opp. אָחִיְ), [29:21](https://ref.ly/Deut29:21) (\+ אֲשֶׁר יָבאֹ מֵאֶרֶץ רְחוֺקָה); as predicate, [2 S 15:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:19) (‖גֹּלֶה), נָכְרִיָּה [Ru 2:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:10); pl. נָכְרִים *foreigners* [La 5:2](https://ref.ly/Lam5:2) (זָרִים), Ob:[11](https://ref.ly/Obad1:11) (‖ *id.*); cf. יַלְדֵי נָכְרִים [Is 2:6](https://ref.ly/Isa2:6); fpl. *alien women* [Gn 31:15](https://ref.ly/Gen31:15) (E; i.e. not of one's father's family). **2\.** נָכְרִיָּה *foreign woman*, as term. techn., in Pr, for *harlot* (perh. because harlots were orig. chiefly foreigners): [Pr 2:16](https://ref.ly/Prov2:16) (‖אִשָּׁה זָרָה), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Prov7:5) (‖ *id.*), [5:20](https://ref.ly/Prov5:20) (‖זָרָה), [6:24](https://ref.ly/Prov6:24) (‖אֵשֶׁת רָע), [23:27](https://ref.ly/Prov23:27) (‖זוֺנָה).—On [20:16](https://ref.ly/Prov20:16), [27:13](https://ref.ly/Prov27:13) v. infr. **3\.** fig. *unknown, unfamiliar:* נָכְרִי הָיִיתִי בְּעֵינֵיהֶם [Jb 19:15](https://ref.ly/Job19:15) *an alien am I become in their eyes;* לִבְנֵי אִמִּי ´נ [Psalm 69:9](https://ref.ly/Ps69:9) (‖מוּזָר הָיִיתִי לְאֶחָי); ´אִישׁ נ [Ec 6:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:2); of ´י's judgment, נָכְרִיָּה עֲבֹדָתוֺ [Is 28:21](https://ref.ly/Isa28:21) *strange is his task!* (‖זָר מַעֲשֵהוּ); גֶּפֶן נָכְרִיָּה [Je 2:21](https://ref.ly/Jer2:21) *an alien vine* (opp. זֶרַע אֱמֶת), fig. of degenerate Israel; as subst., נָכְרִים [Pr 20:16](https://ref.ly/Prov20:16) Kt (\> Qr נָכְרִיָּה), *alens*, persons unknown to him (‖זָר), so read also (for MT נָכְרִיָּה) ‖ [27:13](https://ref.ly/Prov27:13).

> \[Str5234] † \[נכר] **vb.denom.** act or treat as foreign, or strange; disguise, misconstrue;—**Niph.** *Impf.* יִנָּכֵר [Pr 26:24](https://ref.ly/Prov26:24) with his lips a hater *disguiseth himself* (dissembles, speaks what is *foreign* to his thought). **Pi.** *Pf.* נִכַּר [1 S 23:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:7), but v. infr.; *Impf.* וַיְנַכְּרוּ [Je 19:4](https://ref.ly/Jer19:4) *and they have treated* this place *as foreign* (profane); יְנַכְּרוּ צָרֵהוּ [Dt 32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27) lest *their adversaries should misconstrue* (it).—בְּיָדִי ´נִכַּר אֹתוֺ א [1 S 23:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:7) *God hath alienated him into my hand*, but improbable; LXX מָכַר (cf. Ju [4:9](https://ref.ly/Judg4:9)) *hath sold him*, so Th Klo HPS (cf. We); \< סִכַּר (as [Is 19:4](https://ref.ly/Isa19:4)) Krochm Dr; Kit either of these; \> סִגַּר Bu; Löhr either סִגַּר or סִכַּר. **Hithp.** *Impf.* וַיִּתְנַכֵּר אֲלֵיהֶם [Gn 42:7](https://ref.ly/Gen42:7) *he acted as a stranger toward them; Pt.* f. מִתְנַכֵּרָה [1 K 14:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:5), [14:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:6) *feign to be a stranger\-woman*.

> \[Str5238] †\[נְכֹת] **n. \[f.]** treasure (so context demands) (LXXL [2 K 20:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:13) τῆς ὑπάρξεως, cf. SyrVer Targum; der. uncertain; DlProl. 141 cp. As. *bit nakamti (nakanti), house of treasure, √ nakâmu, heap up* \[DlHWB 462], whence ´נ ´ב perh. borrowed; this favoured by NöZMG xl (1886\), 731 HptZA ii. 256 (reading *nakavâti \= nakamâti*, and Heb. נִכְוֺתָיו ´ב or נְכוֺתָו ´ב)).—Only sf. בֵּית נְכֹתֹה \= *his treasure\-house* [2 K 20:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:13) \= [Is 39:2](https://ref.ly/Isa39:2).

## נלה (id: BDB06145)

\[Str5239] נלה doubtful √ (Ar. نيل, نَالَ is *obtain, attain*, نَيْلٌ *what one obtains* by another's bounty, Lane3040);—for supposed **Hiph.** *Inf.* sf. כַּנְּלֽתְ [Is 33:1](https://ref.ly/Isa33:1) \= כְּהַנְלֽתְ (Ki GesLgb. 87 Köi. 574\), read כְּכַלֹּתְ (**Pi.** Inf. of כלה; so Ra Capp GesThes Ew Kn Che Brd Di Du), cf. ‖כַּהֲתִֽמְ.

> \[Str4512] † \[מִנְלֶה] **n. \[m.]** ? gain acquisition ?—Only sf. לאֹ יִטֶּה לָאָרֶץ מִנְלָם [Jb 15:29](https://ref.ly/Job15:29) (si vera l.) *their acquisition*, but very dub.; Di (formerly) שִׁבֳּלִים, Hi מְלִלִים *ears*, cf. Bu; other conj. v. in Di; Du thinks hopelessly corrupt.—LXX σκιάν \[i.e. צִלָּם], SyrVer *their word* \[מִלָּתָם].

> \[Str5240] נְמִבְזָה [1 S 15:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:9) v. בזה **Niph.** p. 102

> \[Str5241] † נְמוּאֵל **n.pr.m.** (cf. GrayProp. N. 307\);—**1\.** son of Simeon [Nu 26:12](https://ref.ly/Num26:12), [1 Ch 4:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:24) (\= יְמוּאֵל, q. v., [Gn 46:10](https://ref.ly/Gen46:10), [Ex 6:15](https://ref.ly/Exod6:15)), LXX Ναμουηλ. **2\.** a Reubenite (brother of Dathan and Abiram) [Nu 26:9](https://ref.ly/Num26:9), LXX *id.*

> \[Str5242] † נְמוּאֵלִי **adj.gent.** of **1** supr., only c. art. ´הַגּ as n. coll. [Nu 26:12](https://ref.ly/Num26:12), LXX Ναμουηλει.

## נמר (id: BDB06152)

נמר (√ of foll., meaning dub.; poss. cp. As. *namâru, shine, gleam* (fr. glossy coat of panther and leopard), \[v. Ar. نَمِرٌ *pardus*, and *limpidus, purus* (of water; cf. infr. נמרים)] so HomNS 294 f.; ag. this NöZMG xl (1886\), 736 SchwallyIdiot. 121; נָמֵר is comm. Sem.: As. *nimru* DlHWB 468; Ar. ܢܡܪܢ, نَمِرٌ Lane2853; Eth. ንምር፧ Syr. ܢܶܡܪܳܐ, Targum נִמְרָא; NH נָמֵר).

> \[Str5247] † נִמְרָה **n.pr.loc.** place E. of Jordan [Nu 32:3](https://ref.ly/Num32:3) (\+ עֲטָרוֺת, דִּיבֹן, חֶשְׁבּוֺן, etc.), LXX Ναμβρα, A Αμβραμ, LXXL Μαμβραν. Perh. \= *Tel Nimrîn*, c. 6m. E. of Jordan, nearly opp. Jericho, cf. BuhlGeogr. 264\.—Cf. ´בֵּית נ (q.v.), also in NH (cf. נמרה n.pr.m. Sinait. Inscr. SACook81 Lzb323; meaning dub.; NöZMG xxix (1875\), 437 thinks from *spotted* or *striped* appearance of ground; *place of leopard* also GrayProp. Names 92 (on tribal names from leopard v. RSK 201 also Sab n.pr. אנמרם Levy\-Osl. c., etc.); WeHeid:2, 82 thinks of *clear water* \[cf. foll.]).

> \[Str5249] † נִמְרִים **n.pr.loc.** in Moab (cf. Sab. נמרן attrib. of Barḳm, DHMZMG xxix (1875\), 592, v. also CISiv. No. 103, p. 169; مَآءٌ نَمِيرٌ *wholesome water* Lane2853, also Wel. c.);—only מֵי נִמְרִים [Is 15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6), [Je 48:34](https://ref.ly/Jer48:34); prob. \= Βηνναμαρειμ LagOnom. 284:33, in *Wady Numêre* at SE. end of Dead Sea, BuhlGeogr. 272\.

> \[Str5248] † נִמְרֹד, נִמְרוֺד **n.pr.m** Nimrod (etym. and meaning wholly unknown; Thes (dub.) sub מָרַד *rebel* (of which Hebr. may have thought \[cf. LagBN 105]); in fact prob. Bab. name; **1\.** \= a god e.g. Marduk, WeComp. Hex. (2\), 308 f.; Nimrod, Encycl. Brit. (9\). xvii. 511, RS Sem. i. 91 n.; 2d ed. 92; HomPSBA xv (1893\), 291–300 prop. *Narûdu* \= *\*Namra\-uddu*, a star\-god. **2\.** \< name of Bab. king or prince: *Nu\-marad* \= ‘Man of Marad’ cf. DlPa 220 De:[Gn 10:8](https://ref.ly/Gen10:8) \[1887]; more plausibly \= *Nazi\-maraddash* (*marattash, murudas*), HptAndover Rev. July, 1884, 93 f. DlK (1884\) SayAthen. Feb. 16, 1895, Acad. > \[Str5250] † נִמְשִׁי **n.pr.m.** grandfather of Jehu [2 K 9:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:2), [9:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:14); ´יֵהוּא בֶן־נ [1 K 19:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:16), [2 K 9:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:20), [2 Ch 22:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:7); LXX Ναμεσθει, Ναμες(ς)ει.

## נָסָה (id: BDB06160)

\[Str5254] † \[נָסָה] **vb. Pi.** test, try (NH *id.,* Pi.; Targum Pa. נַסִּי; Syr. Pa. ܢܣܝ; cf. Eth. መንሰት፡ *temptation*);—**Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִסָּה [Dt 4:34](https://ref.ly/Deut4:34) \+ 3 t.; sf. נִסָּהוּ [Ex 15:25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25); 3 fs. נִסְּתָה [Dt 28:56](https://ref.ly/Deut28:56); 1 s. נִסִּיתִי [Ec 7:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:23), \+ 4 t. Pf.; *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יְנַסֵּם [Dn 1:14](https://ref.ly/Dan1:14); 1 s. אֲנַסֶּה Ju [6:39](https://ref.ly/Judg6:39); sf. אֲנַסְּכָה [Ec 2:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:2); 3 mpl. יְנַסּוּ [Nu 14:22](https://ref.ly/Num14:22); 2 mpl. תְּנַסָּוּן [Ex 17:2](https://ref.ly/Exod17:2), \+ 7 t. Impf.; *Imv.* נַס [Dn 1:12](https://ref.ly/Dan1:12); sf. נַסֵּנִי [Psalm 26:2](https://ref.ly/Ps26:2); *Inf. cstr.* נַסּוֺת [Ex 20:20](https://ref.ly/Exod20:20), \+ 8 t. Inf.; *Pt.* מְנַסֶּה [Dt 13:4](https://ref.ly/Deut13:4); —**1\.** *test, try* (syn. בחן), abs. [1 S 17:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:39) (a sword); c. בְּ Ju 7:39, [Ec 2:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:1); c. acc. [Dn 1:12](https://ref.ly/Dan1:12), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Dan1:14); acc. \+ בְּ [1 K 10:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:1) \= [2 Ch 9:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:1), [Ec 7:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:23). **2\.** *attempt, assay, try* to do a thing, c. Inf. [Dt 4:34](https://ref.ly/Deut4:34), [28:56](https://ref.ly/Deut28:56); c. acc. [Jb 4:2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2) (*venture* a word). **3\.** *test, try, prove, tempt* \[but not in modern sense of the word: v. Dr:[Dt 6:16](https://ref.ly/Deut6:16); PsalmPsalm 453, 483] **a.** God *tests* or *proves* Abr. [Gn 22:1](https://ref.ly/Gen22:1) (E), Isr. [Ex 15:25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25), [20:20](https://ref.ly/Exod20:20) (E), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Exod16:4) (J), [Dt 8:2](https://ref.ly/Deut8:2), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Deut8:16), [13:4](https://ref.ly/Deut13:4); c. בְּ Ju [2:22](https://ref.ly/Judg2:22), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Judg3:1), [3:4](https://ref.ly/Judg3:4); tribe of Levi [Dt 33:8](https://ref.ly/Deut33:8) (poem); Hezekiah [2 Ch 32:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:31); psalmist [Psalm 26:2](https://ref.ly/Ps26:2). **b.** Isr. *tests*, or *tries* God: [Ex 17:2](https://ref.ly/Exod17:2), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Exod17:7), [Nu 14:22](https://ref.ly/Num14:22) (J), [Dt 6:16](https://ref.ly/Deut6:16), [Psalm 78:18](https://ref.ly/Ps78:18), [78:41](https://ref.ly/Ps78:41), [78:56](https://ref.ly/Ps78:56), [95:9](https://ref.ly/Ps95:9), [106:14](https://ref.ly/Ps106:14); so Ahaz, [Is 7:12](https://ref.ly/Isa7:12).

> \[Str4531] †II. מַסָּה **n.f.** test, trial, proving;—´מ [Psalm 95:8](https://ref.ly/Ps95:8); cstr. מַסַּת [Jb 9:23](https://ref.ly/Job9:23); pl. מַסֹּת [Dt 4:34](https://ref.ly/Deut4:34), [7:19](https://ref.ly/Deut7:19), מַסּוֺת [Dt 29:2](https://ref.ly/Deut29:2); —*testings*or *provings* of Pharaoh and Egyptians at Exodus, [Dt 4:34](https://ref.ly/Deut4:34) (v. Dr), [7:19](https://ref.ly/Deut7:19), [29:2](https://ref.ly/Deut29:2) (‖אֹתֹת);—*test, trial,* of innocent person [Jb 9:23](https://ref.ly/Job9:23) acc. to Hi al., but v. I. מַסָּה sub מסס.
> 
> 650:b. III. מַסָּה: v. GallKultstätten 32 interprets as *place of trial* (ancient judgment\-place) and identif. with Ḳadesh, called also מְרִיבָה (מֵי) and עֵין מִשְׁפָּט.

> \[Str4532] †III. מַסָּה **n.pr.loc** where Israel tried ´י in the wilderness, [Ex 17:7](https://ref.ly/Exod17:7) (JE), [Dt 6:16](https://ref.ly/Deut6:16), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Deut9:22), [Psalm 95:8](https://ref.ly/Ps95:8) (all LXX (ὁ)πειρασμός), where Levi was tested [Dt 33:8](https://ref.ly/Deut33:8) (LXX πείρᾳ).
