## נָסַח (id: BDB06163)

\[Str5255] † \[נָסַח] **vb.** pull or tear away (NH נָסַח, Targum נְסַח both *remove;* As. *nasâḫu* \= BH, so OAram. נסח Cook82 Lzb323; Ar. نَسَخَ *annul, supersede, change by substitution, copy;* cf. As. *nisḫu, nusḫu, extract, excerpt* DlHWB 472 MeissnZA iv (1889\), 267; NH נָסְחָה, Nab. נסחת *id.,* also Aram. נוסחא (whence Ar. نُسْخَةٌ) Hoffm: ZMG xxxii. 760 cf. Frä251; Syr. ܢܣܰܚ *copy*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. ´בְּית גֵּאִים יִסַּח י [Pr 15:25](https://ref.ly/Prov15:25) *the house of proud men will* ´י *tear down;* וְיִסָּֽחְֲ מֵאֹהֶל [Psalm 52:7](https://ref.ly/Ps52:7) *yea, he* \[´י] *shall tear thee away, without a tent;* 3 mpl. יִסְּחוּ מִן [Pr 2:22](https://ref.ly/Prov2:22) transgressors *shall* (men) *tear away from* the land (rd. perh. יִנָּֽסְחוּ *shall be torn away;* ‖יִכָּרֵָתוּ; cf. Ges§ 144, 3b. R DaSynt. § 108\. b). **Niph.** *Pf.* 2 mpl. consec. וְנִסַּחְתֶּם מֵעַל הָאֲדָמָה [Dt 28:63](https://ref.ly/Deut28:63) *and ye shall be torn away from off the land.*

> \[Str4535] מַסָּח [2 K 11:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:6), v. p. 587 a supr.

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 28:63; Proverbs 15:25; Psalms 52:7; 2 Kings 11:6; Proverbs 2:22


## I נָסַךְ (id: BDB06165)

\[Str5258] †I. נָסַךְ **vb.** pour out (NH *id., pour, cast* (metals, Pi. *make libation;* Ph. נסך *cast* gold or iron Lzb323 f.; Targum נְסַח *make libation;* Syr. نَسَكَ *pour out;* Ar. نَسَكَ, orig. *be\-pour, water* NöZMG xii (1887\), 719 (rare), usu. *worship* Lane3032 (cf. RS Sem. i. 213; 2d ed. 229\), i.e. *pour out* blood of victim, *pour out* wine WeHeid. 2, 114, 118, 142; —**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. נָסַךְ [Is 29:10](https://ref.ly/Isa29:10), [40:19](https://ref.ly/Isa40:19); נָסָךְ [44:10](https://ref.ly/Isa44:10); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִסְּכוּ [Ho 9:4](https://ref.ly/Hos9:4); 2 ms. תִּסְּכוּ [Ex 30:9](https://ref.ly/Exod30:9); *Inf.* לִנְסֹח [Is 30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1); *Pt. Pass.* f. נְסוּכָה [25:7](https://ref.ly/Isa25:7); —**1\.** *pour out*, c. עַל (the spirit of deep sleep) [Is 29:10](https://ref.ly/Isa29:10). **2\.** *pour out libations*, c. acc. נֶסֶךְ [Ex 30:9](https://ref.ly/Exod30:9) (P); מַסֵּכָה [Is 30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1); יַיִן [Ho 9:4](https://ref.ly/Hos9:4). **3\.** *cast metal images*, c. acc. פֶּסֶל [Is 40:19](https://ref.ly/Isa40:19), [44:10](https://ref.ly/Isa44:10).

 **Piel** *Impf.* יְנַסֵךְ [1 Ch 11:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:18) *pour out as libation*, c. acc. מַיִם \+ ליהוה (\= [2 S 23:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:16) Hiph.). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. הִסִּכוּ [Je 32:39](https://ref.ly/Jer32:39); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיַּסֵח [Gn 35:14](https://ref.ly/Gen35:14) \+ 2 t.; 1 s. אַסִּיח [Psalm 16:4](https://ref.ly/Ps16:4); 3 mpl. וַיַסִּיכוּ Ez [20:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:28); *Imv.* הַסֵח [Nu 28:7](https://ref.ly/Num28:7); *Inf. abs.* הַסֵּיח Je 44:l7; הַסֵח [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18) \+ 3 t.; *cstr.* לְהַסֵח [Je 44:19](https://ref.ly/Jer44:19), [44:25](https://ref.ly/Jer44:25) (Köi. 309\);—*pour out libations*, c. acc. נֶסֶךְ [Gn 35:14](https://ref.ly/Gen35:14) (E), [2 K 16:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:13), [Nu 28:7](https://ref.ly/Num28:7) (P), מַיִם 2 S 23:l6 ליהוה; elsewhere, נְסָכִים to other gods [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18), [19:13](https://ref.ly/Jer19:13), [32:29](https://ref.ly/Jer32:29), [44:17](https://ref.ly/Jer44:17), [44:18](https://ref.ly/Jer44:18), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Jer44:19), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Jer44:19), [44:25](https://ref.ly/Jer44:25), Ez [20:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:28), [Psalm 16:4](https://ref.ly/Ps16:4). **Hoph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יֻסַּךְ *be poured out*, [Ex 25:29](https://ref.ly/Exod25:29), [37:16](https://ref.ly/Exod37:16), of holy wine, c. בְ of vessels.

## II נָסַךְ (id: BDB06169)

\[Str5259] † II. \[נָסַךְ] **vb.** weave (Ar. نَسَجَ has same meaning);—**Qal** *Pt. pass.* f. נְסוּכָה [Is 25:7](https://ref.ly/Isa25:7); perh. also *Inf. cstr.* לִנְסֹח [30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1); on both v. foll.

> \[Str4541] † II. מַסֵּכָה **n. f.** woven stuff, web, esp. as covering;—abs. הַנְּסוּכָה עַ־ל ´הַמּ [Is 25:7](https://ref.ly/Isa25:7) *the web that is woven over* all the nations (i.e. *mourning\-veil*, symb. of distress; ‖הַלּוֺט הַלּוֺט); \= bed\- *covering* [28:20](https://ref.ly/Isa28:20) (in fig.). Perhaps also ´לִנְסֹךְ מ [30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1) *weave a web* (‖לַעֲשוֺת עֵצָה), ref. to negotiations with Egypt (so Vulgate Aq Ew De Che Di; \> LXX Ges Hi Kn Brd Du *pour a libation;* ´מ not elsewhere \= נֶסֶךְ).

> \[Str4545] † \[מַסֶּ֫כֶת] **n. f.** web of unfinished stuff, on loom (v. GFMPAOS, Oct. 1889, clxxvii);—only abs. מַסָּכֶת Ju [16:13](https://ref.ly/Judg16:13), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Judg16:14).—מָסָח, מְסֻכָה v. סכך.

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 16:13; Judges 16:14; Isaiah 25:7; Isaiah 28:20; Isaiah 30:1


## III נָסַךְ (id: BDB06172)

\[Str5258] † III. \[נָסַךְ] **vb.** set, install (As. *nasâku, id.,* whence *nasîku, prince*, DlHWB 472; v. also Muss\-ArnoltHbr. vi. 89\. R. 17 and reff.);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 S. נָסַכְתִּי מַלְכִּי עַל־צִיּוֺן [Psalm 2:6](https://ref.ly/Ps2:6) *I have installed my king upon Zion*. **Niph.** *Pf.* 1 S. נִסַּכְתִּי [Pr 8:23](https://ref.ly/Prov8:23) *I was installed* (of Wisdom).

> \[Str5257] † II. \[נָסִיךְ] **n. m.** prince;—pl. cstr. נְסִיכֵי [Jos 13:21](https://ref.ly/Josh13:21), Ez [32:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:30), [Mi 5:4](https://ref.ly/Mic5:4); sf. נְסִיכֵמוֺ [Psalm 83:12](https://ref.ly/Ps83:12).

## I נָסַס (id: BDB06175)

\[Str5263] † I. \[נָסַס] **vb.** be sick (?; cf. Syr. ܢܰܣ *id.*, and esp. ܢܰܣܺܝܣ *debilis, infirmus, aegrotans*, etc., PS2387; Targum נְסַס *be sad;* As. *nasâsu, wail, lament* DlHWB 472; Ar. نَسَّ is *go at a gentle pace*);—only **Qal** *Pt. act.* (si vera l.) כִּמְסֹס נֹסֵס [Is 10:18](https://ref.ly/Isa10:18) like the wasting away *of a sick man* (so Thes Ew De Che Di Du).

\[Str5264] II. נסס (√ of foll. \= *be high* or *conspicuous ?* **Hithpoïl.** *Inf.* לְהִתְנוֺסֵס [Psalm 60:6](https://ref.ly/Ps60:6), acc. to some denom. from נֵס *that it may be displayed*, AV, cf. De; \< v. נוס; *Pt.* מִתְנוֺסְסוֺת [Zc 9:16](https://ref.ly/Zech9:16) *raised ? prominent ?* (עַל־אַדְמָתוֺ; of precious stones, in fig.) very dub.; We Now del.; Gr מִתְנוֺצְצוֺת).

> \[Str5251,3071] † נֵס **n.\[m.]** standard, ensign, signal, sign (NH *id., flag*, usu. *sign*, i.e. *miracle;* Targum נֵס, נִסָּא *sign, miracle*);—´נ abs. [Nu 21:8](https://ref.ly/Num21:8) \+; cstr. [Is 11:10](https://ref.ly/Isa11:10); sf. נִסִּי [Ex 17:15](https://ref.ly/Exod17:15), [Is 49:22](https://ref.ly/Isa49:22); —**1\. a.** *standard*, as rallying\-point, נִסִּי ´י [Ex 17:15](https://ref.ly/Exod17:15) (E), ´י *my standard* (name of altar); conspicuously set on hill [Is 30:17](https://ref.ly/Isa30:17) (sim. of solitariness; ‖תֹּרֶן עַל־ראֹשׁ הָהָר); signal of war \[v. SchumacherZPV ix. 232, Across the Jordan, 104 f.] (esp. to the nations, for destruction or deliverance of Judah, Is Je), לַגּוֺיִם ´נָשָא נ [Is 5:26](https://ref.ly/Isa5:26), [11:12](https://ref.ly/Isa11:12) cf. [18:3](https://ref.ly/Isa18:3) (on mts., ‖תָּקַע שׁוֺפָר); אֶל־עַמִּים ´אָרִים נ [49:22](https://ref.ly/Isa49:22) (‖אֶשָּא אֶל־גּוֺיִם יָדִי),—in all these subj. ´י; cf. עֹמֵד לְנֵס עַמִּים [Is 10:11](https://ref.ly/Isa10:11) (of Mess. kg.); ´שְאוּ נ [13:2](https://ref.ly/Isa13:2) (on mt.), [Je 50:2](https://ref.ly/Jer50:2), [51:12](https://ref.ly/Jer51:12) (c. אֶ־ל *against*), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Jer51:27) (‖תִּקְעוּ שׁוֺפָר בַּגּוֺיִם), עַל־עַמִּים ´הָרִימוּ נ [Is 62:10](https://ref.ly/Isa62:10), cf. [Je 4:21](https://ref.ly/Jer4:21) (‖קוֺל שׁוֺפָר); לְהִתְנוֺסֵס ´נ…נָתַתָּה [Psalm 60:6](https://ref.ly/Ps60:6) *thou hast set a standard* (only) *for fleeing*. **b.** *signal*, צִיּ֫וֺנָה ´שְאוּ נ [Je 4:6](https://ref.ly/Jer4:6) (i.e. to direct refugees to Zion). **2\.** *standard*, as pole, supporting serpent of bronze [Nu 21:8](https://ref.ly/Num21:8), [21:9](https://ref.ly/Num21:9) (JE). **3\. a.** *ensign, signal*, מִמִּצְרַיִם הָיָה מִפְרָשֵׁח לִהְיוֺת לָח לְנֵס…שֵׁשׁ Ez [27:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:7) *byssus from Egypt was thy sail, to serve* *thee for an ensign* (so DaToy; on lack of pennon on ancient ships v. Co; \> del. Co Berthol); so **b.** (since sails were the only ensign) \= *sail* [Is 33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23) (Che Di al.; \> *pennon* Ges Hi Ew De Du). **4\.** *sign* (cf. NH) \= *warning* [Nu 26:10](https://ref.ly/Num26:10) (P).

## I נָסַע (id: BDB06178)

\[Str5265] I. נָסַע:147 **vb.** pull out or up, set out, journey (NH *id., move, march;* As. *nisû, set out, withdraw*, etc. DlHWB 470; NöZMG x1(1886\), 723 cp. also Ar. نَزَعَ, Eth. ነዝዐ፡ \[rare] *pull up, away*, so Baes 51\);—**Qal**:137 *Pf.* 3 ms. ´נ [Gn 33:17](https://ref.ly/Gen33:17) \+, 3 mpl. נָֽסְעוּ [Dt 10:6](https://ref.ly/Deut10:6), נָסָעוּ [Nu 2:34](https://ref.ly/Num2:34) \+, etc.; *Impf.* יִסַּע [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20), וַיִּסַּע [Gn 12:9](https://ref.ly/Gen12:9) \+, sf. וַיִּסָּעֵם Ju [16:3](https://ref.ly/Judg16:3); 3 mpl. יִסְעוּ [Ex 40:36](https://ref.ly/Exod40:36) \+, יִסָּעוּ [Nu 2:9](https://ref.ly/Num2:9) \+; 2 mpl. תִּסְעוּ [Jos 3:3](https://ref.ly/Josh3:3); 1 pl. נִסְעָה cohort. [Gn 33:12](https://ref.ly/Gen33:12), וַנִּסַּע [Dt 1:19](https://ref.ly/Deut1:19), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Deut2:1), וַנִּסְעָה [Ezr 8:31](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:31), etc.; *Imv.* סְעוּ [Dt 2:24](https://ref.ly/Deut2:24) \+; *Inf. abs.* [Gn 12:9](https://ref.ly/Gen12:9); *cstr.* נְסֹעַ [Jos 3:14](https://ref.ly/Josh3:14) \+; sf. נָסְעָם [Gn 11:2](https://ref.ly/Gen11:2), [Nu 10:34](https://ref.ly/Num10:34); *Pt.* נֹסֵעַ [Ex 14:10](https://ref.ly/Exod14:10), [Nu 10:33](https://ref.ly/Num10:33); pl. נֹסְעִים [Nu 10:29](https://ref.ly/Num10:29); —**1\.** *pull out* or *up*, city\-gate and gate\-posts Ju [16:3](https://ref.ly/Judg16:3); loom v:[14](https://ref.ly/Judg16:14) (on text cf. GFM), tent\-pegs [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20) (fig. of Jerusalem; cf. also **Niph.**). **2\.** hence (from pulling up tent\-pegs), **a.** *set out* [Gn 35:5](https://ref.ly/Gen35:5), [Nu 12:15](https://ref.ly/Num12:15) (both E), [Je 4:7](https://ref.ly/Jer4:7) (‖יָצָא מִמְּקוֺמוֺ); even of lifeless things, e.g. the ark, הָאָרוֺן [Nu 10:35](https://ref.ly/Num10:35) (JE); oft. in P: [Ex 14:15](https://ref.ly/Exod14:15), [40:37](https://ref.ly/Exod40:37), [Nu 1:51](https://ref.ly/Num1:51), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Num4:5), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Num4:15), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Num9:17) \+ 8 t. Nu 9, [10:5](https://ref.ly/Num10:5), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Num10:6), [10:17](https://ref.ly/Num10:17), [10:18](https://ref.ly/Num10:18), [10:21](https://ref.ly/Num10:21), [10:22](https://ref.ly/Num10:22), [10:25](https://ref.ly/Num10:25), so prob. also [2:8](https://ref.ly/Num2:8) \+ 6 t. Nu 2 (v. infr.; even of lifeless things, דֶּגֶל [10:18](https://ref.ly/Num10:18), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Num10:12), מִשְׁכָּן [1:51](https://ref.ly/Num1:51), אֹהֶל מוֺעֵד [2:17](https://ref.ly/Num2:17)), [33:3](https://ref.ly/Num33:3); וַיָּבֹא…וַיִּסַּע [Gn 46:1](https://ref.ly/Gen46:1) (E) *and* Isr. *set out, and came*, etc., [Ex 16:1](https://ref.ly/Exod16:1), [19:2](https://ref.ly/Exod19:2), [Nu 20:22](https://ref.ly/Num20:22), [Jos 9:17](https://ref.ly/Josh9:17) (all P), [Dt 1:7](https://ref.ly/Deut1:7), נִסְעָה וְנֵלֵָכָה [33:12](https://ref.ly/Deut33:12) (J); (וַיַּעֲלוּ…) וַיִּסְעוּ Ju [18:11](https://ref.ly/Judg18:11), סְעוּ וְעִבְרוּ [Dt 2:24](https://ref.ly/Deut2:24); ´נ oft. c. מִן [Gn 35:16](https://ref.ly/Gen35:16) (E), [Nu 10:34](https://ref.ly/Num10:34) (JE), מֵאָהֳלֵיהֶם לַעֲבֹר ´נ [Jos 3:14](https://ref.ly/Josh3:14) (JE), *set out from their tents, to cross*, etc.; cf. ´נ לָלֶכֶת…מִן [Ezr 8:31](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:31); c. מִן \+ ויבא, [Jos 3:1](https://ref.ly/Josh3:1) (JE), [Nu 33:9](https://ref.ly/Num33:9) (P), \+ וַיֵּלֶח v:[3](https://ref.ly/Num33:3) (D), [Dt 1:19](https://ref.ly/Deut1:19), \+ וַיַּעַבְרוּ [Nu 33:8](https://ref.ly/Num33:8) (P); \+ וַיָּשָׁב \+ וַיַּחֲ֔נוּ [Nu 33:7](https://ref.ly/Num33:7) (P). **b.** *depart*, c. מִן [Gn 37:17](https://ref.ly/Gen37:17) (J), [2 K 3:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:27), [19:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:8), [19:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:36) \= [Is 37:8](https://ref.ly/Isa37:8), [37:37](https://ref.ly/Isa37:37); even of עַמּוּד הֶעָנָן [Ex 14:19](https://ref.ly/Exod14:19) (J); of angel v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod14:19) (E; \+ וַיֵּלֶח). **3\.** *journey, march* (by stages) [Gn 12:9](https://ref.ly/Gen12:9), [35:21](https://ref.ly/Gen35:21) (both J), [Nu 10:13](https://ref.ly/Num10:13), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Num10:14), [10:28](https://ref.ly/Num10:28) (P), (poss. also [Nu 2:9](https://ref.ly/Num2:9) etc., cf. **2** supr.); [Ex 40:36](https://ref.ly/Exod40:36) (all P); sq. ה loc. [Gn 12:9](https://ref.ly/Gen12:9), [33:17](https://ref.ly/Gen33:17) (both J), [Dt 1:40](https://ref.ly/Deut1:40) [2:1](https://ref.ly/Deut2:1); סְעוּ לָכֶם הַמִּדְבָּר [Nu 14:25](https://ref.ly/Num14:25) (JE); sq. מִקֶּדֶם *eastward* (v. מִן **1\. c**) [Gn 11:2](https://ref.ly/Gen11:2), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Gen13:11) (both J), אַחֲרֵי [Ex 14:10](https://ref.ly/Exod14:10) (J), אֶ־ל [Nu 10:29](https://ref.ly/Num10:29), לִפְנֵי v:[33](https://ref.ly/Num10:33) (of ark; all J); sq. מִן \+ ה loc. [Gn 20:1](https://ref.ly/Gen20:1) (E), [Dt 10:6](https://ref.ly/Deut10:6), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Deut10:7), [Ex 12:37](https://ref.ly/Exod12:37) (P), \+ acc. loc. [Nu 11:35](https://ref.ly/Num11:35) (JE); sq. מִן \+ acc. of distance, דֶּרֶח [10:33](https://ref.ly/Num10:33) (JE), [21:4](https://ref.ly/Num21:4) (P); esp. וַיַּחֲנוּ…וַיִּסְעוּ מִן [Nu 21:12](https://ref.ly/Num21:12), [21:13](https://ref.ly/Num21:13) (JE), [Ex 13:20](https://ref.ly/Exod13:20), [Nu 21:11](https://ref.ly/Num21:11), [33:5](https://ref.ly/Num33:5) \+ 37 t. Nu 33 (all P); of shepherds [Je 31:24](https://ref.ly/Jer31:24).—נָֽסְעוּ כְמוֺ צאֹן [Zc 10:2](https://ref.ly/Zech10:2) is dub.; We נָעוּ or יָנֻעוּ, so Now. **4\.** of wind, ´רוּחַ מֵאֵת י ´נ [Nu 11:31](https://ref.ly/Num11:31) *there set forth* (sprang up) a wind from ´י. † **Niph.** *Pf. be pulled up, removed:* 3 ms. דּוֺרִי נִסַּע וְנִגְלָה מִנִּי כְּאֹהֶל רֹעִי [Is 38:12](https://ref.ly/Isa38:12); of tent\-peg הֲלאֹ נִסַּע [Jb 4:21](https://ref.ly/Job4:21) (reading יְתֵדָם for יִתְרָם, v. II. יֶתֶר). † **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יַסַּע [Psalm 78:26](https://ref.ly/Ps78:26), וַיַּסַּע [Ex 15:22](https://ref.ly/Exod15:22) \+ 2 t.; 2 ms. תַּסִּיעַ [Psalm 80:9](https://ref.ly/Ps80:9), 3 fs. תַּסִּיעִי [2 K 4:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:4); 3 mpl. וַּיַּסִּ֫עוּ [1 K 5:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:31); *Pt.* מַסִּיעַ [Ec 10:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:9); —**1\.** *cause to set out, lead out*, c. acc. Pers. [Ex 15:22](https://ref.ly/Exod15:22) (J; \+ מִן, וַיֵּצְאוּ), [Psalm 78:52](https://ref.ly/Ps78:52) (כַּצּאֹן; ‖וַיְנַהֲגֵם), [80:9](https://ref.ly/Ps80:9) (מִן; fig. of vine); fig. of a wind, *cause to spring up*, יַסַּע קָדִים [Psalm 78:26](https://ref.ly/Ps78:26) (‖וַיְנַהֵג; cf. **Qal 4**). **2\.** *remove*, c. acc. rei [2 K 4:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:4); וַיַּסַּע כָּעֵץ תִּקְוָתִי [Jb 19:10](https://ref.ly/Job19:10); specif., *remove* from quarry, *quarry:* [1 K 5:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:31) *and they quarried* great stones, cf. [Ec 10:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:9).

> \[Str4550] † מַסַּע **n. \[m.]** pulling up, breaking camp, setting out, journey;—abs. ´מ [Nu 10:2](https://ref.ly/Num10:2), [Dt 10:11](https://ref.ly/Deut10:11); pl. cstr. מַסְעֵי [Nu 10:28](https://ref.ly/Num10:28), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Num33:1); sf. מַסָּעָיו [Gn 13:3](https://ref.ly/Gen13:3), מַסְעֵיהֶם [Ex 17:1](https://ref.ly/Exod17:1) \+ 6 t.;—**1\. a.** *pulling up, breaking* camp (c. acc. מַחֲנֶה; Ges§ 45 e, 115 d) [Nu 10:2](https://ref.ly/Num10:2). **b.** *setting out* [Nu 10:6](https://ref.ly/Num10:6), cf. v:[23](https://ref.ly/Num10:23). **2\.** *station, stage, journey* (by stages), ´וַיֵּלֶךְ לְמ [Gn 13:3](https://ref.ly/Gen13:3) *and he went by* (acc. to) *his journeyings* (cf. הלך, p. 231 a supr.); לְמַסְעֵיהֶם [Ex 17:1](https://ref.ly/Exod17:1) cf. [40:36](https://ref.ly/Exod40:36); [40:38](https://ref.ly/Exod40:38), [Nu 10:12](https://ref.ly/Num10:12) (all P; all c. נָסַע), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Num33:1), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Num33:2) (‖מוֺצָאֵיהֶם), v:[2](https://ref.ly/Num33:2) (‖ *id.*); קוּם לֵךְ לְמַסַּע [Dt 10:11](https://ref.ly/Deut10:11) *for journeying* (v. Dr).

> \[Str4551] † I. מַסֶּע **n.\[m.]** quarry or quarrying;—only abs. as adv. acc., אֶבֶן שְׁלֵמָה מַסָּע [1 K 6:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:7) *stone perfect* (at the) *quarry* (or *at quarrying*, i.e. when it was quarried; cf. Benz; text dub.).

II. נסע (√ of foll.; poss. Ar. نَسَغَβ *throw* (*puncture, wound*, etc.), Kam Frey, so Buhl13).

## נסק (id: BDB06183)

\[Str5266] נסק whence אֶסַּק [Psalm 139:8](https://ref.ly/Ps139:8) acc. to Thes al.; but v. סלק

> \[Str5268] † נִסְרֹךְ **n. pr. div.** Assyr. god, worshipped by Sennach., [2 K 19:37](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:37) \= [Is 37:38](https://ref.ly/Isa37:38); LXX Εσδραχ, A Εσθραχ, LXXL Ασραχ(Κ); LXX Νασαραχ, א Ασαρακ, A Ασαραχ (Is); JosAnt. x. 1, 5 Αρασκη; no such god in cuneif. inscr.; see views in MeinhJesaiaerzählungen (1898\) ad loc.; crpt. form of *Nusku* (\= נסוך) acc. to HalJAs. xiii (1879\), 387 \= Mêl. de. Crit. 177 Muss\-ArnoltJBL xi.1 (1892\), 86; Hbr. vii. 89 R. 17; *Nusku* is a solar diety, cf. JastrRel. Bab. 220 f.

> \[Str5269–70] נֵעָה, נֹעָה v. נוע

> \[Str5272] † נְעִיאֵל **n. pr. loc.** on border of Asher [Jos 19:27](https://ref.ly/Josh19:27); LXX Ιναηλ, A Ανιηλ, LXXL Ναειηλ.

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Kings 19:37; Joshua 19:27; Isaiah 37:38; Psalms 139:8

## נָעַל (id: BDB06187)

\[Str5274] † נָעַל **vb.** bar, bolt, lock (NH *id.*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. נָעַל [2 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:18), נָעָל Ju [3:23](https://ref.ly/Judg3:23); *Imv.* נְעֹל [2 S 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:17); *Pt. pass.* נָעוּל Ct [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12); fpl. נְעֻלוֺת Ju [3:24](https://ref.ly/Judg3:24); —*bar, bolt, lock*, usu. c. acc. דֶּלֶת, דְּלָתוֺת: Ju [3:23](https://ref.ly/Judg3:23), \+ אַחֲרֵי *after, behind*, v:[24](https://ref.ly/Judg3:24), [2 S 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:17), [13:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:18); elsewh. only גַּן נָעוּל Ct [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12) *a garden barred* (fig. of Shulamite), so also v:[12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12) b (reading גַּן for גַּל, Vrss Gr Bu).

## II נָעַר (id: BDB06210)

\[Str5287] † II. \[נָעַר] **vb.** shake, shake out or off (NH נָעַר *shake, stir up*, esp. Pi. Niph.; Targum נְעֹרֶת *tow* (v. infr.); Ar. نَغِرَ, نَغَرَ *boil, be in violent commotion, be very angry*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 S. נָעַ֫רְתִּי [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13); *Pt. act.* נֹעֵר [Is 33:9](https://ref.ly/Isa33:9), [33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15); *pass.* [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13); —*shake out*, to shew emptiness, ´חָצְנִי נ [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13) (symbol.), כַּפָּיו מִתְּמֹח בַּשֹּׁחַד ´נ [Is 33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15); pt. *shaken out, emptied* וָרֵק ´נ [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13) (i.e. stripped of possessions); *shake off* (leaves) [Is 33:9](https://ref.ly/Isa33:9) (cf. ‖ cl.). **Niph.** *Pf.* 1 S. נַנְעַ֫רְתִּי כָאַרְבֶּה [Psalm 109:23](https://ref.ly/Ps109:23) *I am shaken out like a locust* (fr. fold of a garment; sim. of perishing helplessly); cf. *Impf.* 3 mpl. וְיִנָּֽעֲרוּ רְשָׁעִים מִמֶָּנָּה [Jb 38:13](https://ref.ly/Job38:13) to seize the corners of the earth *that wicked men may be shaken out of it;* 1 S. אִנָּעֵר Ju [16:20](https://ref.ly/Judg16:20) *I will shake myself* (free ? cf. Hithp. and GFM). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. juss. יְנַעֵר [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13) *so may* God *shake utterly out* every man from (מִן) his house, etc.; וַיְנַעֵר [Ex 14:27](https://ref.ly/Exod14:27) *and* ´י *shook off* the Egypt. into (ב) the sea, \= *Pf.* נִעֵר [Psalm 136:15](https://ref.ly/Ps136:15) (also c. acc. \+ ב). **Hithp.** *Imv.* fs. הִתְנַעֲרִי מֵעָפָר [Is 52:2](https://ref.ly/Isa52:2) *shake thyself* (*free*), etc. (of personif. Zion).

> \[Str5288] † I. נַ֫עַר **n.\[m.]** usu. interpr. as a shaking scattering, abst. for concr. \= *scattered ones*, only [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16), but text corrupt, v. We Now.

> \[Str5296] † נְעֹ֫רֶת **n. f.** tow (as *shaken off* from the flax when beaten; on form cf. BaNB 64\);—in sim. ´פְּתִיל־הַנּ Ju [16:9](https://ref.ly/Judg16:9) *a thread of tow* (snapping from heat); ´נ [Is 1:31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:31) (fig.; as inflammable).

## III נער (id: BDB06213)

III. נער (√ of foll.; meaning unknown).

> \[Str5289] II. נַ֫עַר:239 **n. m. 1\.** boy, lad, youth. **2\.** retainer (not in P) (NH as BH; Ph. נער Lzb324);—´נ abs. [Gn 37:2](https://ref.ly/Gen37:2) \+; cstr. [1 S 2:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:13) \+; sf. נַעֲרוֺ Ju [19:3](https://ref.ly/Judg19:3) \+, נַעֲרָהּ [2 K 4:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:24); pl. נְעָרִים [1 S 25:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:5) \+, cstr. נַעֲרֵי [Ex 24:5](https://ref.ly/Exod24:5) \+; sf. נְעָרַי [Ne 4:10](https://ref.ly/Neh4:10), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Neh5:10), etc.;—**1\.** *boy, lad, youth* (c. 133 t.) **a.** of infant [Ex 2:6](https://ref.ly/Exod2:6) (E; 3 months old), to be born Ju [13:5](https://ref.ly/Judg13:5), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Judg13:7), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Judg13:8), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Judg13:12), just born [1 S 4:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:21), not weaned [1 S 1:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:22); also [Is 8:4](https://ref.ly/Isa8:4), cf. [7:16](https://ref.ly/Isa7:16) \+. **b.** of lad just weaned [1 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:24) (del. 2nd נער, cf. further Dr Bu HPS), v:[25](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:25); v:[27](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:27), etc. **c.** \= youth: of young Ishmael [Gn 21:12](https://ref.ly/Gen21:12) f. (E), Isaac [22:5](https://ref.ly/Gen22:5), [22:12](https://ref.ly/Gen22:12) (E), Joseph [Gn 37:2](https://ref.ly/Gen37:2) (E; 17 years old), Benj. [43:8](https://ref.ly/Gen43:8), [44:22](https://ref.ly/Gen44:22) f. (J), sons of Samuel 1 S 2:l7, of Jesse [1 S 16:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:11); קָטֹן ´נ *little lad* [1 S 20:35](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:35), [1 K 3:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:7), [2 K 5:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:14), [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6), קָטָן ´נ [1 K 11:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:17), pl. [2 K 2:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:23). **d.** ´נ with special stress on youthfulness Ju [8:20](https://ref.ly/Judg8:20), [1 S 17:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:33), [17:42](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:42), [Ho 11:1](https://ref.ly/Hos11:1), [Is 3:4](https://ref.ly/Isa3:4) cf. [Ec 10:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:16), [Is 3:5](https://ref.ly/Isa3:5), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Isa10:19), [Je 1:6](https://ref.ly/Jer1:6), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Jer1:7), [1 Ch 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:5), [29:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:1), [2 Ch 13:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:7), [34:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:3); so in phr. וְעַד זָקֵן ´מִנּ [Gn 19:4](https://ref.ly/Gen19:4) (J), [Jos 6:21](https://ref.ly/Josh6:21) (J), [Est 3:13](https://ref.ly/Esth3:13) cf. [Ex 10:9](https://ref.ly/Exod10:9) (E), [Dt 28:50](https://ref.ly/Deut28:50), [Is 20:4](https://ref.ly/Isa20:4), [65:20](https://ref.ly/Isa65:20), [Je 51:22](https://ref.ly/Jer51:22), [La 2:21](https://ref.ly/Lam2:21), [Psalm 37:25](https://ref.ly/Ps37:25), [148:12](https://ref.ly/Ps148:12), [Pr 22:6](https://ref.ly/Prov22:6). **e.** of marriageable age [Gn 34:19](https://ref.ly/Gen34:19) (J), warrior Absalom [2 S 18:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:5), [18:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:12) \+, Zadok [1 Ch 12:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:28), etc. **2\.** *servant, retainer* (c. 105 t): **a.** \= personal attendant, household servant, [Nu 22:22](https://ref.ly/Num22:22) (J), Ju [7:10](https://ref.ly/Judg7:10), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Judg7:11), [19:3](https://ref.ly/Judg19:3), [1 S 9:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:3) f., [2 K 4:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:12), [4:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:25) \+ oft. **b.** \= retainer, follower [Gn 14:24](https://ref.ly/Gen14:24), [1 S 25:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:5) f., [2 S 2:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:14) f., [1 K 20:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:14) f., [2 K 19:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:6) \= [Is 37:6](https://ref.ly/Isa37:6), [Jb 1:15](https://ref.ly/Job1:15), [1:16](https://ref.ly/Job1:16), [1:17](https://ref.ly/Job1:17), [Ne 4:10](https://ref.ly/Neh4:10), [4:17](https://ref.ly/Neh4:17) \+ oft.—[Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16) is corrupt, cf. We Now.—*Note.* נער occurs in Pent. as Kt with Qr perpet. נַעֲרָה q. v.

> \[Str5290] † נֹ֫עַר **n. m.** youth, early life (rare poet. synon. for נְעוּרִיס);—abs. ´נ [Jb 33:25](https://ref.ly/Job33:25) (\= youthful vigour), [36:14](https://ref.ly/Job36:14), ´מִנּ [Pr 29:21](https://ref.ly/Prov29:21), [Psalm 88:16](https://ref.ly/Ps88:16) *from youth up*.

[Dt 22:15](https://ref.ly/Deut22:15) b, [22:20](https://ref.ly/Deut22:20)); מְאֹרָשָה ´נ *betrothed girl* [Dt 22:25](https://ref.ly/Deut22:25), [22:27](https://ref.ly/Deut22:27), young widow [Ru 2:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:6), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:12); of a concubine Ju [19:3](https://ref.ly/Judg19:3), [19:4](https://ref.ly/Judg19:4), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Judg19:5), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Judg19:6), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Judg19:8), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Judg19:9), a prostitute [Am 2:7](https://ref.ly/Amos2:7). **2\.** of female attendants, *maids* (alw. pl.), [Gn 24:61](https://ref.ly/Gen24:61) (J), [Ex 2:5](https://ref.ly/Exod2:5) (E), [1 S 25:42](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:42), [Pr 9:3](https://ref.ly/Prov9:3), [27:27](https://ref.ly/Prov27:27), [31:15](https://ref.ly/Prov31:15), [Est 2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9), [4:4](https://ref.ly/Esth4:4), [4:16](https://ref.ly/Esth4:16); gleaners [Ru 2:5](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:5), [2:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:8), [2:22](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:22), [2:23](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:23), [3:2](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:2).—*Note*. נער Kt in Pent. as fem. ## נָפַח (id: BDB06228)

\[Str5301] † \[נָפַח] **vb.** breathe, blow (כור נפוח [Ecclus 43:4](https://ref.ly/Sir43:4) *a furnace blown upon* (heated hot); NH נָפַח *id.*, Targum נְפַח, Syr. ܢܦܰܚ; As. *napâḫu, inflame, nappaḫu, smith* DlHWB 474; Ar. (نَفَحَ and) نَفَخَ *blow;* Eth. ነፍኀ፡ or ነፍሐ፡ DI712);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. נָֽפְחָה [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9); וְנָפַ֫חְתִּי [Hg 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9); וְנָפַחְתִּ֫י Ez [22:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:21); *Impf.* וַיִּפַּח [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7); *Imv.* fs. פְּחִי Ez [37:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:9); *Inf.* פַ֫חַת(לָ) Ez [22:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20); *Pf. act.* נֹפֵחַ [Is 54:16](https://ref.ly/Isa54:16); *pass.* נָפוּחַ [Je 1:13](https://ref.ly/Jer1:13), [Jb 41:12](https://ref.ly/Job41:12); —*breathe, blow*, וַיִּפַּח בְּאַפּוֺ נִשְׁמַת חַיִּים [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7) (J); sq. ב also Ez [37:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:9), [Is 54:16](https://ref.ly/Isa54:16) and [Hg 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9) *blow* into it (to scatter it); sq. עַ־ל: לָפַחַת עָלָיו אֵשׁ Ez [22:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20) *to blow fire upon it* (ore, for melting), so fig. Ez [22:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:21) *and I will blow upon you with* (ב) *the fire of my wrath;* נַפְשָׁהּ ´נ [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9) *she hath breathed out her life* (of a mother, cf. **Hiph.**); abs. סִיר נָפוּחַ [Je 1:13](https://ref.ly/Jer1:13) *a blown* (i.e. well\-heated, boiling) *pot*, cf. ´דּוּד נ [Jb 41:12](https://ref.ly/Job41:12) (sim.). **Pu.** *Pf.* אֵשׁ לאֹ נֻפָּח [Jb 20:26](https://ref.ly/Job20:26) *a fire not blown* (by any human breath). So perh. also [Nu 21:30](https://ref.ly/Num21:30) (reading ´עַד־נֻפַּח אֵשׁ וגו *until fire was blown* \[hot] as far as Medeba), cf. Di. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 1 S. נֶפֶשׁ בְּעָלֶיהָ חִפָּחְתִּי [Jb 31:39](https://ref.ly/Job31:39) (or if) *the life of its* (the land's) *owners I have caused them to breathe out;* וְהִפַּחְתֶּם אוֺתוֺ [Mal 1:13](https://ref.ly/Mal1:13) *and ye have sniffed at it* (in contempt).

> \[Str5302] נֹ֫פַח appar. **n. pr. loc.** [Nu 21:30](https://ref.ly/Num21:30) (\= נֹבַח ?) perhaps נֻפַּח (LXX προσεξέκαυσαν), v. נפח **Pu.**

> \[Str4646] † \[מַפָּח] **n. \[m.]** breathing out;—only cstr. מַפַּח נֶפֶשׁ [Jb 11:20](https://ref.ly/Job11:20) *a breathing out of life* \= expiring (cf. נפח [Jb 31:39](https://ref.ly/Job31:39), [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9)).

> \[Str4647] † מַפֻּחַ **n. m.** bellows;—´נָחַר מ [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29) (fig. of vain prophetic labour).

> \[Str8598] † I. תַּפּוּחַ **n. \[m.]** apple\-tree, apple (from aromatic *scent* (breath), Thes LagBN 111, 129 Post (v. infr.); Ar. تُفّاحٌ is loan\-wd. Frä: 140; on meaning *apple* v. LöwNo. 109 RSJPh xiii. 65 f. PostHast. Dict. Bib. ‘ Apple ’)—**1\.** sg. תַּפּוּחַ *apple\-tree* [Jo 1:12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12), Ct [2:3](https://ref.ly/Song2:3), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5). **2\.** pl. תַּפּוּחִים *apples* Ct [2:5](https://ref.ly/Song2:5), ´רֵיחַ אַפֵּךְ כַּתּ [7:9](https://ref.ly/Song7:9); תַּפּוּחֵי זָהָב [Pr 25:4](https://ref.ly/Prov25:4) (in sim.).
> 
> 656:a. 1\. תַּפּוּחַ, v. also HomA. u. A. 100, 106, 107; TristrNHB 334 f. thinks ´ת: \= *apricot* (so Id.:Smith DB (2\) APPLE); M’Lean\-DyerEB APPLE makes ´ת: \= *quince*.

> \[Str8599] † II. תַּפֻּחַ **n. pr. m.** son of Hebron, acc. to [1 Ch 2:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:43); LXX Θαπους, A Θαφφου, LXXL Φεθρουθ.

> \[Str8599] † III. תַּפּוּחַ **n. pr. loc. 1\.** in lowland of Judah [Jos 12:17](https://ref.ly/Josh12:17) (LXX Ταφουτ, A Θαφφου, LXXL Θαπφου), [15:34](https://ref.ly/Josh15:34) (LXX Ιλουλωθ, LXXL Θαφφουα). **2\.** on border between Ephraim and Manasseh [Jos 16:8](https://ref.ly/Josh16:8) (LXX Ταφου, LXXL Θαπφουε), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Josh17:8), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Josh17:8) (LXX Θαφεθ, A Θαφθωθ, LXXL Θαφωθ), \= ´עֵין ת v:[7](https://ref.ly/Josh17:7) (LXX Θαφθωθ, LXXL Ναφθωθ); so read also perh. [2 K 15:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:16) (for MT תִּפְסַח; LXXL Th KmpKau Benz; LXX Θερσα, A Θαιρα, but LXXL τὴν Ταφωε). On locality v. BuhlGeogr. 178\.—Vid. also בֵּית תַּפּוּחַ.

> \[Str5300,5304] † נפיסים **n. pr.** Kt, in phr. ´בְּנֵי נ [Ezr 2:50](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:50), Qr נְפוּסִים, of family of returned exiles; LXX Ναφεισων, A Νεφουσειμ, LXXL Νεφωσειμ; \= נפושׁסים [Ne 7:52](https://ref.ly/Neh7:52) Kt, Qr נְפִישְׁסִים, LXX Νεφωσασει, etc., LXXL Νεφουσειμ;—Kt Ezr and Qr Ne, respectively, are prob. to be preferred (cf. נָפִישׁ sub נפשׁ), but Ne has mixture of two variants, נפיסים, נפישׁים.

> \[Str5300] נְפִישְׁסִים v. foregoing.

> \[Str5306] † נֹ֫פֶךְ **n.\[m.]** a precious stone which Tyre gained by trade Ez [27:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:16) (where ´נ doubted by Berthol, queried by Toy: Hpt), cf. [28:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:13) (symbol. of wealth and honour of king of Tyre); in high priest's breastplate [Ex 28:18](https://ref.ly/Exod28:18), [39:11](https://ref.ly/Exod39:11). Perh. \= *ruby* or *carbuncle* LXX Joseph. ἄνθραξ, Vulgate *carbunculus*, cf. DIEx KauEx SgfrEzek Co Berthol; Targum AV *emerald*.

* **Associated Passages:** Song of Songs 8:5; Exodus 39:11; Proverbs 25:4; Job 20:26; Jeremiah 15:9; Ezekiel 28:13; Joshua 17:7; Jeremiah 6:29; Genesis 2:7; Jeremiah 1:13; Song of Songs 7:9; Malachi 1:13; Ezekiel 22:20; Ezekiel 22:21; Job 31:39; Numbers 21:30; Ezekiel 27:16; Joshua 12:17; Joshua 16:8; Sirach 43:4; Haggai 1:9; Ezra 2:50; Ezekiel 37:9; Joshua 15:34; Song of Songs 2:5; Song of Songs 2:3; Nehemiah 7:52; Exodus 28:18; Isaiah 54:16; 1 Chronicles 2:43; Joshua 17:8; Job 11:20; Joel 1:12; 2 Kings 15:16; Job 41:12
* **Associated Articles:** נָפַח (ID: `BDB06228@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## נָפַל (id: BDB06238)

## I נָפַץ (id: BDB06245)

\[Str5310] † I. \[נָפַץ] **vb.** shatter (NH *id., dash, beat;* As. *napâṣu, shatter, destroy* DlHWB 475; Targum נְפַע Pa. *shatter*, but also נְפַץ Pa., *id.,* Syr. ܢܦܰܨ *shake, break*, so Mand., NöM. 240, and ZMG xxxii (1878\), 406\);—**Qal** *Inf. abs.* וְנָפוֺץ c. acc. Ju [7:19](https://ref.ly/Judg7:19) *and a shattering* (\= *and shattered*) the jars; *Pt. pass.* עֶצֶב נִבְזֶה נָפוּץ [Je 22:28](https://ref.ly/Jer22:28) (fig.) *a vessel to be despised* (*and*) *shattered* (cf. Ew Gie RothsteinKau); also *Pt. act.* נֹפֵץ *shatterer* of the holy people [Dn 12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7) (Bev MartiKau, rdg. כִּכְלוֺת יַד נֹפֵץ). **Pi.** *dash to pieces:* *Pf.* 3 ms. וְנִפֵּץ consec. [Psalm 137:9](https://ref.ly/Ps137:9) c. acc. infants ag. (אֶל) rock; 1 S. וְנִפַּחְתִּ֫י of ´י dashing all in pieces with (ב) Babyl. as war\-club [Je 51:20](https://ref.ly/Jer51:20), [51:21](https://ref.ly/Jer51:21), [51:21](https://ref.ly/Jer51:21), [51:22](https://ref.ly/Jer51:22), [51:22](https://ref.ly/Jer51:22), [51:22](https://ref.ly/Jer51:22), [51:23](https://ref.ly/Jer51:23), [51:23](https://ref.ly/Jer51:23), [51:23](https://ref.ly/Jer51:23), sf. וְנִפַּחְתִּים אִישׁ אֶל־אָחִיו [13:14](https://ref.ly/Jer13:14); *Impf.* 2 ms. sf. כִּכְלִי יוֺצֵר תְּנַפְּחֵם [Psalm 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ps2:9) (i.e. nations); 3 mpl. נִבְלֵיהֶם יְנַפֵָּחוּ [Je 48:12](https://ref.ly/Jer48:12) (symbol. of destroying Moab); *Inf.* נַפֵּץ יַד־עַם־קֹדֶשׁ [Dn 12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7) *when* (*they*) *finish shattering the hand* (fig. for *power*) *of the holy people*, but rd. poss. נֹפֵץ, v. **Qal. Pu.** *Pt.* fpl. מְנֻפָּחוֺת [Is 27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9) like chalk\-stones *pulverized*.

> \[Str5311] † נֶ֫פֶץ **n.\[m.]** driving storm (lit. *bursting* of clouds, CheComm.), וָזֶרֶם וְאֶבֶן בָּרָד ´נ [Is 3:30](https://ref.ly/Isa3:30) (theoph.).

> \[Str4660] † \[מַפָּץ] **n.\[m.]** shattering;—כְּלִי מַפָּחוֺ Ez [9:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:2) with *his shattering\-weapon* in his hand.

> \[Str4661] † מַפֵּץ **n. m.** war\-club (lit. *a shatterer*);—מַפֵּץ־אַתָּה לִי [Je 51:20](https://ref.ly/Jer51:20) *a war\-club art thou to me* (´י addressing Babylon, cf. Gf Gie).

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 7:19; Jeremiah 51:22; Ezekiel 9:2; Jeremiah 51:23; Psalms 137:9; Jeremiah 22:28; Jeremiah 51:20; Jeremiah 48:12; Isaiah 3:30; Isaiah 27:9; Jeremiah 51:21; Jeremiah 13:14; Psalms 2:9; Daniel 12:7


## II נָפַץ (id: BDB06249)

\[Str5310] † II. נָפַץ **vb.** disperse, be scattered (oft. taken as secondary √ fr. Niph. of פוץ, yet cf. NH נפץ, Qal (Pt.), Pi., *scatter;* Syr. ܢܦܰܨ *pour out, throw down*, Targum נְפַץ; Ar. نَفَضَ *shake* (irreg. correspondence of sibilants cf. Nöl. c.), Eth. ነፈፀ፡ *dissipari, aufugere* Di 713\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. הָעָם מֵעָלַי ´נ [1 S 13:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:11) *the people were scattered from me;* 3 fs. נָֽפְצָה כָל־הָאָרֶץ [Gn 9:19](https://ref.ly/Gen9:19) (J) thence *dispersed all* (the population of) *the earth;* 3 pl. נָֽמְצוּ גּוֺיִם [Is 33:3](https://ref.ly/Isa33:3) *nations were scattered; Pt. pass.* fpl. cstr. נְפֻצוֺת יְהוּדָה [Is 11:12](https://ref.ly/Isa11:12) *the dispersed of Judah* he shall collect (‖נִדְחֵי יִשְרָאֵל).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 11:12; Genesis 9:19; 1 Samuel 13:11; Isaiah 33:3


## נפשׁ (id: BDB06250)

נפשׁ (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. نَفَسٌ *soul, life, person, living being, blood, desire*, نَفَسٌ *breath, sweet odour*, نَفُسَ *be high in estimation, become avaricious;* III. *desire a thing, aspire to it*, v. *breathe, sigh*. As. *napâšu, get breath, be broad, extended; napištu, life*, less frequently *soul, living being, person*. Vbs. appar. denom.; nouns in all Semitic languages: Ph. נפש, CISi. 86 B 5, Eth. ነፍስ፡; in the foll. \= both *soul, person*, and *tombstone, monument* (as representing person, v. esp. DuvalRev. Sêmit. ii (l894\), 259 ff.): NH נפשׁ v. LevyZMG xii (1858\), 215; O Aram. נפש, Nab. Palm. נפש, v. Lzb325 Cook82; Syr. ܢܰܦܫܳܐ, v. 1 Macc 13:27, 13:28; Sab. נפס Levy\-OsZMG xix (1865\), 255\. 290 Mordtmib, xxxii (1878\), 202 cf. Liḥyan., DHMEpigr. Denkm. 67, Min. HomSüdarab. Chrest. 128\).

> \[Str5315] נֶ֫פֶשׁ:756 **n. f.**:[Gn 49:6](https://ref.ly/Gen49:6) (so even [Gn 2:19](https://ref.ly/Gen2:19), [Nu 31:28](https://ref.ly/Num31:28), [1 K 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:2) v. AlbrechtZAW xvi (1896\), 42 SS) soul, living being, life, self, person, desire, appetite, emotion, and passion ([Ecclus 3:18](https://ref.ly/Sir3:18), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Sir4:1), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Sir4:1), [4:2](https://ref.ly/Sir4:2), [4:2](https://ref.ly/Sir4:2), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Sir13:12), [14:11](https://ref.ly/Sir14:11));—´נ [Gn 1:20](https://ref.ly/Gen1:20) \+; ָ\+נפֶשׁ [Gn 37:21](https://ref.ly/Gen37:21) \+; sf. נַפְשִׁי [Gn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Gen12:13) \+; pl. נְפָשׁוֺת Ez [13:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:18) \+ 13 t.; נְפָשֹׁת [Ex 12:4](https://ref.ly/Exod12:4), [Lv 27:2](https://ref.ly/Lev27:2); *cstr.* נַפְשׁוֺת [Gn 36:6](https://ref.ly/Gen36:6) \+ 4 t.; נַפְשֹׁת [Lv 21:11](https://ref.ly/Lev21:11); נְפָשִׁים Ez [13:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:20) (but rd. חָפְשִׁים, v. Co Berthol Toy); sf. נַפְשׁוֺתָם [2 S 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:17) \+; נַפְשֹׁתָם [Nu 17:3](https://ref.ly/Num17:3) \+:—**1\.** \= *that which breathes, the breathing substance or being* \= ψυχή, anima, *the soul, the inner being of man:* **a.** disting. fr. בָּשָר: מִנֶּפֶשׁ וְעַד בָּשָר [Is 10:18](https://ref.ly/Isa10:18); הַנֶּפֶשׁ עִם הַבָּשָר [Dt 12:23](https://ref.ly/Deut12:23); fr. שְׁאֵר [Pr 11:17](https://ref.ly/Prov11:17); fr. בֶּטֶן *body* [Psalm 31:10](https://ref.ly/Ps31:10). **b.** both the inner נפשׁ and the outer בשר are conceived as resting on a common substratum: אַךְ בְּשָרוֺ :עָלָיו יִכְאָב וְנַפְשׁוֺ עָלָיו תֶּאֱבָֽל [Jb 14:22](https://ref.ly/Job14:22) *only his flesh upon him is in pain, and his soul upon him mourneth;* cf. [Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5), [42:7](https://ref.ly/Ps42:7), [131:2](https://ref.ly/Ps131:2), [Jb 30:16](https://ref.ly/Job30:16), [La 3:20](https://ref.ly/Lam3:20) \[v. עַל **1 d**], all poetical (cf. **6 c**). **c.** ´נ departs at death and returns with life: וַיְתִי בְּצֵאת נַפְשָׁהּ כִּי מֵתָה [Gn 35:18](https://ref.ly/Gen35:18) (E) *and it came to pass when her soul was going forth* (*for she died*); נָֽפְחָה נַפְשָׁהּ [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9) *she breathed out her soul*, cf. [1 K 17:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:21), [17:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:22), [Jb 11:20](https://ref.ly/Job11:20), [31:39](https://ref.ly/Job31:39). **d.** oft. desired that the נפשׁ may be delivered: fr. Shₑ’ôl [Psalm 16:10](https://ref.ly/Ps16:10), [30:4](https://ref.ly/Ps30:4), [49:16](https://ref.ly/Ps49:16), [86:13](https://ref.ly/Ps86:13), [89:49](https://ref.ly/Ps89:49), [Pr 23:14](https://ref.ly/Prov23:14); fr. שַׁחַת, the pit of Shₑ’ôl, [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17), [Jb 33:18](https://ref.ly/Job33:18), [33:22](https://ref.ly/Job33:22), [33:28](https://ref.ly/Job33:28), [33:30](https://ref.ly/Job33:30). **2\.** The נפשׁ becomes a living being: by God's breathing נשׁמת חיים into the nostrils of its בשר; of man [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7) (J); by implication of animals also [Gn 2:19](https://ref.ly/Gen2:19) (J); so [Psalm 104:29](https://ref.ly/Ps104:29), [104:30](https://ref.ly/Ps104:30) cf. [66:9](https://ref.ly/Ps66:9); man is נֶפֶשׁ חַיָּה, a living, breathing being [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7) (J); elsewh. נפשׁ חיּה alw. of animals [Gn 1:20](https://ref.ly/Gen1:20), [1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24), [1:30](https://ref.ly/Gen1:30), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Gen9:12), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Gen9:15), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Gen9:16) (all P), Ez [47:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:9); so נפשׁ החיּה [Gn 1:21](https://ref.ly/Gen1:21), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Gen9:10) (both P), [Lv 11:10](https://ref.ly/Lev11:10), [11:46](https://ref.ly/Lev11:46) (H); נפשׁ השׁרצת [Lv 11:46](https://ref.ly/Lev11:46) (H); נפשׁ כל חי [Jb 12:10](https://ref.ly/Job12:10). נפשׁ is frequently used with the verb חיה: † וחיתה נפשׁ [Gn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Gen12:13), [19:20](https://ref.ly/Gen19:20) (both J), [1 K 20:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:32) (E), [Psalm 119:175](https://ref.ly/Ps119:175), [Je 38:17](https://ref.ly/Jer38:17), [38:20](https://ref.ly/Jer38:20); † חֵי נַפְשְׁ [1 S 1:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:26), [17:55](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:55), [20:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:3), [25:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:26), [2 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:11), [14:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:9), [2 K 2:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:2), [2:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:4), [2:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:6), [4:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:30) (all JE); cf. † ´יְחַיֶּה נ [1 K 20:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:31) (E), Ez [18:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:27), [Psalm 22:30](https://ref.ly/Ps22:30); also [Gn 19:19](https://ref.ly/Gen19:19), [Is 55:3](https://ref.ly/Isa55:3), [Pr 3:22](https://ref.ly/Prov3:22). **3\.** The נפשׁ (without חיה noun or verb) is specif.: **a.** a living being whose life resides in the blood \[so in Arab. WeSkizzen iii. 217 G. JacobArab. Dicht. iv. 9 f.] (hence sacrificial use of blood, and its prohib. in other uses; first in D), [Dt 12:23](https://ref.ly/Deut12:23), [12:24](https://ref.ly/Deut12:24) *only be sure that thou eat not the blood, for the blood is the living being* (הַדָּם הוּא הַנֶּפֶשׁ); *and thou shalt not eat the living being with the flesh* (הַנֶּפֶשׁ עִם הַבָּשָר); *thou shalt pour it upon the earth as water;* this is enlarged in H, [Lv 17:10](https://ref.ly/Lev17:10), [17:11](https://ref.ly/Lev17:11), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Lev17:12), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Lev17:14) and in P [Gn 9:4](https://ref.ly/Gen9:4), [9:5](https://ref.ly/Gen9:5), cf. [Je 2:34](https://ref.ly/Jer2:34). **b.** a serious attack upon the life is an attack upon this inner living being [2 S 1:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:9), [Je 4:10](https://ref.ly/Jer4:10), [Jon 2:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:6), [Psalm 69:2](https://ref.ly/Ps69:2), [124:4](https://ref.ly/Ps124:4), [124:5](https://ref.ly/Ps124:5), [Jb 27:3](https://ref.ly/Job27:3). **c.** נפשׁ is used for life itself 171 t., of animals [Pr 12:10](https://ref.ly/Prov12:10), and of man [Gn 44:30](https://ref.ly/Gen44:30) (J); נפשׁ תחת נפשׁ *life for life* [Ex 21:23](https://ref.ly/Exod21:23) (E), [Lv 24:18](https://ref.ly/Lev24:18) (H), [1 K 20:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:39), [20:42](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:42), [2 K 10:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:24); נפשׁנו תחתיכם [Jos 2:14](https://ref.ly/Josh2:14) (J); נפשׁ בנפשׁ [Dt 19:21](https://ref.ly/Deut19:21); בנפשׁ *for the life of* [2 S 14:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:7) [Jon 1:14](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:14); (1\) נַפְשִׁי \= *me:* אל תבא נפשׁי [Gn 49:6](https://ref.ly/Gen49:6) *let me not enter* (poem in J); מות ישׁרים ´תָּמֹת נ [Nu 23:10](https://ref.ly/Num23:10) *let me die*, etc. (poem); תָּמוֺת נַפְשְׁי Ju [16:30](https://ref.ly/Judg16:30) (J); אמרה נפשׁי [La 3:24](https://ref.ly/Lam3:24) *I say*. (2\) נַפְשְׁ \= *thee:* לְאֻמִּים תַּחַת נַפְשְֶָׁ [Is 43:4](https://ref.ly/Isa43:4) *peoples instead of thee;* אָֽמְרוּ לְנַפְשֵׁךְ [51:23](https://ref.ly/Isa51:23). (3\) נַפְשׁוֺ \= *he:* נפשׁו בטוב תלין Psalm *he will not dwell in good circumstances*. (4\) נַפְשֵׁנוּ \= *we:* נַפְשֵׁנוּ כְּצִפּוֺר נִמְלְטָה מִפַּח יוֺקְשִׁים [Psalm 124:7](https://ref.ly/Ps124:7). (5\) נַפְשָׁם \= *they, them:* נַפְשָׁם בַּשְּׁבִי הָלָכָה [Is 46:2](https://ref.ly/Isa46:2) *they are gone into captivity;* אוי לנפשׁם [Is 3:9](https://ref.ly/Isa3:9). **b.** \= reflexive, *self*, 53 t.: אסר על נפשׁ *bind oneself* [Nu 30:3](https://ref.ly/Num30:3), [30:5](https://ref.ly/Num30:5), [30:5](https://ref.ly/Num30:5), [30:6](https://ref.ly/Num30:6), [30:7](https://ref.ly/Num30:7), [30:8](https://ref.ly/Num30:8), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Num30:9), [30:10](https://ref.ly/Num30:10), [30:11](https://ref.ly/Num30:11), [30:12](https://ref.ly/Num30:12); ´לְעַנֹּת נ [Nu 30:14](https://ref.ly/Num30:14) *to afflict oneself* . (1\) נַפְשִׁי \= *myself:* לא אדע נפשׁי [Jb 9:21](https://ref.ly/Job9:21) *I know not myself*. (2\) נַפְשְׁ \= *thyself:* ´שׁמר נ [Dt 4:9](https://ref.ly/Deut4:9) *keep thyself*. (3\) נַפְשׁוֺ \= *himself:* ´אהב כנ [1 S 18:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:1), [18:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:3), [20:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:17) *loved as himself*. (4\) נַפְשָׁהּ \= *herself:* ´צדּקה נ Je 3:1l *justified herself*. (5\) נַפְשָׁם \= *themselves:* ´הציל נ *deliver themselves* [Is 47:14](https://ref.ly/Isa47:14), Ez [14:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:14), [14:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:20). (6\) נַפְשְׁכֶם, נַפְשֹׁתֵיכֶם \= *yourselves:* ´אַל־תַּשִּׁאוּ נ [Je 37:9](https://ref.ly/Jer37:9) *deceive not yourselves*, also [42:20](https://ref.ly/Jer42:20), [44:7](https://ref.ly/Jer44:7); ´ענּה נ [Lv 16:29](https://ref.ly/Lev16:29), [16:31](https://ref.ly/Lev16:31), [23:27](https://ref.ly/Lev23:27), [23:32](https://ref.ly/Lev23:32), [Nu 29:7](https://ref.ly/Num29:7) (P); ´נשׁמרתם לנ [Dt 4:15](https://ref.ly/Deut4:15) [Jos 23:11](https://ref.ly/Josh23:11) (D). (7\) נַפְשׁוֺתֵינוּ \= *ourselves:* ´על נ [Je 26:19](https://ref.ly/Jer26:19)  *against ourselves*. **c.** \= *person* of man, individual, 144 t., first in D2; esp. in H, P, and kindred writers: (1\) c. אָדָם: אדם ´נ [Lv 24:17](https://ref.ly/Lev24:17) opp. בהמה ´נ [24:18](https://ref.ly/Lev24:18) (both H), and so אדם ´נ *human persons* [Nu 31:35](https://ref.ly/Num31:35), [31:40](https://ref.ly/Num31:40), [31:46](https://ref.ly/Num31:46) (P) [1 Ch 5:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:21), Ez [27:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:13). elsewhere only [Hag 2:13](https://ref.ly/Hag2:13). **5\.** נפשׁ \= *seat of the appetites*, in all periods (46 t.)—**a.** hunger: רְעֵבָה ´נ *hungry soul* [Psalm 107:9](https://ref.ly/Ps107:9), [Pr 27:7](https://ref.ly/Prov27:7); with noun or verb of שבע *satisfy* [Is 56:11](https://ref.ly/Isa56:11), [58:10](https://ref.ly/Isa58:10), [Je 50:19](https://ref.ly/Jer50:19), Ez [7:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:19), [Psalm 63:6](https://ref.ly/Ps63:6), [107:9](https://ref.ly/Ps107:9), [Pr 13:25](https://ref.ly/Prov13:25), [27:7](https://ref.ly/Prov27:7); ´מתוק לנ [Pr 16:24](https://ref.ly/Prov16:24) *sweet to the taste*. **b.** thirst: עֲיֵפָה ´נ *weary soul* [Pr 25:25](https://ref.ly/Prov25:25), [Je 31:25](https://ref.ly/Jer31:25); כארץ עיפה ´נ [Psalm 143:6](https://ref.ly/Ps143:6); ´צמאה נ [Psalm 42:3](https://ref.ly/Ps42:3), [63:2](https://ref.ly/Ps63:2). **c.** appetite in general: יַקִּיפוּ עָלָיו ´אֹיְבַי בנ [Psalm 17:9](https://ref.ly/Ps17:9) *my enemies compass me about with greed;* הִרְחִיבָה שְׁאוֺל נַפְשָׁהּ וּפָֽעֲרָה פִיהָ [Is 5:14](https://ref.ly/Isa5:14) *Shₑ’ôl enlarged her appetite*, etc., cf. Hb [2:5](https://ref.ly/Hab2:5); [Pr 23:2](https://ref.ly/Prov23:2) בַּעַל נֶפֶשׁ; Ec uses נפשׁ only in the sense of **a, b, c**; the נפשׁ craves, lacks, and is filled with good things: [Ec 2:24](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:24), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:8), [6:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:2), [6:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:3), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:7), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:9), [7:28](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:28). **6\.** \= ´נ *seat of emotions and passions* (151 t.)— **a.** desire: † ´אִוְּתָה נ *soul desires* [Dt 12:20](https://ref.ly/Deut12:20), [14:26](https://ref.ly/Deut14:26), [1 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:16), [2 S 3:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:21), [1 K 11:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:37), [Jb 23:13](https://ref.ly/Job23:13), [Pr 13:4](https://ref.ly/Prov13:4), [21:10](https://ref.ly/Prov21:10), [Mi 7:1](https://ref.ly/Mic7:1); ´תַּאֲוַת נ [Psalm 10:3](https://ref.ly/Ps10:3), [Is 26:8](https://ref.ly/Isa26:8); † ´אַוַּת נ [Dt 12:15](https://ref.ly/Deut12:15); [12:20](https://ref.ly/Deut12:20); [12:21](https://ref.ly/Deut12:21), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Deut18:6), [1 S 23:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:20), [Je 2:24](https://ref.ly/Jer2:24); בחלה בי ´וגם נ [Zc 11:8](https://ref.ly/Zech11:8) *and their soul also fell a loathing against me*. **c.** sorrow and distress: † ´מרי נ *bitter, gloomy, discontented of soul* Ju [18:25](https://ref.ly/Judg18:25) (E) [2 S 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:8), [Jb 3:20](https://ref.ly/Job3:20), [Pr 31:6](https://ref.ly/Prov31:6); [Jb 7:11](https://ref.ly/Job7:11), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Job10:1), [Is 38:15](https://ref.ly/Isa38:15), Ez [27:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:1); ´עָֽגְמָה נ [Jb 30:35](https://ref.ly/Job30:35) *grieved;* ´אגמי נ *sad of soul* [Is 19:10](https://ref.ly/Isa19:10); ´תבכה נ *my soul shall weep* [Je 13:17](https://ref.ly/Jer13:17); ירעה ´נ *his soul trembleth* [Is 15:4](https://ref.ly/Isa15:4); ´צרת נ *distress of soul* [Gn 42:21](https://ref.ly/Gen42:21) (E). † **d.** joy: תגיל ´נ *my soul rejoiceth* [Psalm 35:9](https://ref.ly/Ps35:9), [Is 61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10); ´שמּח נ *rejoice the soul* [Psalm 86:4](https://ref.ly/Ps86:4); also [Psalm 94:19](https://ref.ly/Ps94:19), [138:3](https://ref.ly/Ps138:3), Pr 29:l7\. † **e.** love: ´אהבה נ *my soul loveth* Ct [1:7](https://ref.ly/Song1:7), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Song3:1), [3:2](https://ref.ly/Song3:2), [3:3](https://ref.ly/Song3:3), [3:4](https://ref.ly/Song3:4); ´יְדִדוּת נ *darling of my soul* [Je 12:7](https://ref.ly/Jer12:7); ´ב ´דבקה נ *his soul clave unto* [Gn 34:3](https://ref.ly/Gen34:3) (J), c. אחרי [Psalm 63:9](https://ref.ly/Ps63:9); חשׁקה ´ב ´נ *soul is attached to* [Gn 34:8](https://ref.ly/Gen34:8) (P). **f.** alienation, hatred, revenge: † מן ´תֵּקַע נ *the soul is alienated from* [Je 6:8](https://ref.ly/Jer6:8), Ez [23:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:17), [23:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:18); מן ´נקעה נ Ez [23:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:18), [23:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:22), [23:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:28); ´שְנֻאי נ *hated of soul* [2 S 5:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:8) (Qr); ´שנאה נ [Psalm 11:5](https://ref.ly/Ps11:5), [Is 1:14](https://ref.ly/Isa1:14). **g.** other emotions and feelings: ´השׁיב נ  *bring back soul* [La 1:11](https://ref.ly/Lam1:11), [1:19](https://ref.ly/Lam1:19) (i.e. revive, cf. c. שׁוּב [1 K 17:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:21) f.), hence fig.  *refresh, cheer,* v:[16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:16), [Psalm 19:8](https://ref.ly/Ps19:8), [Pr 25:14](https://ref.ly/Prov25:14), [Ru 4:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:15); ´שׁובב נ [Psalm 23:3](https://ref.ly/Ps23:3); ´מַחְמַל נ *your souls' compassion* Ez [24:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:21); ´קצרה נ *soul was impatient* [Nu 21:4](https://ref.ly/Num21:4) (E), Ju [10:16](https://ref.ly/Judg10:16), [16:16](https://ref.ly/Judg16:16), [Zc 11:8](https://ref.ly/Zech11:8); כי ´אאריך נ *that I should prolong my patience* Jb 6:1l הגר ´ידעתם את־נ *ye know the feeling of the stranger* [Ex 23:9](https://ref.ly/Exod23:9) (RD).
> 
> **7\.** נפשׁ is used occasionally for mental acts \+ לבב (see **10**); poss. also alone, owing to unconscious assimilation by late writers; but most, if not all, exx. may be otherwise explained: נפשׁי ידעת מאד [Psalm 139:14](https://ref.ly/Ps139:14) *my soul knoweth well* (or *I know well;* cf. **4a**); בלא לא טוב ´דעת נ [Pr 19:2](https://ref.ly/Prov19:2) *that the soul be without knowledge is not good* (but RVm *desire without knowledge,* cf. **6 a**); ´דעה חכמה לנ [Pr 24:14](https://ref.ly/Prov24:14) *know wisdom for thy soul* (or  *according to thy desire,* cf. **6 a**); ´אל תדמי בנ [Est 4:13](https://ref.ly/Esth4:13) *think not in thy soul* (or *in thyself,* cf. **4 b**); כמו שׁער ´בג [Pr 23:7](https://ref.ly/Prov23:7) *as he reckoneth in his soul* (but RV *in himself*, cf. **4 b**); ´מה תאמר נ [1 S 20:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:4) (but AV RV foll. LXX ἐπιθυμεῖ \= תְּאַוֶּה; v. **6 a** ). **8\.** נפשׁ for acts of the will is dub.: אם ישׁ נפשׁכם (את) *if it is your purpose* [Gn 23:8](https://ref.ly/Gen23:8) (P) [2 K 9:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:15) (or *if it is your desire,* **6 a**); ´בחרה נ *my soul chooseth* Jb 7:l5; ´מאנה נ *my soul, re\-fuseth* [Jb 6:7](https://ref.ly/Job6:7), [Psalm 77:3](https://ref.ly/Ps77:3); חפצה ´נ *their soul delighteth in* [Is 66:3](https://ref.ly/Isa66:3); רצתה ´נ *my soul delighteth in* [Is 42:1](https://ref.ly/Isa42:1); (all perhaps emotional, **6 b, d, g**). **9\.** נפשׁ \= character is still more dub.: בּוֺ ´לאֹ־יָֽשְׁרָה נ Hb [2:4](https://ref.ly/Hab2:4) *his soul is not right in him* (but LXX οὐκ εὐδοκεῖ ἡ ψυχή μου ἐν αὐτῷ ἔὐδοκεῖ \= רצתה], v.**6\.**); נַפְשִׁי לאֹ מְטֻמָּאָה Ez [4:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:14) *my soul hath not* (or *I have not,* **4 a**) *been polluted.* †**10\.** ´נin D, when used with לבב, is assim. to it, and shares with it the mngs. of **7, 8, 9**; and so in later writers influenced by D (unless we may think that ´ל is used of intellect, while ´נ is used of the feelings): thus, בכל לבב ובכל נפשׁ (see לבב): c. דרשׁ [Dt 4:29](https://ref.ly/Deut4:29), [2 Ch 15:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:12); עשה [Dt 26:16](https://ref.ly/Deut26:16); אהב [Dt 6:5](https://ref.ly/Deut6:5), [13:4](https://ref.ly/Deut13:4), [30:6](https://ref.ly/Deut30:6); ידע [Jos 23:14](https://ref.ly/Josh23:14); עבד [Dt 10:12](https://ref.ly/Deut10:12), [11:13](https://ref.ly/Deut11:13), [Jos 22:5](https://ref.ly/Josh22:5); שׁמע בקלו [Dt 30:2](https://ref.ly/Deut30:2); שׁוב אל [Dt 30:10](https://ref.ly/Deut30:10), [1 K 8:48](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:48) \= [2 Ch 6:38](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:38), [2 K 23:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:25); שׁמר מצות [2 K 23:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:3) \= [2 Ch 34:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:31); הלך לפני[1 K 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:4); and [Dt 11:18](https://ref.ly/Deut11:18), [1 S 2:35](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:35), [1 Ch 22:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:19), [28:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:9), [Je 32:41](https://ref.ly/Jer32:41), [Psalm 13:8](https://ref.ly/Ps13:8), [Pr 2:10](https://ref.ly/Prov2:10), [24:12](https://ref.ly/Prov24:12). Note.—In three cases is generally found closer approach to supposed radical meaning *breath:*—**a.** שׁמן וקטרת ישמח לב ומתק רעהו מעצת נפשׁ [Pr 27:9](https://ref.ly/Prov27:9). Ges (after Döderlein) renders a. ´מעצת נ *more than odorous trees,* so later edds., even Buhl; but Vulgate De SS transp.) מֵעֲצַת רֵעֵהוּ ´וּמֶתֶק נ. LXX Hi BI ומתקרעה \= *the soul is rent asunder by cares*. In any case נפשׁis ‖לב, cf. **10\. b.** נַפְשׁוֺ גֶּחָלִים תְּלַהֵט [Jb 41:13](https://ref.ly/Job41:13) *his breath kindleth coals* (of the crocodile).The piece is one of the latest in the book; primitive meaning hardly in such a passage; context favours ref. to *passion* or *fury;* perhaps sub **6 f,** *his passion* or *fury* kindleth coals. **c.** בָּתֵּי הַנֶּפֶשׁ [Is 3:20](https://ref.ly/Isa3:20) *perfume boxes;* mng. evident from context; but not necessarily *scent (breath)\-boxes;* may be **6 a,** *boxes of desire,* or **5,** *boxes exciting the sense of smell; \= smelling* boxes or bottles. No sufficient evid. in BH, therefore, for meaning *breath, odour*.—See, for complete study of נֶפֶשׁ (all passages), BrJBL l897,17 ff.

> \[Str5314] † \[נָפַשׁ] **vb. denom.** (cf. Syr. ܢܰܦܶܫ *refresh;* ܐܶܬܢܦܶܫ, ܐܶܬܢܰܦܰܫ  *be refreshed;* Eth. ነፍሰ፡);—**Niph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִנָּפֵשׁ [Ex 23:12](https://ref.ly/Exod23:12) (E) [2 S 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:14); וַיִּנָּפַָשׁ [Ex 31:17](https://ref.ly/Exod31:17) (P);—*take breath, refresh oneself.*

> \[Str5305] † נָפִישׁ **n.pr.m.** son of Ishmael, acc. to [Gn 25:15](https://ref.ly/Gen25:15) (P), \= [1 Ch 1:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:31) (LXX Ναφες, LXXL Ναφεις); \= (Arab) tribe [5:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:19) (LXX Ναφεισαδαιων, A LXXL Ναφισαιων).

\[Str5323] † \[נָצָא] **vb.** \= נָצָה \= fly, acc. Thes Gf al.;—only **Qal** *Inf. abs.* (si vera 1\.) נָצֹא תֵצֵא [Je 48:9](https://ref.ly/Jer48:9), but improb.; Targum Symm יָצֹא cf. Ol:§ 245 h Gie; \> SchwallyZAW viii (1888\), l97 נָצֹה תִּצֶּה √III. נצה.

## נָצַב (id: BDB06261)

\[Str5324] † נָצַב vb. **Niph** take one's stand, stand (NH נִצָּב *id.* (rare), הַצָּבָה n. *taking a stand;* Ph. (Pun.) נצב, מנצבת, מצבת *monument* Lzb325; Nab. Palm. נצב *set up*; Zinj. *id., statue*, all Lzb325 Cook82; Palm. מצבא *image*, Nab. נצב *id.,* DHMVOJ viii (1894\), 12, cf. Lzbl.c.; perhaps As. *naṣâbu* Tel Am, WklTA 24\*b; Aram. נְצַב, ܢܨܰܒ; Ar. نَصَبَ *set up, erect, نَصْبٌ sign, mark, way\-mark;* Sab. נצב *cippus* CISiv. 23 Sab Denkm95 DHMZMG xxx (1876\), 115 f. DerenbJas, 1883, Aug.–Sept. 244; Min. מצב *statue* (\= מנצב HomSüdar. Chrest. 128\);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נִצְּבָה [Psalm 45:10](https://ref.ly/Ps45:10), נִצָּ֫בָה [Gn 37:7](https://ref.ly/Gen37:7), [Pr 8:2](https://ref.ly/Prov8:2); 2 ms. וְנִצַּבְתָּ֫ [Ex 7:15](https://ref.ly/Exod7:15) \+ 2 t.; 3 pl. נִצְּבוּ [15:8](https://ref.ly/Exod15:8), וְנִצְּבוּ consec. [33:8](https://ref.ly/Exod33:8); Pt. נִצָּב [Gn 24:13](https://ref.ly/Gen24:13) \+, f. נִצָּבָה [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16) נִצֶּ֫בֶת [1 S 1:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:26); mpl. נִצָּבִים [Ex 5:20](https://ref.ly/Exod5:20) \+; fpl. נִצָּבוֺת [1 S 1:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:26); —**1\. a.** *station oneself, take one's stand*, for definite purpose, c. עַל loc. (*by, on*) [Gn 24:13](https://ref.ly/Gen24:13), [24:43](https://ref.ly/Gen24:43), [28:13](https://ref.ly/Gen28:13), [Ex 7:15](https://ref.ly/Exod7:15), [33:21](https://ref.ly/Exod33:21), [34:2](https://ref.ly/Exod34:2) (all J), [Ex 17:9](https://ref.ly/Exod17:9), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Exod18:14) (E), [Nu 23:6](https://ref.ly/Num23:6), [23:17](https://ref.ly/Num23:17) (JE); [Am 7:7](https://ref.ly/Amos7:7), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Amos9:1), [Pr 8:2](https://ref.ly/Prov8:2); of ´י [Is 3:13](https://ref.ly/Isa3:13) *taketh his stand to plead;* c. ב loc. [Ex 5:20](https://ref.ly/Exod5:20), [Psalm 82:1](https://ref.ly/Ps82:1) (of God), cf. [Nu 22:23](https://ref.ly/Num22:23), [22:31](https://ref.ly/Num22:31); [22:34](https://ref.ly/Num22:34); עִמְּכָה ´נ [1 S 1:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:26); ´לִפְנֵי י [Dt 29:9](https://ref.ly/Deut29:9); c. פֶתַח [Nu 16:27](https://ref.ly/Num16:27) (JE), Ju [18:16](https://ref.ly/Judg18:16), [18:17](https://ref.ly/Judg18:17); abs. [La 2:4](https://ref.ly/Lam2:4) (on text v. Löhr). **b.** *stand \= be stationed* (by appointment, or in fulfilment of duty), c. עַל Pers. (sitting or lying) [Gn 45:1](https://ref.ly/Gen45:1), [1 S 4:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:20), [22:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:6), [22:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:7), [22:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:17); לְַ־מִשְׁמַרְתַּי ´נ [Is 21:8](https://ref.ly/Isa21:8) *stand at my watchman's post* (‖עֹמֵד); לִמִינְ [Psalm 45:10](https://ref.ly/Ps45:10); abs. [2 S 13:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:31). **c.** *take an upright position, stand*, ´יָקוּמוּ וְנ [Ex 33:8](https://ref.ly/Exod33:8) (E; \+ פֶּתַח loc.), cf. [Gn 37:7](https://ref.ly/Gen37:7) (E; of sheaf); of waters, כְמוֺ־נֵד ´נ [Ex 15:8](https://ref.ly/Exod15:8) (song). **2\.** *be stationed* \= *appointed* over (על), [1 S 22:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:9), [Ru 2:5](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:5), [2:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:6); with Samuel *presiding over* (על) *them* [1 S 19:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:20). Hence **3\.** Pt. as subst. deputy, prefect (*as appointed, deputed*), only [1 K 4:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:5), [4:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:7), [5:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:7), [5:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:30), [9:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:23), [2 Ch 8:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:10) (all of Sol.'s officers; so also [2 Ch 8:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:10) Qr, \< Kt נציב), [1 K 22:48](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:48) (of Edom). **4\.** *stand firm*, fig. כָּל־אָדָם נִצָּב [Psalm 39:6](https://ref.ly/Ps39:6) *every man*, (even) *the firm\-standing*, is wholly vanity (but expression strange, and text dub.); בַּשָּׁמָיִם ´נ [119:89](https://ref.ly/Ps119:89) (of ´י's word); *that which stands firm* [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16) (Isr. under fig. of sheep), but dub.; We GASm leave untranslated; Now prop. הַנַּחֲלָה *diseased*.

 **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִצִּיב [1 K 16:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:34); 2 ms. הִצַּבְתָּ [Gn 21:29](https://ref.ly/Gen21:29), [Psalm 74:17](https://ref.ly/Ps74:17); 3 mpl. הִצִּ֫יבוּ [Je 5:26](https://ref.ly/Jer5:26); *Impf.* יַצִּיב [Jos 6:26](https://ref.ly/Josh6:26), יַצֵּב [Dt 32:8](https://ref.ly/Deut32:8) (dub.; poet. for ´וַיּ Ew§ 233a Di; rhythmical shortening Ges§ 109 k; read יַצִּב Dr DaSynt. p. 94\), וַיַּצֶּ־ב [2 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:18); sf. וַיַּצִּיבֵ֫נוּ [La 3:12](https://ref.ly/Lam3:12) *Imv.* fs. הַצִּ֫יבִי [Je 31:21](https://ref.ly/Jer31:21); *Inf. cstr.* הַצִּיב [1 S 13:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:21), [1 Ch 18:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:3); *Pt.* מַצִּיב [1 S 15:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:12) (but rd. הִצִּיב LXX We Dr Bu Kit HPS):—**1\.** *station, set:* c. acc., ewes [Gn 21:28](https://ref.ly/Gen21:28), [21:29](https://ref.ly/Gen21:29) (E; לְבַד *apart*), trap [Je 5:26](https://ref.ly/Jer5:26); fig. *set me before thee* (לְפָנֶיְ) [Psalm 41:13](https://ref.ly/Ps41:13); *set me* as a target [La 3:12](https://ref.ly/Lam3:12). **2\.** *set up, erect* pillar (מַצֵּבָה) [Gn 35:14](https://ref.ly/Gen35:14), [35:20](https://ref.ly/Gen35:20) (E), [2 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:18), \+ אֲשֵׁרִים [2 K 17:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:10); altar [Gn 33:20](https://ref.ly/Gen33:20) (E), stone\-heap [2 S 18:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:17); monument (יַד) [1 S 15:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:12); city\-gates (c. ב), [Jos 6:26](https://ref.ly/Josh6:26) (JE) \= [1 K 16:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:34). **3\.** *cause to stand erect*, waters, בְּמוֺ־נֵד [Psalm 78:13](https://ref.ly/Ps78:13) (cf. **Qal** [Ex 15:8](https://ref.ly/Exod15:8)). **4\.** *fix, establish* boundary (subj. ´י) [Dt 32:8](https://ref.ly/Deut32:8), [Psalm 74:17](https://ref.ly/Ps74:17), [Pr 15:25](https://ref.ly/Prov15:25); dominion (יַד) [1 Ch 18:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:3) (of king).—[1 S 13:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:21) is dub.; AV *sharpen*, RV *set;* Kit ‘*gerade machen’*; Klo נִצַּב subst., v. infr.; cf. HPS. **Hoph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הֻצַּב [Na 2:8](https://ref.ly/Nah2:8) *it is fixed, determined* (Kl Or), but very dub.; perh. n. pr., or epith., of queen, cf. We Now GASm, q. v,; *Pt.* סֻלָּם נֻצּב אַ֫רְצָה [Gn 28:12](https://ref.ly/Gen28:12) (E) *a ladder set up on the earth;* ´אֵלוֺן מ Ju [9:6](https://ref.ly/Judg9:6), read אֵלוֺן הַמַּצֵּבָה GFM, v. infr.—Cf. also יצב **Hithp.**

## I נָצָה (id: BDB06270)

\[Str5132] †I. \[נָצָה] **vb.** fly (?);—only **Qal** Pf. 3 pl. (si vera l.) נָצ֫וּ גַּ־םָ\+נעוּ [La 4:15](https://ref.ly/Lam4:15) (of homeless exiles); text very dub.; rd. perh. נָ֫דוּ (cf. [Gn 4:12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14)), so Bu; Löhr (more venturesomely) כִּי לאֹ יִמְצְאוּ מָנוֺחַ; Buhl13 perhaps כִּי גַּם נָֽגְעוּ.

> \[Str5133] † נוֺצה **n.f.** plumage (cf. poss. Ar. نَاصِيَةٌ *hair over forehead, fore\-lock* of horse, Lane3033; v. also As. *na\-aṣ kappe*);—of eagle ´מָלֵא הַגּ Ez [17:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:3) (fig. of Nebuch.; \+ כָּנָף, אֵבֶר); also ´רַב נ v:[7](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:7) (fig. of kg. of Egypt; \+ כָּנָף); of ostrich נֹצָה [Jb 39:13](https://ref.ly/Job39:13) (‖כנף, \+ אֶבְרָה; cf. Di Bu). Here belongs prob. also בְּנֹצָתָהּ [Lv 1:16](https://ref.ly/Lev1:16), rd. בְּנֹצָתֹה (Dr\-WhHpt), בנצתו (Sam), or נֹצָתוֺ (אֶ־ת)וְ (cf. Di), *its feathers,* of bird for burnt\-offering; LXX σὺν τοῖς πτεροῖς, Vulgate *et plumas;* so AV RVm; \> with *its filth* (i.e. of the crop) Targum Onk SyrVer Ges Kn Ke Kal Ew RV, meaning without evidence elsewhere.

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 4:14; Ezekiel 17:7; Ezekiel 17:3; Leviticus 1:16; Job 39:13; Genesis 4:12; Lamentations 4:15


## II נָצָה (id: BDB06272)

\[Str5327] †II. \[נָצָה] **vb. Niph. Hiph.** struggle (NH *id.,* Hithp. (Jastrow928); Aram. נְצָא, ܢܨܳܐ *quarrel;* Ar. نَصَا *apprehendit, arripuit* aliquem antiis suis, Eth. ነጸየ፡ *vellere, evellere*, are perh. denom. from نَاصِيَةٌ, (v. נוֺצָה), so Thes);—**Niph.** recipr. *struggle with each other: Impf.* 3 mpl. יִנָּצוּ [Ex 21:22](https://ref.ly/Exod21:22) (E), [Lv 24:10](https://ref.ly/Lev24:10) (H), [2 S 14:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:6); ´יִנּ יַחְדָּו [Dt 25:11](https://ref.ly/Deut25:11); *Pt.* נִצִּים [Ex 2:13](https://ref.ly/Exod2:13) (E); all of physical struggle, wrestling, and the like. **Hiph.** *engage in a struggle* against: *Pf.* 3 pl. הִצּוּ [Nu 26:9](https://ref.ly/Num26:9) (עַל); *Inf. cstr.* sf. הַצֹּחָם v:[9](https://ref.ly/Num26:9) (עַל); הַצּוֺתוֺ אֶת [Psalm 60:2](https://ref.ly/Ps60:2) (title); only here of *war*, hence Klo's suggestion (cf. Buhl13) הַכּוֺתוֺ is plausible (cf. [2 S 8:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:3), [8:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:5), [8:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:9)).

> \[Str4683] †II. מַצָּה **n.f.** strife, contention;—abs. ´מ [Is 58:4](https://ref.ly/Isa58:4), [Pr 13:10](https://ref.ly/Prov13:10), [17:19](https://ref.ly/Prov17:19).—I. מַצָּה v. מצץ.

> \[Str4695] † \[מַצּוּת] **n.f.** id.;—only sf. אַנְשֵׁי מַצֻּתְֶָ [Is 41:12](https://ref.ly/Isa41:12) i.e. *the men who strove with thee* (‖אַנְשֵׁי מִלְהַמְתְֶָּ).

* **Associated Passages:** Exodus 21:22; 2 Samuel 8:3; Numbers 26:9; Proverbs 17:19; Psalms 60:2; Leviticus 24:10; Isaiah 58:4; Isaiah 41:12; Exodus 2:13; Proverbs 13:10; 2 Samuel 8:5; 2 Samuel 8:9; 2 Samuel 14:6; Deuteronomy 25:11

## I נָצַח (id: BDB06277)

\[Str5329] †I. \[נָצַח] **vb.** be pre\-eminent, enduring (Ecclus \[Pi.] *make brilliant* [43:5](https://ref.ly/Sir43:5), [43:13](https://ref.ly/Sir43:13) \[?] (cf. Oxford ed.:xxxiii); NH נָצַח *conquer,* etc.; Ph. נצח, Aram. נְצַח, ܢܨܰܚ *shine, be illustrious, pre\-eminent, victorious*; Ar. نَصَحَ *be pure, reliable;* Eth. ነድሐ፡ *be pure, innocent;* both classes of meaning prob. derived form *shine, be bright, brilliant,* v. also Dr:[1 S 15:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:29));—**Niph.** *Pt. act.* f. נִצַּ֫חַת [Je 8:5](https://ref.ly/Jer8:5) *enduring* (מְשֻׁבָה *apostasy*).

 **Piel** \= *act as overseer, superintendent, director*, only Chr. and Psalm\-titles;—*Inf.* לְנַצֵּחַ [1 Ch 15:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:21) \+ 4 t.; pt. לַמְנַצֵּחַ Hb [3:19](https://ref.ly/Hab3:19), [Psalm 4:1](https://ref.ly/Ps4:1) \+ 54 t. PsalmPsalm; *pl.* מְנַצְּחִים [2 Ch 2:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:1), [2:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:17), [34:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:13); —**1\.** in building temple [2 Ch 2:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:1), [2:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:17), [Ezr 3:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:8), [3:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:9), cf. [2 Ch 34:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:12), [34:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:13). **2\.** in ministry of house of ´י [1 Ch 23:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:4). **3\.** in liturgical service of song, על־השׁמינית, over the bass voices, leading them with כִּנֹּרוֺת [1 Ch 15:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:21); לַמְנַצֵּחַ in titles PsalmPsalm has prob. sim. meaning, \= *musical director* or *choirmaster;* Gk. Vrss. of 2nd cent. A.D., Aq Theod Sym, and so Jer, think of Aram. *victorious*, but no clear explan.; LXX εἰς τὸ τέλος (prob. לַמִּנְצָח) follows ordinary meaning of noun נֵצַח, which may be expl. in eschatological sense as referring to end of age of world after Euseb Theod ; or for *full rendering* after De; neither satisfactory. Chr., near in date, gives the clue which is intrinsically prob.: לְ indicates, not assignment (nothing special in these PsalmPsalm to suggest it, and assignment of PsalmPsalm for such use a matter of course), but that these PsalmPsalm were taken by final editor from an older major Psalter known as the Director's Collection, cf. the still earlier collections known as דוד(ל), אסף(ל), בני קרח(ל). The 55 PsalmPsalm with למנצח were taken 39 from Davidic, 9 from Korahite, and 5 from Asaphic Psalters; only 2 anonymous. Hb 3 originally belonged to Director's Psalter. This and other Director's PsalmPsalm have musical directions in titles. The Director's Psalter was prob. the prayer\-book of synagogue of Gk. period, presupposed by our Hab., the collection of the twelve Minor Prophets, the prophetic canon, and Daniel (v. BrGen. Intr. 123\). 

> \[Str5331] †1\. נֵ֫צַח **n.m.**:[La 3:18](https://ref.ly/Lam3:18) eminence, enduring, everlastingness, perpetuity (לנצח [Ecclus 40:14](https://ref.ly/Sir40:14) as 4 infr.);—נֵ֫צַח [1 S 15:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:29) \+ 3 t.; נֶ֫צַח [Am 1:11](https://ref.ly/Amos1:11) \+ 36 t.; sf. נִצְחִי [La 3:18](https://ref.ly/Lam3:18); pl. נְצָחִים 34:10; —**1\.** *eminence:* נצח ישראל *the Eminence of Israel* (´י) [1 S 15:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:29) (DrSam. 98 *glory of Israel*); attribute of God [1 Ch 29:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:11) (‖הוד,תפארת et al.). **2\.** *enduring of life,* and so *life* itself as enduring: אָבַד נִצְחִי [La 3:18](https://ref.ly/Lam3:18) *my endurance doth vanish* (De sap of life, v. II. ´נ). **3\.** *endurance in time:* ´משׁאות נ [Psalm 74:3](https://ref.ly/Ps74:3) *perpetual desolations;* יְדַבֵּר ´לנ [Pr 21:28](https://ref.ly/Prov21:28) *speaketh continually* (?; v. Toy); ´עד־נ [Psalm 49:20](https://ref.ly/Ps49:20) *unto the end* (a long duration), so Jb [34:36](https://ref.ly/Job34:36). **4\.** *everlastingness, ever,* usu. לָנֶצַח *for ever*, [2 S 2:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:26), [Is 13:20](https://ref.ly/Isa13:20), [25:8](https://ref.ly/Isa25:8), [28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28), [33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20), [57:16](https://ref.ly/Isa57:16), [Je 3:5](https://ref.ly/Jer3:5), [50:39](https://ref.ly/Jer50:39), [Am 8:7](https://ref.ly/Amos8:7), Hb [1:4](https://ref.ly/Hab1:4), [Psalm 9:7](https://ref.ly/Ps9:7), [9:19](https://ref.ly/Ps9:19), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ps10:11), [44:24](https://ref.ly/Ps44:24), [49:10](https://ref.ly/Ps49:10), [52:7](https://ref.ly/Ps52:7), [68:17](https://ref.ly/Ps68:17), [74:1](https://ref.ly/Ps74:1), [74:10](https://ref.ly/Ps74:10), [74:19](https://ref.ly/Ps74:19), [77:9](https://ref.ly/Ps77:9), [79:5](https://ref.ly/Ps79:5), [89:47](https://ref.ly/Ps89:47), [103:9](https://ref.ly/Ps103:9), [Jb 4:20](https://ref.ly/Job4:20), [14:20](https://ref.ly/Job14:20), [20:7](https://ref.ly/Job20:7), [23:7](https://ref.ly/Job23:7), [36:7](https://ref.ly/Job36:7), [La 5:20](https://ref.ly/Lam5:20); לנצח נצחים (cf. עולם עולמים) [Is 34:10](https://ref.ly/Isa34:10); seldom abbr. נֶצַח [Am 1:11](https://ref.ly/Amos1:11) (´לָנ LXX We Now GASm), [Je 15:18](https://ref.ly/Jer15:18), [Psalm 13:2](https://ref.ly/Ps13:2), [16:11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:11).

מִמּוֺת [Jos 2:13](https://ref.ly/Josh2:13) (J), [Psalm 33:19](https://ref.ly/Ps33:19), [56:14](https://ref.ly/Ps56:14), [Pr 10:12](https://ref.ly/Prov10:12) \= [11:4](https://ref.ly/Prov11:4); מִשְּׁאוֺל [Psalm 86:13](https://ref.ly/Ps86:13), [Pr 23:14](https://ref.ly/Prov23:14). † **4\.** *deliver from sin and guilt* (in late writings), מִכָּל־פְּשָׁעַי [Psalm 39:9](https://ref.ly/Ps39:9) *from all my trangression;* מִדָּמִים [Psalm 51:6](https://ref.ly/Ps51:6) *from bloodguiltiness;* הַצִּילֵנוּ וְכַפֵּר עַל־חַטּאֹתֵינוּ [Psalm 79:9](https://ref.ly/Ps79:9) *deliver us and cover over all our sins;* prob. also כְּאִמְרָֽתְ הַצִּילֵנִי [Psalm 119:170](https://ref.ly/Ps119:170). †**Hoph.** *Pt.* מֻצּל;—בְּאוּד מֻצָּל מִשְּרֵטָה [Am 4:11](https://ref.ly/Amos4:11) *as a brand plucked out of the burning;* מֵאֵשׁ [Zc 3:2](https://ref.ly/Zech3:2) *from the fire.* †**Hithp.** וַיִּתְנַצְּלוּ אֶת־עֶדְיָם [Ex 33:6](https://ref.ly/Exod33:6) (E) *and they stripped themselves* of their ornaments.

## I נָצַץ (id: BDB06285)

\[Str5340] † I. \[נָצַץ] **vb.** shine, sparkle (NH נִצְנֵץ; akin to Ar. نَاضَ *sparkle, flash,* Targum נְעָא);—**Qal** *Pt.* וְנֹצְצִים כְּעֵין נְחשֶׁת Ez [1:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:7) (of cherubim);—PerlesAnal.45 prop. נוֺצִיצָם *their plumage* (´נ ‖ form of נוֺצָה), cf. Ew.

> \[Str5213] † נִיצוֺץ **n.\[m.] spark**;—[Is 1:31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:31) (fig.).

> \[Str5322] †I. \[נֵץ] **n.m.** only sf. נִצָּהּ [Gn 40:10](https://ref.ly/Gen40:10), v. foll.

> \[Str5328] † נִצָּה **n.f.** blossom (perhaps fr. above √, blossom, flower, as *shining* amid leaves);—abs. ´נ of vine [Is 18:5](https://ref.ly/Isa18:5) (‖פֶּרַח; \= *berry\-cluster* acc. to Di Kit CheHpt cf. J. DerenbZAW v (1885\), 301 f.; vi (1886\), 98 f.); sf. נִצָּתוֺ [Jb 15:33](https://ref.ly/Job15:33) (of olive); read also נִצָּתָהּ [Gn 40:10](https://ref.ly/Gen40:10) (of vine) for MT נִצָּהּ, cf. Di Holz.

> \[Str5339] † \[נִצָּן] **n.\[m.]** id., only pl. נִצָּנִים Ct [2:12](https://ref.ly/Song2:12).

> \[Str5006,5132] † \[נָצַץ] **vb. denom.** bloom, blossom (NH *id.;* Jewish\-Aram. נְצַץ *id.*);—**Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. הֵנֵ֫צוּ Ct [6:11](https://ref.ly/Song6:11) (no dagh., v. Köi. 381 Ges: § 67, 8, R. 11\) the pomegranates *have put forth blossoms,* so [7:13](https://ref.ly/Song7:13); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָנֵאץ [Ec 12:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:5) the almond\-tree *wears blossoms.*

* **Associated Passages:** Song of Songs 2:12; Ecclesiastes 12:5; Job 15:33; Isaiah 18:5; Isaiah 1:31; Genesis 40:10; Song of Songs 6:11; Song of Songs 7:13; Ezekiel 1:7


## II נצץ (id: BDB06291)

II. נחץ (√ of foll.; cf. Ph. n.pr.loc. אי־נחם *isle (coast) of hawks,* Lzb213; Lzb325; Syr. ܢܶܨܳܐ *hawk*; Targum (cf. Me Chrest. 241\)).

> \[Str5322] †II. נֵץ **n.m.** a bird of prey, generic name, incl. hawk and falcon (TristrNHB; *accipiter* Bo Hieroz. ii. 266 ff.; ed. Rosenm. iii. 5 ff., cf. NowArch. i.116\);—unclean bird, [Lv 11:16](https://ref.ly/Lev11:16) (P), [Dt 14:15](https://ref.ly/Deut14:15) (varieties, לְמִינֵהוּ); bird of passage [Jb 39:26](https://ref.ly/Job39:26).

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 11:16; Deuteronomy 14:15; Job 39:26


## I נָצַר (id: BDB06293)

\[Str5341] † I. \[נָצַר] **vb.** watch, guard, keep (NH *id., observe;* As. *naṣâru, watch over, protect;* OAram. נצר *protect* Lzb325 Cook83; Palm. in n. pr. Vog150, 4 Cook124; Aram. נְמַר, ܢܛܰܪ (cf. Wetzst in DeJob (2\) on [27:18](https://ref.ly/Job27:18)); Ar. نَظَرَ *look at, consider, examine* (نَاطُورٌ, *overseer* is Aram. loan\-word Frä138); Sab. להנצרהמו *to aid them* Hal Rev.Sêm. iv (1896\), 71; Eth. ነጸረ፡ *spectare, intueri*, etc., DI701;—cf. also נטר);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. נְצָרָ֫תַם [Psalm 119:129](https://ref.ly/Ps119:129); 1 S נָצָרְתִּי [Psalm 119:22](https://ref.ly/Ps119:22), [119:56](https://ref.ly/Ps119:56), [119:100](https://ref.ly/Ps119:100); 3 mpl. נָֽצְרוּ [Pr 22:12](https://ref.ly/Prov22:12); *Impf.* יִצִּר [Pr 3:1](https://ref.ly/Prov3:1); sf. יִצְּרֶ֫נְהוּ Ges§ 58, 4 R.) [Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10); f. sf. תִּנְצְרֶָכָּה [Pr 2:11](https://ref.ly/Prov2:11) (Ges§ 58, 4 R.); 1 S. אֶצֹּר [Psalm 119:69](https://ref.ly/Ps119:69); אֶצְּרָה [119:34](https://ref.ly/Ps119:34), [119:115](https://ref.ly/Ps119:115); אֶצָֹּרָה [Psalm 119:145](https://ref.ly/Ps119:145); sf. אֶצָּרְ [Is 42:6](https://ref.ly/Isa42:6), [49:8](https://ref.ly/Isa49:8) (Ges Bö Ew al. from יצר); אֶצֳּרֶָנָּה [Is 27:3](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3); 3 mpl. יִצְּרוּ [Pr 20:28](https://ref.ly/Prov20:28); יִנְצָֹרוּ [Dt 33:9](https://ref.ly/Deut33:9) (on forms v. Ges§ 66, 2, R. I) \+ 15 t. Impf.; *Imv.* נְצֹר [Psalm 34:14](https://ref.ly/Ps34:14) \+ 3 t.; נִצְּרָה [Psalm 141:3](https://ref.ly/Ps141:3) (d. f. dirim. Ges§ 20, 2 b); sf. נִצְּרֶָה [Pr 4:13](https://ref.ly/Prov4:13); *Inf. abs.* נָצוֺר [Na 2:2](https://ref.ly/Nah2:2); *cstr.* נְצֹר [Pr 2:8](https://ref.ly/Prov2:8); *Pt.* נוֺצֵר [Pr 28:7](https://ref.ly/Prov28:7); נֹצֵר [Ex 34:7](https://ref.ly/Exod34:7) \+ 7 t.; sf. נֹצְרָהּ [Is 27:3](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3); pl. נוֺצְרִים [2 K 17:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:9), [18:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:8), etc.; *pass.* נָצוּר Ez [6:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:12); pl. נְצוּרִים [Is 65:4](https://ref.ly/Isa65:4) (LXX ἐν τοἱς σπηλαίοις, בַּמְּערוֺת); cstr. נְצוּרֵי (Kt נְצִירֵי only here) [Is 49:6](https://ref.ly/Isa49:6); f. נְצוּרָה [Is 1:8](https://ref.ly/Isa1:8) (Di rds. נְצוֺרָה Niph. Pt. √ צוּר); cstr. נְצֻרַת [Pr 7:10](https://ref.ly/Prov7:10); pl. נְצֻרוֺת [Is 48:6](https://ref.ly/Isa48:6) (Che rds. בְּחֻררוֺת).

 **1\.** *Watch, guard, keep,* a vineyard [Jb 27:18](https://ref.ly/Job27:18) (cf. Wetzst in DeJob (2\) ad loc.), [Is 27:3](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3), fig\-tree [Pr 27:18](https://ref.ly/Prov27:18), fortification [Na 2:2](https://ref.ly/Nah2:2); נֹצְרִים *watchmen* [Je 31:6](https://ref.ly/Jer31:6); ´מִגְדַּל נ *tower of watchmen* [2 K 17:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:9), [18:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:8); in ethical sense of men, guarding the mouth [Pr 13:3](https://ref.ly/Prov13:3), the way [Pr 16:17](https://ref.ly/Prov16:17) לֵב [Pr 4:23](https://ref.ly/Prov4:23); the tongue מֵרַע [Psalm 34:14](https://ref.ly/Ps34:14); c. על, over the door of the lips [Psalm 141:3](https://ref.ly/Ps141:3); of God נֹצֵר הָאָדָם [Jb 7:20](https://ref.ly/Job7:20) (thou) *watcher of men* (iron.). **2\.** *Guard from dangers, preserve,* c. acc., subj. God or his attributes [Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10), [Psalm 25:21](https://ref.ly/Ps25:21), [31:24](https://ref.ly/Ps31:24), [40:12](https://ref.ly/Ps40:12), [61:8](https://ref.ly/Ps61:8), [Is 26:3](https://ref.ly/Isa26:3), [42:6](https://ref.ly/Isa42:6), [49:8](https://ref.ly/Isa49:8), [Pr 2:8](https://ref.ly/Prov2:8), [20:28](https://ref.ly/Prov20:28), [22:12](https://ref.ly/Prov22:12); נֹצֵר נֶפֶשׁ [Pr 24:12](https://ref.ly/Prov24:12); c. acc. and מן *from* which [Psalm 12:8](https://ref.ly/Ps12:8), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ps32:7), [64:2](https://ref.ly/Ps64:2), [140:2](https://ref.ly/Ps140:2), [140:5](https://ref.ly/Ps140:5). In WisdLit. subj. is abstr.: חבמה [Pr 4:6](https://ref.ly/Prov4:6); תבונה [Pr 2:11](https://ref.ly/Prov2:11); צדקה [Pr 13:6](https://ref.ly/Prov13:6); נְצוּרֵי ישראל [Is 49:6](https://ref.ly/Isa49:6) *preserved of Israel*. **3\.** *Guard with fidelity, keep, observe:* of ´י, נֹצֵר חֶסֶד לאלפים [Ex 34:7](https://ref.ly/Exod34:7) (J); elsewhere of man observing the covenant [Dt 33:9](https://ref.ly/Deut33:9) (poem) [Psalm 25:10](https://ref.ly/Ps25:10), the divine law [Psalm 78:7](https://ref.ly/Ps78:7), [105:45](https://ref.ly/Ps105:45), [119:2](https://ref.ly/Ps119:2), [119:22](https://ref.ly/Ps119:22), [119:33](https://ref.ly/Ps119:33), [119:34](https://ref.ly/Ps119:34), [119:56](https://ref.ly/Ps119:56), [119:69](https://ref.ly/Ps119:69), [119:100](https://ref.ly/Ps119:100), [119:115](https://ref.ly/Ps119:115), [119:129](https://ref.ly/Ps119:129), [119:145](https://ref.ly/Ps119:145); commands of parents Pr 60:20, [28:7](https://ref.ly/Prov28:7); and discipline of Wisdom [Pr 3:1](https://ref.ly/Prov3:1), [3:21](https://ref.ly/Prov3:21), [4:13](https://ref.ly/Prov4:13), [5:2](https://ref.ly/Prov5:2). **4\.** *Guard, keep secret*, dub.: נְצֻרוֺת *secret things* [Is 48:6](https://ref.ly/Isa48:6); נְצוּרִים *secret places* [Is 65:4](https://ref.ly/Isa65:4) (see forms above); נְצֻרַת לֵב *secret, wily minded* [Pr 7:10](https://ref.ly/Prov7:10) (of harlot, so RVm *close*, i.e. *secretive*). **5\.** *Kept close, blockaded*, dub.: הַנָּצוּר *the blockaded* (so Ew Hi Co Toy; but Ke Bth Kau *preserved;* Hi Co Bth Toy del. והנשׁאר) Ez [6:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:12); עִיר נְצוּרָה *blockaded city* [Is 1:8](https://ref.ly/Isa1:8) (see form above); נֹצְרִים *blockaders* [Je 4:16](https://ref.ly/Jer4:16) (but LXX συστροφαι \= צררים i.e. *foes*). 

> \[Str5341] נְצוּרִים v. I נצר **Qal** *Pt. pass.* p. 665

> \[Str5336] † \[נציר] **adj.** preserved;—only pl. cstr. נצירי [Is 49:6](https://ref.ly/Isa49:6) Kt, Qr נְצוּרֵי q. v. נצר *Pt.* נִצְּרָה [Psalm 141:3](https://ref.ly/Ps141:3) v. I. נצר Qal Imv.

## II נצר (id: BDB06296)

II. נחר (√ of foll.; cf. perh. Ar. نَضُرَ *be fresh, bright, grow green;* [Ecclus 40:15](https://ref.ly/Sir40:15) נוצר, marg. נחר, *branch;* NH נֵחֶר \= BH).

> \[Str5342] † נֵ֫צְר **n.m.** sprout, shoot, in fig. uses:—abs. ´נ [Is 11:1](https://ref.ly/Isa11:1), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Isa14:19) (sim.); cstr. [60:21](https://ref.ly/Isa60:21) (מַטָּעַי ´נ), [Dn 11:7](https://ref.ly/Dan11:7) (שָׁרָשֶׁיהָ ´מִנּ; but LXX Bev ‘poss.’ מִשֶּׁרָשֶׁיהָ ´נ).
