## I נָקַב (id: BDB06298)

\[Str5344] †I. \[נָקַב] **vb.** pierce (NH *id.;* SI1.1\.4 הנקבה *the piercing, boring through;* Aram. נְקַב, ܢܩܰܒ *id;* נֶקְכָּא *hole*, etc.; Sinait. n.pr.m. נקבו Eut: 441:2, נקיבו Eut99,2; 153, 2 (Lzb325. 326 Cook33); As. *naḳbu, depth, spring* of water; Ar. نَقَبَ *perforate, pierce, scrutinize*, etc.,نَقِيبَةٌ *sagacity*, etc.; نَقِيبٌ *leader, chief* (one who *scrutinizes*));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. sf. נְקָבָהּ [2 K 18:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:21), [Is 36:6](https://ref.ly/Isa36:6); 2 ms. נָקַבְתָּ; Hb [3:14](https://ref.ly/Hab3:14); *Impf.* יִנְקָ־ב [Jb 40:24](https://ref.ly/Job40:24), וַיִּקֹּב [2 K 12:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:10); sf. יִקָּבֶָנּוּ [Is 62:2](https://ref.ly/Isa62:2); 2 ms. תִּקֹּב [Jb 40:26](https://ref.ly/Job40:26); *Imv.* ms. נָקְבָה [Gn 30:28](https://ref.ly/Gen30:28); *Pt. pass.* נָקוּב [Hg 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6); pl. cstr. נְקֻבֵי; [Am 6:1](https://ref.ly/Amos6:1); —**1\.** *pierce, bore* c. acc. [2 K 18:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:21) \= [Is 36:6](https://ref.ly/Isa36:6), Hb [3:14](https://ref.ly/Hab3:14) (all in fig.), [Jb 40:24](https://ref.ly/Job40:24), [40:26](https://ref.ly/Job41:2); c. acc. cogn. \+ ב, וַיִּקֹּב חֹר בְּרַלְתּוֺ [2 K 12:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:10) *and he bored a hole in its lid;* צְרוֺר נָקוּב [Hg 1:8](https://ref.ly/Hag1:8) *a bag pierced*, i.e. with a hole in it. **2\.** *prick off*, designate, wages [Gn 30:28](https://ref.ly/Gen30:28) (J), name [Is 62:2](https://ref.ly/Isa62:2) (´י subj.; cf. **Niph.**); pt. pass. *noted, distinguished* [Am 6:1](https://ref.ly/Amos6:1). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. in phr. נִקְּבוּ בְשֵׁמוֺת (אֲשֶׁר) *who were pricked off, designated, by name* [Nu 1:17](https://ref.ly/Num1:17), [1 Ch 12:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:32) (v:[31](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:31) van d. H), [16:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:41), [2 Ch 28:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:3), [31:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:19), cf. [Ezr 8:20](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:20).

> \[Str5345] †I. \[נֶ֫קֶב] **n.\[m.]** term. techn. of jeweller's work, prob. some hole or cavity (Hi\-Sm Co Berthol; ToyHpt leaves untransl.),—only pl. sf. תֻּפֶּיְ וּנְקָבֶיְ Ez [28:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:13) *thy sockets and thy grooves* (Da), or *thy settings and thy sockets*.

## I נקד (id: BDB06305)

I. נקד ( √ of foll.; NH נָקַד *point, furnish with points;* cf. Syr. ܢܩܰܙ (very rare) PS2453; n. ܢܽܘܩܙܳܐ but also ܢܽܘܩܕܳܐ *point, mark;* vbs. perh. denom.; Ar. نَقَطَ *point* or *dot* (letter, word, etc.), II. *make specks, spots,* on garment, etc., prob. loan\-word Frä195; DILex. 648 cp. Eth. ነቅዘ፡ *be decayed, worm\-eaten* (of wood, bread, etc.)).

> \[Str5348] † נָקֹד **adj.** speckled;—only of sheep and goats; ´נ [Gn 30:32](https://ref.ly/Gen30:32), [30:32](https://ref.ly/Gen30:32), fpl. נְקֻדּוֺת v:[35](https://ref.ly/Gen30:35) (all ‖טָלוּא), mpl. נְקֻדִּים v:[39](https://ref.ly/Gen30:39) (‖ *id.* \+ עֲקֻדִּים;—a11 these J); mpl. also [31:8](https://ref.ly/Gen31:8), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Gen31:8) and (\+ עֲקֻדִּים, בְּרֻדִּים) v:[10](https://ref.ly/Gen31:10); v:[12](https://ref.ly/Gen31:12) (all E).

> \[Str5350] † \[נִקּוּד] **n.\[m.]** perh. what is crumbled or easily crumbles, crumb (cf. נְקֻדָּה);—only pl. **1\.** *crumbs:* כֹּל לֶחֶם צֵידָם יָבֵ֭שׁ הָיָה נִקֻּדִים [Jos 9:5](https://ref.ly/Josh9:5) *all the bread of their provision was dry* (and) *become crumbs* (LXX βεβρωμένος\[οι]; Targum *in frusta comminuti*), cf. ´יָבֵשׁ וְהָיָה נ v:[12](https://ref.ly/Josh9:12) (v. Di Benn). **2\.** appar. a kind of (hard) biscuit or cake [1 K 14:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:3) (brought as present; \+ לֶחֶם, etc.).

> \[Str5351] † \[נְקֻדָּה] **n.m.** point, or drop;—only pl. cstr. תּוֺרֵי זָהָב נַעֲשֶה־לָּח עִם נְקֻדּוֺת הַכָּסֶף Ct [1:11](https://ref.ly/Song1:11) rows (strings or chains) of gold will we make for thee, with *points of silver*.

Dr; cf. [7:14](https://ref.ly/Amos7:14), where read נוקד for בוקר acc. to We Now Dr, not GASm, v. Bu infr.); perh. gloss, cf. BuKohut Memorial.106 f. Nowad loc.

## נָקָה (id: BDB06312)

\[Str5352] † \[נָקָה] **vb.** be empty(?), clean ([Ecclus 16:11b](https://ref.ly/Sir16:11) Niph. *Impf.* ינקה *be unpunished,* so [40:15](https://ref.ly/Sir40:15), [31:5](https://ref.ly/Sir31:5) \= [Pr 28:20](https://ref.ly/Prov28:20); NH Pi. \= BH; Aram. נְקָא Pa. \= BH, adj. נְקֵא *clean;* Ph. נקי adj. *pure;* Palm. נקא *be clean* (perh.) in n.pr. SachauZMG xxxv (1881\), 735 Lzb325; As. *nâkû, pour out, pour libation, make offering, nikû, libation, offering;* OAram. נקיה perhaps \= נוּקָיָה *libation* (Lzb326), cf. Syr. ܢܽܘܩܳܝܳܐ Cook83; Syr. ܢܰܩܺܝ Pa. *pour out, make a libation;* on the other hand, Ar. نَقِىَ *purus, mundus fuit* Kam Frey; II. *cleanse,* etc., Lane3037 .—Orig. meaning prob.*empty out,* hence *pour* and *be empty, clean*);—**Qal** *Inf. abs.* נָקֹה [Je 49:12](https://ref.ly/Jer49:12) (נָקֹה תִּנָּקֶה, prob. error for ´הנקה ת, cf. [Je 25:29](https://ref.ly/Jer25:29)).

## נָקַם (id: BDB06318)

\[Str5358] † \[נָקַם] **vb.** avenge, take vengeance ([Ecclus 46:1](https://ref.ly/Sir46:1) e להנקם נקמי אויב (Niph. \+ acc. cogn.); נקם, n. also [12:6](https://ref.ly/Sir12:6) (cf. [Dt 32:41](https://ref.ly/Deut32:41)), [39:30](https://ref.ly/Sir39:30), [48:7](https://ref.ly/Sir48:7); NH נָקַם esp. in deriv.; Aram. נְקַם (rare), ܢܩܰܡ in der. species; Ar. نَقَمَ *take vengeance* (عَلَ *upon*), Lane3037; cf. Eth.ቂም፡ DI458 *vindictae studium, ultionis cupido;* As. *iḳḳimu, vengeance*, *requital*, is poss. from √ אקם JägerBAS ii. 279\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. נְקָמֵ֫נִי [1 S 24:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:13); *Impf.* יִקּוֺם [Dt 32:43](https://ref.ly/Deut32:43); יִקֹּם [Jos 10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13); 2 ms. תִּקֹּם [Lv 19:18](https://ref.ly/Lev19:18); *Inf. abs.* נָקֹם [Ex 21:20](https://ref.ly/Exod21:20); cstr. נְקֹם Ez [24:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:8), [25:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:12); *Imv.* נְקֹם [Nu 31:2](https://ref.ly/Num31:2); *Pt.* נִקֵם [Na 1:2](https://ref.ly/Nah1:2) \+ 3 t.; f. נִקֶ֫מֶת [Lv 26:25](https://ref.ly/Lev26:25); —**1\.** *avenge, take vengeance:* **a.** God subj. דַּם עֲבָדָיו יִקּוֺם [Dt 32:43](https://ref.ly/Deut32:43) *the blood of his servants he avengeth* (against those who slew them) (\+ [1 K 2:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:5) LXXL Klo); ´נ abs. [Na 1:2](https://ref.ly/Nah1:2), [1:2](https://ref.ly/Nah1:2) c. לְ (agst. Nineveh) [Na 1:2](https://ref.ly/Nah1:2); נָקָם ´נ (agst. Jerus.) Ez [24:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:8); עַל־עֲלִילוֺהָם *on their doings* (his people's) [Psalm 99:8](https://ref.ly/Ps99:8); נְקַם בְּרִית ´נ [Lv 26:25](https://ref.ly/Lev26:25) (H) *the vengeance of the covenant* (against those who break it); נְקָמַנִי[1 S 24:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:13), c. מִן of Pers. *from* whom vengeance is taken (cp. [Je 20:10](https://ref.ly/Jer20:10)). **b.** *Israel and its leaders against their enemies*, נקמה ´נ c. מִן against whom [Nu 31:2](https://ref.ly/Num31:2) (P); c. acc. יִקֹּם אֹיְבָיו [Jos 10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13) (poem). **c.** Edom against Judah נקם לְ ´נ Ez [25:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:12). **d.** in administration of justice agst. murder [Ex 21:20](https://ref.ly/Exod21:20) (E). **2\.** *entertain revengeful feelings* (opp. אהב) against neighbour forbidden [Lv 19:18](https://ref.ly/Lev19:18) (H).

 **Niph.** *Pf.* 1 s. נִקַּ֫מְתִּי Ju [15:7](https://ref.ly/Judg15:7), [1 S 14:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:24); 3 mpl. נִקְּמוּ Ez [25:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:12); *ImPf.* יִנָּקֵם [Ex 21:20](https://ref.ly/Exod21:20); 1 s. אִנָּֽקְמָה Ju [16:28](https://ref.ly/Judg16:28), [Is 1:24](https://ref.ly/Isa1:24); 3 mpl. יִנָּֽקְמוּ Ez [25:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:15); *Inf.* הִנָּקֵם [Je 46:10](https://ref.ly/Jer46:10) \+ 2 t.; *Imv.* הִנָּ֫קֶם [Je 15:15](https://ref.ly/Jer15:15); pl. הִנָּֽקְמוּ [Je 50:15](https://ref.ly/Jer50:15):—**1\.** *avenge oneself*, **a.** ´י subj. c. מִן of adversary [Is 1:24](https://ref.ly/Isa1:24), [Je 46:10](https://ref.ly/Jer46:10); c. מִן advers., לְ for whom [Je 15:15](https://ref.ly/Jer15:15); c. בְּ adversary [Je 50:15](https://ref.ly/Jer50:15). **b.** of Israel and champions c. מִן Ju [16:28](https://ref.ly/Judg16:28), [1 S 14:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:24), [Est 8:13](https://ref.ly/Esth8:13); c. בְּ Ju [15:7](https://ref.ly/Judg15:7), [1 S 18:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:25). **c.** of enemies against Israel נקם ´נ Ez [25:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:15); c. בְּ Ez [25:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:12). **2\.** *suffer vengeance* (of law against murder) [Ex 21:20](https://ref.ly/Exod21:20) (E). **Piel** *Pf.* 1 s. נִקַּ֫מְתִּי [2 K 9:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:7), [Je 51:36](https://ref.ly/Jer51:36); —*avenge*, ´י subj. דָּמִים מיד [2 K 9:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:7) *blood at hands of;* נִקְמָתְֵ ´נ [Je 51:36](https://ref.ly/Jer51:36) *take vengeance for thee*. **Hoph.** *Impf.* יֻקַּם [Gn 4:24](https://ref.ly/Gen4:24), [Ex 21:21](https://ref.ly/Exod21:21), יֻקָּם [Gn 4:15](https://ref.ly/Gen4:15) *be avenged, vengeance be taken* (for blood). **Hithp.** *Impf.* תִּתְנַקֵּם [Je 5:9](https://ref.ly/Jer5:9), [5:29](https://ref.ly/Jer5:29), [9:8](https://ref.ly/Jer9:8); *Pt.* מִתְנַקֵּם [Psalm 8:3](https://ref.ly/Ps8:3), [44:17](https://ref.ly/Ps44:17); *avenge oneself*, ´י subj. לאֹ תִתְנַקֵּם ´נַפְשִׁי בְּ [Je 5:9](https://ref.ly/Jer5:9), [5:29](https://ref.ly/Jer5:29), [9:8](https://ref.ly/Jer9:8); of Israel's enemies אוֺיֵב וּמִתְנַקֵּם [Psalm 8:3](https://ref.ly/Ps8:3), [44:17](https://ref.ly/Ps44:17). 

> \[Str5359] † נָקָם **n.m.** vengeance;—´נ [Dt 32:35](https://ref.ly/Deut32:35) \+ 14 t.; cstr. נְקַם [Lv 26:25](https://ref.ly/Lev26:25), Ju [16:28](https://ref.ly/Judg16:28); —**1\.** taken by God, abs. Ez [24:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:8), [Psalm 58:11](https://ref.ly/Ps58:11); בְּדִית ´נ *vengeance of the covenant* [Lv 26:25](https://ref.ly/Lev26:25) (H); יוֺם נקם [Is 34:8](https://ref.ly/Isa34:8), [61:2](https://ref.ly/Isa61:2), [63:4](https://ref.ly/Isa63:4); ´בִּגְדֵי נ [Is 59:17](https://ref.ly/Isa59:17); לִי נָקָם וְשִׁלֵּם [Dt 32:35](https://ref.ly/Deut32:35); ´לקח נ [Is 47:3](https://ref.ly/Isa47:3); יבוא ´נ [Is 35:4](https://ref.ly/Isa35:4); c. acc. adversary ´עשה נ [Mi 5:14](https://ref.ly/Mic5:14); c. לְ advers. ´חשׁיב נ [Dt 32:41](https://ref.ly/Deut32:41), [32:43](https://ref.ly/Deut32:43). **2\.** Samson against Philistines, ´נקם נ Ju [16:28](https://ref.ly/Judg16:28). **3\.** enemies against Judah, abs. Ez [25:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:12), [25:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:15); individual enemy ´יום נ [Pr 6:34](https://ref.ly/Prov6:34).

## נָקַע (id: BDB06321)

\[Str5361] † \[נָקַע] **vb.** be \[severed, fig.] alienated, estranged (cf. Ar. نَقَعَ *split, rend, sacrifice* RS Sem. i. 471 f.; 2d ed. 491; Eth ነቅዐ፡ *split up, open* (intrans.) DI647; NH נֶקַע cleft, Syr. ܢܶܩܥܳܐ *id.*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. נָֽקְעָה נַפְשִׁי מֵעַל Ez [23:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:18) *my soul was estranged from* her sister (‖יָקַע), cf. v:[22](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:22); v:[28](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:28) (both c. מִן).

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 23:18; Ezekiel 23:22; Ezekiel 23:28


## I נָקַף (id: BDB06322)

\[Str5362] †I. \[נָקַף] **vb.** strike off (NH *strike, wound*, Aram. נְקַף *id.;* Ar. نَقَفَ *fregit* caput, ut cerebrum appareret; Vulg. Ar. *snap* with the finger (Dozyii. 716\); As. *naḳpu* prob.\= *mutilated;* Eth. ነቀፈ፡ *peel, flay* DI649);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3ms. וְנִקַּף סִבְכֵי הַיַּ֫עַר consec. [Is 10:34](https://ref.ly/Isa10:34) and the thickets of the forest shall be *struck away* (on sg. vb. cf. Ges§ 145 o DaSynt. § 113 b; or. rd. נִקְּפוּ;—form poss. Pi., but cf. Di Du CheHpt). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 pl. וְאַחַר עוֺרִי נִקְּפוּ־זאֹת [Jb 19:25](https://ref.ly/Job19:25) *after my skin, which they* (Ges§ 144 g) *have struck off* (alluding to ravages of his disease)—*this!* but text dub.; Bu כָּזאֹת נִקַּף **Niph.** *which has been thus struck off.*

> \[Str5363] † נֹ֫קֶף **n.\[m.]** striking off;—only cstr. זַיִת כְּנֹקֶף  *as (at) the striking of olive\-trees* [Is 17:6](https://ref.ly/Isa17:6), [24:13](https://ref.ly/Isa24:13) (sim. of fewness of people after ´י's judgment).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 10:34; Isaiah 17:6; Isaiah 24:13; Job 19:25


## II נָקַף (id: BDB06324)

\[Str5362] II. \[נָקַף **vb.**] go around (intrans.), ([Ecclus 45:9](https://ref.ly/Sir45:9) Hiph. ויקיפהו *surround something with* (2 acc.); Pf. 3 fs. הקיפה [43:12](https://ref.ly/Sir43:12) (acc.\+ ב instr.); NH Hiph. הִקִּיף \= BH; also \= *cling to, be attached, joined to*, and so Aram. אַקֵּיף (Aph.), ܢܩܶܦ); **Qal** *Impf.* חַגִּים יִנְקָֹפוּ [Is 29:1](https://ref.ly/Isa29:1) (fig.) *let feasts go around*, i.e. run the round (of the year). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִקִּיף [Jb 19:6](https://ref.ly/Job19:6); 3 fs. הִקִּ֫יפָהִ [Is 15:8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8); 3 pl. הִקִּ֫יפוּ [Jb 1:5](https://ref.ly/Job1:5), [Psalm 88:18](https://ref.ly/Ps88:18), וְהִקִּיפוּ consec. [2 Ch 23:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:7), sf. הִקִּיפוּנִי [Psalm 22:17](https://ref.ly/Ps22:17); 2 mpl. וְהִקַּפְתֶּם consec. [2 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:8); *Impf.* וַיַּקַּף [La 3:5](https://ref.ly/Lam3:5); 3 mpl. יַקִּ֫יפוּ [Psalm 17:9](https://ref.ly/Ps17:9), וַיַּקִּ֫פוּ [2 K 6:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:14); 2 mpl. תַּקִּ֫פוּ [Lv 19:27](https://ref.ly/Lev19:27); *Imv.* mpl. sf. הַקִּיפ֫וּהָ [Psalm 48:13](https://ref.ly/Ps48:13); *Inf. abs.* הַקֵּיף [Jos 6:3](https://ref.ly/Josh6:3), הַקֵּף v:[11](https://ref.ly/Josh6:11); *Pt.* pl. פִים(י)מַקִּ [1 K 7:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:24), 2 Ch [4:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:3); —**1\.** *go around, surround, encompass:* **a.** *go around*, c. acc. of city, [Jos 6:3](https://ref.ly/Josh6:3) cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/Josh6:11) (acc. om.), [Psalm 48:13](https://ref.ly/Ps48:13) (a11 ‖סָבָב); also (fig.) הִקִּ֫יפָה הַזְּעָקָה אֶ־ת [Is 15:8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8) *the cry hath gone about* the border of Moab. **b.** *surround, encompass, enclose,* usu. c. עַ־ל: וַיַּקִּ֫פוּ עַל־הָעִיר [2 K 6:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:14) *and they closed in upon the city* (beleaguered it); c. עַל Pers. [2 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:8) (\+ סָבִיב), [Psalm 17:9](https://ref.ly/Ps17:9), [88:18](https://ref.ly/Ps88:18) (‖סָבַב), \+ acc. rei [Jb 19:6](https://ref.ly/Job19:6) *he hath closed his net in upon me* cf. [La 3:5](https://ref.ly/Lam3:5); less oft. c. acc. Pers. [Psalm 22:17](https://ref.ly/Ps22:17) (‖סָבַב), [2 Ch 23:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:7) (‖ [2 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:8); \+ סָבִיב), also of inanim. things [1 K 7:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:24) \= [2 Ch 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:3) (both \+ סָבִיב, ‖סָבַב). **2\.** *make the round*, i.e. *complete the circuit* יְמֵי הַמִּשְׁתֶּה ´הִקּ [Jb 1:5](https://ref.ly/Job1:5) when *the days of feasting had completed their circuit*. **3\.** *make round, round off* (lit.): פְּאַת ראֹשְׁכֶם ´לאֹ חַקּ [Lv 19:27](https://ref.ly/Lev19:27) (‖לאֹ תַשְׁחִית אֵת פְּאַת זְקֶָ\+נְ), of heathen rite.

> \[Str5364] † נִקְפָּה **n.f.** encircling rope (of captive), תַּחַת חֲגוֺרָה נִקְפָּה [Is 3:24](https://ref.ly/Isa3:24).

## נקק (id: BDB06326)

נקק (√ of foll. cf. Eth. ንቅኣቅ፡ *rima, fissura* DI645; NH נָקִיק (rare) *cleft of rock*).

> \[Str5357] † \[נָקִיק] **n.m.** cleft of rock;—only cstr.: sg. נְקִיק הַסָּלַע [Je 13:4](https://ref.ly/Jer13:4); pl. נְקִיקֵי הַסְּלָעִים [Is 7:19](https://ref.ly/Isa7:19) (‖נַחֲלֵי הַבַּתּוֺת), [Je 16:16](https://ref.ly/Jer16:16).

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 16:16; Isaiah 7:19; Jeremiah 13:4


## נָקַר (id: BDB06328)

\[Str5365] † \[נָקַר] **vb. bore, pick, dig** (NH *id.,* Aram. נְקַר ܢܩܰܪ *id.;* Ar. نَقَرَ *perforate, bore out, hollow out* Lane2838; Eth. ነቈረ፡ be one\-eyed DI645);—**Qal** *Impf* 3 mpl. sf. יִקְּרוּהָ [Pr 30:17](https://ref.ly/Prov30:17); *Inf.* נְקוֺר [1 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:3), both of *boring*, or *picking out* eye (acc.). **Pi.** *Impf.*. 2 ms. תְּנַקֵּד [Nu 16:14](https://ref.ly/Num16:14) (JE), 3 mpl. וַיְנַקְּרוּ Ju [16:21](https://ref.ly/Judg16:21) both of *boring out* eyes (acc.); *Pf.* 3 ms. נִקַּר [Jb 30:17](https://ref.ly/Job30:17) *he* (or *it,* i.e. the night) *boreth* my bones מֵעָלָי (of effects of elephantiasis; Bu rds. נֻקַּר). **Pu.** *Pf.* 2 mpl. נֻקַּרְתֶּם [Is 51:1](https://ref.ly/Isa51:1) the quarry (fr. wh.) *ye were digged*.

> \[Str5366] † \[נְקָרָה] **n.f. hole, crevice**;—cstr. נִקְרַת הַצּוּר [Ex 33:22](https://ref.ly/Exod33:22); pl. cstr. נִקְרוֺת הַצֻּדִים [Is 2:21](https://ref.ly/Isa2:21) (‖סְעִפֵי הַסְּלָעִים).

* **Associated Passages:** Exodus 33:22; Proverbs 30:17; Isaiah 51:1; 1 Samuel 11:3; Isaiah 2:21; Job 30:17; Numbers 16:14; Judges 16:21


## נָקַשׁ (id: BDB06330)

\[Str5367] † \[נָקַשׁ] **v.b. knock, strike** only in specif. sense of hit, strike or bring down (a bird), and only fig. (Ecclus ינקשׁ [41:2](https://ref.ly/Sir41:2) c *striketh* against (ב; marg. ונוקשׁ), so נוקשׁ ב [13:2c](https://ref.ly/Sir13:2); NH *knock, strike* e.g. the knees, or a door (so Hiph.); Aram. (incl. Europa 123 f.);—**Qal** *Pt.* נוֺקֵשׁ [Psalm 9:17](https://ref.ly/Ps9:17) he (´י) *striketh down* the wicked (so Hup\-Now De Che); Vrss Ol Ew Bae Kau We BuhlLex rd. נוֺקַשׁ, **Niph.** fr. יקשׁ. **Niph.** *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּנָּקֵשׁ אַחֲרֵיהֶם [Dt 12:30](https://ref.ly/Deut12:30) lest *thou be thrust* (impelled) *after them*. **Pi.** *Impf.* יְנַקֵּשׁ [Psalm 109:11](https://ref.ly/Ps109:11) let the creditor *strike* at, *take aim* at (ל); 3 mpl. וַיְנַקְּשׁוּ [38:13](https://ref.ly/Ps38:13) (abs.). **Hithp.** *Pt.* מִתְנַקֵּשׁ בְּנַפְשִׁי [1 S 28:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:9) why *art* thou *striking at my life?*

## נָשָא (id: BDB06337)

\[Str4984, 5375, 5379] נָשָא **vb.** lift, carry, take (NH \= BH; Ph. נשא *id*. Lzb326; As. *našû*, *id.* DlHWB 484; BAram. נְשָא (rare); Syr. in ܡܰܐܣܰܐܬܳܐ *scales*; Palm. נסא, נשא **n.pr.** Lzb326 Cook81, 83 (cp. נשׁא *forget*); Ar. نَشَأَ intrans. *rise, be high, etc.;* Sab. in n.pr., e.g. CISiv. 5\. 1; also נשא vb. HalRev. Sem. iv (1896\), 69 Min. נשא *take*, HomSüdar. Chrest. 128; Eth. ነሥአ፡ *suscipere, tollere* DI635, so O Eth., DHMEpigr. Denkm. aus Abess. (1894\), 52\);—**Qal**:599 *Pf.* ´נ [Gn 13:6](https://ref.ly/Gen13:6) \+; 2 ms. נָשָאתָ [1 K 2:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:26) \+, נֶשָאתָה [Nu 14:19](https://ref.ly/Num14:19), sf. נְשָאתַ֫נִי [Psalm 102:11](https://ref.ly/Ps102:11); 2 fs. sf. נְשָֹאתִים Ez [16:58](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:58); 3 pl. נָֽשְאוּ [2 S 18:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:28) \+, נָשוּא [Psalm 139:20](https://ref.ly/Ps139:20), וְנָשוּ consec. > \[Str5387] † II. \[נָשִיא] **n.\[m.]** rising mist, vapour (Ar. نَشْءٌ, نَشِيءٌ);—only pl. נְשִאִים *vapours*, forming clouds, and portending rain, [Je 10:13](https://ref.ly/Jer10:13) \= [51:16](https://ref.ly/Jer51:16), [Psalm 135:7](https://ref.ly/Ps135:7), נְשִיאִים [Pr 25:14](https://ref.ly/Prov25:14).

> \[Str4853,4854] † II. מַשָּא **n.m.**:[Psalm 38:5](https://ref.ly/Ps38:5) load, burden, lifting, bearing, tribute;—´מ abs. [Ne 13:19](https://ref.ly/Neh13:19) \+; cstr. [2 K 5:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:17) \+; sf. מַשָּאוֺ [Ex 23:5](https://ref.ly/Exod23:5) \+ 2 t.; מַשַּאֲכֶם [Dt 1:12](https://ref.ly/Deut1:12); מַשָּאָם [Nu 4:27](https://ref.ly/Num4:27) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *load, burden* of ass [Ex 23:5](https://ref.ly/Exod23:5) (E), mules [2 K 5:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:17), camels [8:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:9), cf. [Is 46:1](https://ref.ly/Isa46:1), [46:2](https://ref.ly/Isa46:2); hanging on a peg [Is 22:25](https://ref.ly/Isa22:25) (fig.); forbidden on Sabbath [Je 17:21](https://ref.ly/Jer17:21), [17:22](https://ref.ly/Jer17:22), [17:24](https://ref.ly/Jer17:24), [17:27](https://ref.ly/Jer17:27), [Ne 13:15](https://ref.ly/Neh13:15), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Neh13:19); fig. of people as burden [Nu 11:11](https://ref.ly/Num11:11), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Num11:17) (JE), [Dt 1:12](https://ref.ly/Deut1:12), cf. עַ־ל ´הָיָה לְמ [2 S 15:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:33), [19:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:36) (אֶ־ל), also וָאֶהְיֶה עָלַי לְמַשָּא [Jb 7:20](https://ref.ly/Job7:20); of iniquities [Psalm 38:5](https://ref.ly/Ps38:5).—[Ho 8:10](https://ref.ly/Hos8:10) rd. מְשֹׁחַ, so LXX We Now GASm. **2\.** n. verb., *lifting, uplifting*, only נַפְשָׁם ´מ Ez [24:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:25) *the uplifting of their soul*, i.e. that to which they lift up their soul, their heart's desire. **3\.** n. verb., *bearing, carrying*, [Nu 4:24](https://ref.ly/Num4:24) (\+ לַעֲבֹד), [2 Ch 20:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:25), [35:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:3) (בַּכָּתֵף), so prob. [1 Ch 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:22), [15:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:22), [15:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:27) (cf. Be Kau; \> *singing, uplifting of voice*, e.g. Öt); esp. of *responsibility and duty of bearing* sacred vessels (oft. ‖עֲבֹדָה, פְּקֻדָּה) [Nu 4:15](https://ref.ly/Num4:15), [4:19](https://ref.ly/Num4:19), [4:27](https://ref.ly/Num4:27), [4:27](https://ref.ly/Num4:27), [4:49](https://ref.ly/Num4:49), ´עֲבֹדַת מ v:[47](https://ref.ly/Num4:47), ´מִשְׁמֶרֶת מ v:[31](https://ref.ly/Num4:31); v:[32](https://ref.ly/Num4:32). **4\.** perhaps *what is carried, brought*, i.e. *tribute* ´כֶּסֶף מ [2 Ch 17:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:11) *silver* as *tribute* (\+ מִנְחָה; cf. נשא **Qal**; others, *silver by the load* i.e. in great quantity).—1\. מַשָּא v. supr. P. 601 b.

[Is 14:28](https://ref.ly/Isa14:28) \+; usu. cstr. [13:1](https://ref.ly/Isa13:1) \+;—of prophetic *utterance:* [2 K 9:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:25), [Is 14:28](https://ref.ly/Isa14:28), Ez [12:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:10), [2 Ch 24:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:27) (c. עַל); בָבֶל ´מ [Is 13:1](https://ref.ly/Isa13:1), מוֺאָב ´מ [15:1](https://ref.ly/Isa15:1), cf. [17:1](https://ref.ly/Isa17:1), [19:1](https://ref.ly/Isa19:1), [21:1](https://ref.ly/Isa21:1), [21:11](https://ref.ly/Isa21:11), [22:1](https://ref.ly/Isa22:1), [23:1](https://ref.ly/Isa23:1), [30:6](https://ref.ly/Isa30:6), [Na 1:1](https://ref.ly/Nah1:1); בערב ´מ [Is 21:13](https://ref.ly/Isa21:13) is later ins. (Di); ´מ \= *revelation*, אֲשֶׁר חָזָה חֲבַקּוּק הַנָּבִיא ´הַמּ Hb [1:1](https://ref.ly/Hab1:1); ´י ´מ *utterance of* ´י [Je 23:33](https://ref.ly/Jer23:33), [23:34](https://ref.ly/Jer23:34), [23:36](https://ref.ly/Jer23:36), [23:38](https://ref.ly/Jer23:38), [23:38](https://ref.ly/Jer23:38), [23:38](https://ref.ly/Jer23:38) (cf. [2 K 9:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:25)), vid. also v:[33](https://ref.ly/Jer23:33) b (rd. אַתֶּם הַמַּשָּא), with word\-play,—sq. וְנָטַשְׁתִּי v:[36](https://ref.ly/Jer23:36) b; ´דְבַר י ´מ [Zc 9:1](https://ref.ly/Zech9:1) (\+ בארץ), [12:1](https://ref.ly/Zech12:1) (\+ עַל־יִשְרָאֵל), [Mal 1:1](https://ref.ly/Mal1:1) (\+ ´אֶל־יִש).—[Pr 31:1](https://ref.ly/Prov31:1) v. 1\. מַשָּא, p. 601 supr.; in [30:1](https://ref.ly/Prov30:1) rd. מִמַּשָּא *from Massa* (Mühlau, De), or הַמַּשָּאִי (Wild), which however is perhaps not original, but borrowed from [31:1](https://ref.ly/Prov31:1) (cf. Wildad loc. FrankenbergSpr. p. 5; corrupt name of Agur's home or tribe, Kau).

> \[Str4864] † מַשְאֵת **n.f.** uprising, utterance, burden, portion (on format. cf. O1:§ 109 a Sta§ 272 b Köii. 1, 183; Ph. (Pun) has משאת \= *payment, tax* v. Lzb326);—abs. ´מ Ju [20:40](https://ref.ly/Judg20:40) \+; cstr. מַשְאַת [Gn 43:34](https://ref.ly/Gen43:34) \+; pl. abs. מַשְאֹת [Gn 43:34](https://ref.ly/Gen43:34), \+ (prob.) מַשְאוֺת [La 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lam2:14) (v.infr.); cstr. מַשְאֹת [Gn 43:34](https://ref.ly/Gen43:34); —**1\.** *that which rises, uprising, uplifting:* **a.** הֶעָשָׁן ´מ Ju [20:38](https://ref.ly/Judg20:38) *uprising of smoke*, cf. (´הֶע om.) v:[40](https://ref.ly/Judg20:40). **b.** in gen. \= signal [Je 6:1](https://ref.ly/Jer6:1) (\= נֵס Gf.). **c.** *uplifting* of hands (כַּפַּי) in prayer [Psalm 141:2](https://ref.ly/Ps141:2) (‖תְּפִלָּה). **2\.** *utterance, oracle* (\= III. מַשָּא), [La 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lam2:14) (Bu Löhr). **3\.** *burden* [Zp 3:18](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:18) (of reproach, fig.). **4\. a.** *portion* (carried to some one), esp. from table of king or superior, [Gn 43:34](https://ref.ly/Gen43:34), [43:34](https://ref.ly/Gen43:34), [43:34](https://ref.ly/Gen43:34) (J; on custom v. Di and reff.), [2 S 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:8); so rd. perh. also [2 S 19:43](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:43) (for MT נִשֵּאת) Gr HPS. **b.** perh. also more gen., *present, largess* from a superior [Je 40:5](https://ref.ly/Jer40:5) ( \+ אֲרֻחָה *food\-allowance*), [Est 2:18](https://ref.ly/Esth2:18) (כְּיַד הַמֶּלֶח). **c.** exacted or enforced *gift*, from inferior, בַּר ´מ [Am 5:11](https://ref.ly/Amos5:11) (cf. Ph. supr.; v. also מנחה). **d.** sacred *contribution, tax* משֶׁה ´מ (´י) עֶבֶד [2 Ch 24:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:6), [24:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:9). **e.** *offering* to ´י Ez [20:40](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:40).

> \[Str7863] † \[שִיא] **n.m.** *loftiness*, fig. of pride:—אִם־יַעֲלֶה לַשָּׁמַיִם שִיאוֺ [Jb 20:6](https://ref.ly/Job20:6) (of wicked).

> \[Str7865] † שִיאֹן **n.pr.mont.,** i.e. Hermon, ´הַר ש הוּא חֶרְמוֺן [Dt 4:48](https://ref.ly/Deut4:48) (cf. Di Dr).

> \[Str7613] † שְאֵת **n.f. 1\.** exaltation, dignity. **2\.** swelling. **3\.** uprising;—´ש abs. [Gn 4:7](https://ref.ly/Gen4:7) \+, cstr. [Lv 13:28](https://ref.ly/Lev13:28) \+; sf. שְאֵתוֺ [Jb 13:11](https://ref.ly/Job13:11) \+; שֵּתוֺ(מִ) [41:17](https://ref.ly/Job41:17); —**1\.** *dignity* [Gn 49:3](https://ref.ly/Gen49:3) (dignity of first\-born), [Psalm 62:5](https://ref.ly/Ps62:5), of Chaldeans Hb [1:7](https://ref.ly/Hab1:7); of God, *his exaltation, loftiness* [Jb 13:11](https://ref.ly/Job13:11), [31:23](https://ref.ly/Job31:23). **2\.** *swelling*, eruption, בְּעוֺר בֶּשָר [Lv 13:2](https://ref.ly/Lev13:2) ( \+ סַפַּחַת, בַּהֶרֶת ), cf. [14:56](https://ref.ly/Lev14:56) (\+ *id.*); also [13:10](https://ref.ly/Lev13:10), [13:10](https://ref.ly/Lev13:10), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Lev13:19), [13:28](https://ref.ly/Lev13:28), הַנֶּגַע v:[43](https://ref.ly/Lev13:43) (all P). **3\.** *uprising*, [Jb 41:17](https://ref.ly/Job41:17) *at his uprising the mighty are in dread*. **4\.** [Gn 4:7](https://ref.ly/Gen4:7) is dub.;—is there not *acceptance ?* SyrVer Vulgate al.; *forgiveness* Targum Holz al.; *uplifting* of countenance, cheerfulness, Ew De Ke Di Kau.

## נָשַג (id: BDB06350)

\[Str5381] † \[נָשַג] **vb.** only **Hiph.** reach, overtake ([Ecclus 14:13](https://ref.ly/Sir14:13) והשיגת ידך, [35:10](https://ref.ly/Sir35:10) ובהשגת יד (cf. Schechter on [14:13](https://ref.ly/Sir14:13)), cf. **3** infr.; ישיג מענה [32:14](https://ref.ly/Sir32:14) *shall obtain an answer;*—**Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְהִשִּיג consec. [Lv 25:6](https://ref.ly/Lev25:6), sf. וְהִשִּיגוֺ consec. [Dt 19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6); 2 ms. sf. וְהִשַּגְתָּם consec. [Gn 44:4](https://ref.ly/Gen44:4), etc.; *Impf.* יַשִּיג [Lv 26:5](https://ref.ly/Lev26:5), וַיַּשֵּג [Gn 31:25](https://ref.ly/Gen31:25), sf. וַיַּשִּגֵם [Gn 44:26](https://ref.ly/Gen44:26), etc.; *Inf. abs.* הַשֵּג [1 S 30:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:8); *Pt.* מַשִּיג [1 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26), sf. מַשִּיגֵ֫הוּ [Jb 41:18](https://ref.ly/Job41:18); f. מַשֶּגֶת [Lv 14:21](https://ref.ly/Lev14:21), [1 Ch 21:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:12) (but rd. וְאִם הֱיוֺת as ‖ [2 S 24:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:13) WeSm Kit);—**1\.** *overtake*, c. acc., oft. after רָדַף: **a.** lit. [Gn 31:25](https://ref.ly/Gen31:25) (E), [44:4](https://ref.ly/Gen44:4), [44:6](https://ref.ly/Gen44:6) (J), [Ex 14:9](https://ref.ly/Exod14:9) (P), [15:9](https://ref.ly/Exod15:9) (song), [Dt 19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6), [Jos 2:5](https://ref.ly/Josh2:5) (JE), [1 S 30:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:8), [30:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:8), [30:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:8), [2 S 15:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:14), [2 K 25:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:5) \= [Je 39:5](https://ref.ly/Jer39:5) \= [52:8](https://ref.ly/Jer52:8), [Ho 2:9](https://ref.ly/Hos2:9), cf. [Psalm 7:6](https://ref.ly/Ps7:6), [18:38](https://ref.ly/Ps18:38) (וָאַשְׁמִידֵם in ‖ [2 S 22:38](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:38)), [La 1:3](https://ref.ly/Lam1:3) (metaph.). **b.** fig. of battle [Ho 10:9](https://ref.ly/Hos10:9), sword [Je 42:16](https://ref.ly/Jer42:16); on [1 Ch 21:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:12) v. supr.; of blessings [Dt 28:2](https://ref.ly/Deut28:2), curses v:[15](https://ref.ly/Deut28:15); v:[45](https://ref.ly/Deut28:45), predictions [Zc 1:6](https://ref.ly/Zech1:6), justice [Is 59:9](https://ref.ly/Isa59:9), wrath of ´י [Psalm 69:25](https://ref.ly/Ps69:25), iniquities [40:13](https://ref.ly/Ps40:13), calamities [Jb 27:20](https://ref.ly/Job27:20). **2\. a.** *reach, attain to*, [Jb 41:18](https://ref.ly/Job41:18) (if one) *reach it* (crocodile) with the sword (2 acc.); fig. paths of life [Pr 2:19](https://ref.ly/Prov2:19), joy, etc., [Is 35:10](https://ref.ly/Isa35:10) also [51:11](https://ref.ly/Isa51:11) (CheHpt יַשִּיגוּם, for MT יַשִּיגוּן; PerlesAnalekten 64 יִשְגּוּן, fr. שגה); of time [Gn 47:9](https://ref.ly/Gen47:9) my days *have not reached* the days ... of my fathers, cf. [Lv 26:5](https://ref.ly/Lev26:5), [26:5](https://ref.ly/Lev26:5). **b.** *cause to reach, bring, put*, מַשִּיג יָדוֺ אֶל־פִּיו [1 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26) no one *put his hand to his mouth*, but rd. מֵשִׁיב LXX Targum Klo Dr Bu Kit HPS Löhr. **3\.** fig. הִשִּׁיגָה יָדוֺ *one's hand has reached*, i.e. one is able, or has enough, [Lv 5:11](https://ref.ly/Lev5:11) (c. לְ), [14:21](https://ref.ly/Lev14:21), [25:26](https://ref.ly/Lev25:26), [25:49](https://ref.ly/Lev25:49); c. acc. \= *able to secure, get* [14:22](https://ref.ly/Lev14:22), [14:30](https://ref.ly/Lev14:30), [14:31](https://ref.ly/Lev14:31), [14:32](https://ref.ly/Lev14:32), [27:8](https://ref.ly/Lev27:8), [Nu 6:21](https://ref.ly/Num6:21) (all P), Ez [46:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:7); abs. \= gain (riches) [Lv 25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47) (H).—[Jb 24:2](https://ref.ly/Job24:2) v. סוּג.

## I נָשָׁא (id: BDB06354)

\[Str5378,5383] † I. \[נָשָׁא] **vb.** lend on interest, or usury, be a creditor (cf. Ar. نَسَأَ *postpone, delay; sell on credit;* ‖ form of I. נָשָׁה, v. Ges§ 75 oo);—**Qal** *Pt. act.* נשֶׁא (anal. ה ´ל) [1 S 22:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:2) every man that had *a creditor;* אֲשֶׁר נשֶׁא בוֺ [Is 24:2](https://ref.ly/Isa24:2) *against whom is a ceditor*, and pl. נשְׁאִים [Ne 5:7](https://ref.ly/Neh5:7) Kt (נשִׁים Qr, from נָשָׁה), \+ acc. cogn. מַשָּׁא. **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. לאֹ־יַשִּׁיא אוֺיֵב בּוֺ [Psalm 89:23](https://ref.ly/Ps89:23) *an enemy shall not act the creditor against him, make exactions of him* (‖יְעַנֶּנּוּ).—[1 K 8:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:31), [2 Ch 6:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:22) v. נשא **Qal 1 b** (5\).

> \[Str4855] † מַשָּׁא **n.m.**:[Ne 5:10](https://ref.ly/Neh5:10) lending on interest, or usury;—[Ne 5:7](https://ref.ly/Neh5:7) (as acc. cogn.), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Neh5:10).

> \[Str4859] † \[מַשָּׁאָה] **n.f.** loan (on pledg);—cstr. כִּי תַשֶּׁה בְרֵעְֲ מַשַּׁאת מְאוּמָה [Dt 24:10](https://ref.ly/Deut24:10); pl. עֹרְבִים מַשָּׁאוֺת [Pr 22:26](https://ref.ly/Prov22:26) *those pledging* (giving security for) *debts*.

* **Associated Passages:** Nehemiah 5:7; 1 Samuel 22:2; 2 Chronicles 6:22; Psalms 89:23; 1 Kings 8:31; Proverbs 22:26; Nehemiah 5:10; Isaiah 24:2; Deuteronomy 24:10

## II נָשָׁא (id: BDB06357)

\[Str5377] †II. נָשָׁא **vb.** only **Niph. Hiph.** beguile, deceive;—(kindred with I. שׁוא, from which, however, the forms can hardly be derived (conj. WeComp. 2\. 351\));—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. נִשְּׁאוּ [Is 19:13](https://ref.ly/Isa19:13) the princes *have been beguiled* (‖נוֺאֲלוּ, הִתְעוּ). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִשִּׁיא [Je 49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16), sf. הִשִּׁאַ֫נִי [Gn 3:13](https://ref.ly/Gen3:13), הִשִּׁיאְֶָ Ob:[3](https://ref.ly/Obad1:3); 2 ms. הִשֵּׁאתָ [Je 4:10](https://ref.ly/Jer4:10); 3 pl. sf. הִשִּׁיא֫וְּ Ob:[7](https://ref.ly/Obad1:7); *Impf.* only juss. יַשִּׁיא [2 Ch 32:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:15), so [Psalm 55:16](https://ref.ly/Ps55:16) Qr (v. infr.); יַשִּׁא [2 K 18:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:29), [Is 36:14](https://ref.ly/Isa36:14), sf. יַשִּׁאְֲ [2 K 19:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:10), [Is 37:10](https://ref.ly/Isa37:10); 2 mpl. תַּשִּׁאוּ [Je 37:9](https://ref.ly/Jer37:9); *Inf. abs.* הַשֵּׁא [Je 4:10](https://ref.ly/Jer4:10); —*beguile*, usu. c. acc. Pers. [Gn 3:13](https://ref.ly/Gen3:13) (J; not elsewh. Hex); [Je 49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16), Ob:[3](https://ref.ly/Obad1:3), Ob:[7](https://ref.ly/Obad1:7); אַל־יַשִּׁאְֲ [2 K 19:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:10) *let not thy God beguile thee* \= [Is 37:10](https://ref.ly/Isa37:10), of Hezekiah [2 Ch 32:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:15) (cf. infr.), אַל־תַּשִּׁאוּ נַפְשֹׁתֵיכֶם [Je 37:9](https://ref.ly/Jer37:9) *do not deceive yourselves;* also sq. ל Pers. אַל־יַשִּׁא לָכֶם [2 K 18:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:29) \= [Is 36:14](https://ref.ly/Isa36:14) (\= [2 Ch 32:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:15), v. supr.), [Je 29:8](https://ref.ly/Jer29:8); הַזֶּה הַשֵּׁא הִשֵּׁאתָ לָעָם [4:10](https://ref.ly/Jer4:10) *thou hast utterly beguiled this people;* ישׁימות עָלֵימוֺ [Psalm 55:16](https://ref.ly/Ps55:16) Kt appar. \= *desolations (be) upon them!* (but elsewh. only in n.pr.loc., cf. p. 445 supr.), \< Qr יַשִּׁיא מָוֶת עלימו Ew Ol Pe De NowHup, cf. LXX, *let death* (*beguile them*, i.e.) *come deceitfully upon them!* Brüll Che, cf. Bae, conj. plausibly יַשִּׁיא מָוֶת יִבְלָעֵמוֺ.

> \[Str4860] † מַשָּׁאוֺן **n.\[m.]** guile, dissimulation ( \> acc. to LagBN 196 der. fr. מַשָּׁא, *lending on usury*); תִּכַּסֶּה שִנְאָה בְּמַשָּׁאוֺן [Pr 26:26](https://ref.ly/Prov26:26) *hatred may hide itself with dissimulation*.

> \[Str4876,4875] † מַשּׁוּאוֺת **n.f.pl.** *deceptions* [Psalm 73:18](https://ref.ly/Ps73:18), [74:3](https://ref.ly/Ps74:3), but meaning not suitable; read prob. מְשׁוֺאוֺת, v. מְשׁוֺאָה sub שׁוא (so Klo NowHup Bae). P. 996

\[Str5378] †I. נָשָׁה **vb.** lend, become a creditor (cf. I. נשׁא);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 s. נָשִׁ֫יתִי [Je 15:10](https://ref.ly/Jer15:10); 3 pl. נָשׁוּ v:[10](https://ref.ly/Jer15:10); *Pt.* נשֶׁה [Ex 22:24](https://ref.ly/Exod22:24) \+ 4 t.; pl. נשִׁים [Ne 5:10](https://ref.ly/Neh5:10), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11) \+ v:[7](https://ref.ly/Neh5:7) Qr (Kt נשְׁאִים, v. I. נשׁא), sf. נוֺשַׁי [Is 50:1](https://ref.ly/Isa50:1); —*lend,* usu. c. ב Pers.: לאֹ־נָשִׁיתִי וְלאֹ־נָשׁוּ־בִי [Je 15:10](https://ref.ly/Jer15:10) *I have not lent, and they have not lent to me*, [Dt 24:11](https://ref.ly/Deut24:11), [Ne 5:7](https://ref.ly/Neh5:7) (Qr; \+ acc. cogn.), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Neh5:10), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11) (both \+ acc. rei); pt. as subst. *creditor, usurer* [Ex 22:24](https://ref.ly/Exod22:24) (E), [2 K 4:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:1), [Is 24:2](https://ref.ly/Isa24:2), [50:1](https://ref.ly/Isa50:1), [Psalm 109:11](https://ref.ly/Ps109:11). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יַשֶּׁה בְּ [Dt 15:2](https://ref.ly/Deut15:2) *who lendeth to* his neighbour; 2 ms. תַּשֶּׁה בְּ [24:10](https://ref.ly/Deut24:10) when *thou lendest to* thy neighbour (\+ acc. cogn.).

> \[Str5386] † \[נְשִׁי] **n.\[m.]** debt;—only sf. 2 fs. נשׁיכי Kt, נַשְׁיֵח Qr [2 K 4:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:7) (so Baer; van d.H. Ginsb נִשְׁיֵח) *pay* (שַׁלְּמִי) *thy debt*.

> \[Str4874] † \[מַשֶּׁה] **n.m.** loan;—only cstr. שָׁמוֺט כָּ־ל בַּעַל מַשֵּׁה יָדוֺ אֲשֶׁר יַשֶּׁה בְּרֵעֵהוּ [Dt 15:2](https://ref.ly/Deut15:2) *every possessor of a loan of his hand shall renounce what he lends to his neighbour* (cf. Dr).

## II נָשָׁה (id: BDB06365)

\[Str5382] †II. \[נָשָׁה] **vb.** forget (NH *id.;* As. *nišîtu* perh. *forgottenness* DlHWB 486; Aram. נְשָׁא (esp. Ithpe., Aph.), ܢܫܳܐ *forget;* Ar. نَسِىَ *forget, neglect;* cf. Eth. ንኣሕለየ፡ *ignoscere, condonare, indulgere* DI633);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 s. וְנָשִׁיתִי אֶתְכֶם נָשׁאֹ [Je 23:39](https://ref.ly/Jer23:39) *I will forget you finally* (but LXX Vulgate SyrVer Che Gie וְנָשָאתִי, sq. וְנָטַשְׁתִּי as v:[33](https://ref.ly/Jer23:33) b, v. III. מַשָּא); נָשִׁיתִי טוֺבָה [La 3:17](https://ref.ly/Lam3:17) *I have forgotten prosperity; Impf.* 1 s. הַאֶשֶּׁה *shall I forget ?* i.e. overlook, rd. by We [Mi 6:10](https://ref.ly/Mic6:10) plausibly for MT הַאִשׁ; *Inf. abs.* נָשׁאֹ (א ´ל) [Je 23:39](https://ref.ly/Jer23:39) (v. supr.). **Niph.** *Impf.* 2 ms. sf. תִּנָּשֵׁנִי [Is 44:21](https://ref.ly/Isa44:21) *thou shalt not be forgotten of me*, but dub.; Ges§ 117\* תִּנְשֵׁנִי (Qal); CheHpt תִּטְּשֵׁנִי (cf. [Dt 32:15](https://ref.ly/Deut32:15)). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. נַשַּׁ֫נִי [Gn 41:51](https://ref.ly/Gen41:51) (E) *God hath made me forget* \+ acc. rei (—ַ in 1st syllable only here, infl. of מְנַשֶּׁה Ges§ 52 m). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הִשָּׁהּ חָכְמָה [Jb 39:17](https://ref.ly/Job39:17) *God caused her* (the ostrich) *to forget* wisdom, i.e. did not endow her with it. *Impf.* יַשֶּׁה לְ מִ־ן [Jb 11:6](https://ref.ly/Job11:6) Eloah *allows a part of* thy guilt *to be forgotten thee*, i.e. does not reckon it to thee in full (LXX \= יִשְׁוֶה לְ מֵאֱלוֺהַּ כַּעֲוֶֺ\+נְ \[Du] *there is equivalent to thee from Eloah, acc. to thine iniquity*, cf. BI CheJob and Sol.; Bu reads נָשָׁה for יַשֶּׁה; Du rejects ver. as gloss).

Ιεσια, LXXL Ιεσσουε. **2\.** יִשִּׁיָּה, a man of Issachar [1 Ch 7:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:3), LXX Εισια, Ιεσια, LXXL Ιωσια (and so in foll.). **3\.** Levites: **a.** [1 Ch 23:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:20), [24:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:25), [24:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:25). **b.** [1 Ch 24:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:21), LXX Ισεια, etc. **4\.** one of those strange wives [Ezr 10:31](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:31), LXX Ιεσς(ε)ια.

> \[Str5384] † נָשֶׁה **n.\[m.]** a vein (or nerve) in the thigh (etym. dub.; on format. cf. LagBN 50; Ar. نَسًا appar. *the portion of the principal vein of the leg which is in the thigh*, vulg. *sciatic vein*, or (mod.) *sciatic nerve* and even *sciatica*, Lane3033);—only גִּיד הַנָּשֶׁה [Gn 32:33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32) a *the sinew of the thigh\-vein* (or *thigh\-nerve*, אֲשֶׁר עַל־כַּף הַיָּרֵח), cf. v:[33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32) b.—On sacredness of thigh, cf. ## נָשַׁךְ (id: BDB06370)

\[Str5391] † נָשַׁךְ **vb.** bite (NH \= BH, but also transp. נְכַשׁ Hiph.); As. *našâku* DlHWB 486; Targum נְכֵית (esp. of snakes); Syr. ܢܟܰܬ, ܢܟܶܬ; Eth. ነሰከ፡ DI642; BaZMG xliii (1889\), 188 cp. Ar. نَكَثَ, *undo* threads, *untwist* rope, *disintegrate, dissolve*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. נָשַׁךְ [Nu 21:9](https://ref.ly/Num21:9), sf. וּנְשָׁכוֺ consec. [Am 5:19](https://ref.ly/Amos5:19), וּנְשָׁכָם consec. [9:3](https://ref.ly/Amos9:3); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִשֹּׁח [Ec 10:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:11), יִשָּׁח [Pr 23:32](https://ref.ly/Prov23:32), sf. יִשְּׁכֶנּוּ [Ec 10:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:8); *Pt. act.* נשֵׁךְ [Gn 49:17](https://ref.ly/Gen49:17); pl. נשְׁכִים [Mi 3:5](https://ref.ly/Mic3:5), sf. נשְׁכֶיְ Hb [2:7](https://ref.ly/Hab2:7); *pass.* נָשׁוּח [Nu 21:8](https://ref.ly/Num21:8); —*bite*, chiefly of serpent, c. acc., [Am 5:19](https://ref.ly/Amos5:19), [9:3](https://ref.ly/Amos9:3), [Gn 49:17](https://ref.ly/Gen49:17) (poem in J), [Nu 21:8](https://ref.ly/Num21:8) (pt. pass., abs.), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Num21:9), [Ec 10:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:8) also v:[11](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:11) (abs.), [Pr 23:32](https://ref.ly/Prov23:32) (sim. of wine); fig. of prophets בְּשִׁנֵּיהֶם ´הַנּ [Mi 3:5](https://ref.ly/Mic3:5) *who are biting with their teeth*, and crying, Peace! fig. of oppression, etc., נשְׁכֶיְ Hb [2:7](https://ref.ly/Hab2:7) (with play on sense *thy debtors, those that give thee interest*). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 pl. וְנִשְּׁכוּ אֶתְכֶם [Je 8:17](https://ref.ly/Jer8:17) *and they shall bite you fatally* (fig.); *Impf.* 3 mpl. וַיְנַשְּׁכוּ אֶת־הָעָם [Nu 21:5](https://ref.ly/Num21:5) *and they bit the people fatally* (both of serpents).

> \[Str5392] † נֶ֫שֶׁךְ **n.\[m.]** interest, usury (lit. *something bitten off*);—´נ abs. [Ex 22:24](https://ref.ly/Exod22:24) \+; cstr. [Dt 23:20](https://ref.ly/Deut23:20); —c. שִים עַ־ל [Ex 22:24](https://ref.ly/Exod22:24) (E); c. לָקַח מִן [Lv 25:36](https://ref.ly/Lev25:36), הֵשִׁיב מִן Ez [18:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:17), לָקַח [22:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:12) (no prep.), בְּנֶשֶׁךְ [Lv 25:37](https://ref.ly/Lev25:37) *at interest*, so [Psalm 15:5](https://ref.ly/Ps15:5), Ez [18:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:8), [18:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:13); ´בְּנ [Pr 28:8](https://ref.ly/Prov28:8) *by usury;* as acc. cogn. [Dt 23:20](https://ref.ly/Deut23:20), [23:20](https://ref.ly/Deut23:20), [23:20](https://ref.ly/Deut23:20).

> \[Str5391] † \[נָשַׁךְ] **vb. denom.** pay, give interest (cf. GerberVerba denom. 89\);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִשָּׁח [Dt 23:20](https://ref.ly/Deut23:20) interest of anything off which *one giveth interest* (cf. Dr). **Hiph.** *make* one *give interest; Impf.* 2 ms. תַּשִּׁיךְ [Dt 23:20](https://ref.ly/Deut23:20) ( \+ acc. cogn.) v:[21](https://ref.ly/Deut23:21), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Deut23:21), all c. לְ Pers.

> \[Str5393] † נִשְׁכָּה **n.f.** chamber (rare ‖ of לִשְׁכָּה, q.v.; cf. WSG 67\);—abs. ´נ [Ne 13:7](https://ref.ly/Neh13:7), sf. נִשְׁכָּתוֺ [Ne 3:30](https://ref.ly/Neh3:30), both rooms of individuals; pl. abs. נְשָׁכוֺת [12:44](https://ref.ly/Neh12:44) (Chr) \= treasuries, storehouses.

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 28:8; Leviticus 25:36; Nehemiah 13:7; Deuteronomy 23:21; Exodus 22:24; Habakkuk 2:7; Numbers 21:5; Deuteronomy 23:20; Psalms 15:5; Ezekiel 22:12; Micah 3:5; Nehemiah 12:44; Ecclesiastes 10:8; Numbers 21:9; Nehemiah 3:30; Genesis 49:17; Ezekiel 18:8; Proverbs 23:32; Jeremiah 8:17; Ezekiel 18:13; Leviticus 25:37; Amos 9:3; Ecclesiastes 10:11; Ezekiel 18:17; Numbers 21:8; Amos 5:19


## נָשַׁל (id: BDB06374)

\[Str5394] † נָשַׁל **vb. 1\.** intrans. slip or drop off; also trans.: **2\.** draw off. **3\.** clear away (NH נָשַׁל prob. intrans., Qal not used, but n. נְשִׁילָה *a falling off*, etc.; Pi. Hiph. *cast off, let fall off*, Niph. *be cast off, fall off;* Targum Aph. אַשֵּׁיל *cast out* (fr. house); Ar. نَسَلَ *excidit* pluma, etc.; but also trans., avis *mutavit rejecitque* plumas (Frey);—نَشَلَ *celeriter extraxit*, etc., is denom. fr. loan\-word acc. to Frä88);—**Qal** *Pf.* וְנָשַׁל consec. [Dt 7:1](https://ref.ly/Deut7:1) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִשַּׁל [Dt 28:40](https://ref.ly/Deut28:40); *Imv.* ms. שַׁל [Ex 3:5](https://ref.ly/Exod3:5), [Jos 5:15](https://ref.ly/Josh5:15); —**1\.** *slip or drop off*, וְנָשַׁל הַבַּרְזֶל מִן־הָעֵץ [Dt 19:5](https://ref.ly/Deut19:5) *and the iron slippeth off from the wood* (i.e. axe\-head from helve); יִשַּׁל זֵיתְֶָ [Dt 28:40](https://ref.ly/Deut28:40) *thine olives shall drop off* (abs.). **2\.** *draw off* sandal fr. (מֵעַל) foot [Ex 3:5](https://ref.ly/Exod3:5) (E), [Jos 5:15](https://ref.ly/Josh5:15) (JE). **3\.** *clear away* nations מִפָּנֶיְ [Dt 7:1](https://ref.ly/Deut7:1), [7:22](https://ref.ly/Deut7:22). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיְנַשֵּׁל [2 K 16:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:6) *he cleared* the Jews *entirely* out of (מִן) Elath.

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 7:22; Deuteronomy 19:5; Deuteronomy 28:40; Deuteronomy 7:1; 2 Kings 16:6; Exodus 3:5; Joshua 5:15


## נשׁם (id: BDB06375)

\[Str5395] † \[נשׁם] **vb.** pant (NH in nn. נְשִׁימָה, נְשָׁמָה, Targum נִשְׁמָא, נִשְׁמְתָא; late Aram. Ithpe.; Syr ܢܫܰܡ *breathe, blow;* Ar. نَسَمَ *gently breathe* (of wind), etc.; v. *seek a thing with labour and perseverance* (Lane3032); نَسَمَةٌ *a soul*, Laneib.);—*pant*, of the deep and strong breathing of a woman in travail; **Qal** *Impf.* 1 S. אֶשֹּׁם [Is 42:14](https://ref.ly/Isa42:14).

> \[Str5397] † נְשָׁמָה **n.f.** breath;—´נ [Dt 20:16](https://ref.ly/Deut20:16) \+ 9 t.; cstr. נִשְׁמַת (cf. LagBN 82\) [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7) \+ 10 t.; sf. נִשְׁמָתִי [Jb 27:3](https://ref.ly/Job27:3); נִשְׁמָתוֺ [Jb 34:14](https://ref.ly/Job34:14); pl. נְשָׁמוֺת [Is 57:16](https://ref.ly/Isa57:16); —**1\.** *breath* of God as hot wind kindling a flame [Is 30:33](https://ref.ly/Isa30:33); as destroying wind [2 S 22:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:16) \= [Psalm 18:16](https://ref.ly/Ps18:16), [Jb 4:9](https://ref.ly/Job4:9); as cold wind producing ice [Jb 32:8](https://ref.ly/Job32:8), [33:4](https://ref.ly/Job33:4). **2\.** *breath of* man [1 K 17:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:17), [Is 42:5](https://ref.ly/Isa42:5), [Jb 27:3](https://ref.ly/Job27:3), [Dn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Dan10:7); breath of life נשׁמת חיים [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7) (J); cf. נִשְׁמַת רוּחַ חַיִּים [Gn 7:22](https://ref.ly/Gen7:22) (J); as breathed in by God it is God's breath in man [Jb 34:14](https://ref.ly/Job34:14), [36:4](https://ref.ly/Job36:4); and is characteristic of man אדם אשׁר נשׁמה באפו [Is 2:22](https://ref.ly/Isa2:22) *man in whose nostrils is* but *a breath* (late gloss). **3\.** syn. נפשׁ כָּל נְשָׁמָה *every breathing thing* [Dt 20:16](https://ref.ly/Deut20:16), [Jos 11:11](https://ref.ly/Josh11:11), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Josh11:14), [1 K 15:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:29); כָּל־הַנְּשָׁמָה [Jos 10:40](https://ref.ly/Josh10:40), [Psalm 150:6](https://ref.ly/Ps150:6); נְשָׁמוֺת [Is 57:16](https://ref.ly/Isa57:16). **4\.** *spirit* of man, ‖רוּחַ, אָדָם ´נ ´נֵר י [Pr 20:27](https://ref.ly/Prov20:27) *the spirit of man is a lamp of* ´י.

> \[Str8580] † תִּנְשֶׁ֫מֶת **n.f.** an animal;—´ת [Lv 11:18](https://ref.ly/Lev11:18), תִּנְשָּׁמֶת [Lv 11:30](https://ref.ly/Lev11:30), [Dt 14:16](https://ref.ly/Deut14:16):—**1\.** unclean bird, the *ibis, water\-hen*, LXX. πορφυρίων, (acc. TristarNHB 249\); or species of *owl* (Di Bu SS); *pelican* (Ges): [Lv 11:18](https://ref.ly/Lev11:18) \= [Dt 14:16](https://ref.ly/Deut14:16). **2\.** unclean שֶׁרֶץ [Lv 11:30](https://ref.ly/Lev11:30), *lizard* (Saad TristrNHB 120\), or *chameleon* (BoHieroz. i. 1078 ff.; ed. Rosenm. ii. 503 ff.), so most; (deriving name from alleged living on air, PlinNH viii. 51; but this not applicable to **1**; cf. LagBN 130f.).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 27:3; 2 Samuel 22:16; Isaiah 42:5; 1 Kings 15:29; Job 4:9; Job 34:14; Job 33:4; Joshua 11:11; Psalms 150:6; Isaiah 42:14; Leviticus 11:18; Job 32:8; Deuteronomy 20:16; Joshua 11:14; Leviticus 11:30; 1 Kings 17:17; Isaiah 2:22; Joshua 10:40; Isaiah 30:33; Genesis 7:22; Job 36:4; Psalms 18:16; Daniel 10:7; Deuteronomy 14:16; Genesis 2:7; Isaiah 57:16; Proverbs 20:27


## נָשַׁף (id: BDB06378)

\[Str5398] † נָשַׁף **vb.** blow (NH \= BH; Chr.\-Pal.\-Aram. ܢܫܦ SchwallyIdiot.58; Ar نَسَفَ (vulg.) *id.* (Dozyii. 667; on usu. mng. cf. WetzstZPV xiv. 7\); ‖ form of נשׁב, cf. JenZA iv (1889\), 268\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. בָּהֶם ´נ [Is 40:24](https://ref.ly/Isa40:24) *he hath blow upon them* (of ´י under fig. of wind); 2 ms. נָשַׁפְתָּ בְרוּחְֲ [Ex 15:10](https://ref.ly/Exod15:10) (song).

> \[Str5399] † נֶ֫שֶׁף **n.m.** twilight (prop. *twilight\-breeze,* cf. לְרוּחַ הַיּוֺם Gn [3:8](https://ref.ly/Gen3:8); NH id.; Targum נְשֶׁף, נִשְׁפָּא;—´נ abs. 1 S [30:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:17) \+, ָ\+נשֶׁף [Je. 13:16](https://ref.ly/Jer13:16); cstr. [Is 21:4](https://ref.ly/Isa21:4); sf. נִשְׁפּוֺ [Jb 3:9](https://ref.ly/Job3:9); —**1\.** *evening twilight* [2 K 7:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:5), [7:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:7) (cf. לַיְלָה v:[12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:12)); opp. בֹּקֶר [Is 5:11](https://ref.ly/Isa5:11); time of concealment [Jb 24:15](https://ref.ly/Job24:15), [Pr 7:9](https://ref.ly/Prov7:9) (\+ עֶרֶב יוֺם, אִישׁוֺן לַיְלָה וַאֲפֵלָה, to emphasize sin that shuns the day); of refreshing [Is 21:4](https://ref.ly/Isa21:4) (חִשְׁקִי ´נ); of stumbling, in dim light [Is 59:10](https://ref.ly/Isa59:10) (opp. צָהֳרַיִם), so הָרֵי ָ\+נשֶׁף [Je 13:16](https://ref.ly/Jer13:16) (both fig.); כּוֺכְבֵי נִשְׁפּוֺ [Jb 3:9](https://ref.ly/Job3:9) *the stars of its twilight* (i.e. of night of Job's birth).—so וְעַד־הָעֶרֶב ´מֵהַנּ לְמָחֳרָתָם 1 S 30 Klo Kit HPS (rd. לְהַחֲרִמָם for ´לְמָח, We Kit Bu); Th, after Luth, sub so BuhlKex SS; Löhr allows either. **2\.** *morning twilight* [Jb 7:4](https://ref.ly/Job7:4) (opp. עֶרֶב). appar. also [Psalm 119:147](https://ref.ly/Ps119:147).

> \[Str3244] † יַנְשׁוּף and ([Is 34:11](https://ref.ly/Isa34:11)) יַנְשׁוֺף **n.\[m.]** a bird (with harsh, *strident* note ? BoHeroz, ii. 284; ed. Rosenm. iii. 29, TristrNHB 192 think of נֶשֶׁף—i.e. a *twilight* bird; on format. cf. LagBN 127 BaNB 231\);—unclean [Lv 11:17](https://ref.ly/Lev11:17), [Dt 14:16](https://ref.ly/Deut14:16); inhabiting deserts [Is 34:11](https://ref.ly/Isa34:11). Prob. a kind of *owl* Bol.c., ‘*Great Owl,*’ Egyptian Eagle\-owl (*bubo ascalaphus*) Tristrl.c. cf. SyrVer Targum Di Dr \- WhiteHpt (Lv), \> LXX. Vulgate Lv Is *ibis*.

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Kings 7:12; Isaiah 5:11; Job 7:4; Isaiah 21:4; Job 3:9; 2 Kings 7:5; Isaiah 34:11; 1 Samuel 30:17; Job 24:15; Leviticus 11:17; Isaiah 59:10; Genesis 3:8; Proverbs 7:9; Isaiah 40:24; Psalms 119:147; Deuteronomy 14:16; 2 Kings 7:7; Jeremiah 13:16; Exodus 15:10


## I נָשַׁק (id: BDB06381)

\[Str5401] †I. נָשַׁק **vb.** kiss (NH *id.,* As. *našâku*, Targum נְשֵׁיק, נְשַׁק, Syr. ܢܫܰܩ, *kiss* (orig. *smell* \[نَشِقَ] acc. to LagNov. Psalt. Spec. 24 f.): Ar. نَسَقَ is *fasten together, arrange in order*, Eth. ንሱቅ pt. *ordine dispositus, apte sertus* DI641, pos. akin to II. נשׁק);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´נ [1 K 19:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:18), [2 S 15:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:5); 3 fs. נָֽשְׁקָה [Pr 7:13](https://ref.ly/Prov7:13); 3 pl. נָשָׁקוּ [Psalm 85:11](https://ref.ly/Ps85:11); *Impf.* יִּשַּׁק [Gn 41:40](https://ref.ly/Gen41:40), וַיִּשַּׁק [27:27](https://ref.ly/Gen27:27) \+; sf. יִשָּׁקֵ֫נִי Ct [1:2](https://ref.ly/Song1:2); 1 s.. cohort. אֶשֳּׁקָ־ה [1 K 19:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:20); 3 mpl. יִשָּׁקָוּן [Ho 13:2](https://ref.ly/Hos13:2), etc.; *Imv.* וּשֲׁקָה [Gn 27:26](https://ref.ly/Gen27:26); *Inf. cstr.* נְשָׁ־ק [2 S 20:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:9); —*kiss*, usu. c. לְ Pers.: [Gn 27:26](https://ref.ly/Gen27:26), [27:29](https://ref.ly/Gen27:29), [50:1](https://ref.ly/Gen50:1), [Ex 4:27](https://ref.ly/Exod4:27) (all J), [Gn 48:10](https://ref.ly/Gen48:10), [Ex 18:7](https://ref.ly/Exod18:7) (both E), [2 S 15:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:5), [14:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:33), [19:40](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:40), [20:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:9), [1 K 19:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:20), [Pr 7:13](https://ref.ly/Prov7:13), [Ru 1:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:9), [1:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:4); so of idol\-worship [1 K 19:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:18) (cf. WeSkizzen iii. 105; Heid. 109\), cf. וַתִּשַּׁק יָדִי לְפִי [Jb 31:27](https://ref.ly/Job31:27) *and my hand hath kissed my mouth;* rarely c. acc. Pers. [1 S 20:41](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:41) (אִישׁ אֶת־רֵעֵהוּ), sf. Pers. [1 S 10:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:1), Ct [1:2](https://ref.ly/Song1:2) (\+ מִגְּשִׁיקוֺת), [8:1](https://ref.ly/Song8:1), also [Gn 33:4](https://ref.ly/Gen33:4) וַֹיִֹּשָֹּׁקֵֹהֹוֹּ (J; Holz E; wd. suspicious, Di rejects); ´עֲגָלִים יִשּׁ [Ho 13:2](https://ref.ly/Hos13:2) *claves do they kiss;* שְׁפָתַיִם יִשָּׁק [Pr 24:26](https://ref.ly/Prov24:26) *lips he kisses;* abs. [Psalm 85:11](https://ref.ly/Ps85:11) (fig., recipr.) .—עַל־פִּיְ יִשַּׁק [Gn 41:40](https://ref.ly/Gen41:40) is dub., Di. ‘to thy mouth all my people shall yield’ (fit, adapt themselves, cf. Ar. √), so Kau BuhlLex, but this meaning uncertain, and text perh. corrupt, v. Di Holz). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיְנַשֵּׁק [Gn 32:1](https://ref.ly/Gen32:1), [45:15](https://ref.ly/Gen45:15), וַיְנַשֶּׁ־ק [29:13](https://ref.ly/Gen29:13); *Imv.* mpl. נַשְּׁק־וּ [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12); *Inf. cstr.* נַשֵּׁק [Gn 31:28](https://ref.ly/Gen31:28); —*kiss* \= **Qal**: c. לְ Pers. [Gn 29:13](https://ref.ly/Gen29:13)(J), [31:28](https://ref.ly/Gen31:28), [32:1](https://ref.ly/Gen32:1), [45:15](https://ref.ly/Gen45:15) (all E); נַשְּׁקו־בַר [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12) is dub., cf. I. בַּר p. 135 a supr. **Hiph.** *Pt.* מַשִּׁיקוֺתּ Ez [3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:13) of wings of cherubim, *gently touching* each other (c. אֶ־ל).

> \[Str5390] † \[נְשִׁיקָה] **n.f.** kiss;—only pl. cstr.: יִשָּׁקֵנִי מִנְּשִׁיקוֺת פִּיהוּ Ct [1:2](https://ref.ly/Song1:2); נְשִׁיקוֺת שוֺנֵא [Pr 27:6](https://ref.ly/Prov27:6) *kisses of an enemy*.

## II נָשַׁק (id: BDB06383)

\[Str5401] †II. \[נָשַׁק] **vb.** exact meaning uncertain; prob, either handle, or be equipped with (cf. perh. Ar. نَسَقَ etc., sub I. נשׁק);—only **Qal** *Pt.* pl. cstr. נשְׁקֵי:—קֶשֶׁת ´נ [1 Ch 12:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:2) *equipped with the bow*, so [2 Ch 17:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:17) (\+ מָגֵן); רוֺמֵי קֶשֶׁת ´נ [Psalm 78:9](https://ref.ly/Ps78:9) is difficult, ´ר apposs. of ´נ acc. to Hup\-Now Bae al. (Ges§ 130 e); del. רוֺמֵי as explan. gloss Hup Hi Che Kau; del. ver. as gloss Hup Kau Bae.

> \[Str5402] † נֶ֫שֶׁק, נֵ֫שֶׁק **n.\[m.]** equipment, weapons (coll.) armoury;—abs. נֶ֫שֶׁק Ez [39:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:9) \+ 2 t., נֵ֫שֶׁק [1 K 10:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:25), [2 Ch 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:24), נָֽשֶׁק [2 K 10:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:2) \+ 2 t., cstr. ´נֶ [Is 22:8](https://ref.ly/Isa22:8), ´נֵ [Jb 20:24](https://ref.ly/Job20:24); —**1\.** *equipment, weapons* [1 K 10:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:25), [2 K 10:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:2), [Is 22:8](https://ref.ly/Isa22:8), [2 Ch 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:24), Ez [39:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:9) (gen. term, foll. by specif.), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:10); בַרְזֶל ´נ [Jb 20:24](https://ref.ly/Job20:24) (קֶשֶׁת נְחשֶׁת); יוֺם נָֽשֶׁק [Psalm 140:8](https://ref.ly/Ps140:8) i.e. day of battle; so ´נ alone [Jb 39:21](https://ref.ly/Job39:21). **2\.** appar. *armoury* [Ne 3:19](https://ref.ly/Neh3:19).

* **Associated Passages:** Nehemiah 3:19; 2 Chronicles 9:24; Ezekiel 39:9; 1 Kings 10:25; 2 Chronicles 17:17; 2 Kings 10:2; Psalms 78:9; Isaiah 22:8; Job 39:21; Job 20:24; Ezekiel 39:10; Psalms 140:8; 1 Chronicles 12:2


## נֶשֶׁר (id: BDB06385)

\[Str5404] † נֶשֶׁר **n.m.**:[Dt 32:11](https://ref.ly/Deut32:11) griffon–vulture, eagle (NH *id.;* As. *nasûru*; Targum נַשְׁרָא; Syr. ܢܶܫܪܳܐ Ar. نَسْرٌ *vulture* (Lane2780), vulg. نِسْرٌ Eth. ንስር፡ DI641; Sab. נסר n.pr. dei, and ביתן נסר DHMZMG. xxix (1875\), 600; xxxvii (1883\), 356\);—abs. ´נ [Ho 8:1](https://ref.ly/Hos8:1) \+; נָֽשֶׁד [Dt 28:49](https://ref.ly/Deut28:49) \+; pl נְשָׁרִים [2 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:23) \+, cstr. נִשְׁרֵי [La 4:19](https://ref.ly/Lam4:19); —sts. (perhaps not always) the *griffon\-vulture* (TristrNHB 172 ff. Dr:[Dt 14:12](https://ref.ly/Deut14:12) NowArchi i. 84 Lane2780), [Mi 1:16](https://ref.ly/Mic1:16) (bald, in sim.) flying swiftly to pery Hb [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hab1:8), [Jb 9:26](https://ref.ly/Job9:26), cf. [Pr 30:17](https://ref.ly/Prov30:17) (´בְּנֵי־נ, unclean [Lv 11:13](https://ref.ly/Lev11:13), [Dt 14:12](https://ref.ly/Deut14:12); soaring [Jb 30:27](https://ref.ly/Job30:27), also in sim. [Pr 23:5](https://ref.ly/Prov23:5), [Is 40:31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31), Ob:[4](https://ref.ly/Obad1:4), building nest high [Je 49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16) (cf. also [Jb 39:27](https://ref.ly/Job39:27)); as swift also [2 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:23), esp. of Bab. and Assyr. invader [Je 4:13](https://ref.ly/Jer4:13), [La 4:19](https://ref.ly/Lam4:19), cf. [Ho 8:1](https://ref.ly/Hos8:1), [Dt 28:49](https://ref.ly/Deut28:49), [Je 48:40](https://ref.ly/Jer48:40), [49:22](https://ref.ly/Jer49:22) (all in comparisons), הַנֶּשֶׁר הַגָּדוֺל Ez [17:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:3) (fig.of Nebuch.), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:7) (fig. of king of Egypt); leaving no trace of light [Pr 30:19](https://ref.ly/Prov30:19); as renewing youth (by moulting) [Psalm 103:5](https://ref.ly/Ps103:5); as training and supporting its young [Ex 19:4](https://ref.ly/Exod19:4) (E), [Dt 32:11](https://ref.ly/Deut32:11) (song); ´פְּני נ of cherubim in vision Ez [1:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:10), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:4).—Only [Mi 1:16](https://ref.ly/Mic1:16) seems to compel ref. to vulture (on eating fresh carrion by eagles v. reff. in Di:[Lv 11:13](https://ref.ly/Lev11:13)), and ´נ may be a more comprehensive word, incl. both vulture and eagle.

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 14:12; Ezekiel 10:4; Jeremiah 4:13; Micah 1:16; Jeremiah 48:40; Hosea 8:1; Proverbs 30:17; Deuteronomy 32:11; Deuteronomy 28:49; Job 9:26; Isaiah 40:31; Leviticus 11:13; Ezekiel 17:3; Jeremiah 49:22; Jeremiah 49:16; Psalms 103:5; Ezekiel 17:7; Ezekiel 1:10; Habakkuk 1:8; Job 39:27; Proverbs 30:19; Exodus 19:4; 2 Samuel 1:23; Proverbs 23:5; Job 30:27; Lamentations 4:19


## נָשַׁת (id: BDB06386)

\[Str5405] † נָשַׁת **vb.** be dry, parched;—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. לְשׁוֺנָם בַּצָּמָא נָשָׁתָּה [Is 41:17](https://ref.ly/Isa41:17) (on D. f. affect. v. Ges§ 20 i); also fig. נָֽשְׁתָה גְבוּרָתָם [Je 51:30](https://ref.ly/Jer51:30), *their might is dry*, i.e. fails. **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. consec. וְנִשְּׁתו־מַיִם מֵהַָ\+יּם [Is 19:5](https://ref.ly/Isa19:5) *and water shall be dried up from the sea*; cf. *Impf.* יַנָּֽתְשׁוּ מַיּם [Je 18:14](https://ref.ly/Jer18:14), where read prob. יִנָּֽשְׁחוּ (Gf Gie al.).

> \[Str5406] † נִשְׁתְּוָן **n.\[m.]** letter (usu. der. fr. Pers. نُبِشْتن, نَتَبَ i.e. *writing*, HoffmZA ii (1887\), 52 yet cf. MeyerJudenthum 22\);—abs. ´נ [Ezr 4:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:7), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:11).

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 51:30; Ezra 4:7; Isaiah 41:17; Isaiah 19:5; Ezra 7:11; Jeremiah 18:14


## נתב (id: BDB06388)

נתב (√ of foll.; Ar. ܢܬܟ *swell forth, become prominent, protuberant*, Lane2760).

> \[Str5410] † נָתִיב **n.m.**:[Jb 28:7](https://ref.ly/Job28:7) path, pathway (as *raised*);—abs. ´נ [Jb 18:10](https://ref.ly/Job18:10) \+ 3 t.; cstr. נְתִיב [Psalm 119:35](https://ref.ly/Ps119:35); sf. 3 fs. נְתִיבָהַ [Pr 12:28](https://ref.ly/Prov12:28) (si vera l., v. infr.; on om. of Mappik v. Ges§ 91 e, v. infr.) ;—*path* [Jb 18:10](https://ref.ly/Job18:10) (fig.) [28:7](https://ref.ly/Job28:7), [41:24](https://ref.ly/Job41:24) (track); [Psalm 78:50](https://ref.ly/Ps78:50) (fig.); נְתִיב מִצְוֺתֶיְ [Psalm 119:35](https://ref.ly/Ps119:35) *the path of thy commands;* דֶּרֶח נְתִיבָה אַל־מָוֶת [Pr 12:28](https://ref.ly/Prov12:28) *the journey of her pathway* is *no\-death!* ‖בְּאֹרַח צְדָקָה חַיִּים Ew Be De. v. אַל a supr.; others rd. אֶ־ל, and either transl. ´נ *by\-paths* (Hi Str), or substitute a syn. of ‘wickedness’ (cf. Now Frankenb; PerlesAnalekten 87 f. נִתְעָבִים after LevyCh WB i. 28 b).

> \[Str5410] † נְתִיבָה **n.f.** id.;—abs. ´נ [Is 43:16](https://ref.ly/Isa43:16), sf. נְתִּיבָתִי [Jb 30:13](https://ref.ly/Job30:13) \+ 2 t. נְתִיבָתָס [Pr 1:15](https://ref.ly/Prov1:15); pl. abs. נְתִיכוֺת [Pr 8:2](https://ref.ly/Prov8:2) \+; cstr. *id.* [Jb 38:20](https://ref.ly/Job38:20) \+; sf. נְתִיבֹתַי [Jb 19:8](https://ref.ly/Job19:8), [La 3:9](https://ref.ly/Lam3:9), נְתִיבֹתָיו [Jb 24:13](https://ref.ly/Job24:13), etc.;—*path:* **1\.** lit הֹלְכֵי נְתִיבוֺת Ju [5:6](https://ref.ly/Judg5:6) *goers on paths*, i.e. travellers, [Pr 8:2](https://ref.ly/Prov8:2), [Is 58:12](https://ref.ly/Isa58:12) (Oort Lag Klo נְתִיצוֺת but against this Du); *path* through sea [Is 43:16](https://ref.ly/Isa43:16) (in fig.); of path to house of (personif.) light and darkness [Jb 38:20](https://ref.ly/Job38:20). **2\.** oft. ‖דֶּרֶח **a.** \= course of life [Jb 19:8](https://ref.ly/Job19:8), [30:13](https://ref.ly/Job30:13), [La 3:9](https://ref.ly/Lam3:9), [Psalm 119:105](https://ref.ly/Ps119:105), [142:4](https://ref.ly/Ps142:4), of Isr. [Ho 2:8](https://ref.ly/Hos2:8); of path of God's appointment [Is 42:16](https://ref.ly/Isa42:16). **b.** of moral action, and character: paths of wisdom [Pr 3:17](https://ref.ly/Prov3:17), justice [8:20](https://ref.ly/Prov8:20) (‖אֹרַח צְדָקָה); light [Jb 24:13](https://ref.ly/Job24:13); עוֺלָם ´נ [Je 6:16](https://ref.ly/Jer6:16) (‖דֶּרֶח הַטּוֺב); path of wicked [Pr 1:15](https://ref.ly/Prov1:15), [Is 59:8](https://ref.ly/Isa59:8) (crooked), path of adulteress [Pr 7:25](https://ref.ly/Prov7:25); נְתִיבוֺת [Je 18:15](https://ref.ly/Jer18:15) is rendered ‘by\-paths,’ by Gf Rothst al.; \< Gie makes cstr. bef. foll. סְלוּלָה דֶּרֶח לאֹ.—נְתִיבָהַ [Pr 12:28](https://ref.ly/Prov12:28) v. נָתִיב.

## נָתַךְ (id: BDB06393)

\[Str5413] † \[נָתַךְ **vb.** pour forth (intrans.), be poured out (NH *id.*, Niph. *flow*, Hiph. *melt* (trans.); As *natâku, flow* DlHWB 487 JägerBAS ii. 292; Targum Aph. *pour out*, esp. *melt;* Zinj. ליתכה (Haph.) NöZMG xlvii (1893\), 98 Cook 83; Syr. نوشتن (rare) PS2480);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּתַּח [Je 42:18](https://ref.ly/Jer42:18) \+ 3 t., וַתִּתַּח [Je 44:6](https://ref.ly/Jer44:6), [Dn 9:11](https://ref.ly/Dan9:11); 3 mpl. וַיִּתְּכוּ [Jb 3:34](https://ref.ly/Job3:34); —*pour forth*, alw. fig., כַמַּיִם שַׁאֲגֹתָי ´וַיִתּ [Jb 3:24](https://ref.ly/Job3:24) *and my groans have poured forth like water;* usu. of divine wrath (חֵמָה) [Je 42:18](https://ref.ly/Jer42:18) (c. עַ־ל Pers.), [44:6](https://ref.ly/Jer44:6) (abs.), [2 Ch 12:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:7), [34:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:25) (both c. בְּ loc. *against*); cf. also [Dn 9:11](https://ref.ly/Dan9:11) v:[27](https://ref.ly/Dan9:27) (both c. עַ־ל Pers.) **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִתַּח [Ex 9:33](https://ref.ly/Exod9:33) \+ 2 t.; 3 fs. נִתְּכָה [Na 1:6](https://ref.ly/Nah1:6), [2 Ch 34:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:21), וְנִתְּכָה consec. Ez [24:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11); 2 mpl. וְנִתַּכְתֶּם Ez [22:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:21); *Pt.* f. נִתֶּ֫בֶת [Je 7:20](https://ref.ly/Jer7:20):—*be poured,* of rain, אָרְצָה [Ex 9:33](https://ref.ly/Exod9:33) (J), cf. מַיִם עֲלֵיהֶם ´נ [2 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:10); *be poured out*, of wrath of ´י [Na 1:6](https://ref.ly/Nah1:6) (abs.), [Je 7:20](https://ref.ly/Jer7:20) (c. אֶ־ל loc.), [42:18](https://ref.ly/Jer42:18) (c. עַ־ל Pers.), [2 Ch 34:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:21) (c. בְּ Pers.); *be poured forth* i.e. melted, annihilated in the midst of (בְּתוֺח) the fire of ´י's fury Ez [22:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:21) (subj. persons), so [24:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11) (subj. impurity, fig. of caldron). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. הִתִּיכוּ עֲבָדֶיְ אֶת־הַכֶּסֶף [2 K 22:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:9) *thy servants have poured out the money* (‖ [2 Ch 34:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:12) infr.); 1 s. וְהִתַּכְתִּ֫י אֶתְכֶם consec. Ez [22:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20) *and I will, melt you* (fig. of judgment); *Inf. cstr.* לְהַנְתִּיח Ez [22:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:20) *in order to melt* it (i.e. metal in furnace ; Co after LXX. SyrVer reads לְהִנָּתֵח **Niph.** *Inf.,* so Berthol, cf. Toy); *Impf.* 3 mpl. וַיַּתִּיכוּ [2 Ch 34:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:17) (‖ [2 K 22:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:9) supr.); הֲלאֹ כֶחָלָב תַּתִּיכֵנִי [Jb 10:10](https://ref.ly/Job10:10) *dost* (\= didst) *thou not pour me out like milk?* (of beginnings of life). **Hoph.** *Impf.* 2 mpl. כֵּן תֻּתְּכוּ Ez [22:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:22) *so shall ye be melted* (fig. of judgement).

> \[Str5416] נָתָן:42 **n. pr. m.** LXX Ναθαν;—† **1\.** a son of David [2 S 5:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:14) \= [1 Ch 3:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:15), [1 Ch 14:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:4). **2\.** the prophet of David's time [2 S 7:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:2), [7:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:3), [7:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:4), [7:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:17) \= [1 Ch 17:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:1), [17:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:2), [17:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:3), [17:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:15), [2 S 12:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:1) \+ 6 t. 2 S 12, [1 K 1:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:8) \+ 10 t. 1 K 1, [2 Ch 29:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:23), [Psalm 51:2](https://ref.ly/Ps51:2) (title); דִּבְרֵי נָתָן הַנָּבִיא as name of a book [1 Ch 29:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:29), [2 Ch 9:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:29). † **3\.** father of one of David's heroes [2 S 23:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:36). † **4\.** father of officers of Solomon, [1 K 4:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:5), [4:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:5) (\= **2** ?). † **5\.** name in Judah: **a.** [1 Ch 2:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:36), [2:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:36). **b.** [11:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:38). † **6\.** companion of Ezra from Babylon [Ezr 8:16](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:16). † **7\.** one of those who took strange wives [10:39](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:39). † **8\.** head of a family [Zc 12:12](https://ref.ly/Zech12:12).

> \[Str5419] † נְתַן־מֶ֫לֶךְ **n. pr. m.** an official of Judah, Josiah's time [2 K 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:11); LXX Ναθαν βασιλέΩς (τοῦ εὐνούχου), LXXL Ναθαν εὐνούχου τοῦ βασιλέΩς.

[1 Ch 9:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:2)), v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:20), [Ne 10:29](https://ref.ly/Neh10:29), [11:3](https://ref.ly/Neh11:3); living on the Ophel [3:26](https://ref.ly/Neh3:26), [11:21](https://ref.ly/Neh11:21), with their own overseers v:[21](https://ref.ly/Neh11:21), having a house known (in part) by their name [3:31](https://ref.ly/Neh3:31).—On ´נ v. further BauPriesterthum 100, 142 f.

N. 264\);—LXX Ναθαναηλ;—**1\.** a chief of Issachar [Nu 1:8](https://ref.ly/Num1:8), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Num2:5), [7:18](https://ref.ly/Num7:18), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Num7:23), [10:15](https://ref.ly/Num10:15). **2\.** 4th son of Jesse [1 Ch 2:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:14). **3\.** name of priests: **a.** [1 Ch 15:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:24). **b.** [Ne 12:21](https://ref.ly/Neh12:21). **4\.** priest's son (who had strange wife) [Ezr 10:22](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:22), perh. \= [Ne 12:36](https://ref.ly/Neh12:36). **5\.** Levite name: **a.** [1 Ch 24:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:6). **b.** [26:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:4). **c.** [2 Ch 35:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:9). **6\.** a prince of Jehoshaphat [2 Ch 17:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:7).

> \[Str5418] † נְתַנְיָ֫הוּ, נְתַנְיָה **n. pr. m.** (cf. GrayProp. N. 294\), LXX Ναθανιας;—**1\.** נְתַנְיָ֫הנּ father of Jehudi [Je 36:14](https://ref.ly/Jer36:14). **2\.** ´נ father of Ishmael Gedaliah's murderer [Je 40:8](https://ref.ly/Jer40:8), [41:9](https://ref.ly/Jer41:9) \= נְתַנְיָה [2 K 25:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:23), [25:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:25), [Je 40:14](https://ref.ly/Jer40:14), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Jer40:15), [41:1](https://ref.ly/Jer41:1) \+ 9 t. Je 41. **3\.** נְתַנְיָ֫הוּ Levite name: **a.** [2 Ch 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:8). **b.** [1 Ch 25:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:12) \= נְתַנְיָה v:[2](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:2).

> \[Str4976] † I. מַתָּן **n. m.**:[Pr 18:16](https://ref.ly/Prov18:16) **coll.** gift(s);—abs. ´מ [Gn 34:12](https://ref.ly/Gen34:12) \+ 2 t.; cstr. *id.* [Pr 18:16](https://ref.ly/Prov18:16) (v. Baerad loc. Ges§ 92 g Köii 1, 98\); sf. מַתָּנָם [Nu 18:11](https://ref.ly/Num18:11); —marriage\-*gifts* [Gn 34:12](https://ref.ly/Gen34:12) (J; \+ מֹהַר); *offerings* [Nu 18:11](https://ref.ly/Num18:11) (P; ´תְּרוּמַת מ); elsewh. gen., *gifts, presents*, [Pr 18:16](https://ref.ly/Prov18:16), [21:14](https://ref.ly/Prov21:14) (‖שֹׁחַד); אִישׁ מַתָּן [19:6](https://ref.ly/Prov19:6) \= *a giver of gifts*.

> \[Str4977] † II. מַתָּן **n. pr. m. 1\.** priest of Baa1 at Jerusalem [2 K 11:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:18) \= [2 Ch 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:17); LXX Μαγθαν, Ματθαν, LXXL Ματθαν. **2\.** father of Shephatiah, in Judah [Je 38:1](https://ref.ly/Jer38:1); LXX Ναθαν, Μαθθā.

> \[Str4979] † I. מַתָּנָה **n. f.** gift;—abs. ´מ [Nu 18:6](https://ref.ly/Num18:6) \+ 4 t.; cstr. מַתְּנַת [Dt 16:17](https://ref.ly/Deut16:17); pl. מַתָּנֹת [Gn 25:6](https://ref.ly/Gen25:6) \+, cstr. מַתְּנֹת [Ex 28:38](https://ref.ly/Exod28:38), sf. מַתְּנֹתֵיכֶם Ez [20:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:31) \+, etc.;—*gift*, [Gn 25:6](https://ref.ly/Gen25:6) (J) Ez [46:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:16), [46:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:17), [2 Ch 21:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:3); offerings to ´י [Ex 28:38](https://ref.ly/Exod28:38) (מַתְּנֹת קָדְשֵׁיכֶם), [Lv 23:38](https://ref.ly/Lev23:38) (disting. from נֶדֶר and נְדָבָה), [18:29](https://ref.ly/Lev18:29) (cf. מַתָּנָם v:[11](https://ref.ly/Lev18:11); —all P); rejected (idolatrous) offerings Ez [20:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:26), [20:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:31), [20:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:39); אִישׁ כְּמַתְּנַת יָדוֺ [Dt 16:17](https://ref.ly/Deut16:17), *each man acc. to the gift of his hand*, i.e. acc. to his ability; cf. בָאָדָם ´לָקַחְתָּ מ [Psalm 68:19](https://ref.ly/Ps68:19) *thou* (´י) *hast received gifts among men* (i.e. either tribute gained by battle, cf. Che, or the offerings of those who turn to ´י Hup\-Now; \> *gifts consisting of men* JDMich Hgst Bö Ew DeWette); ´מ used of Levites, as God's *gift* to Aaron and his sons, [Nu 18:6](https://ref.ly/Num18:6), whose priesthood is ´עֲבֹדַת מ v:[7](https://ref.ly/Num18:7), i.e. a service which is a gift from God; מַתָּנוֺת לָאֶבְיֹנִים [Est 9:22](https://ref.ly/Esth9:22) *gifts to the poor;* \= bribe [Pr 15:27](https://ref.ly/Prov15:27), [Ec 7:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:7).

> \[Str4980] † II. מַתָּנָה **n. pr. loc** a station of Isr. E. of Jordan [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18), [21:19](https://ref.ly/Num21:19) (JE), LXX Μανθαναειν.

> \[Str4982] מַתְּנַי v. מַתַּנְיָה infr.

> \[Str4991] † \[מַתַּת] **n. f.** id.;—abs. מַתָּת [1 K 13:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:7); cstr. מַתַּת [Pr 25:14](https://ref.ly/Prov25:14) \+ 4 t.;—*gift* \= reward [1 K 13:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:7); מַתַּת יָדוֺ *the gift of his hand*, i.e. of his power, what he is able to give Ez [46:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:5), [46:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:11) (cf. מַתָּנָה and יָד **2**); אֱלֽהִים ´מ [Ec 3:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:13) *the gift of God* (of the enjoyment of man), so [5:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:18); שָׁקֶר ´מ [Pr 25:14](https://ref.ly/Prov25:14) *a gift of falsity*, i.e. one promised and not given.

> \[Str4983] † מַתַּנְיָ֫הוּ, מַתַּנְיָה **n. pr. m.** (cf. GrayProp. N. 294, No. 82\);—LXX Μαθθας, Μανθανιας, Ματθανιας, etc.;—**1\.** מַתַּנְיָה, last king of Judah, name changed to Zedekiah [2 K 24:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:17). **2\.** מַתַּנְיָ֫הוּ, Levite name: **a.** son of Heman [1 Ch 25:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:4). **b.** Asaphite [1 Ch 25:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:16). **c.** *id.,* [2 Ch 29:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:13) (Hezekiah's time). **3\.** מַתַּנְיָה, Levite name: **a.** Asaphite [1 Ch 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:15), [Ne 11:17](https://ref.ly/Neh11:17), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Neh11:22), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Neh12:8), [12:35](https://ref.ly/Neh12:35), perh. \= **2 b, c** (but much confusion and uncertainty; e.g. ´מ is of Hezekiah's time [2 Ch 29:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:13), and 4th generation before Neh., [Ne 11:12](https://ref.ly/Neh11:12), [12:35](https://ref.ly/Neh12:35); while contemp. of Zerub. [12:8](https://ref.ly/Neh12:8) and of [Neh. 11:17](https://ref.ly/Neh11:17)). **b.** other Levites: (1\) [2 Ch 20:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:14), (2\) [Ne 12:25](https://ref.ly/Neh12:25), (3\) [13:13](https://ref.ly/Neh13:13). **4\.** מַתַּנְיָה, name of several who took strange wives: **a.** [Ezr 10:26](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:26). **b.** v:[27](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:27). **c.** v:[30](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:30). **d.** v:[37](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:37).

> \[Str4982] † מַתְּנַי **n. pr. m.** (abbrev. fr. foregoing);—LXX Μαθ(θ)ανια, Μαθθαναι;—**1\.** a priest [Ne 12:19](https://ref.ly/Neh12:19). **2\.** name of two who took strange wives: **a.** [Ezr 10:33](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:33). **b.** v:[37](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:37).

> \[Str4993] † מַתִּתְיָ֫הוּ, מַתִּתְיָה **n. pr. m.** (cf. GrayProp. N. 294, No. 81\);—LXX Ματταθιας, etc;—**1\.** a Levitical musician, מַתִּתְיָ֫הוּ [1 Ch 15:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:18), [15:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:21), [25:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:3), [25:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:21) \= מַתִּתְיָה [16:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:5). **2\.** מַתִּתְיָה, Levitical name [1 Ch 9:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:31). **3\.** ´מ, attendant of Ezra (also Levite ?) [Ne 8:4](https://ref.ly/Neh8:4). **4\.** one of those who took strange wives [Ezr 10:43](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:43).

> \[Str4992] † מַתַּתָּה **n. pr. m.** one of those who took foreign wives [Ezr 10:33](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:33), LXX Αθα, A LXXL Μαθθαθ(α).

## נָתַס (id: BDB06411)

\[Str5420] † \[נָתַס] **vb.** tear or break down (\= נָחַץ);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. נָֽתְסוּ נְתִיבָתִי [Jb 30:13](https://ref.ly/Job30:13) *they have broken down my path*, made it impassable (fig.).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 30:13


## נָתַע (id: BDB06412)

\[Str5421] † \[נָתַע] **vb.** break, break down, or out;—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. שִׁנֵּי כְפִירִים נִתָּעוּ [Jb 4:10](https://ref.ly/Job4:10), —Aramaic (?) form, or \< textual error for נִתָּצוּ.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 4:10


## נָתַץ (id: BDB06413)

\[Str5422] † נָתַץ **vb.** pull down, break down (NH Hoph., and deriv.; cf. perh. Eth. ነሠተ፡ (with transp. and weakening of sibilant), *destruere, demoliri, excidere* DI634);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´נ Ju [6:30](https://ref.ly/Judg6:30) \+, 3 pl. נָֽתְצוּ [2 K 25:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:10), [Je 52:14](https://ref.ly/Jer52:14), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִתֹּץ Ez [26:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:9) \+ v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:12) (Co, for MT יִתְּצוּ); וַיִּתֹּץ Ju [9:45](https://ref.ly/Judg9:45), [2 K 23:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:7); sf. יִתְּצֵנִי [Jb 19:10](https://ref.ly/Job19:10), יִתָּצְ [Psalm 52:7](https://ref.ly/Ps52:7); 1 S. אֶתֹּץ Ju [8:9](https://ref.ly/Judg8:9); 3 mpl. וַיִּתְּצוּ [2 K 10:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:27), [10:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:27); 2 mpl. תִּתָֹּצוּ [Dt 7:5](https://ref.ly/Deut7:5), תִּתֹּצָוּן [Ex 34:13](https://ref.ly/Exod34:13), Ju [2:2](https://ref.ly/Judg2:2); וַתִּתְַצוּ [Is 22:2](https://ref.ly/Isa22:2), etc.; *Imv.* נְתֹץ [Psalm 58:7](https://ref.ly/Ps58:7); *Inf. cstr.* לִנְתַוֺץ [Je 1:10](https://ref.ly/Jer1:10), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Jer18:7), לִנְתַֹץ [31:28](https://ref.ly/Jer31:28) (on תַ v. Ges§ 45 g); *Pt. pass.* pl. הַנְּתֻצִים [Je 33:4](https://ref.ly/Jer33:4); —**1\.** lit. *pull down* a structure (acc.): altar Ju [2:2](https://ref.ly/Judg2:2), [6:30](https://ref.ly/Judg6:30), [6:31](https://ref.ly/Judg6:31), [6:32](https://ref.ly/Judg6:32), [Ex 34:13](https://ref.ly/Exod34:13) (J), [Dt 7:5](https://ref.ly/Deut7:5), [2 K 23:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:12); high place (במה) v:[8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:8), בָּמָה \+ מִזְבֵּחַ v:[15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:15); מַצֵּבָה [10:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:27); בֵּית הַבַּעַל v:[27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:27), [11:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:18) \= [2 Ch 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:17), cf. [2 K 23:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:7); a tower Ju [8:9](https://ref.ly/Judg8:9), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Judg8:17), cf. Ez [26:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:9) (בְּחַרְבוֺתָיו); (any) house [Is 22:10](https://ref.ly/Isa22:10), Ez [26:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:12), [Lv 14:45](https://ref.ly/Lev14:45), so הַנְּחֻצִים [Je 33:4](https://ref.ly/Jer33:4) (strangely \+ אֶל, cf. Gf Gie); city\-wall [Je 39:8](https://ref.ly/Jer39:8), [2 K 25:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:10) \= [Je 52:14](https://ref.ly/Jer52:14); city Ju [9:45](https://ref.ly/Judg9:45). **2\.** fig.: **a.** *pull down* a nation, break its power, וּלְהַאֲבִיד ´וְלִנְתוֺשׁ וְלִנ (פָוְלַהֲרוֺס) (obj. not expressed) [Je 1:10](https://ref.ly/Jer1:10) (so [Ecclus 49:7](https://ref.ly/Sir49:7) b), \= [18:7](https://ref.ly/Sir18:7), cf. [31:18](https://ref.ly/Sir31:18). **b.** an individual, subj. God, יִתְּצֵנִי סָבִיב [Jb 19:10](https://ref.ly/Job19:10), יִתָּצְ [Psalm 52:7](https://ref.ly/Ps52:7). **c.** jaw\-teeth of lions (metaph.) [Psalm 58:7](https://ref.ly/Ps58:7) (*break down, break off*, so only here, but v. נָתַע). **Niph.** *be pulled, broken, down: Pf.* 3 pl. ´נִתְּצוּ מִפְּנֵי י [Je 4:26](https://ref.ly/Jer4:26) (of cities), so מִמֶּנּוּ ´נ [Na 1:6](https://ref.ly/Nah1:6) (of rocks). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִתַּץ [2 Ch 33:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:3); 3 pl. וְנִתְּצוּ Ez [16:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:39) (consec.); 2 mpl. וְנִתַּצְתֶּם [Dt 12:3](https://ref.ly/Deut12:3); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיְנַתֵּץ [2 Ch 34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7); 3 mpl. וַיְנַתְּצוּ [31:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:1) \+ 2 t.;—*tear down* (chiefly late): c. acc. altars [Dt 12:3](https://ref.ly/Deut12:3), \+ בָּמוֺת [2 Ch 31:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:1), \+ הַחַמָּנִים, etc., [34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4), \+ אֲשֵׁרִים v:[7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7); בָּמוֺת alone [33:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:3), רָמֹתַיִח Ez [16:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:39); city wall [2 Ch 36:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:19). **Pu.** *be torn down: Pf.* 3 ms. נֻתַּץ Ju [6:28](https://ref.ly/Judg6:28) (of altar \+ אֲשֵׁרָה). **Hoph.** (or **Qal** *pass.* Ges§ 53 u) *be broken down, broken*, only *Impf.* 3 ms. ייֻתָּץ [Lv 11:35](https://ref.ly/Lev11:35) (of תַּנּוּר, v. Dr\-WhiteHpt, and כִּירַיִם, v. supr., p. 468 b).
