## נָתַק (id: BDB06414)

\[Str5423] † \[נָתַק] **vb.** pull, draw, tear away, apart, off (NH *id.;* Targum נְתַק in der. species (rare), *pull off, tear off;* Chr Pal Aram. ܢܬܩ *shake off*, SchwallyIdiot. 58; Ar. نَتَقَ *pull off, draw out, shake;* DI660 cp. Eth. ነተገ, ነትገ፡ *detrahere*, etc.);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 pl. sf. וּנְתַקְּנוּהוּ consec. Ju [20:32](https://ref.ly/Judg20:32) (on d. f. dirim. v. Ges§ 20 h); *Impf.* 1 S. sf. אֶתְּקֶנְָ֑ [Je 22:24](https://ref.ly/Jer22:24) (cf. Ges§ 58, i); *Pt. pass.* נָתוּק [Lv 22:24](https://ref.ly/Lev22:24); —**1\.** *draw away* warriors from (מִן) city, unto (אֶ־ל) high road Ju [20:32](https://ref.ly/Judg20:32). **2\.** *draw* or *pull off* ring from (מִן) finger [Je 22:24](https://ref.ly/Jer22:24) (fig. of rejection of king of Judah by ´י). **3\.** *pull, tear away* [Lv 22:24](https://ref.ly/Lev22:24) (pass., of testicles, \+ מָעוּח, כָּתוּת, כָּרוּת). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִתַּק [Is 5:27](https://ref.ly/Isa5:27); 3 pl. נִתְּקוּ [Jos 4:18](https://ref.ly/Josh4:18), [Jb 17:11](https://ref.ly/Job17:11), נִתָּקוּ [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29), [10:20](https://ref.ly/Jer10:20); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִנָּתֵק Ju [16:9](https://ref.ly/Judg16:9) \+ 2 t.; 3 mpl. וַיִּנָּֽתְקוּ [Jos 8:16](https://ref.ly/Josh8:16), יִנָּתֵָקוּ [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20); —**1\.** *be drawn away* from (מִן) city [Jos 8:16](https://ref.ly/Josh8:16) (cf. **Qal 1**); of soles of feet, *be drawn out* (from water) unto (אֶ־ל) dry ground [4:18](https://ref.ly/Josh4:18). **2\.** *be torn apart*, or *in two, snapped:* of sandal\-thong [Is 5:27](https://ref.ly/Isa5:27), strand of tow Ju [16:9](https://ref.ly/Judg16:9) (sim.), cord [Ec 4:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:12); esp. tent\-cord (in fig.) [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20), [Je 10:20](https://ref.ly/Jer10:20), so יִנָּתֵק מֵאָהֳלוֺ מִבְטַחוֺ [Jb 18:14](https://ref.ly/Job18:14); metaph. of plans (זִמֹּת) [17:11](https://ref.ly/Job17:11). **3\.** *be separated*, in smelting, fig., רָעִים לאֹ נִתָּקוּ [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29). **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 S. נִתַּקְתִּי [Je 2:20](https://ref.ly/Jer2:20); 3 pl. נִתְּקוּ [5:5](https://ref.ly/Jer5:5); *Impf.* 3 ms. יְנַתֵּק Ez [17:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:9), [Psalm 107:14](https://ref.ly/Ps107:14), וַיְנַתֵּק Ju [16:9](https://ref.ly/Judg16:9), sf. וַיְנַתְּקֵם v:[12](https://ref.ly/Judg16:12); 2 fs. תְּנַתֵָּקִי Ez [23:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:34); 1 S. אֲנַתֵּק [Na 1:13](https://ref.ly/Nah1:13); 1 pl. נְנַתְּקָה cohort. [Psalm 2:3](https://ref.ly/Ps2:3); 2 mpl. תְּנַתֵָּקוּ [Is 58:6](https://ref.ly/Isa58:6); —**1\.** *tear apart, snap*, c. acc. הַיְתָרִים Ju [16:9](https://ref.ly/Judg16:9) *the cords*, v:[12](https://ref.ly/Judg16:12) (\+ מֵעַל זְרֹעֹתָיו); esp. c. acc. מוֺסֵרוֺת, *bonds* (fig.) [Na 1:13](https://ref.ly/Nah1:13), [Je 2:20](https://ref.ly/Jer2:20), [5:5](https://ref.ly/Jer5:5), [30:8](https://ref.ly/Jer30:8), [Psalm 2:3](https://ref.ly/Ps2:3), [107:14](https://ref.ly/Ps107:14); obj. מוֺטָה [Is 58:6](https://ref.ly/Isa58:6) (‖פַּתֵּחַ, and הַתֵּר אֲגֻדּוֺת מוֺטָה). **2\.** *tear out, up*, obj. roots Ez [17:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:9) (in fig.); *tear out, away*, obj. breasts [23:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:34) (in violent fig. of Jerus. as drunken woman). **Hiph. 1\.** *Inf. cstr.* sf. הַתִּיקֵנוּ: *draw away* warriors from (מִן) city [Jos 8:6](https://ref.ly/Josh8:6) (\= **Qal 1**). **2\.** *Imv.* ms. sf. הַתִּקֵם *drag them away* like sheep to slaughter [Je 12:3](https://ref.ly/Jer12:3) (of ´י's dealing with wicked). **Hoph.** *Pf.* (si vera l.) הָנְתְּקוּ מִן־הָעִיר Ju [20:31](https://ref.ly/Judg20:31) *they were drawn away from the city* (cf. **Niph. 1**), but prob. gloss, v. GFM.

> \[Str5424] † נֶ֫תֶק **n. m.**:[Lv 13:32](https://ref.ly/Lev13:32) scab, an eruption of skin, on head or in beard, causing suspicion of leprosy (lit. *a tearing off*, i.e. what one is inclined to scratch or tear away, cf. *scabies* fr. *scabere, Krätze* fr. *kratzen*, etc., v. Di:[Lv 13:30](https://ref.ly/Lev13:30));—´נ abs. [Lv 13:30](https://ref.ly/Lev13:30), [13:32](https://ref.ly/Lev13:32), [13:32](https://ref.ly/Lev13:32), [13:33](https://ref.ly/Lev13:33), [13:34](https://ref.ly/Lev13:34), [13:34](https://ref.ly/Lev13:34), [13:35](https://ref.ly/Lev13:35), [13:36](https://ref.ly/Lev13:36), [13:37](https://ref.ly/Lev13:37), [13:37](https://ref.ly/Lev13:37); ָ\+נתֶק [14:54](https://ref.ly/Lev14:54); נֶגַע הַנֶּתֶק [13:31](https://ref.ly/Lev13:31), [13:31](https://ref.ly/Lev13:31) (all P).

## I נָתַר (id: BDB06416)

\[Str5425] † I. \[נָתַר] **vb.** spring or start up;—**Qal** *Impf.* וְיִתַּר מִמְּקוֺמוֺ [Jb 37:1](https://ref.ly/Job37:1) *yea, it* (the heart) *starts up from its place* (‖יֶחֱרַד). **Pi.** *Inf.* לְנַתֵּר בָּהֵן עַל־הָאָרֶץ [Lv 11:21](https://ref.ly/Lev11:21) (P), *to leap with them* (i.e. its legs) *upon the earth* (of locust). **Hiph.** *Impf.* וַיַּתֵּר גּוֺיִם Hb [3:6](https://ref.ly/Hab3:6) he (´י) looked, *and made nations start up*.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 37:1; Leviticus 11:21; Habakkuk 3:6


## II נָתַר (id: BDB06417)

\[Str5425] † II. \[נָתַר] **vb.** be free, loose (? usu. placed sub I., but connexion not obvious; cf. also NH Niph. *free oneself*, Hiph. \= BH; perh. cp. Ar. نَتَرَ *rend* a garment, *break* string (of bow), etc.);—**Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. juss. יַתֵּר [Jb 6:9](https://ref.ly/Job6:9) sf. וַיַּתִּירֵהוּ [Psalm 105:20](https://ref.ly/Ps105:20); *Inf. abs.* הַתֵּר [Is 58:6](https://ref.ly/Isa58:6); *Pt.* מַתִּיר [Psalm 146:7](https://ref.ly/Ps146:7); —**1\.** *unfasten, loosen*, c. acc. rei; thongs of yoke [Is 58:6](https://ref.ly/Isa58:6) (‖פַּתֵּחַ, תְּנַתֵָּקוּ). **2\.** *set free, unbind* c. acc. Pers., prisoners [Psalm 105:20](https://ref.ly/Ps105:20) (‖וַיְפַתְּחֵהוּ), [146:7](https://ref.ly/Ps146:7); fig. יַתֵּר יָדוֺ [Jb 6:9](https://ref.ly/Job6:9) *that he would let loose his hand* and cut me off,—וַיַּתֵּר [2 S 22:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:33) was poss. connected by Mass. with תּוּר (v. Comm.), but rd. וַיִּתֵּן (as ‖ [Psalm 18:33](https://ref.ly/Ps18:33)).

> \[Str5427] † נֶ֫תֶר **n.\[m.]** natron, or carbonate of soda, a mineral alkali (NH \= BH; Aram. נִתְרָא, ܢܶܬܪܳܐ; cf. Gk. νίτρον, λίτρον, Lat. *nitrum* (v. Lexx.));—אִם־תְּכַבְּסִי בַּנֶּתֶר [Je 2:22](https://ref.ly/Jer2:22) though thou wash thyself with *natron* (fig.; ‖בֹּרִית; on use of ‘ nitrum ’ for handwashing among Greeks v. MeinekeFragm. Comicorum ii. 638\); חֹמֶץ עַ־לָ\+נתֶר [Pr 25:20](https://ref.ly/Prov25:20) (fig. of the incompatible).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 105:20; Proverbs 25:20; 2 Samuel 22:33; Isaiah 58:6; Psalms 146:7; Job 6:9; Jeremiah 2:22; Psalms 18:33


## נָתַשׁ (id: BDB06419)

\[Str5428] † נָתַשׁ **vb.** pull or pluck up, root out, esp. in Je and later (Ecclus *Impf.* 3 fs. תנתש נטע [3:9](https://ref.ly/Sir3:9); NH \= BH; Targum נְתַשׁ *id.;* Syr. ܢܬܰܫ *tear off, away;* Ar. نَتَشَ is loan\-wd.
